# Archetype forensic audit (audit 3): every archetype against its spirit, its lineage and the FMP catalogue

Scope: all 213 archetypes in `archetype_tags.py` at the time of the audit (44 multibagger-study, 52 forensic / exceptional-return, 73 singles, 44 named-investor and uploaded-document families), each read against (1) the spirit it was written to capture (the study report, the practitioner document, the compendium or master-reference entry), (2) the lineage of every input it reads (EDGAR, Yahoo, the FMP annual / quarterly / weekly layers, the event feeds), (3) the prior audits A1 (`docs/archetype_upgrade_audit.md`) and A2 (`docs/fmp_catalog_upgrade_audit.md`), and (4) the FMP catalogue (`fmp_catalog.md`) for incremental augmentations. No back-testing or statistical analysis was requested or done; every finding is a reading of the code and its sources. The seven slice reports were written by seven independent reviewers and are reproduced in full below; this head records what was then changed in the engine and what was deliberately left open.

## 1. Executive summary

1. The engine codes the FORM of its sources faithfully where the source is a checklist (Cundill, Greenblatt, Frazzini-Pedersen, the Lynch multiples, the lindy family, the multibagger studies). The divergences the reviewers found are concentrated in five recurring mechanisms, and almost all of them were wiring rather than fetching: the data to fix them was already on disk.
2. Recurring mechanism 1, the 99 fill. `net_debt_ebitda` is filled 99 both when net debt was never fetched and when EBITDA is at or below zero, so `nde <= X` failed every loss-maker (tangible_value, capital_discipline, post_reorg) and `~(nde > X & nde < 90)` passed every one (regime_cyclical, fixed_cost, the XR trough gates). Fixed: a balance-sheet leverage read (total debt / assets, debt / equity, a known net-cash position) wherever nde is unmeasurable, through one helper.
3. Recurring mechanism 2, missing means passing. Neglect legs read a missing coverage count as "most neglected"; share-count vetoes read a missing count as "not diluting"; the EPS-durability and no-deficit tests read a missing share as "no deficits". Fixed where the audit showed the sign was wrong (observed neglect where any coverage field exists, the filled global share columns, the global no-deficit chain), and denoted as a watch tier where the data does not reach a name rather than dropped.
4. Recurring mechanism 3, one threshold on two statistics. A minimum and a median under one 15% cut (lindy_margin), pre-tax ROCE and after-tax ROIC under one 25% cut (greenblatt), a sales-growth ratio under an earnings-growth name (lynch_evgy), a clipped Yahoo growth re-entering a band at its top edge (lynch_pegy). Fixed by judging each statistic at its own bar or by demoting the changed-meaning fallback to a surfaced watch.
5. Recurring mechanism 4, the proxy in place of the thing. Deleveraging read as a rising EBITDA denominator or a financing outflow that includes dividends, while the quarterly net-debt change was on disk; order conversion read as an ordinary growing microcap while the deferred-revenue build was one of several alternatives; the seal of approval read as any +10% growth print; the asset floor at 40% of the cap. Fixed: the literal measure is the core and the proxy is the watch.
6. Recurring mechanism 5, count hygiene. A negative risk flag, two mix descriptors and a payout modifier added archetype density, and same-fact siblings (six forensic gates on three facts, three inflection gates, three reinvestment gates) were counted as independent confirmations. Fixed: the descriptors are surfaced but not counted, and eleven same-thesis clusters count once.
7. Reach. The tax family read only the EDGAR rate (5% of the universe) while the global FMP rate sat merged and unread; the buyback legs read only the EDGAR yield while the FY repurchase yield was on disk; the insider and 13D legs are SEC-only by construction and remain so (denoted). The first two were wired; the third is an honest limit recorded in section 4.
8. What the audit did not change: no archetype's thesis was rewritten, no core was given a hard veto beyond what the audit showed to be a methodological bug or a fallback that changed meaning, and where a tightening was in the spirit of the source the previous rule survives as a surfaced `_watch` column so nothing is lost from the books.

## 2. What was changed, by slice

The fixes were applied in four commits after the slices were read; the multibagger and XR commits preceded this document, the singles and sourced-family commit and the tax-rate commit followed it. Each line names the archetype and the change; the reviewers' reasoning is in the slice report below.

### 2.1 Multibagger-study archetypes (both slices)
- Ranks: `_crank` falls back to the global rank in markets with under 50 base names; industry frames are ranked among industries, not among names.
- fallen_below_cycle and siblings: the multiple-vs-own-history test replaces the price-vs-5-year-average restatement of the drawdown; the margin gap is one source (the TTM levels `fqx_opm_ttm` and siblings were added to the quarterly layer); acceleration is date-matched; the turn must be recent (twelve months).
- Stress and survival: an EBITDA-negative stress path (negative equity, net cash below minus 100% of the cap, debt to equity at or above 2); survival, recency and perception lenses on every trough archetype; the blow-up lens.
- Informed buyers: the same-meaning insider fallback (two distinct buyers or positive net dollars), global corporate-conviction and 13F-arrival legs, and the confluence archetype requires two arrivals literally.
- Sell side: the unturned sell side requires a known buy share; hiring_beating requires observed coverage and a surprise floor; the target chase flag vetoes; ignored believers need two analysts.
- Other: FCF evidence required where the FCF leg was assumed; the incremental margin domain-limited; a composite cheapness axis; cheap assets bound on P/B; disclosed R&D and SG&A only; an operator population for the peer-worst rule; measured non-dilution (the fill flag was being read as growth); biotech runway measured within one statement set; the model score ranked among the liquid base with a 42-day staleness guard.

### 2.2 Forensic / exceptional-return archetypes (both slices)
- Data-quality gates on the segment, value-unlock, neg-EV growth and SOTP gates; the observable one-off charge and the observable bath (CFO minus NI minus D&A) on the bath and one-off-mask gates.
- Deleveraging and paydown from operations with net debt actually falling; balance-sheet leverage for the deep-trough survivability where nde is 99; the depreciation cliff excludes amortisation and needs cash behind the P/E escape.
- Cannibals need material buybacks and a returns floor; the clean net-net needs a corroborated buyback or an uncut dividend; the compounding deployer must be deploying now; the controlled-subsidiary cap unless aligned; the forced seller on both lines with the selling cleared.
- Float not shrinking and no SBC add-back; the deferred-revenue book not shrinking; backlog converting; the gross-margin lead sign-aware with a dilution guard; the peer margin gap needs an observed turn; the pre-scale margin floor; growth capex needs returns on it; working-capital normalisation measured on working capital with the fraud reading vetoed.
- The two broadest gates (audited_streak_unrerated, gaap_profit_crossover) no longer carry the engine family in confluence on their own; GAAP crossover on operating profit with materiality and no serial issuer; the NOL shield on the cash tax rate with taxable income and no dilution.

### 2.3 Single archetypes (both slices)
- Leverage read on the balance sheet where nde is unmeasurable: regime_cyclical, fixed_cost_demand_shock, post_reorg (the 99 fill no longer passes), tangible_value (the 99 fill no longer fails the loss-making asset play the gate exists to find).
- Count hygiene: concentrated_segments, diversified_segments, geographic_global and forensic_payout_confirmed are surfaced but not counted; eleven same-thesis clusters count once (awakening / rerating; the three cannibals; the three float gates; the three inflection gates; the three NOL gates; the three reinvestment gates; the two sales scalers; the two expensed-growth gates; the four sub-book value gates; the two D&A gates; the three earnings-verified gates).
- Perception: the panel tape first on analyst_awakening, institutional_accumulation, dead_option and quiet_compounder; deep coverage dropped from the conviction stack; the confirmed re-rating needs a change lens; clinical developers denoted out of the awakening (biotech_awakening_watch); the EPS-only asleep branch to asleep_eps_watch; the no-rerate test per share and on two lenses; observed neglect where coverage exists on blindspot, flyover, coiled_base, liger_asset_backed.
- Balance sheet and payout: balance_sheet_return needs the balance sheet to fund the payout; net_cash_returner on the sane, uncontradicted net cash with the reverse-split guard and the FY repurchase lens; dividend_verified_value covered over the cycle with no cut; forensic_payout_confirmed material, covered and excluding the payout-defined members; capital_returner's unknown-FCF case needs a flat count and the over-30% yields are routed to special_return_event_flag.
- Levered stubs: net debt not rising and a service floor on asymmetric_assembly, levered_inflection (core capped at 8x, the levered_stub_tier column), weschler_levered_equity (never uncovered through the cycle); seasonality-robust lenses only in the strict conjunctions.
- Inflections: kpi_threshold on an operating-line crossing with a 2pp confirmation (the cash-line first positives count only beside it); double_inflect and roic_inflect sized on the dated TTM with an impairment-year guard; micro_activist on a shock-sized inflection with the reverse-split veto; narrative_lag's breadth needs one shock-sized lens; wolf-style growth_algo and cheap_sales_scaler on seasonality-robust leverage with the count not growing.
- Quality descriptors: qarp with a sane ROIIC band, a base ROIC and two cheapness lenses; reinvest_inflect floors and caps with a TTM confirmation; midcap_garp's growth at 8% and a ROIIC floor; quiet_compounder's global path with the same durability as the US path; owner_operator's controlled-subsidiary flag from 50% and the same FCF bar on both paths; large_cap_quality's returns operating (interest burden); cheap_per_roiic with an absolute multiple cap; buyback_compounder on the global buyback yield with a split guard and FCF per share not shrinking; bottleneck's worst-year gross margin where five years are on file; cash_quality and cash_reinvest aligned with their siblings.
- Forensic singles: owner_earnings_power cheap on both sources with a held top line; overdepreciated_assets on the date-matched capex to D&A with an old base; understated_earnings with the SBC-pollution veto and a capex-aware corroboration; tax_verified_earnings with the income-quality and cash-tax corroboration; retained_earnings_discount earning now; cash_adjusted_pe with the one-off gain guard; lifo after tax; the pension surplus haircut by half; the NOL shell cash-backed; dta_reversal sustained, currency-coherent, undiluted; customer_float persistent and not a stretched payables line; capex_famine corroborated by the quarterly capex to D&A; expensed_growth_value with a burn veto; fastest_segment material; financials_value with a capital floor; the global FMP tax rate behind tax_efficient and tax_verified_earnings.
- Setups and events: the ignition counts volume once; the fallen angels are per share, survived and intact; crisis_asset_backed_recovery on measured asset heaviness with a senior-claims guard; derate_through_growth needs half the compression through growth and a profitability level; discounted_vehicle's catalyst and governance-trap flags surfaced; special_situation's deals die when the stock falls over 20% in the quarter; biotech_deep_value's runway two years (or one year financed) at investable scale; strong_coverage admits the corroborated royalty margin; sustainable_scaler's missing share history no longer passes and earnings must grow with sales; self_funded_returner with no issuance behind the outflow and an FCF yield; hidden_assets needs a disclosed stake.

### 2.4 Named-investor and uploaded-document archetypes
- Frazzini-Pedersen: both beta ranks low where both exist and a payout leg on the low-beta long; the synthesis no looser than either parent with the EV sanity band on the cheap leg; a market-relative volatility compression and an operating profit on "becoming", with the Yahoo-band proxy as bab_becoming_proxy_watch.
- Capital discipline: the insider path only with the persistent financing outflow; a split-guarded, globally-read action leg where a pure debt-repayment outflow needs an owner return; a NaN-aware clean balance sheet; a not-yet-re-rated leg; the value_up_reform_flag catalyst lens.
- Cluseau: the realizable book net of debt with a material, covered return; the FY repurchase yield satisfies the accelerator's pace with a split guard and cash read on the balance sheet.
- Cundill: half the former high on the 5-year weekly high first; the no-deficit test global. Greenblatt: the EV sanity band and investable scale. Kullamägi: near the high means within 10%, no clinical binaries. Weinstein: overhead on the 5-year high too. O'Neil: C not decelerating on continuing operations, A in the core.
- Liger: the floor carries the cap on asset_backed with a dilution leg and observed neglect (a name with no coverage field is neglected only under $500M); growth AND margin on the lagging inflection; runway to the catalyst for burners and no dilution or reverse split on the neglected survivor.
- Lindy: a level on lindy_fcf with the SBC and decline guards; the still-durable margin and the median judged at its own bar; per-share and still-growing-now legs on lindy_growth with the high-inflation listings denoted as lindy_growth_hi_inflation_watch.
- Lynch: the sales-ratio fallback to lynch_evgy_sales_watch with the EV sanity band; the clipped Yahoo growth unmeasured, per-share growth and the one-off gain guard on pegy; the earnings-line gap over three years as the unpaid test and two multi-year lenses on lynch_reward.
- Oak Bloke: a floor that is a floor (the old rule as oak_asset_floor_watch); the author's discount legs, dilution reject and panel-first crash on deep value; net debt not rising and low growth capex on deleveraging; the vehicle test in any sector with a leverage cap and several "discount addressed" lenses on nav_discount; the forward book required on order conversion (the P&L-only shape as oak_order_conversion_watch); producers only, the cost position through the cycle and no dilution on resource leverage.
- Spin-offs: the orphan price action on value and asset (spinoff_value_watch keeps the plain shape); not already delivered and a corroborated roce on quality; the dirty_spin_flag surfaced. Templeton: the panel near-low first, the EV sanity band, the acquisitive-base guard.
- Tenbagger: an after-tax terminal margin with no floor above the name's own; a small-cap core (the $1-10B band as tenbagger_path_watch); the filled global share columns on credible with missing history as tenbagger_credible_watch.
- Wolf: three ANDs, the balance sheet, no dilution and the EV sanity band on the trifecta; a cost-cut turn as the alternative and a P&L turn behind any cash first-positive on the turnaround; a present double-digit print and the EV sanity band on value_catalyst with the dated catalyst surfaced; the earnings inflection on the seal; the streak and 5% dilution on the compounder; the population from the industry with growth and dilution legs on emerging.

## 3. Deliberately not changed (the audit suggested it; the reason it was left)

- The SBC-zero fill (low_sbc_quality): FMP reports a zero for both "no SBC" and "line absent"; the two cannot be told apart at this layer, so a zero still reads as unmeasured. Recorded as a data limit, not a rule.
- Real index series for beta, Mansfield RS and down-capture (A1 D#4), the global spin anchor (A1 A.5 / A2 A.4), N-PORT as the non-US informed buyer, grades-news lead / chase, earnings-surprises universe-wide, the per-share financial-growth fields, the dated EV series as the primary multiple-compression read: these need fetches or new layers and were catalogued, not wired.
- Tightenings the reviewers proposed that would change a thesis rather than its measurement (the catalyst as a core leg on discounted_vehicle and wolf_value_catalyst, demoting strong_coverage / no_dilution / owner_operator to flags, one merged "earnings verified" family, the price-lindy core on qarp): surfaced as flags or lenses where the data allowed, otherwise left to the user's call.
- The pension surplus is haircut by half rather than to a quarter (the reviewer's US excise reading), because the population is nine names and the reversion route varies by plan.

## 4. Honest limits carried into the books

- Every insider, 13D, Form 4, RPO, exec-comp, NOL, LIFO, pension, reorg and special-situation leg is SEC-only by construction; the archetype books denote them as US-reach.
- The dividend yield is quote-time and can mix GBp / GBP or ZAc units on UK and South African lines; the cash-flow payout lines corroborate where they exist.
- The high-inflation listings (TRY, ARS, EGP, NGN and the rest of the set) cannot have their nominal growth deflated at this layer; they are denoted, not scored, on the nominal-growth archetypes.
- The `_watch` columns created by this audit (asleep_eps_watch, biotech_awakening_watch, bab_becoming_proxy_watch, lynch_evgy_sales_watch, oak_asset_floor_watch, oak_order_conversion_watch, spinoff_value_watch, tenbagger_path_watch, tenbagger_credible_watch, lindy_growth_hi_inflation_watch) and the flags (levered_stub_tier, value_up_reform_flag, special_return_event_flag, dirty_spin_flag, discounted_vehicle_catalyst_flag, discounted_vehicle_governance_trap_flag, wolf_value_catalyst_dated_flag) are written by the engine for the books; none of them gates anything.

# Slice mb_a: Multibagger-study archetypes, slice A (22)

## Audit 3, slice mb_a: 22 multibagger-study archetypes (archetype_tags.py)

Read-only audit. Firer counts are from the current archetype_tags.csv (46,526 rows; `_mb_base` = 18,661 liquid operating names in 47 markets). Coverage of the overlays that matter here: usf_* 7,021 names (SEC only); evt_sc13d_date 3,309; sent_n_analysts 12,440, sent_buy_share_d12 11,486, sent_pt_rev_q 2,896; evt_pt_rev_90d 2,547; tc_med_opm 32,565; fqx_fcfm_streak 23,689; ts_* ~33k.

Shared facts used below (stated once, referenced as S1-S6):
- S1 `_mb_base = is_operating & mcap>=10e6 & ts_dvol26_usd>=250k`; `_mb_fallen = ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.40`; `_mb_deep = ev_ebit in (0,6] | pb in (0,0.7] | p_s in (0,0.3]` (absolute, cross-sector). All NaN comparisons `.fillna(False)` -> a missing value FAILS a leg unless the leg is negated (`~(x > k)`), where NaN silently PASSES.
- S2 `_ins_2q = usf_ins_buy_quarters_4q >= 2 | (usf NaN & _mb_insider)`; `_mb_insider = insider_buy_flag==1 | insider_distinct_buyers>=2 | fmp_insider_net_usd_12m>0` (EDGAR Form 4 / FMP insider-trading: SEC filers only). `_13d_recent = age(evt_sc13d_date)<=365 & mcap<2e9` (SEC only; `ev_sc13d_date` is the latest SC 13D OR 13D/A, so an amendment reporting a sell-down refreshes it). `_informed = _ins_2q | _13d_recent | _mb_insider`. Net effect: every "informed / insider" leg is US-only, and the fallback for a US name with no usf row weakens "2 of 4 quarters" to "any single purchase / net USD > 0".
- S3 `_crank(x)` = percentile within `src` (listing market) among `_mb_base`; `_q_lo/_q_hi` = <=0.20 / >=0.80. 18 of 47 markets have < 50 base names (392 names), 11 have < 25: a "bottom quintile" there is 2-5 names. `rel_ind_*` = percentile within industry (>=12 operating names, else sector); `ind_tape_*` = that peer group's median.
- S4 `_mb_opm_gap = op_margin (asymmetry_global TTM, EDGAR/Yahoo) - tc_med_opm (FMP annual median, <= 8 FYs)`: two sources, two operating-income definitions; `rev_accel` (yartseva) = quarterly yoy minus prior-quarter yoy in percentage points, falling back to annual yoy differences (a different horizon) and read through `s()` so NaN -> 0.
- S5 `_cheap_own = ev_sales_change_yoy < 0 | price_vs_5y_avg < 1.0` (yartseva, Yahoo 5y daily closes, snapshot). Among fallen_below_cycle firers price_vs_5y_avg < 1 holds for 93 %: for a name at <= 50 % of its 5-year high the "below own history" leg is nearly implied by the drawdown leg itself.
- S6 Prior audits A1 (docs/archetype_upgrade_audit.md) and A2 (docs/fmp_catalog_upgrade_audit.md) contain no `arch_mb_*` rows; the still-open A2 items reused here are A2 §A.2 (observed analyst counts from `analyst-estimates`; `earnings-surprises-bulk`; `grades` lead/chase), A2 §A.3 (`acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` with percentOfClass/typeOfReportingPerson; `insider-trading/search` types and sizes; N-PORT `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` for non-US holders), A2 §A.1 (`owner-earnings` maintenance/growth capex; `historical-employee-count`), A2 §A.5 (`historical-market-capitalization` for own-history multiples).

---

### arch_mb_asset_trough_informed  (line 7833)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_SEGMENTS.md "Asset-based businesses", GMM cluster 3 (2.2x, activist 38 %, insiders 12 %, weeks since 5y low = 1) and the winners-vs-lookalikes table (gap_sales_1y +0.06 the only conviction separator): an asset business at a fresh low whose sales per share have run ahead of the price, with an informed buyer arriving; deleveraging was NEGATIVE. Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference I-E "The Surviving Thing" (survive the trough, tighter next cycle) and Wolf Archetype 6 (commodity producers with operational leverage) + III "insider buying clusters at sentiment troughs".
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_seg_asset` (sector Energy/Materials/Real Estate/Utilities or industry regex shipping|mining|oil|steel|... and not a drug developer) & `_mb_fallen` (<= 40 % of 5y high) & `_nl_sales_pos` (any of nl_sales_1y/2y/3y > 0) & `_informed` (S2). Lenses: sales lag 1y/3y (+), weeks since 5y low (-), insider quarters (+), 13D recency (+), P/S (-), analysts (-), size (-), D/E (+), MA30 slope (-), drawdown time share (+), r13 (-).
- Lineage: ts_dist_hi260 -> ts_snapshot (FMP weekly, PIT); nl_sales_* -> engine narrative-lag lenses from fq_rev_growth / fmp_st_sales_ps_*_g minus ts_r52/r156/r260 (global); `_informed` -> usf_* (insider-trading/statistics, SEC), evt_sc13d_date (sec-filings, SEC), EDGAR Form 4 flags (SEC). Result: 6 firers, 100 % US.
- Spirit gaps: the study's population is global (asset businesses in every market) but the conviction leg is SEC-only, so the archetype cannot fire outside the US. "Fresh low" (weeks since 5y low = 1) is a lens, not a leg. Leverage present "is part of the lever" (comment) but nothing reads it. No commodity-tape frame (the segment study added "the commodity's own tape").
- Methodology: (a) `_nl_sales_pos` uses `> 0` where the study used a within-population RANK (top feature): for a name down 60 %+ over the horizon, log(1+sales g) - log(1+price change) > 0 is close to automatic, so the leg mostly restates `_mb_fallen`. (b) `_seg_asset` regex on the industry string: 'gas' also matches "Gas Utilities" (already excluded by is_operating) and 'holding' catches conglomerates. (c) `evt_sc13d_date` includes 13D/A, so a long-standing holder's amendment (even a reduction) counts as "arriving" (S2). (d) mcap < 2e9 inside `_13d_recent` is a hidden size cap on the activist path only.
- FMP augmentations: `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US) -> percentOfClass rising / typeOfReportingPerson IN|IA to make the 13D leg a genuine NEW holder (A2 §A.3 open); `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` (global holdings by US funds, `change` > 0) -> a non-US "informed arrival" leg; `historical-market-capitalization` (global, H) -> sales-per-share vs price gap on a dated basis rather than the Yahoo-snapshot fallback.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] Add a non-SEC path to `_informed` (N-PORT holder `change` > 0 or `fmp_st_buyback_yield_y0` > 0 as revealed corporate conviction) or label the archetype "US filers only" in its description; today it is a 6-name US screen wearing a global study's name. 2 [method] Rank `nl_sales_1y` within `_seg_asset` (e.g. `_crank` on the asset segment) instead of `> 0`, per the study's "top feature" framing. 3 [spirit] Promote `ts_wks_since_lo260` (fresh low) from lens to a soft leg or the exceptional tier. 4 [method] Filter 13D/A from `ev_sc13d_date` or require a percentOfClass increase (A2 §A.3 open).

### arch_mb_biotech_financed_hiring  (line 7855)

Result: 3 firers, 100 % US. Not gated on `_mb_base` (no liquidity floor), unlike every sibling.
- Spirit gaps: "cash on hand" in the study is runway (cash vs burn); `net_cash_pct_mcap > 0` is a net-cash sign test that a $5m-cash, $4m-market-cap shell passes. No catalyst / data-readout dimension (the "one proof point restores probability mass" of I-F). Downgrades as a positive condition only in lenses.
- Methodology: (a) `_bio_committed` is SEC-only on both branches, so the global `_is_drug_dev` population collapses to US names. (b) `fq_financing_cf > 0` includes debt draws and is a single quarter. (c) The missing liquidity gate lets illiquid OTC biotechs qualify (the rest of the family removes them). (d) `_hi260 <= 0.50` is looser than the study's 22 % median; fine as a core, but "fallen" here is a different depth from `_mb_fallen` used elsewhere without saying so.

Cash runway: `cash-flow-statement` quarterly (global) -> cash / trailing-4q operating burn (computable from fq_cfo, fq_cash_sti already present).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Gate on `_mb_base` (or at least `_mb_liquid`) like the siblings; the study's population was liquid month-ends. 2 [spirit] Replace `net_cash_pct_mcap > 0` with a runway measure from fq_cash_sti and trailing fq_cfo (quarters of burn covered), which is what "cash on hand" meant for a burner. 3 [data] Give `_bio_committed` a global branch (share issuance with growing R&D: fmp_rd_to_revenue rising, or fq_sga/fq_revenue trend) so the archetype is not US-only by construction. 4 [spirit] Move `sent_downgrades_12m` from lens to the exceptional tier, per the 10x patterns.

### arch_mb_cheap_growth_targets_up  (line 7191)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_OPERATORS.md "Not fallen" k-means, cluster 7 (39 % of not-fallen multibaggers, 1.25x, blow-up 8 %; ISCTR, ALARK, STRL, 6920.T, PGSUS, TRIL.NS): revenue +17 %, EV/EBIT 8.5, FCF yield + growth the top axis, targets revised up, 70 % of the 5y high. Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference II Yartseva (FCF/P the dominant driver) + Lynch PEG / GARP; the compendium's Andreola screen ("room to expand: < 20x EBITDA").
- As coded: `_mb_base` & fq_rev_growth >= 0.15 & `_cheap_for_growth` (ev_ebit in (0,10] | fcf_yield + fq_rev_growth >= 0.20) & `_pt_up` (sent_pt_rev_q > 0 | evt_pt_rev_90d > 0) & ts_dist_hi260 >= 0.60. Lenses: fcf_yield + growth (+), EV/EBIT (-), rel_ind_ev_ebit (-), growth (+), rel growth (+), pt revision (+), pt premium (+), roce (+), size (-), vol (+).
- Lineage: fq_rev_growth -> fmp_quarterly (global, currency-neutral); ev_ebit, fcf_yield -> asymmetry_global (same-currency ratios); sent_pt_rev_q -> price-target endpoints via fmp_sentiment (2,896 names) and evt_pt_rev_90d -> price-target-news (US/CA, 2,547 names). Result: 191 firers, 98 % US, although the study's cluster is dominated by Turkish, Indian and Japanese names.
- Spirit gaps: "targets revised up" is the perception half of the thesis and is only observable for US/CA names; the study measured it on a global panel (pt_rev_6m). The size dimension (Yartseva small cap) is a lens only. No "why now" (the revision should be recent and not chasing price: `ev_pt_lead_flag` / `ev_pt_chase_flag` exist and are unused).
- Methodology: (a) `_pt_up` is an OR of a quarter-vs-year average and a 90-day median revision: a target raised because the price ran (chase) passes; `ev_pt_chase_flag` is the built veto and is not applied. (b) `fcf_yield + fq_rev_growth >= 0.20`: growth alone at 0.15 needs only a 5 % FCF yield; with fcf_yield NaN the sum is NaN and the leg falls to EV/EBIT <= 10, so a name without FCF data is judged on a different axis. (c) `_hi260 >= 0.60` is the study's population boundary, fine.
- FMP augmentations: `analyst-estimates` (global, snapshot; A2 §A.2 open) stored at each refresh -> consensus revenue/EPS revision as a global proxy for target revision; `price-target-summary` bulk (US-centric, S) for lastQuarterCount as attention; `grades` (dated, US-centric) for the lead/chase split (A2 §A.2 open).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Apply `ev_pt_chase_flag == 0` (or `ev_pt_lead_flag`) as a veto inside `_pt_up`: a target lifted after a +20 % run is not "targets rising ahead of price". 2 [data] Build a global revision series from successive `analyst-estimates` snapshots so the archetype can fire on the IS/IN/JP names the study found (A2 §A.2 open). 3 [method] Require fcf_yield.notna() in the FCF branch or read the EV/EBIT branch only when FCF is missing explicitly, so the two branches are not a silent fallback.

### arch_mb_cheap_vs_sector_recovering  (line 7310)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_UNCOVERED.md "Covered, not fallen" 3x candidates (sister of C9): well off the 52-week low, volatile, P/S bottom quintile of peers, FCF margin rising >= 2 quarters; robust 4.7x, 98 events, 16 markets (ADANIENT 2020, CLS 2023, LMB 2022, SHYF 2016). Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference I-E "Surviving Thing" layer (iii) and Yartseva "negative 6-month return then recovery"; Wolf Archetype 9 Turnaround (evidence of structural improvement before the re-rate).
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_q_hi(ts_dist_lo52)` (top quintile of close / 52w low within market) & `_q_hi(ts_vol_1y)` & rel_ind_p_s <= 0.20 & fqx_fcfm_streak >= 2. Lenses: dist lo52 (+), vol (+), rel P/S (-), fcfm streak (+), fcfm slope (+), size (-).
- Lineage: ts_dist_lo52, ts_vol_1y -> ts_snapshot (PIT, global); rel_ind_p_s -> engine peer rank of p_s (asymmetry_global, same currency); fqx_fcfm_streak -> fmp_quarterly_ext (date-matched, global). 28 firers, 29 % US. Cleanly global.
- Spirit gaps: "recovering" is tape-only (off the low); the study's sister C9 had a volume surge. No own-history cheapness (the name may be cheap vs peers because the whole industry is cheap AND it is the worst of them). No profitability floor: a name with FCF margin rising from -30 % to -25 % over two quarters has a streak of 2.
- Methodology: (a) `_q_hi(ts_dist_lo52)`: the top quintile of "distance above the 52w low" within a market is dominated by names that already tripled off the low in a rebound year; the study's "well off the low" is right, but with no cap the leg can admit a completed run (ts_dist_hi52 unused). (b) S3 small-market quintiles. (c) `rel_ind_p_s` falls back to the sector rank where the industry has < 12 names; the "sector" in the archetype's name is then literal but the peer group changes silently. (d) Well built otherwise; the four legs are independent facts.
- FMP augmentations: none essential; `historical-market-capitalization` (global) -> P/S vs own 3y history as a second cheapness frame (A2 §A.5 open).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Add `ts_dist_hi52 < 0.9` (or the sequence archetype's `_not_ignited`) as a veto so "recovering" excludes "already recovered". 2 [spirit] Add `fcf_margin >= 0` or `op_margin >= 0` as a soft leg (the study's examples were profitable operators) or surface the loss-making members as a separate tier. 3 [data] Expose `peer_frame` in the description so users see when "sector" replaced "industry".

### arch_mb_compounder_insiders_at_high  (line 7168)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_OPERATORS.md "Near highs" 10x patterns (lift 90-108x on tiny support; NVDA 2015/2019, MSTR 2020, TSLA 2019, ABMD 2013, ETSY 2017): within 15 % of the 52w high, ROCE in the industry's top quartile, R&D-heavy, insiders buying, recent quarters NOT beating. Human counterpart: archetype_compendium Part I-1 Meyer Phelps 100x (sustained ROIC, founder alignment, "be a reluctant seller") and master_archetype_reference VI Andreola (buy at 52-week highs with low institutional ownership); O'Neil/Tortoriello "buy near highs".

Lenses: rel roce (+), roce (+), R&D/rev (+), insider quarters (+), distinct buyers (+), dist hi52 (+), upgrades (+), beat share (-), fcf yield (+), net cash (+).
- Lineage: ts_dist_hi52 -> ts_snapshot; rel_ind_roce -> peer rank of roce (asymmetry_global); fmp_rd_to_revenue -> ratios-ttm (global); insider legs S2 (SEC only). Result: 13 firers, 100 % US.
- Spirit gaps: the study's discriminator "recent quarters NOT beating (expectations not yet set)" is a lens, not a leg. No growth or reinvestment-runway leg (Phelps tests 3 and 7); no size framing (the "small starting cap" of Phelps is in the lenses only; the study's own examples were large). No founder/owner-operator alignment (Phelps test 4) beyond the insider purchase.
- Methodology: (a) `(_ins_2q | _mb_insider)` is redundant: `_ins_2q` already ORs in `_mb_insider` when usf is NaN, so the expression equals `usf >= 2 | _mb_insider`, i.e. a single Form-4 purchase suffices even when the usf record shows only one buying quarter. (b) `rel_ind_roce` uses the raw `roce` percentile; a negative-EV or tiny-capital name can rank top quartile on a noisy ROCE. (c) US-only by S2. (d) `_rd_heavy` reads `fmp_rd_intensive_flag` which is itself `fmp_rd_to_revenue >= 0.10` (line 654), so the OR adds nothing.

- Suggestions: 1 [data] Add a global "conviction at the high" path: buyback executed at the high (`fmp_st_buyback_yield_y0 > 0` or `fqx_share_shrink_now`), so non-US compounders are not excluded for want of Form 4 data; keep insider buying as the exceptional tier. 2 [spirit] Add a compounding leg (fqx_eps_pos_share_8 or `financial-growth` per-share 3y growth) so "compounder" is measured, not implied by ROCE rank. 3 [method] Simplify the insider expression to what it computes and drop the redundant `fmp_rd_intensive_flag` branch.

### arch_mb_conviction_confluence  (line 7069)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_LIFT2.md §5 Intersections: fallen_insider_conviction AND smart_money_wreckage (n = 1,739, lift 5.76x vs 3.92x / 4.69x, blow-up 31 %). Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference III "insider buying clusters at sentiment troughs" (Lakonishok-Lee: purchases, small firms) crossed with Wolf Archetype 10 distressed/deep value; the compendium's "insider/activist floor" as the bounded downside.
- As coded: arch_mb_fallen_insider == 1 & arch_mb_smart_money_wreckage == 1. Lenses: mb_smart_money_legs (+), insider quarters (+), buyback yield (+), dist hi260 (-), weeks since low (-), size (-).
- Lineage: inherits both parents; smart_money_wreckage = `_mb_base & _mb_fallen & _mb_deep & (smart_legs >= 1)` with smart_legs = `_ins_2q + _13d_recent + (usf_emp_g1 >= 0.10)`. 34 firers, 100 % US (S2).
- Spirit gaps: the name promises a confluence of independent informed buyers (insiders AND a 13D holder or hiring). It does not deliver that: see below. The parents' "why it works" (deep value + insiders after a crash) is captured; the timing dimension (LIFT2 §2, a fresh low) is a lens.
- Methodology: (a) Algebra: fallen_insider implies `_mb_base & _mb_fallen & _ins_2q`, and `_ins_2q` alone makes smart_legs >= 1, so `conviction_confluence == fallen_insider & _mb_deep`. The 13D and headcount legs never contribute to membership; the archetype is "fallen insider + deep value", which is exactly LIFT2 §1's `deep_value` refinement row (n = 1,739, 1.47x inside the archetype) and matches the §5 count. (b) The lens `mb_smart_money_legs` is therefore the only place the confluence idea lives. (c) NaN semantics are inherited: `_operating_not_asset` and `_margins_not_consistent` in the parent are negated tests, so missing ncav / opm_consist silently pass.
- FMP augmentations: `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US, H) for a real "new >= 5 % holder" leg with percentOfClass and IN/IA type (A2 §A.3 open); `institutional-ownership/extract-analytics/holder` (US, H) isNew / firstAdded for arriving institutions (A2 §A.3 open); `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` (global) for non-US arrivals.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] Make the confluence literal: require `mb_smart_money_legs >= 2` (insiders plus a second, independent arrival) for the core and keep today's set as the watch tier; otherwise rename to what it is. 2 [method] Document that the current definition reduces to fallen_insider & `_mb_deep` so the tiers/lenses are not read as measuring three arrivals. 3 [data] Add a non-SEC second-arrival leg (N-PORT `change` > 0) so the archetype can exist outside the US.

### arch_mb_divergence_cheapest_pb  (line 7278)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_UNCOVERED.md "Covered, all" 3x candidates (C7): fundamentals ahead of price (narrative lag, or margins above through-cycle norm at half the high), P/B bottom quintile of the industry, wide range; robust 4.2-4.4x, ~200 events, 14-23 markets (0412.HK, DAC, MEG.TO, AXTI, PR, LXU). Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference I-A(15) "Narrative lag after fundamental inflection" and archetype_compendium Part I-8 "First-time coverage & earnings divergence" (behaviour change high, reaction low); the compendium's Kerrisdale "mismarked balance sheet" for the P/B angle.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_divergence` (narrative_lag_extent >= log 1.5 | (`_mb_opm_gap` >= 0.02 & ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.5)) & rel_ind_pb <= 0.20 & `_crank(ts_vol_1y)` >= 0.6. Lenses: lag extent (+), rel pb (-), vol (+), industry growth (-), dist hi260 (-), nl_fcfps_3y (+).
- Lineage: narrative_lag_extent -> engine (fq_rev_growth, fqx_ebit_ttm_g, fmp_st_sales_ps_*_g vs ts_r52/r156/r260; global, per-share adjusted); `_mb_opm_gap` S4 (mixed sources); rel_ind_pb -> peer rank of pb > 0 (asymmetry_global, same currency); ts_vol_1y -> ts_snapshot. 218 firers, 25 % US. Global.
- Spirit gaps: the study's members are asset-heavy (shipping, energy, chemicals) where P/B is the right frame; the engine applies it to every operating industry including software, where "cheapest P/B in the industry" is a negative-book or goodwill artefact rather than a mismarked asset. No own-history P/B (study row "pb own low"). No catalyst.
- Methodology: (a) `rel_ind_pb` excludes pb <= 0 (correct) but a P/B of 0.9 can be the industry's bottom quintile in software; the rank is relative, the spirit is absolute cheapness of assets — a `pb <= 1.5` sanity bound or the asset-like population would keep the leg honest. (b) The two `_divergence` branches are different facts (a 1y+ per-share lag vs margins above an 8-year median with a halved price); the second branch has no growth test, so a shrinking name with a still-high margin at half its high passes. (c) NaN: narrative_lag_extent is NaN when no lens fired, so a name with no fq data can only enter via the margin branch; fine. (d) S3 small-market vol rank.
- FMP augmentations: `historical-market-capitalization` (global, H) -> P/B and P/S vs own 3-5y history (A2 §A.5 open); `earnings` / `earnings-surprises-bulk` (global, H) -> `ev_ignored_beats_2y` already feeds narrative_lag_lenses, so no new pull; `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US) for the missing catalyst as an exceptional tier.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Bound the P/B leg absolutely (pb <= ~1.5, or restrict to `_asset_like` | tangible-asset intensity >= 0.3 via `asset_intensity_ppe`) so "cheapest P/B" means cheap assets, not the least-expensive software name. 2 [spirit] Add "pb own history low" as a lens from `fmp_dyn_*` or the market-cap history (A2 §A.5 open). 3 [method] Require `fq_rev_growth >= 0` in the margin-branch of `_divergence` so "margins above norm" is not a shrinking-into-quality artefact.

### arch_mb_fallen_below_cycle  (line 6979)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_LATENT_CLUSTERS.md cluster 2 (47 % of 2018+ multibaggers, 1.5x): far below the 5y high, margins under the own through-cycle norm, the multiple below its own history; MULTIBAGGER_LIFT2.md "fallen_below_cycle" refinements (buy_share_d12 LOW 2.3x, n_analysts LOW, insiders; avoid R&D LOW, vol LOW, wks_since_lo260 HIGH). Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference I-E "The Surviving Thing" three-layer test (priced for old-cycle economics; balance sheet survived; next cycle tighter) and archetype_compendium Part II-D Capital Cycle ("depressed valuations after years of poor returns"); Yartseva "price near 12-month low".
- As coded: `_mb_base` & ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.50 & `_mb_opm_gap` <= -0.02 & `_cheap_own` (S5). Lenses: dist hi260 (-), tc_med_opm - op_margin (+), ev_sales change (-), tc_opinc_pos (+), size (-), 13D (+), weeks since low (-), mb_fund_up_unturned (+), buy share (-), analysts (-), R&D/rev (+), vol (+).
- Lineage: op_margin (asymmetry_global TTM) vs tc_med_opm (fmp_throughcycle, FMP annual; S4 mixed sources); ev_sales_change_yoy, price_vs_5y_avg -> yartseva (Yahoo, snapshot; S5). 1,842 firers (largest fallen archetype), 21 % US; tc_years median 8 among firers.
- Spirit gaps: layer (ii) "balance sheet survived the downcycle" is absent: no interest cover, net debt or FCF-positive-years leg (tc_min_ic, tc_fcf_pos, fq_interest_cover all exist). Layer (iii) "next cycle tighter" (industry capex withdrawal, consolidation) is absent. The LIFT2 refinements that carried the lift (sell side unturned, few analysts) are lenses only, which is the right placement given coverage.
- Methodology: (a) S5: `price_vs_5y_avg < 1` is met by 93 % of firers, so `_cheap_own` is nearly implied by `_hi260 <= 0.5`; the "multiple below own history" leg is in practice a second drawdown leg, and the one true multiple test (`ev_sales_change_yoy < 0`, 67 %) is a one-year change, not a history. `fmp_dyn_ev_sales_change_3y` already exists in the frame and is unused here. (b) S4: `_mb_opm_gap` compares a Yahoo/EDGAR TTM operating margin with an FMP annual median; a definitional difference of 2 pp (the threshold) between sources is plausible. (c) NaN: both legs fail on NaN (good); tc_med_opm over <= 8 FYs makes an 8-year decliner's "norm" already depressed (the archetype then misses the long-cycle names it is meant for; no fix without longer statements). (d) `tc_opinc_pos` is a lens; the spirit's "has been profitable through the cycle" would be a natural gate.
- FMP augmentations: `enterprise-values` (global, H; already in fmp_dynamics) -> EV/sales vs own 3-5y median replaces the snapshot legs; `income-statement` annual limit 10+ (global) -> extend tc_* beyond 8 years for the cycle norm; `grades-historical` (partial global) already wired for the sell-side lenses; A2 §A.2 `analyst-estimates` numAnalysts as observed neglect.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Replace `_cheap_own` with a multiple-vs-own-history test (`fmp_dyn_ev_sales_change_3y < 0`, or EV/sales below its tc-period median) so the leg measures what the cluster measured; keep the price test as a lens. 2 [spirit] Add survival as a leg or veto: `tc_opinc_pos >= tc_years - 2` or `fq_interest_cover >= 1.5`, per the Surviving-Thing layer (ii). 3 [method] Compute the margin gap on one source (FMP TTM operating margin from fq_opinc / fq_revenue vs tc_med_opm) to remove the S4 definitional mismatch. 4 [data] Industry capex trend (sum of fq_capex over `peer_key`, no new endpoint) as a capital-cycle lens.

### arch_mb_fallen_deep_value  (line 6957)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_LATENT_CLUSTERS.md conjunction "fallen_angel + deep_value" (2.49x fit / 3.37x test) and MULTIBAGGER_ARCHETYPES.md 10x patterns (fallen & deep value & FCF streak low). Human counterpart: Wolf Archetype 10 "Distressed / Deep-Value" (crash > 50 % from highs, P/B < 0.5, identifiable assets, a plausible path) and archetype_compendium Part II-D (buy at maximum pessimism); Yartseva high B/M + near lows.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_mb_fallen` & `_mb_deep` (S1). Lenses: dist hi260 (-), EV/EBIT (-), pb (-), fcf yield (+), size (-), 13D (+), rel_ind_ev_ebit (-), vs_ind_dist_hi260 (-).
- Lineage: ts_dist_hi260 (PIT); ev_ebit, pb, p_s -> asymmetry_global (EDGAR/Yahoo/FMP-filled same-currency ratios, snapshot at as_of). 854 firers, 30 % US. Global.
- Spirit gaps: Wolf's "assets identifiable" (cash > 20 % of mcap, carrying values) and "some near-term liquidity or asset-realisation event" are absent; the archetype is the study's box, not the human thesis, which is acceptable if labelled so. The timing dimension (LIFT2 §2: a fresh 5y low outperforms a 1-2-year-old low) is not even in this archetype's lenses (it is in fallen_insider's).
- Methodology: (a) `_mb_deep` is three absolute thresholds across all sectors: P/S <= 0.3 is ordinary for distributors, retailers and contractors (the LIFT2 "avoid" list flags P/S HIGH, so the direction is right, but the level is sector-blind); the peer-relative `rel_ind_ev_ebit` sits in lenses only. (b) EV/EBIT > 0 requirement excludes loss-makers from the EV branch but P/B and P/S branches admit them, so "deep value" mixes a cheap earner with a cheap loss-maker without saying which. (c) NaN fails every branch (good). (d) Well built for what it claims; the superset of fallen_value_turn / _accel / left_for_dead_value, so its members should be read net of those.
- FMP augmentations: `scores-bulk` (global, S; already used) -> `fmp_altman_z` / `fmp_piotroski` as the "not too far gone" veto Wolf asks for; `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US) / `mergers-acquisitions-latest` (US) for the realisation-event tier; `historical-market-capitalization` (global) for P/B vs own history.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] Add `ts_wks_since_lo260` (-) and `ncav_pct_mcap` (+) to the lenses: the fresh-low timing and the asset floor are the two things the human thesis adds to the box. 2 [method] Surface which `_mb_deep` branch fired (an `mb_deep_branch` column) so a P/S-only member is not read as an EV/EBIT-cheap one. 3 [data] Use `fmp_altman_z` as a soft veto for the "too far gone" case (Wolf's reject rule), which the blow-up rate (24 %) says is present.

### arch_mb_fallen_ignored_believers  (line 7020)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_ARCHETYPES.md 3x-in-24m pattern "div_yield LOW & n_analysts LOW & buy_share HIGH & fallen_angel" (8.7x, blow-up 42 %; LMB, TBHC, DXLG 2020) and LIFT2 "fallen_ignored_believers" (n_analysts LOW 3.0x inside, accumulation 2.3x, pt_prem HIGH; avoid rd_rev LOW, dvol HIGH). Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference IV Flyover (< 5 analysts) and archetype_compendium Part I-8 (first-time coverage): the few who look, like it, and nobody else is looking.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_mb_fallen` & sent_n_analysts in [1,5] & sent_buy_share >= 0.6 & ~(dividend_yield > 0.02). Lenses: dist hi260 (-), buy share (+), analysts (-), nl_sales_1y (+), r13 (-), size (-).
- Lineage: sent_n_analysts / sent_buy_share -> grades-historical latest month via fmp_sentiment (12,440 names, snapshot, global-partial); dividend_yield -> asymmetry_global (Yahoo snapshot, currency-neutral); 578 firers, 40 % US; median analysts among firers = 2.
- Spirit gaps: "believers" with 1-2 ratings is one or two houses; the study's LIFT2 refinement `accumulation` (institutions arriving) and `pt_prem_12m HIGH` are the corroboration and are not even lenses here. No recency of the ratings (a 3-year-old Buy still counts: `sent_months_since_up` exists). The dividend leg is right (no yield support) but reads a Yahoo trailing yield that a cut does not update until the next print.
- Methodology: (a) `buy_share >= 0.6` with n = 1 is a single Buy; with n = 2 it needs both. The threshold is a share, the spirit is a count of believers; require `sent_n_analysts >= 2` or measure "believers" as a count. (b) `~(dividend_yield > 0.02)` passes NaN silently (a payer with no yield data qualifies). (c) `.fillna(False)` on the whole conjunction is fine; `_few` NaN -> False, so the archetype needs grades data (12.4k names): its reach is the sentiment overlay's, not the universe's, which should be stated. (d) `sent_buy_share` is a snapshot; the panel study used dated ratings.
- FMP augmentations: `grades-consensus` / `upgrades-downgrades-consensus-bulk` (global-partial, 1 call) -> observed rating counts for every name (A2 §A.2 open); `price-target-consensus` (US-centric) -> pt premium lens; `institutional-ownership/symbol-positions-summary` (US) -> investorsHoldingChange as the `accumulation` refinement; `dividends` (global, H) -> a same-currency, dated yield and cut detection (A2 §A.4).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Require at least two ratings (`sent_n_analysts >= 2`) or express the leg as "buy count >= 2 and buy share >= 0.6" so a lone Buy is not a chorus of believers. 2 [spirit] Add `evt_pt_prem_12m` (+), `fmp_inst_shares_chg_pct_q0` (+) and `sent_months_since_up` (-) to the lenses (the study's own refinements). 3 [method] Make the dividend veto explicit on missing data (`dividend_yield.notna() & <= 0.02`, or read `ev_div_cut_2y`).

### arch_mb_fallen_insider  (line 6971)

Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference III "Insider buying clusters (>= 3 unique insiders, code P, within ~2 weeks) at sentiment troughs" with Lakonishok-Lee (purchases, small firms); archetype_compendium filing cheat-sheet Form 4 ("clusters of 3+ within ~10 days").
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_mb_fallen` & `_ins_2q` (S2) & ~(ncav_pct_mcap >= 0.5) & ~(fqx_opm_consist > 0.5). Lenses: dist hi260 (-), insider quarters (+), distinct buyers (+), size (-), buyback yield (+), capex intensity (+), D/E (+), pb (-), weeks since low (-).
- Lineage: usf_ins_buy_quarters_4q -> insider-trading/statistics (SEC; quarters with totalPurchases > 0, no size); fallback EDGAR Form 4 flags (sec_insider_buys.py) and fmp_insider_net_usd_12m (insider-trading/search, SEC); ncav_pct_mcap -> yartseva; fqx_opm_consist -> fmp_quarterly_ext. 107 firers, 100 % US.

The study's leverage-present finding (low debt 0.4x) is a lens, correctly. No global reach at all (S2), and the doc-string claims "EDGAR flags otherwise" as if that widened coverage; it does not.
- Methodology: (a) Fallback semantics (S2): for a US name with no usf row, `_mb_insider` admits one purchase or a positive net USD; the leg's meaning changes from "persistent buying" to "any buying". (b) `_operating_not_asset = ~(ncav >= 0.5)` and `_margins_not_consistent = ~(opm_consist > 0.5)` both pass NaN silently: a name with no quarterly panel is "not an asset play" and "not consistent" by default. (c) `usf_ins_buy_quarters_4q` counts a quarter with a $1k director purchase the same as a $5m CEO purchase; `usf_ins_net_buy_4q` (transactions net) exists and is unused. (d) The archetype is well aligned with the study's definition otherwise; the study itself was SEC-limited.
- FMP augmentations: `insider-trading/search` (US, H) -> typeOfOwner (officer/director/10 %), price x shares (size / mcap), transactionType P only, dated -> cluster within 14 days (A2 §A.3 open); `insider-trading/statistics` `acquiredDisposedRatio` for net buying; `governance-executive-compensation` (US) salary vs purchase size as a conviction scale. Global: none catalogued for insiders; N-PORT holder change (global) is the only non-SEC arrival signal.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Require the fallback to carry the same meaning: when usf is NaN use `insider_distinct_buyers >= 2` (cluster) or `usf_ins_net_buy_4q > 0`, not `insider_buy_flag == 1`. 2 [method] Make the two negated guards explicit on missing data (`ncav_pct_mcap.notna()` / `fqx_opm_consist.notna()`) or state in the description that unmeasured names pass them. 3 [spirit] Add purchase size / mcap and officer-buy flags from `insider-trading/search` as the exceptional tier (compendium cluster rule). 4 [data] Label the archetype US-filers-only in its description; the count (107) is a US screen.

### arch_mb_fallen_operator_industry_low  (line 7287)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_UNCOVERED.md "Covered profitable operators" 10x candidates (C8): a profitable operator >= 60 % below its high, EV/sales bottom quintile, the industry's own distance from its 5-year high in the bottom quintile, two-year return low or P/S bottom of peers; robust 9-11x, 51-58 events, 7 markets (LMB, DDS, SMCI 2018, ARVIND, GME 2018). Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference I-E(9) "Regime-change cyclical" and archetype_compendium Part II-D Capital Cycle ("the turn occurs at maximum pessimism as the weakest competitor throws in the towel"; find the highest-torque survivor).
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_operator` (op_margin >= 0 & fcf_margin >= 0 & ~`_asset_like`) & `_mb_fallen` & `_q_lo(ev_sales)` & `_q_lo(ind_tape_dist_hi260)` & (`_q_lo(ts_r104)` | rel_ind_p_s <= 0.20). Lenses: EV/sales (-), industry dist hi260 (-), r104 (-), rel P/S (-), dist hi260 (-), roce (+).
- Lineage: ev_sales, op_margin, fcf_margin -> asymmetry_global (snapshot ratios); ind_tape_dist_hi260 -> engine peer-group median of ts_dist_hi260 (PIT); ts_r104 -> ts_snapshot; rel_ind_p_s -> peer rank. 27 firers, 19 % US. Global.
- Spirit gaps: "survivor" is not tested (no balance-sheet leg: the compendium's torque comes with "downside protected by asset value"); "industry far from its high" is tested, "capacity withdrawal" (capex cuts, consolidation) is not. Profitability is a sign test (>= 0), not "still earning through the trough".
- Methodology: (a) `_q_lo(df['ind_tape_dist_hi260'])` ranks an INDUSTRY-level median across the names of a country: a large depressed industry fills the whole bottom quintile and a small depressed industry never reaches it (the quintile is by name count, not by industry). The study did the same, but the engine's `peer_key` sector fallback means "industry" is the sector for thin industries. (b) `_q_lo(_evsv)` is a cross-sector quintile within a market, so low-margin industries (retail, EMS, distribution) dominate; that is the study's finding (LMB, DDS, SMCI) but it makes the archetype an industry filter in disguise. (c) S3 small-market quintiles: in 18 markets the bottom quintile is under 10 names. (d) `_operator` fails on NaN margins (good); `~_asset_like` is a regex that also removes "Oil & Gas Equipment & Services" style operators.
- FMP augmentations: `income-statement`/`cash-flow-statement` quarterly (global, already cached) -> industry capex / revenue trend by `peer_key` as the capital-cycle "withdrawal" leg (no new endpoint); `tc_min_ic` / `fq_interest_cover` (already present) for survival; A2 §A.5 notes `historical-industry-performance` is stale, so the engine's own `ind_tape_*` remains the right frame.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] Add a survival leg (`fq_interest_cover >= 2` or `tc_fcf_pos >= tc_years - 2`) so "operator in a trough" is also "operator that gets through it". 2 [method] Rank `ind_tape_dist_hi260` among INDUSTRIES (one row per peer_key) rather than among names, so the "industry at its own low" leg is not a function of industry size. 3 [method] Fall back to a regional or global `_crank` when a market has < 50 base names (S3), stated once for all `_q_lo/_q_hi` users. 4 [data] Industry capex trend lens from the cached quarterly panel.

### arch_mb_fallen_stressed  (line 6960)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_LATENT_CLUSTERS.md conjunctions "overlevered_stressed + fallen_angel" (3.09x / 2.67x) and "deep_value + fallen_angel + overlevered_stressed" (3.2x / 3.7x): a crashed, over-levered name where the equity is a call option on survival. Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference VII Akre AMT ("debt panic + visible FCF inflection"), Wolf Archetype 8 "Cash-flow deleveraging" and Archetype 10 (reject if "too far gone"); archetype_compendium post-reorg lens ("diagnose the type of distress: the balance sheet can be rebuilt, the business cannot").
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_mb_fallen` & `_mb_stressed` (net_debt_ebitda in [5, 90) | equity < 0). Lenses: dist hi260 (-), EV/sales (-), fqx_ebit_ttm_g (+), cfo yield (+), size (-), 13D (+).
- Lineage: net_debt_ebitda -> asymmetry_global via `s(..., 99.0)` and forced to 99 when ebitda_ttm <= 0 (line 1075); equity -> asymmetry_global balance sheet (same currency). 606 firers, 39 % US. Global.
- Spirit gaps: the thesis is stress that RESOLVES (deleveraging, refinancing, FCF inflection); nothing tests the path: `fq_netdebt_decline_months`, `fq_deleveraging_flag`, `fq_interest_cover`, `fmp_st_financing_years` exist and are unused even as lenses. No "not diluting" (that is the sibling `mb_stressed_not_diluting`, slice B). No maturity / refinancing catalyst.
- Methodology: (a) `nde` is 99 both when net_debt_ebitda is missing and when EBITDA <= 0, and `_mb_stressed` requires nde < 90: a heavily indebted LOSS-MAKER (EBITDA < 0, the most stressed case) is excluded unless equity < 0. The leg measures "levered but EBITDA-positive", which is narrower than the study's overlevered_stressed state. (b) `equity < 0` on a same-currency balance sheet is fine but treats a buyback-driven negative book (a franchise like a US restaurant chain) as distress. (c) NaN fails (good) apart from the 99 sentinel path above. (d) Well built as the study's box; the lenses (EBIT growth, CFO yield) carry the "resolving" idea.
- FMP augmentations: `company-notes` (US, S) listed notes exist -> maturity-wall context; `balance-sheet-statement` quarterly (global, cached) -> net debt trend already in fq_netdebt_*; `scores-bulk` `altmanZScore` (global) as the "too far gone" veto; `sec-filings-8k` (US) for refinancing / amendment events as an exceptional tier.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Add a second stress path for EBITDA <= 0 names (net debt / market cap >= 1 or debt_to_equity >= 2) so the 99-sentinel does not exclude the most stressed cases. 2 [spirit] Add the resolution legs as lenses: `fq_netdebt_decline_months` (+), `fq_interest_cover` (+), `fmp_st_financing_outflow_years` (+). 3 [data] `fmp_altman_z` (already in the frame from scores-bulk) as a soft veto or a -1 lens, per Wolf's "reject if irreparable".

Human counterpart: archetype_compendium Part II-D Capital Cycle (returns mean-revert as capacity leaves; buy at maximum pessimism) and master_archetype_reference I-E; Wolf Archetype 6 "Commodity/resource producers with operational leverage".

- Lineage: `_mb_opm_gap` S4 (Yahoo/EDGAR TTM vs FMP annual median, <= 8 FYs); rev_accel S4 (yartseva; quarterly yoy delta in pp, annual fallback, NaN -> 0 so a missing value fails). 444 firers, 26 % US. Global.
- Spirit gaps: no industry frame: a margin 5 pp under its own norm while the industry's margins are also compressed is a cyclical trough; the same gap while peers are fine is an idiosyncratic problem — `ind_tape_opm_d1` exists and is unused. No survival leg (see fallen_below_cycle). "Capacity leaving" (industry capex down) untested.

(b) S4 source mismatch on the gap. (c) `_mb_trough` shares `rev_accel > 0` with `_mb_accel` (>= 0.10) in `fallen_value_accel`; the two archetypes overlap by construction on deep-value names. (d) Both legs fail on NaN (good).

- Suggestions: 1 [method] Use `fqx_rev_accel_now == 1` (or `fqx_m_since_rev_accel` in [0, 6]) in place of, or alongside, `rev_accel > 0`, so acceleration is date-matched and recent. 2 [spirit] Add `ind_tape_opm_d1` (-) as a lens or require the industry's margin change to be <= 0 for the "cyclical" reading. 3 [method] One-source margin gap (fq_opinc / fq_revenue TTM vs tc_med_opm).

### arch_mb_fallen_value_turn  (line 6958)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_LATENT_CLUSTERS.md conjunction "deep_value + fallen_angel + turnaround" (2.73x / 3.60x). Human counterpart: archetype_compendium Part II-B Andreola ("the first profitable quarter is the strongest footprint") and master_archetype_reference I-A(15) narrative lag after inflection; Wolf Archetype 9 Turnaround.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_mb_fallen` & `_mb_deep` & `_mb_turn` (fqx_eps_turned | bs_ebit_turned | ebitda/ni/fcf_first_positive > 0 | fmp_dyn_opinc/ni_turned_positive). Lenses: dist hi260 (-), EV/EBIT (-), fqx_ebit_ttm_g (+), fcf yield (+), size (-), 13D (+).
- Lineage: fqx_eps_turned (date-matched, global), bs_ebit_turned (base_snapshot), *_first_positive (yartseva, EDGAR/Yahoo TTM), fmp_dyn_* (latest quarter > 0 with any of the prior 4 <= 0; global). 422 firers, 30 % US. Global.
- Spirit gaps: the study's turn is recent (FORENSICS §4: profit turns positive ~12 months before the run); the OR of seven flags from four sources has heterogeneous look-backs, and none of them requires the turn to be RECENT except fmp_dyn_* (within 4 quarters). `fqx_m_since_turn_positive` exists and is unused. Nothing tests that the market ignored the turn (`evt_react_last`, `evt_ignored_beats_2y`).
- Methodology: (a) Seven flags on different definitions (EPS, EBIT, EBITDA, NI, FCF) and windows: a "turn" can be an EBITDA sign flip with EBIT still negative; the archetype cannot say which. (b) `ebitda_first_pos` etc. are read through `s()` (NaN -> 0 = no turn; fine). (c) With `_mb_deep` requiring EV/EBIT in (0,6] OR P/B OR P/S, a just-turned name qualifies on P/S <= 0.3 while its EV/EBIT is undefined; that is the intended "cheap on sales, just turned" case.
- FMP augmentations: `earnings` (global, H; wired as evt_react_last) -> "turn ignored by the price"; `income-statement` quarterly (cached) -> unify the turn on one line (TTM operating income) with a dated first-positive quarter.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] Add `fqx_m_since_turn_positive` in [0, 12] as the core turn definition (or a lens) so the archetype fires in the study's 12-month window, not on any historical flip. 2 [method] Emit which flag fired (`mb_turn_source`) so an EBITDA-only turn is distinguishable from an EPS turn. 3 [spirit] Lens `evt_react_last` (-) / `evt_ignored_beats_2y` (+): the turn the market shrugged at is the one worth owning.

### arch_mb_grew_into_valuation_turning  (line 7035)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_ARCHETYPES.md Archetype B (grew into the valuation: revenue +13 %, margin at the 64th pct of own history) with the profit turn arrived. Human counterpart: archetype_compendium Part I-3 Gardner Rule Breaker Sub-B "Already re-rated: the multiple compressed 50 %+ through earnings growth, not price decline" (NVDA 131x -> 40x, SE, CRWD, MELI, NU) — an exact match of the derate-through-growth idea.
- As coded: arch_derate_through_growth == 1 (multi-year per-share advance outran a falling multiple; is_operating & mcap >= 10e6, NO liquidity gate) & (`_margin_trend_up` (fqx_opm_slope8 > 0 & consist >= 0.6 | fqx_margin_inflect_now) | `_mb_turn`) & fq_rev_growth >= 0.08. Lenses: derate_growth_absorbed (+), opm slope (+), opm consist (+), growth (+), r13 (-), analysts (-).
- Lineage: derate legs -> bs_coil_rev/ebit (base_snapshot), fmp_st_sales_ps_3y/5y_g (global), ts_r104/r156/r260 (PIT); fqx_* (global, date-matched); fq_rev_growth (global). 2,480 firers (largest in the slice), 30 % US; median mcap $516m; sent_n_analysts present for 47 %.
- Spirit gaps: Sub-B's premise is that the multiple is NOW reasonable; the archetype has no valuation ceiling, so a name that de-rated from 60x to 35x EV/EBIT qualifies. The "turning" branch via `_mb_turn` (any historical sign flip) is much weaker than the margin-trend branch and is the reason the count is so large. No tape test (the de-rating may still be in progress: ts_r13 is a lens).
- Methodology: (a) Not gated on `_mb_base`: the only mb_ archetype without the liquidity floor (illiquid names inherit `arch_derate_through_growth`). (b) `_margin_trend_up | _mb_turn` mixes an 8-quarter trend with a one-off flip; either alone passes. (c) `fq_rev_growth >= 0.08` is below the Archetype-B median (13 %); fine, but with the OR above it is the only binding fundamental. (d) NaN fails both fundamental legs (good).
- FMP augmentations: `enterprise-values` (global, H; fmp_dynamics) -> the multiple's level vs its own 3-5y range as the "now reasonable" test; `financial-growth` (global, H) per-share 3/5y growth to confirm the advance (A2 §A.1 open); `analyst-estimates` numAnalysts for the neglect lens (A2 §A.2 open).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Gate on `_mb_base` like every sibling (the study's population was liquid month-ends). 2 [spirit] Add a "multiple now fair" leg or lens: `fmp_dyn_ev_sales_change_3y < 0` plus an absolute cap (ev_ebit <= 25 as in inflecting_operator) so Sub-B's "already re-rated" is measured. 3 [method] Drop `_mb_turn` from the OR, or keep it only with `fqx_m_since_turn_positive <= 12`, so "turning" means the margin trend the study described.

### arch_mb_growth_past_capex_peak  (line 7246)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_UNCOVERED.md "Uncovered, not fallen" 3x candidates (U4): two-year revenue growth top quintile (or capex/D&A high), no payout, capex rolling off while still above D&A, ROIC at its own high or volatile vs industry; robust 7.1-7.5x, 80-104 events, 9-12 markets (TSLA 2018, FNOX.ST, 001570.KS, IIVI, HTHT). Human counterpart: archetype_compendium Part II-D Capital Cycle at company level ("falling capex / depreciation ratios" as the investment phase ends) and master_archetype_reference VII Akre AMT ("visible FCF inflection" after the build-out); the engine's xr_growth_capex_masked is the forensic sibling.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & ts_dist_hi260 >= 0.60 & (`_q_hi(_g2)` | `_q_hi(fq_capex_to_da)`) & `_no_payout` (~(dividend_yield > 0.005 | buyback_yield > 0.005)) & `_capex_rolloff` (|fq_capex| < |fq_capex_p| & fq_capex_to_da > 1) & (`_roic_own_hi` (fqx_roic_ttm > roic_lindy | fqx_roic_streak >= 3) | vol crank >= 0.8). Lenses: revenue CAGR (+), capex/D&A (+), capex drop (+), roic vs lindy (+), vol (+), dividend yield (-).
- Lineage: `_g2 = fmp_st_revenue_cagr.fillna(fq_rev_growth)` (FMP annual CAGR over the available span, up to ~8 FYs, falling back to ONE-year growth); fq_capex / fq_capex_p -> fmp_quarterly (single quarter vs the prior comparable period); roic_lindy -> EDGAR median (US) filled by fmp_st_roic_lindy (global); yields -> asymmetry_global snapshot. 92 firers, 10 % US. Global.
- Spirit gaps: the FCF inflection that the capex roll-off is supposed to produce is not tested (fcf_margin_delta_yoy, fqx_fcfm_slope8 exist). "Growth capex" vs maintenance is not separated, so a roll-off can be a cut in maintenance. The study's "two-year growth" is replaced by a variable-span CAGR.
- Methodology: (a) `_g2` ranks a multi-year CAGR and a 1-year growth rate in the same quintile: the fallback changes the leg's meaning per name. (b) `_capex_rolloff` compares one quarter with its prior-period value: seasonal and lumpy; the quarterly panel allows TTM vs prior TTM. (c) `_no_payout` is a negated test, so NaN yields pass silently (a payer without yield data is "no payout"). (d) `_roic_own_hi` compares TTM ROIC with the multi-year MEDIAN (roic_lindy is a median, edgar_roic_roiic.py line 222), a low bar for "ROIC at its own high"; the study's row was returnOnInvestedCapital vs own history HIGH (rank). (e) `_hi260 >= 0.60` is the population boundary.
- FMP augmentations: `owner-earnings` (global, H) -> `growthCapex` / `maintenanceCapex` so the roll-off is of growth capex specifically (A2 §A.1 open); `financial-growth` (global, H) -> threeYRevenueGrowthPerShare as a fixed-span growth leg (A2 §A.1 open); `cash-flow-statement` quarterly (cached) -> TTM capex path.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Build `_capex_rolloff` on TTM capex vs prior-TTM from the quarterly panel, and `_g2` on a fixed 2-3-year span (`fmp_st_revenue_3y_cagr` exists) with no 1-year fallback. 2 [spirit] Add the FCF inflection as a leg or lens (`fqx_fcfm_slope8 > 0` or fcf_margin_delta_yoy > 0): the roll-off matters because cash appears. 3 [data] `owner-earnings` growthCapex for the growth/maintenance split (A2 §A.1 open). 4 [method] Make `_no_payout` explicit on missing yields.

### arch_mb_hiring_beating_uncovered  (line 7180)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_OPERATORS.md "Uncovered" k-means, the only lifted cluster (2.1x, blow-up 15 %; 2930.T, BEL.NS, HARVIA.HE, TRIDENT.NS, TATAELXSI): headcount +36 %, margins at the top of own history, beats and surprises, EV/sales already rising, new 13F holders, 56 % of the 5y high, no analyst. Human counterpart: archetype_compendium Part II-B Andreola footprint (earnings surprise, new capacity, while coverage is near zero; the discovery cycle "buy at catalyst, before retail/institutional") and master_archetype_reference VI.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_hiring` (usf_emp_g1 >= 0.15 | (usf NaN & fq_rev_growth >= 0.20)) & `_mb_opm_gap` >= 0.02 & `_beating` (evt_beat_share_8q >= 0.6 | evt_surprise_4q > 0 | fmp_earnings_beat_rate >= 0.6) & sent_n_analysts.fillna(0) <= 1 & ts_dist_hi260 in [0.35, 0.80]. Lenses: headcount g (+), margin gap (+), surprise (+), beat share (+), EV/sales change (+), 13F shares change (+), analysts (-), vol (+), size (-), dist hi260 (-).
- Lineage: usf_emp_g1 -> historical-employee-count (SEC only); evt_* -> earnings endpoint via fmp_events (global, 19k names); fmp_earnings_beat_rate -> earnings via fmp_enrich; sent_n_analysts -> grades-historical (12.4k names); `_mb_opm_gap` S4. 65 firers, 26 % US.
- Spirit gaps: headcount is the study's commitment signal; for non-US names the fallback substitutes revenue growth >= 20 %, a different fact (the study's cluster examples are all non-US, so most firers carry the substitute). "New 13F holders" is a US lens. "Uncovered" is inferred from missing grades data.
- Methodology: (a) `sent_n_analysts.fillna(0) <= 1` treats no sentiment row as no analyst, while `_beating` requires an EPS ESTIMATE to have existed (a beat needs a consensus): a name that beat consensus has at least one estimate-providing analyst, so "uncovered" and "beating" contradict on the missing-data path (the A2 §A.2 "missing = neglected" item, open). (b) `_hiring` fallback (S2-style) changes meaning. (c) `evt_surprise_4q > 0` admits a single tiny positive surprise. (d) `_mb_opm_gap >= 0.02` S4 sources. (e) The `_hi260` band is the study's population; fine.
- FMP augmentations: `analyst-estimates` numAnalystsEps (global, S) -> observed coverage count replacing "missing = 0" (A2 §A.2 open); `earnings-surprises-bulk` (global, H) -> universe-wide beat record (A2 §A.2 open); `profile.fullTimeEmployees` (global, S) stored per refresh -> a non-SEC headcount growth leg; `institutional-ownership/extract-analytics/holder` isNew (US) for the "new holders" refinement (A2 §A.3 open).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Define "uncovered" as an OBSERVED count <= 1 (`analyst-estimates` numAnalysts, or grades-consensus bulk) and treat NaN as unknown, not zero (A2 §A.2 open). 2 [data] Store `profile.fullTimeEmployees` over time so `_hiring` has a global headcount branch instead of the revenue substitute. 3 [method] Require beat share OR a surprise above a materiality floor (`evt_surprise_4q >= 0.05`) rather than `> 0`.

### arch_mb_improving_unturned_sellside  (line 7148)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_OPERATORS.md "improving_unturned_sellside" refinements (gap_perc_buyshare HIGH 2.75x where the sell side EXISTS and has not turned; hypergrowth 2.1x; vol HIGH; div yield LOW; avoid new_activist, vol LOW, flat base): a low-gross-margin operator whose revenue and margin rise and whose tape is re-rating while the analysts' buy share has not moved. Human counterpart: master_archetype_reference I-A(15) "Narrative lag after fundamental inflection" and archetype_compendium Part I-8 "First-time coverage & earnings divergence"; Andreola's discovery cycle before the institutional phase.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_fund_up` (fq_rev_growth >= 0.10 & op_margin_delta_yoy > 0) & `_sellside_unturned` (sent_n_analysts >= 1 & ~(sent_buy_share_d12 > 0)) & `_low_gm` (rel_ind_gross_margin <= 0.35 | gross_margin < 0.25) & `_tape_rerating` (price_vs_5y_avg > 1 | ts_r52 > 0.20) & vol crank >= 0.40 & ~(dividend_yield > 0.02). Lenses: opm delta (+), growth (+), buy share d12 (-), rel gm (-), price vs 5y (+), r52 (+), vol (+), pt premium (+).
- Lineage: sent_n_analysts / sent_buy_share_d12 -> grades-historical (12,440 / 11,486 names; snapshot at latest month); op_margin_delta_yoy -> asymmetry_global; gross_margin -> asymmetry_global; price_vs_5y_avg -> yartseva/Yahoo snapshot; ts_r52 PIT. 154 firers, 24 % US.
- Spirit gaps: "the tape already re-rating" in the study was P/B above its own history; the engine uses price vs 5y average, which is a price, not a multiple, so a name whose price rose with earnings (no re-rating) passes. Lead/chase of any rating action is untested.
- Methodology: (a) The code's own comment says "an unknown buy share is not an unturned one", yet `_sellside_unturned = (sent_n_analysts >= 1) & ~(sent_buy_share_d12 > 0)` passes NaN d12: roughly 950 names have ratings now but none 12 months ago (new coverage), and for them the leg reads "unturned" when the sell side has in fact just ARRIVED — the opposite fact. (b) `~(dividend_yield > 0.02)` passes NaN silently. (c) `_low_gm` fallback: `gross_margin < 0.25` absolute when the peer rank is missing. (d) `ts_r52 > 0.20` alone satisfies "re-rating" with no multiple.
- FMP augmentations: `grades` / `grades-news` (US-centric, H) -> dated actions with priceWhenPosted for lead vs chase (A2 §A.2 open); `grades-consensus` bulk (global-partial) -> current buy/hold/sell counts for a same-day buy share; `enterprise-values` (global, H) -> EV/sales vs own history as the re-rating test (fmp_dyn_ev_sales_change_3y is already in the frame).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Require `sent_buy_share_d12.notna()` inside `_sellside_unturned`; the comment already states the rule, the code does not apply it. 2 [spirit] Use a multiple for "re-rating" (`fmp_dyn_ev_sales_change_3y > 0` or P/B vs own history) with the price test as fallback. 3 [method] Explicit NaN handling on the dividend veto. 4 [data] Lead/chase from `grades` (A2 §A.2 open) as the exceptional tier.

### arch_mb_industry_trough_cheapest  (line 7222)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_UNCOVERED.md "Uncovered, all" 3x candidates (U1): fallen, P/S bottom quintile, the industry's median margin change or growth in the bottom quintile; robust 5.2-5.8x, 110-175 events, 8-12 markets (SARDAEN, VEDL, 300274.SZ, BTE.TO, ADANIENT 2013). Human counterpart: archetype_compendium Part II-D Capital Cycle ("the turn occurs at maximum pessimism as the weakest competitor throws in the towel"; low returns repel capital) and master_archetype_reference I-E(9) Regime-change cyclical.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `_mb_fallen` & `_q_lo(p_s)` & (`_q_lo(ind_tape_opm_d1)` | `_q_lo(ind_tape_rev_growth)`). Lenses: P/S (-), industry opm d1 (-), industry growth (-), dist hi260 (-), nl_sales_3y (+), MA30 slope (-), size (-).
- Lineage: p_s -> asymmetry_global (same currency); ind_tape_opm_d1 -> peer-group median of op_margin_delta_yoy (asymmetry_global); ind_tape_rev_growth -> peer median of fq_rev_growth (fallback yf_revenue_growth); both ranked by `_crank` within market (S3). 306 firers, 32 % US. Global.
- Spirit gaps: the industry is at a trough in MARGIN CHANGE or GROWTH, not in capital: the compendium's buy signals (capex cuts, closures, consolidation, falling capex/depreciation, no new entrants) are not represented; the cheapest name is not tested for survival (the "weakest competitor" is who dies). Sales-per-share ahead of price (study row 29) is a lens only.
- Methodology: (a) As in fallen_operator_industry_low, an industry-level median ranked across names in a country makes the bottom quintile a function of industry size, and the sector fallback for thin industries widens "industry" silently. (b) `_q_lo(p_s)` cross-sector within market -> commodity and contracting names dominate, which is the study's finding (SARDAEN, VEDL) but also means P/S <= bottom quintile is a sector filter. (c) S3 small-market quintiles. (d) NaN in either industry variable fails that branch (good); the OR lets a "growth low" industry with margins expanding pass, which is a mixed trough.
- FMP augmentations: `cash-flow-statement` quarterly (cached, global) -> industry capex / D&A by `peer_key` for the capital-withdrawal leg (no new endpoint); `scores-bulk` altmanZScore (global) for the survivor test; `income-statement` annual (global) -> years of industry-wide margin decline (trough duration).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Rank industry variables among industries (one observation per peer_key), then map back to names, so "industry at its own trough" is independent of headcount. 2 [spirit] Add a survivor leg (`fq_interest_cover >= 1.5` or `fmp_altman_z >= 1.8`) so "cheapest in a troughed industry" is not "the one that will not make it". 3 [data] Industry capex/DA trend as a capital-cycle lens.

### arch_mb_inflecting_operator  (line 6987)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_LATENT_CLUSTERS.md cluster 0 (38 % of 2018+ multibaggers, 1.1x): margins and EBIT rising, returns improving, smaller cap, a fair (not bubble) price; FORENSICS §5 adds that winners had a WEAKER tape than lookalikes (which the sibling quiet_turn encodes). Human counterpart: archetype_compendium Part I-8 "First-time coverage & earnings divergence" and Part II-B Andreola (profitable or at a clear inflection, < $500m); master_archetype_reference I-A(15).
- As coded: `_mb_base` & mcap < 2e9 & `_mb_margin_up` (op_margin_delta_yoy >= 0.01 | fqx_inc_ebit_margin >= 0.20) & `_mb_ebit_up` (fqx_ebit_ttm_g >= 0.10 | ebit_growth_yoy >= 0.10) & `_mb_roic_up` (fqx_roic_ttm > roic_lindy | roce_delta_yoy > 0) & ev_ebit in (0, 25]. Lenses: opm delta (+), EBIT g (+), roic vs lindy (+), incremental margin (+), size (-), 13D (+).
- Lineage: op_margin_delta_yoy, ebit_growth_yoy, roce_delta_yoy, ev_ebit -> asymmetry_global (EDGAR/Yahoo TTM); fqx_* -> fmp_quarterly_ext (date-matched, global); roic_lindy -> EDGAR median filled by fmp_st_roic_lindy (global). 1,379 firers, 14 % US; median mcap $397m; median ts_dist_hi260 0.67.
- Spirit gaps: nothing tests that the inflection is unpriced (no tape, no reaction, no coverage leg); the cluster's "fair price" is an absolute EV/EBIT <= 25 with no peer or own-history frame; "returns improving" accepts any positive roce_delta. The result is the slice's broadest operator screen with no perception half.
- Methodology: (a) `_mb_margin_up` reads raw `fqx_inc_ebit_margin >= 0.20`, not the engine's domain-limited `fqx_inc_ebit_margin_dt` (line 1176 excludes values > 1.0 as losses unwinding); so an incremental margin of 374 % from a loss base passes as "margin up", contradicting the engine's own rule elsewhere. (b) `ebit_growth_yoy >= 0.10` from a negative base is sign-ambiguous (yartseva pct_change); `fqx_ebit_ttm_g` requires both > 0 and is the safe branch. (c) `fqx_roic_ttm > roic_lindy` compares TTM with the multi-year MEDIAN: "above average", not "at its high"; `roce_delta_yoy > 0` is any improvement. (d) `mcap < 2e9` absolute USD; fine for the cluster. (e) Three ORs each with a weak branch: the conjunction can be satisfied entirely by the weak branches (1 pp margin, 10 % EBIT from a negative base, roce +0.1 pp).
- FMP augmentations: `earnings` (global, H; wired) -> `evt_react_last` / `ev_ignored_beats_2y` as the unpriced-inflection lens; `analyst-estimates` numAnalysts (A2 §A.2 open) for neglect; `enterprise-values` (global) for EV/EBIT vs own history in place of the absolute 25.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] Use `fqx_inc_ebit_margin_dt` (domain-limited) in `_mb_margin_up`, consistent with the engine's own drop-through rule. 2 [spirit] Add the perception half as lenses at least: `evt_react_last` (-), `ts_r13` (-), `sent_n_analysts` (-), per FORENSICS §5 (winners had the weaker tape). 3 [method] Require the safe branches when the data exist (`fqx_ebit_ttm_g` when notna; `roic_ttm > lindy` only with `fqx_roic_streak >= 2`) so the conjunction cannot be met on three weak fallbacks. 4 [method] Frame the price leg relatively (`rel_ind_ev_ebit <= 0.6`) with the absolute cap as a sanity bound.
