## Slice summary: the ten most valuable augmentations (ranked)

1. [data/method, 6 archetypes] Every "informed buyer" leg (`_ins_2q`, `_mb_insider`, `_13d_recent`, `usf_emp_g1`) is SEC-only, so asset_trough_informed (6), biotech_financed_hiring (3), compounder_insiders_at_high (13), fallen_insider (107) and conviction_confluence (34) fire on US names only while their studies were global. Either label them US-filers-only or add a catalogued non-SEC arrival leg: N-PORT `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` (`change` > 0, global holdings) and revealed corporate conviction (`fmp_st_buyback_yield_y0`, `fqx_share_shrink_now`).
2. [spirit] conviction_confluence reduces algebraically to fallen_insider & `_mb_deep`; the 13D / hiring legs never bind. Require `mb_smart_money_legs >= 2` for the core to make the name true; `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (percentOfClass, IN/IA type) makes the 13D leg a real new holder (A2 §A.3 open) and filters 13D/A sell-downs out of `ev_sc13d_date`.
3. [method] improving_unturned_sellside passes a NaN `sent_buy_share_d12` as "unturned" although its own comment forbids it; require notna. Same family: hiring_beating_uncovered's "no analyst = NaN" contradicts its own beat leg; use observed counts from `analyst-estimates` numAnalystsEps / `grades-consensus` bulk (A2 §A.2 open).
4. [method] `_cheap_own` (fallen_below_cycle, 1,842 firers) is 93 % a restatement of the drawdown leg; replace with a multiple-vs-own-history test (`fmp_dyn_ev_sales_change_3y`, already in the frame, or `enterprise-values` history) so "the multiple below its own history" is measured.
5. [method] One-source margin gap: `_mb_opm_gap` (fallen_below_cycle, fallen_trough, hiring_beating, divergence) subtracts an FMP annual median from a Yahoo/EDGAR TTM margin; compute the TTM from the cached fq_opinc / fq_revenue so a 2-5 pp threshold is not a definitional artefact.
6. [method] `_crank` quintiles in 18 markets with < 50 base names (industry_trough_cheapest, fallen_operator_industry_low, cheap_vs_sector_recovering, growth_past_capex_peak, divergence, improving_unturned): fall back to a regional/global rank below a frame-size floor; and rank industry-level `ind_tape_*` among industries, not among names.
7. [spirit] Survival is missing from every trough archetype (fallen_below_cycle, fallen_trough, fallen_stressed, industry_trough_cheapest, fallen_operator_industry_low): add `fq_interest_cover` / `tc_min_ic` / `fmp_altman_z` (scores-bulk, global, already pulled) as a leg or veto, per the Surviving-Thing layer (ii) and Wolf's "reject if too far gone"; and a second stress path for EBITDA <= 0 names, which the nde = 99 sentinel currently excludes from fallen_stressed.
8. [spirit] Recency of the turn: fallen_value_turn and grew_into_valuation_turning accept any historical sign flip; `fqx_m_since_turn_positive` in [0, 12] (already computed) restores the study's 12-month window, and grew_into needs the `_mb_base` liquidity gate it alone lacks.
9. [data] cheap_growth_targets_up (98 % US) cannot see the IS/IN/JP names its cluster was made of: build a revision series from successive `analyst-estimates` snapshots (global) and apply the built `ev_pt_chase_flag` veto so a target lifted after the run does not count.
10. [data] growth_past_capex_peak: `owner-earnings` growthCapex / maintenanceCapex (global, H; A2 §A.1 open) plus TTM capex from the cached quarterly panel replace the single-quarter roll-off and the variable-span `fmp_st_revenue_cagr.fillna(fq_rev_growth)` mix; add the FCF inflection (`fqx_fcfm_slope8`) the roll-off is supposed to deliver.

# Slice mb_b: Multibagger-study archetypes, slice B incl. the model ones (21)

## Audit 3 — slice mb_b (21 multibagger-study archetypes incl. the model ones)

Read-only audit of `archetype_tags.py` (line numbers from S/dumps2). Shared facts used below (coverage from S/audit3/cov_mb_b.out, 46,526 master rows):
`_mb_base` = is_operating & mcap_usd >= $10M & `ts_dvol26_usd` >= $250k (ts_snapshot covers 34,470 names, so ~12k names never enter any mb_ archetype).
`_mb_fallen` = `ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.40`. `_mb_deep` = EV/EBIT in (0,6] | P/B in (0,0.7] | P/S in (0,0.3] (ev_ebit only 37% covered; the P/B and P/S legs carry most members).
`_crank` = pct rank within `src` country AMONG `_mb_base`; `_q_lo/_q_hi` = <=0.20 / >=0.80 of that rank (53 src values; the small ones rank pools of <200 names).
Peer frame `rel_ind_*` / `ind_tape_*` / `vs_ind_*` = industry (>=12 operating names, else sector), NOT liquidity-filtered.
Informed-buyer legs are SEC-only in practice: `usf_ins_buy_quarters_4q` 7,021 names (insider-trading/statistics, US), `evt_sc13d_date` 3,309 names (sec-filings-search, US, `_13d_recent` also requires mcap < $2B), `insider_buy_flag`/`insider_distinct_buyers` 4,459 tickers (EDGAR Form 4), `fmp_insider_net_usd_12m` 213 names, `usf_emp_g1` 7,010 names (US 10-K/10-Q headcount).
Sell-side legs: `sent_n_analysts` 12,440 names (27%, grades-historical); every `.fillna(0) <= k` leg treats "no ratings on file" as neglect (A2 §top-item 9, still open). `evt_pt_prem_12m` 3,716 names (8%, price-target-news, US/CA). `evt_ignored_beats_2y` 19,181 (41%, `earnings`, global).
`fmp_rd_to_revenue` (key-metrics-ttm) is present for 38,218 names but its median is 0.0: FMP zero-fills undisclosed R&D, so "R&D low" mostly means "not broken out".
`fmp_filled_*` columns are fill FLAGS: none of the 21 archetypes reads one as a value (checked every leg and lens; `_not_diluting` even carries the warning comment).
Study provenance: MULTIBAGGER_ARCHETYPES.md (§Patterns/Tree recipes), MULTIBAGGER_LIFT2.md (per-archetype refinements, §5 intersections), MULTIBAGGER_OPERATORS.md (§2 patterns, §6 refinements), MULTIBAGGER_UNCOVERED.md (U/C families), MULTIBAGGER_SEGMENTS.md (pre-profit), MULTIBAGGER_MODEL.md, MULTIBAGGER_FORENSICS.md (§4 sequence, §5 dogs). The study boxes themselves are in `multibagger_lift2.archetypes()` and `multibagger_operators.operator_archetypes()`; the mined words come from `multibagger_uncovered.WORDS/FRAME` where "minus industry" = raw (own − industry median), ranked WITHOUT a sign flip (`multibagger_clusters.ranked`).

### arch_mb_leader_in_wave  (line 7126)
- Spirit: OPERATORS §2 3x/24m top patterns (`rd_rev HIGH & n_analysts HIGH & op_ind_vol HIGH & gap_sales_3y LOW`, 12.4x; NVDA 2019, TRIL, 5803.T) and §6 `leader_in_wave` refinement (n=783, 4.09x; op_sec_disthi HIGH 2.0x, rev_g2 HIGH 0.55x, current ratio falling, R&D at own high): the covered, volatile leader whose price runs ahead of sales in an industry whose own tape is up. Engine-native composite of those tables.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `sent_n_analysts >= 8` & price-ahead (`nl_sales_3y < 0 | nl_sales_1y < 0`) & vol rank >= 0.60 (country) & (margin trend up | `op_margin_delta_yoy > 0.01`) & `fq_rev_growth >= 0.15` & `rev_accel > 0` & industry wave (`ind_breadth_up52 >= 0.5 | ind_tape_r52 > 0`) & `rel_ind_dist_hi260 >= 0.70`. Lenses: analysts+, -nl_sales_3y+, opm slope+, rev growth+, R&D/rev+, ind breadth+, ind r52+, vol+, roce+.
- Lineage: sent_n_analysts → grades-historical (G-partial, 27%); nl_* → engine narrative-lag block (master price/sales-per-share, EDGAR/Yahoo/FMP-filled); fqx_opm_slope8/consist, fqx_margin_inflect_now → fmp_quarterly_ext (statements, G); op_margin_delta_yoy → master (52%); fq_rev_growth → fmp_quarterly (TTM, G); rev_accel → master via `s()` (NaN→0, so NaN fails `> 0` correctly); ind_* / rel_ind_* → engine peer frame from ts_snapshot; ts_vol_1y → ts_snapshot; fmp_rd_to_revenue lens → key-metrics (zero-filled).
- Spirit gaps: the study's "growth is NEW" (`rev_g2 HIGH` = 0.55x) is not vetoed; "investing" (current ratio / cash ratio falling, debt-to-capital rising, 1.8-2.0x) has no leg or lens; "R&D at its own high" is approximated by the level lens only; the frame's euphoria (comment: "negative lens") is not a lens (ind_tape_r52 is signed +).
- Methodology: `sent_n_analysts >= 8` is an absolute count across markets where 27% coverage is US-centric; a covered non-US leader (5803.T-type) with few FMP grades is dropped. `rel_ind_dist_hi260` ranks among ALL operating names of the industry (not the liquid base) — harmless here. `_ind_wave` accepts `ind_breadth_up52 >= 0.5` alone, which is true for most industries in an ordinary up-year; the study's wave was `op_ind_vol HIGH`/`ind_tape_r52 HIGH` (top-quintile ranks). No 2021-style guard: the archetype's own note says the 2021 cohort had zero winners.
- FMP augmentations: `analyst-estimates.numAnalystsEps` (G, snapshot) as the coverage count where grades are absent; `key-metrics-ttm.currentRatioTTM`/`cashRatioTTM` and `financial-growth.threeYRevenueGrowthPerShare` (G) for the "investing" and "growth is new" legs; `earnings-surprises-bulk` (G) for a beat-reaction lens; `historical-market-capitalization` (G) for the 2y growth-of-mcap "already re-rated" veto.
- Suggestions: (1) [method] replace the absolute `>= 8` with observed-coverage OR-logic (`numAnalystsEps` or grades count) ranked within market (A2 §top-9 open). (2) [spirit] add "growth is new" veto: 2y per-share revenue growth NOT top quintile (`rev_g2 HIGH` 0.55x in the study). (3) [spirit] add the investing lens (current ratio d4 −, debt-to-capital d4 +) and flip `ind_tape_r52` to a negative "euphoria" lens as the comment intends. (4) [method] tighten `_ind_wave` to top-quintile `ind_tape_r52` within the market (the study's HIGH), not `> 0`.

### arch_mb_lean_rd_stocking_up  (line 7267)
- Spirit: UNCOVERED §"Uncovered, all — 10x within 5 years", the only robust candidate (17.7x, 39 events, 6 years, 5 markets, T 56%: 6920.T 2016 58x, SMCI 2019 52x, CLS.TO 2020, 601012.SS 2013): "rise off 52w low high; inventory / revenue change high; R&D / revenue high; SG&A / revenue low" — a lean R&D manufacturer building inventory into demand. Engine carries it with the thin-support caveat.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & `ts_dist_lo52` top country quintile & `fq_inv_vs_cogs > 0.05` & `fmp_rd_to_revenue` top quintile & (SG&A/revenue bottom quintile | SG&A NaN). Lenses: dist_lo52+, inv_vs_cogs+, R&D/rev+, SG&A/rev −, rev growth+.
- Lineage: ts_dist_lo52 = close / 52w low (ts_snapshot, G); fq_inv_vs_cogs = inventory snapshot growth − TTM COGS growth, guarded inventory_p >= 5% of COGS (fmp_quarterly, G, 36% covered); fmp_rd_to_revenue → key-metrics-ttm (zero-filled for non-disclosers); fq_sga/fq_revenue → fmp_quarterly TTM (72%). No SEC-only leg — a genuinely global archetype.
- Spirit gaps: the study's leg was "inventory / REVENUE change" (`inv_rev_d1`), the engine uses inventory-vs-COGS (closer to channel stuffing forensics) — same direction, different denominator; "R&D at own high" / "SG&A ratio change" siblings (UNCOVERED 10x non-qualifying rows: `receivable days change low`, `share count trend consistency high`, `revenue growth rising streak high`) absent; no demand confirmation (revenue growth streak) beyond a lens.
- Methodology: `(_q_lo(_sga_rev) | _sga_rev.isna())` — a missing SG&A line PASSES the "lean SG&A" leg (28% of names lack fq_sga; many IFRS filers fold SG&A into opex), so the leg is a no-op exactly where FMP standardisation is weakest. `_q_hi(fmp_rd_to_revenue)` ranks a zero-filled column: in countries where most filers do not disclose R&D the top quintile begins at tiny ratios. `fq_inv_vs_cogs > 0.05` is an absolute threshold while the study used a within-market top quintile; with a 36% covered column the NaNs simply fail (fine). The inventory build is unsigned by gross-margin direction: a build with falling GM is the forensic `fq_inventory_build_flag` trap, the same number.
- FMP augmentations: `key-metrics-ttm.daysOfInventoryOutstandingTTM` (+ own-history from `key-metrics` annual, G) as the inventory leg for names without a quarterly panel; `key-metrics-ttm.salesGeneralAndAdministrativeToRevenueTTM` (G) to fill the SG&A leg where fq_sga is NaN; `financial-growth.rdexpenseGrowth` (G) for "R&D at own high"; `income-statement-growth` gross-profit growth vs revenue growth as the GM-direction guard.
- Suggestions: (1) [method] drop `| _sga_rev.isna()` and fill SG&A/revenue from key-metrics before ranking; a missing SG&A must not certify leanness. (2) [method] require `fmp_rd_to_revenue > 0` (disclosed) before ranking, and rank within market among disclosers only. (3) [spirit] add a gross-margin-not-falling guard (`fqx_gm_streak >= 1` or `fq_gm_inflection_flag`) so the inventory build reads as demand, not a stuffed channel. (4) [data] carry the study's market concentration (Japan 56%) as a lens on `src` diversity in the book, since 5 markets/39 events is the whole evidence.

### arch_mb_model_confluence  (line 7872)
- Spirit: MULTIBAGGER_MODEL.md §2: rule archetypes hold 50% of multibagger month-ends, the model's top decile 23%, united 57%; a member of any rule archetype that the model ALSO ranks in its market's top decile — the archetype names the situation, the model says the whole feature profile matches. Engine-native.
- As coded: `mb_rule_count >= 1` (count of every `arch_mb_*` except model_top, computed at line 7866 — includes the two intersections and `arch_mb_biotech_financed_hiring`) & `mb_model_rank_mkt >= 0.90`. NOT gated on `_mb_base` (rule membership implies it except for the biotech rule). Lenses: model p +, rule count +, pct_hist +, dist_hi260 −.
- Lineage: mb_model_scores.csv (multibagger_model.py `score_today`, 35,308 names, fit on the resolved USD-label panel, ~550 features ranked within month×market); `mb_model_rank_mkt` = pct rank of p within `market` over the WHOLE scored universe (mb_today.parquet), not the liquid base; `mb_model_pct_hist` = percentile against the pooled out-of-sample p distribution. - Spirit gaps: MODEL.md §2 says the model's lift on FALLEN names is only 1.5x ("the rules own that ground") vs 3.1x not-fallen — but 25 of the 33 rules are fallen archetypes, so most confluence members sit exactly where the model adds least; no distinction by parent family. The `dist_hi260 −` lens pulls the score toward fallen members, the opposite of the model's documented strength.
- Methodology: the top-decile cut is measured within a rank population that differs from the study's (study: liquid month-ends >= $250k/wk within month×market; today: all 35,308 scored names per market). Illiquid names inflate the denominator, so ">= 0.90" is a looser cut than the backtested one in markets with many illiquid listings. `mb_rule_count` double-counts intersections (a lfd_insider member counts 3), harmless for `>= 1` but the `(mb_rule_count, +1)` lens rewards it. `market` in the model is the exchange key; the engine's `country` is `src` — small markets give a top decile of 2-5 names.

- Suggestions: (1) [method] gate on `_mb_base` and rank `mb_model_p` within market among `_mb_base` names in the engine (recompute the pct from `mb_model_p`) so the decile matches the backtested population. (2) [method] add a staleness guard on the scores' `week` (e.g. <= 5 weeks old) before the cut. (3) [spirit] split the lens/label by parent family: confluence with a NOT-fallen rule (compounder_insiders_at_high, cheap_growth_targets_up, rd_leader_on_volume, growth_past_capex_peak, lean_rd, leader_in_wave) is where MODEL.md measures 2.6-3.1x; drop the `dist_hi260 −` lens.

Within the fallen population that is the name sitting NEARER its high than its industry (its industry fell even more). The engine codes `_q_lo(df['vs_ind_dist_hi260'])` — the BOTTOM quintile — and the comment says "fell more than its industry": the opposite of the 7.0x pattern (the sibling rows `distance from 5y high minus industry high` at 6.9x/6.8x/5.8x all say HIGH). The lens `(vs_ind_dist_hi260, −1)` is inverted the same way. Also `sent_n_analysts.fillna(0) <= 1`: 73% of the universe has no grades on file and passes as "neglected" (A2 §top-9 open).

- Suggestions: (1) [method] fix the sign: `_q_hi(df['vs_ind_dist_hi260'])` (name above its industry's median distance-from-high, i.e. industry more depressed than the name) and flip the lens to +1; re-read the comment ("fell more than its industry" → "in an industry that fell even more"). (2) [spirit] replace `_book_growing` with a 1-year PER-SHARE tangible/book growth (financial-growth or fmp_st twin) so issuance cannot satisfy the "book compounding" leg by itself. (3) [spirit] narrow `_financed` to equity: `commonStockIssued > 0` or `shares_growth >= 5%` with `fcf_yield < 0`; debt raising is the U3 stressed family, not this one. (4) [method] neglect from observed counts, NaN ≠ 0 (A2 §top-9 open).

The study's second leg was `bo_new_holders_12m >= 1` = new >= 5% BENEFICIAL owners (13D AND 13G) from `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership`; the engine uses SC 13D filings only (activist/strategic intent, excludes passive 13G arrivals) and counts 13D/A amendments (including exits). Result: a global "wreckage" archetype that can fire only for US filers.
- Spirit gaps: `buy_share HIGH` / `gap_perc_buyshare` (believers among the few) not a lens; `daysOfInventoryOutstanding HIGH` (1.67x — the inventory-heavy trough) absent; `fund_price_divergence` 1.25x absent (nl_* lenses wired elsewhere); `ps_d4 HIGH` (P/S already re-rating, 0.43x) — no veto.
- Methodology: `_smart_legs >= 1` accepts a headcount jump ALONE as "smart money" — headcount is a company action, not an informed buyer; in the study it was one of three equal legs too, so parity holds, but the label overstates it. `_ins_2q`'s fallback (`usf isna & _mb_insider`) lets a single EDGAR Form-4 purchase equal two quarters of buying. `(net_cash_pct_mcap, −1)` and `(debt_to_equity, +1)` lenses correctly encode the study's "leverage present" finding (the wreckage that re-rates is levered) — good; `(capex_intensity, +1)` matches `capex_rev HIGH`. The blow-up (33%) has no lens.
- FMP augmentations: `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US; `percentOfClass`, `typeOfReportingPerson`, `filingDate`) to rebuild `bo_new_holders_12m` / `bo_increasing_12m` exactly (13D+13G, arrivals only) — catalogued, cached by the event study, unused by the engine; `institutional-ownership` `newPositions` (US-listed) as a third informed leg; `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` (N-PORT, global) as the only non-US arrival signal; `key-metrics-ttm.daysOfInventoryOutstandingTTM` (G) lens; `grades-consensus` (G-partial) for `buy_share`.
- Suggestions: (1) [data] replace `_13d_recent` in `_smart_legs` with a beneficial-ownership NEW-holder count (13D+13G, initial filings or rising `percentOfClass`), restoring the study's leg. (2) [data] add `fmp_inst_new_q0 >= 1` (13F new positions) as a leg for US-listed names beyond the 13D subset and N-PORT `change > 0` for non-US names. (3) [spirit] add lenses `sent_buy_share` (+), `fq_dio` (+) and a `ps` 4-quarter change (−, AVOID 0.43x). (4) [method] mark the archetype US-only in its label/book until (1)-(2) land.

### arch_mb_stressed_not_diluting  (line 7234)
- Spirit: UNCOVERED §"Uncovered, all — 3x within 24 months", top row: `position in 3y range low; share count trend consistency low; state: overlevered stressed; state: fallen angel` (robust 6.04x, 94 events, 10 years, 9 markets, blow-up 25%; SUZLON 2020 37x, ABVE 2025, 600096.SS, SNBR 2020, SNH.JO 2019) and the `debtServiceCoverage vs own low; interestCoverage vs own low; share count trend consistency low` sibling (5.4x). Wolf/Oak Archetype 8 "Deleveraging" reject rule: "growing share count (if paydown funded by equity issuance)". U3.
- As coded: `_mb_base & _mb_fallen` & stressed (`net_debt_ebitda` in [5, 90) | `equity < 0`) & not diluting (NOT `fmp_st_shares_growth_3y > 0.03`) & `ts_dist_lo52 <= 1.15`. Lenses: nde +, shares growth −, dist_lo52 −, dist_hi260 −, netdebt decline months +, mcap −.
- Lineage: nde = `s('net_debt_ebitda', 99)` with EBITDA <= 0 forced to 99 (unknown fails the [5,90) band — a stressed name with NEGATIVE EBITDA cannot be "stressed" here; the study's `overlevered_stressed` state was `nd_ebitda >= 5 | equity < 0` on a panel where negative-EBITDA nd_ebitda is also undefined, so parity is plausible but the loss-making distressed name relies on `equity < 0`, master-covered only 14%); fmp_st_shares_growth_3y (FY diluted shares, unit-normalised, 84%; NaN passes as not diluting); ts_dist_lo52 (G). Global; no SEC leg.
- Spirit gaps: "share count trend consistency LOW" = `tr_shares_consist` (share of the last 8 quarters with the count rising) low — a QUARTERLY, 2-year path; the engine's 3-FY growth <= 3% is coarser and lags (a name that stopped issuing 18 months ago after a big raise 3 years ago fails; one that issued 10% last quarter after two flat years passes if the FY count is not yet printed). "position in 3y range low" → `ts_dist_lo52 <= 1.15` (52-week frame) — a name 15% off its 52w low but at the bottom of its 3y range, or vice versa, differ. Debt-service / interest-coverage-vs-own-history (the sibling) absent.
- Methodology: `_stressed` upper bound 90 excludes the 99 unknown fill but also real nde >= 90 — irrelevant. `~(x > 0.03)` passes NaN (16%) — a name with no share history is certified "not diluting". `equity < 0` uses the master `equity` (14% coverage): fq_equity (fmp_quarterly, 72%) is wired and unused here. The blow-up (25%) has no survivability lens; SUZLON-type names live or die on refinancing.
- FMP augmentations: `ratios-ttm.debtServiceCoverageRatioTTM` / `interestCoverageRatioTTM` + annual `ratios` history (G) for the sibling's coverage-vs-own-history legs (A2 nuanced §13); `fq_equity` (already wired) for negative equity; `financial-growth.weightedAverageSharesDilutedGrowth` (G, per FY) and `fq_shares_yoy` (wired) for a fresher dilution read; `splits` (G) so a reverse split is not read as a share-count fall; `scores-bulk.altmanZScore` (G) as the survivability lens; `company-notes` (US) for listed debt (low).
- Suggestions: (1) [method] build "not diluting" from the quarterly panel: `fq_shares_yoy <= 0.03` AND the 3-FY growth, with NaN failing (not passing). (2) [method] use `fq_equity < 0` (72%) in `_stressed` instead of the 14%-covered master `equity`. (3) [spirit] add interest-coverage / debt-service-vs-own-history (−) and `fq_netdebt_decline_months` (+) — the two mined siblings — plus `fmp_altman_z` (+) as the blow-up lens. (4) [data] reverse-split guard from `splits` (A2 §top-9 open) — a 1-for-10 reverse split reads as a 90% share-count "decline".

### arch_mb_tree_recipe  (line 7050)
- Spirit: ARCHETYPES §"Tree recipes on the narrative axes — 3x within 24 months", top leaf `volatility > 0.61 & size <= 0.13 & fallen > 0.83 & profitability <= 0.46` (rate 16.7%, lift 3.91x, 2,618 month-ends, blow-up 30%); LIFT2 §tree_recipe (14.4%, 3.38x, blow-up 27%; refinements `tr_fcfm_streak LOW` 1.9x, `gap_perc_buyshare HIGH` 1.8x, `n_analysts LOW` 1.7x, `buy_share LOW` 1.5x; AVOID `tr_revg_streak LOW` 0.45x, `flat_base` 0.45x, `wks_since_lo260 HIGH` 0.47x, `roe HIGH` 0.49x); OPERATORS §6 (`buy_share LOW` 3.3x, `n_analysts LOW` 2.1x, `op_roic_x_fcfy HIGH` 1.9x, `rel P/B not high` 0.4x). Faithful to `multibagger_lift2.archetypes()['tree_recipe']`.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & country ranks among the base: vol_1y >= 0.60 & mcap <= 0.13 & (1 − dist_hi260) >= 0.83 & op_margin <= 0.46. Lenses (11): vol +, mcap −, dist_hi260 −, P/S −, nl_sales_1y +, r13 −, wks_since_lo260 −, buy_share −, analysts −, roce×fcfy +, rel_ind_pb −.
- Lineage: ts_vol_1y (annualised weekly-return std, ts_snapshot, G); mcap_usd (81%); ts_dist_hi260 (G); op_margin (master 66%). Global — the only all-tape/size box. Ranks within `src` country among `_mb_base` (comment explains why); the study ranked within month×market over the liquid panel, which is the same population except that the engine's `is_operating` removes financials (the study kept banks).
- Spirit gaps: the tree's fourth split is "profitability" — a NARRATIVE AXIS (composite of opm, npm, roic, roe, gm …), not op_margin alone; using op_margin only is a reasonable proxy but shifts the leaf. `tr_revg_streak LOW` (0.45x) and `flat_base` (0.45x) AVOID rows have no veto/lens; `tr_fcfm_streak LOW` (1.9x) — the strongest refinement — is not a lens here (it is in lfd).
- Methodology: lens `(roce.clip(−1,1) * fcf_yield.clip(−1,1), +1)` is applied to a membership whose profitability is bottom-46% by construction: for a loss-maker with ROCE −25% and FCF yield −15% the product is +0.0375, ranked ABOVE a modestly profitable member (+5% × +2% = 0.001). The study's `op_roic_x_fcfy HIGH` (1.9x) was measured inside PROFITABLE operators where both factors are >= 0; transplanted to this box the lens rewards the deepest cash-burners. Small countries: `_r_size <= 0.13` on a 100-name base is 13 names; the study's month×market ranks had the same issue but with more names per market (whole exchange). `_r_prof` NaN (34% lack op_margin) → `.fillna(False)` drops them — fine.
- FMP augmentations: `grades-consensus` (G-partial) for observed `buy_share`/`n_analysts` (the 3.3x/2.1x refinements need counts that exist); `earnings-surprises-bulk` (G); `historical-market-capitalization` (G) for a PIT size rank; `key-metrics-ttm` ROCE and FCF yield (G) only if the product is re-specified.
- Suggestions: (1) [method] fix the product lens: mask to `roce > 0 & fcf_yield > 0` (else NaN) or use `min(roce, fcf_yield)`; a (−)×(−) product must not rank high. (2) [spirit] add `fqx_fcfm_streak` (−, 1.9x) and `fq_rev_yoy_streak_m` (+; `tr_revg_streak LOW` is the top AVOID) lenses. (3) [method] build the profitability split from a small composite (op_margin, net margin, roce ranks averaged) to follow the tree's axis. (4) [data] observed analyst counts for the `sent_n_analysts −` lens (A2 §top-9 open).

### arch_mb_tree_recipe_10x  (line 7052)
- Spirit: ARCHETYPES §"Tree recipes — 10x within 5 years", top leaf `volatility > 0.62 & size <= 0.12 & cheapness > 0.46 & profitability <= 0.45` (10x rate 5.4%, lift 5.66x, 731 month-ends, blow-up 24%); LIFT2 §tree_recipe_10x (3x rate 12.7%, 2.97x; refinements `tr_fcfm_streak LOW` 1.95x, `gap_perc_buyshare HIGH` 1.8x, `buy_share LOW` 1.7x, `n_analysts LOW` 1.6x, `fund_price_divergence` 1.5x, `capex_rev HIGH` 1.4x; AVOID `dd_time_share_260 LOW` 0.43x, `tr_revg_streak LOW` 0.46x, `flat_base` 0.50x, `R&D LOW` 0.53x, `wks_since_lo260 HIGH` 0.55x, `nd_ebitda LOW` 0.58x, `ins_net_buy_4q LOW` 0.64x). Faithful to the study box.
- As coded: `_mb_base` & ranks: vol >= 0.62 & mcap <= 0.12 & (−P/S where > 0) >= 0.46 & op_margin <= 0.45. Lenses: vol +, mcap −, P/S −, dist_hi260 −, nl_sales_3y +, 13D recency +, wks_since_lo260 −.
- Lineage: as tree_recipe plus p_s (master 64%; `.where(> 0)` so a zero/negative P/S is NaN → fails). The 13D lens is US-only (3,309 names, mcap < $2B). The "cheapness" axis in the tree is a composite (ps, ev_sales, ev_ebit, pb, fcf_yield, earn_yield); the engine uses −P/S only.
- Spirit gaps: the tree swaps FALLEN for CHEAP, so this leaf admits not-fallen names — yet the lenses `dist_hi260 −` and `wks_since_lo260 −` pull toward fallen, fresh-low members; LIFT2 says `dd_time_share_260 LOW` (short time under water) is the worst AVOID (0.43x) — a `ts_dd_time_share_260 +` lens is missing (it exists in lfd). `nd_ebitda LOW` 0.58x / `kr_debtToAssetsRatio LOW` 0.58x: leverage PRESENT is part of the 10x shape — no lens; `capex_rev HIGH` 1.4x — no lens; `R&D LOW` 0.53x — no lens.
- Methodology: "cheapness > 0.46" is a median cut, not a quintile — with −P/S alone the engine's cut is "P/S below the country's liquid-base median", far weaker than the composite's 0.46 (which blends six multiples). Same small-country pool caveat. No product-lens artifact here (not used). `_r_cheap` NaN for 36% of names without P/S → drop; the study's `cheapness` axis would have used another multiple.
- FMP augmentations: `key-metrics-ttm` `evToSalesTTM`, `pbRatioTTM`, `earningsYieldTTM`, `freeCashFlowYieldTTM` (G) to rebuild the cheapness composite where the master lacks P/S; `key-metrics-ttm.netDebtToEBITDATTM` (G) for the leverage lens; `earnings-surprises-bulk` (G); `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US) for the 13D lens semantics (arrivals only).
- Suggestions: (1) [method] build `_r_cheap` as the mean rank of −P/S, −EV/sales, −P/B, +FCF yield (the tree's axis) so the 0.46 cut means what the leaf meant. (2) [spirit] add lenses `ts_dd_time_share_260` (+, top AVOID inverted), `net_debt_ebitda` (+, leverage present), `capex_intensity` (+), `fqx_fcfm_streak` (−). (3) [method] drop or soften the `dist_hi260 −` lens: this leaf is the NOT-necessarily-fallen twin by design.

### arch_mb_wave_neglected_value_accel  (line 7104)
- Spirit: OPERATORS §2 "10x within 5 years" (`tr_debt_streak LOW & accelerating & neglected & deep_value` 20.9x, 42 events, 10x rate 19%, blow-up 6%; `op_ind_size LOW & ps LOW & tr_debt_streak LOW & neglected` 22.4x; ISCTR.IS 2020 84x, TURSG 48x, ASUZU 41x, SASA 35x, SUZLON 33x, CS.TO 19x) generalised by the engine to "in a depressed market/industry frame"; §6 `wave_neglected_value_accel` refinement (n=26,867, 1.31x as first boxed; `gap_perc_buyshare HIGH` 3.6x, `buy_share HIGH` 2.3x, `last_react HIGH` 1.9x, `tr_debt_streak LOW` 1.8x, `up_lo52 HIGH` 1.6x, `vol52 HIGH` 1.6x; LOWER: `dd_time_share_260 LOW` 0.40x, `op_ind_ps HIGH` 0.41x, `range104 LOW` 0.44x, `vol52 LOW` 0.44x, `flat_base` 0.54x, `rd_rev LOW` 0.60x). Lenses (12): mkt tape −, ind tape −, rev_accel +, rel_ind_ev_ebit −, analysts −, netdebt decline +, mcap −, dist_hi260 −, fund_up_unturned +, buy_share +, dist_lo52 +, vol +.

Global — but the study's flagship 10x names (ISCTR, TURSG, GARAN, TSKB: banks/insurers) are excluded by `is_operating` in `_mb_base`; the OPERATORS population did not exclude financials.

- FMP augmentations: `grades-consensus` / `upgrades-downgrades-consensus-bulk` (G-partial, 1 bulk) and `analyst-estimates.numAnalysts*` (G) for observed neglect — the single most valuable fix for this archetype; `earnings-surprises-bulk` (G) for `last_react`; `key-metrics-ttm.netDebtToEBITDATTM` history via annual `key-metrics` (G) for an 8-quarter debt streak; `historical-market-capitalization` (G) for a PIT market-median tape by USD size if the country frame is to be size-weighted.
- Suggestions: (1) [method] neglect from observed counts: `sent_n_analysts <= 2` only where a count exists, else `numAnalystsEps <= 2`, else NaN-fails (A2 §top-9 open). (3) [spirit] add `ts_dd_time_share_260` (+) and `rel_ind_p_s` (−) lenses (0.40x / 0.41x LOWER rows) and `evt_react_last` (+). (4) [method] document that the study's Turkish bank/insurer 10x examples are outside `is_operating`; if a financials twin is wanted it needs a different value leg (P/B, not EV/EBIT).

[spirit] Carry each study's top AVOID/LOWER rows as lenses where they are currently absent: `ts_dd_time_share_260` (+) for wave/tree_10x, SBC-to-revenue (+ via key-metrics, G) for quiet_turn/sequence, `upgrades_12m` (−), `fqx_fcfm_streak` (−) for the tree pair, leverage-present (+) for tree_10x/smart_money.

# Slice xr_a: Forensic / hidden-value / exceptional-return, slice A (26)

## Audit 3 — XR slice A (26 forensic / hidden-value / exceptional-return archetypes)

Read-only audit of `archetype_tags.py` (line numbers from dumps2, current file). Counts are from the current `archetype_tags.csv` joined to `asymmetry_global.csv` on symbol (46,526 rows; `src=='US'` 16,945). "Universe coverage" figures are share-of-rows non-null, US vs non-US. Note: `fmp_filled_*` are 0/1 fill flags. The tags CSV writes only a curated column set, so `ts_dist_hi260`, `tc_*`, `fqx_*`, `evt_*` legs cannot be verified from the output; they ARE merged at runtime (lines 266-273; files present on disk: ts_snapshot 34k rows, fmp_throughcycle 46k, fmp_quarterly_ext 34k, fmp_events 46k).

Shared lineage facts used below (universe coverage US / non-US): `rpo` 7%/0% (EDGAR only); `deferred_revenue` 22%/1% raw, fq-filled globally; `income_taxes_paid_ttm` 29%/1%; `fq_cash_tax_rate` 9%/1%; `goodwill_intangibles_pct_assets` 38%/1% raw, fq-filled; `ppe_net` 30%/1% (deliberately NOT FMP-filled: FMP PP&E includes lease ROU); `da_ttm` 52%/57%; `capex_avg`/`oe_avg`/`ni_avg` ~25-31%/1% raw, fmp_st-filled; `retained_earnings` 37%/1% raw, fq-filled with IFRS-reserve guard; `rev_yoy_streak_q` 32%/1% raw, fq-filled; `p_tb` 25%/1% raw, fq-filled; `income_continuing_ops_ttm` 11%/0% raw, fq-filled; `assets_held_for_sale` 5%/0% (no fill); `investments_associates` 15%/0% (no fill); `effective_tax_rate` 14%/0%; `financing_cf_ttm` 47%/14% raw, fq-filled; `buyback_yield` 22%/0% (EDGAR levels re-yielded) vs `net_buyback_ttm` 38%/26% (Yahoo buybacks−issuance); `normalized_ebitda` 46%/64% (Yahoo 5-yr average, RNO.PA basis repair); `ncav_pct_mcap` 28%/66% (EDGAR and Yahoo paths both net of preferred + NCI); `insider_ownership_pct` 70%/81% (Yahoo heldPercentInsiders, includes corporate parents); `ts_r52` 74%/74%; `fmp_inst_own_chg_q0` 32%/1%. `nde` (net_debt_ebitda) is forced to 99 wherever `ebitda_ttm <= 0` (line 1075).

---

### arch_xr_amortization_mask  (line 4466)
- Spirit: serial-acquirer engine where acquired-intangible amortization crushes GAAP EPS with no cash cost, so owner earnings run far above NI (Constellation/Heico). Engine-native; nearest human screen is Pernas's idea-generation filter "high D&A-to-capex" (Pernas doc, "Pitch extraction and the complete realised-position record") and master ref II (Yartseva: FCF/P, not EPS, drives returns).
- As coded: goodwill+intangibles ≥30% of assets; NI>0 and OE/NI ≥1.5 (OE = NI + D&A − max(capex_ttm, capex_avg)); FCF yield ≥7%; P/E ≥15 or NaN; local mcap/OE ≤12; shares_yoy ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 99 firers (30 US), median mcap $812M.
- Lineage: goodwill share EDGAR→`fq_gw_pct_assets` fill (91 of 99 firers FMP-filled, so global); D&A = EDGAR/Yahoo `da_ttm` else implied EBITDA−EBIT (op_margin>0 only); capex_avg EDGAR→fmp_st fill; P/E, FCF yield master. All legs local-currency or dimensionless. No P/E NaN escapes among firers.
- Spirit gaps: (a) nothing establishes a SERIAL acquirer — one large acquisition gives the same goodwill share (fq_acq_pct_assets exists but is unused); (b) the "no cash cost" claim is true of amortization only, but the D&A leg is TOTAL D&A, so a PP&E-heavy business with one bought subsidiary passes on depreciation; (c) no realisation path (amortization roll-off, adjusted-EPS adoption, or the compounding itself via per-share growth).
- Methodology issues: total D&A vs amortization (above); OE/NI ≥1.5 & FCF yield ≥7% & mcap/OE ≤12 are internally consistent but the FCF-yield leg already prices the cash, so "market prices masked EPS" is asserted by P/E ≥15 alone; the P/E NaN escape is harmless here (NI>0 required). Near-duplicate of forensic_multiple_gap / depreciation_cliff / owner_earnings_power (confluence puts them in one family — correct).
- FMP augmentations: cash-flow `acquisitionsNet` (already `fq_acq_pct_assets`, global) and fmp_st per-FY acquisitions → recurring-acquirer leg; `owner-earnings` ownersEarnings (global, cached; A2 A.1 U*) as second OE basis; `financial-growth` fiveYOperatingCFGrowthPerShare (global) for the "cash compounds regardless" half. FMP D&A is also combined, so the amortization split stays EDGAR (AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets tag).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] add a cadence leg: `fq_acq_pct_assets` ≥3% now OR acquisitions in ≥3 of the last 5 FYs (fmp_st) — the thesis is the engine, not the residue. 2 [method] where the EDGAR amortization tag exists use amortization-only in the OE/NI ratio; else require goodwill share AND D&A/PP&E consistency (fq_da_to_ppe) so depreciation-heavy names are not tagged "amortization". 3 [data] cross-check OE with `owner-earnings` (A2 A.1 U* open). 4 [spirit] per-share OCF compounding (financial-growth) as the exceptional tier.

### arch_xr_asset_owner_catalyst  (line 4683)
- Spirit: Baron Generation I — a completed object below realisable value, an owner-operator who can build the next stage, and a catalyst already in motion. Human sources: Wolf/Oak "Archetype 4: Asset-Backed Floor + Optional Growth" (catalysts: asset sale, buybacks below NAV, M&A), Liger "Asset-backed optionality" (net cash covers a substantial part of value; management willing to redeploy), compendium Part II C (insiders incentivised + hidden value).
- As coded: insider ≥15%; floor = pb<0.8 OR net cash ≥40% OR NCAV ≥80% OR hidden ≥40%; catalyst = any annual first-positive (EBITDA/CFO/FCF/NI) OR rev_accel & oper_lev_any OR buyback (yield ≥2% or net_buyback>0) OR fqx_eps_turned OR SC 13D ≤365d (mcap<$2B); beaten_down_any(0.25); NI>0 or ni_avg>0 or cfo_yield>0; nde<3 or net cash; shares ≤5%; not melting. Lenses: none. 1,169 firers (94 US; KR 233, HK 161, JP 145, IN 130), median mcap $52M.
- Lineage: insider = Yahoo heldPercentInsiders (463 of 1,169 firers ≥60% → parent-controlled subsidiaries, A1 A.3 finding); hidden_pct = EDGAR associates + net cash, so for non-EDGAR rows it collapses to net cash (duplicate of the net-cash floor); first-positive flags annual (FCF-first-positive is the noisiest, A1 kpi_threshold); 13D US-only (exceptional evidence only); buyback = Yahoo net_buyback (global) or EDGAR yield (US).
- Spirit gaps: "owner-operator" is not distinguished from a listed subsidiary; "reproduction value" is proxied by pb<0.8; the catalyst set is too broad for "already in motion" (293 firers carry a buyback; an annual FCF first-positive is not a catalyst); no realisation path (asset sale/tender/wind-up as Wolf lists); 1,169 members for "the original primitive".
- Methodology issues: insider ≥60% not split out; `oper_lev_any` includes raw sequential; the catalyst OR-set mixes dated (13D, fqx) with undated annual flags; hidden floor not independent of net cash outside EDGAR.
- FMP augmentations: `key-executives` "Founder" title (global, snapshot; A2 A.3 N) → owner leg; `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` typeOfReportingPerson IN (US) → individual owner; `insider-trading/statistics` (already `usf_ins_buy_quarters_4q`, US) → owner buying; `shares-float` (global) to separate float from strategic stakes; `mergers-acquisitions-latest` / `sec-filings-search` 8-K 2.01 (US) → dated asset-sale catalyst.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] treat insider ≥60% as controlled-sub unless revealed alignment (usf_ins buys or net_buyback>0) (A1 B.1 owner_operator open). 2 [spirit] tier the catalyst: dated (13D, fqx_eps_turned, buyback with shrinkage, M&A/8-K) = core; annual first-positive = watch; require FCF-first-positive to be corroborated by CFO/EBITDA (A1 kpi_threshold open). 3 [data] founder leg from key-executives (A2 A.3 open; endpoint matrix LEG). 4 [method] make the hidden-asset floor count only when associates are observed (else it is the net-cash floor twice).

### arch_xr_audited_streak_unrerated  (line 6063)
- Spirit: 8+ consecutive filed quarters of revenue or NI growth while the market has not re-rated and the multiple is not rich. Human sources: master ref I.A(15) "Narrative lag after fundamental inflection"; compendium #8 "behaviour change without reaction"; Liger "consistently conservative guidance / underreaction to earnings". Construction engine-native.
- As coded: best streak ≥8 (EDGAR NI/rev streak, fq date-matched streak in quarter-equivalents, fmp_dyn positional streak for quarterly filers); window ≥8 comparisons; no-rerate = any of 6 lenses (EV/S change ≤10%, price_yoy ≤ median(rev,EBITDA growth), expansion-implied ≤10%, fmp_dyn_unrerated_gap ≥15%, bs_coil_rev ≥log1.25, bs_coil_ebit ≥log1.40) OR 1-yr coil (fq TTM sales growth ≥ ts_r52); not rich (P/E ≤25 or EV/EBITDA ≤14 or both unknown); not melting. Lenses: no-rerate lens count, unrerated_gap_1y, fmp_dyn_unrerated_gap. 2,331 firers (657 US), median mcap $949M.
- Lineage: 2,061 of 2,331 firers use the fq-filled streak (global; JP 421); ts_r52 74%; bs_coil from the stale/partial base_snapshot (A1 §0.2); price_yoy snapshot noisy (A1 §0.3). Half-yearly filers: 4 periods ×2 = 8 quarter-equivalents (consistent).
- Spirit gaps: streak has no magnitude (eight +1% quarters qualify); the "told" half (beats) is absent — the source thesis is beats ignored, not growth ignored; "not rich" is absolute, not vs the name's own multiple history (A2 B.1 #5).
- Methodology issues: 6 ORed lenses; `_esc_au ≤ 0.10` is true for any flat multiple (A1 A.2: ≥2 lenses keeps 1,696 of 2,126); on current firers 350 fire on a single lens. The "both multiples unknown" escape is dead code: `ev_ebitda_v` is `s('ev_ebitda', 99.0)` so `.isna()` is never true — the intent (permit unknown) and the behaviour (exclude unknown) disagree; decide explicitly. 2,331 firers is broad for an XR class and inflates confluence's "engine" family.
- FMP augmentations: `earnings-surprises-bulk` (global, dated; A2 A.2 N) → beats ≥6/8 as the "told" leg; `enterprise-values` history (U, fmp_dynamics) → EV/S ≤ own 3-yr median (A2 B.1 #5); `financial-growth` per-share growth (global).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] require ≥2 no-rerate lenses or the measured 1-yr/2-yr coil (A1 A.2 open; A2 B.1 #5 open). 2 [spirit] add a growth-magnitude floor over the streak (e.g. median YoY over the 8 quarters ≥5%, from the fq panel). 3 [data] beats leg from earnings-surprises-bulk (A2 open). 4 [method] resolve the dead "both NaN" escape.

### arch_xr_baron_compounder  (line 4593)
- Spirit: founder/owner-led, decade-length growth, reinvesting so hard that current earnings are suppressed, survivable balance sheet, growth-adjusted price, still small. Human sources: compendium Part I #1 Phelps/Mayer 100x (small cap, ROIC>15%, runway, founder, low start valuation); compendium Part II B (insider 20-58%, no dilution); master ref VI Deden (organic, not acquisition-dependent).
- As coded: not financing-fragile; insider ≥10%; duration = 12q positive share ≥75% OR rev streak ≥6 OR 3y CAGR ≥15%; rev_yoy ≥12%; reinvestment = (GM−OM ≥15pp & GM ≥35%) OR capex intensity ≥8%; nde ≤2 or net cash; evsg ≤0.40 or P/S ≤6; revenue ≥$25M; mcap ≤$25B; roic_after_sbc not negative (EDGAR-only, soft); shares ≤8%; not melting. Lenses: none. 745 firers (87 US; CN 146, IN 135, JP 111), median mcap $693M.
- Lineage: insider Yahoo (236 of 745 ≥60% → controlled subs); `rev_yoy_pos_share_12q` EDGAR 36-month → fq fill is a 24-month share (`fq_rev_pos_share_24m`), so "12 quarters" means 8 for most firers; 3y CAGR master; GM/OM master; evsg master. Global.
- Spirit gaps: FOUNDER unmeasured (insider% ≠ founder); "decade" evidence is ≤3 years; no return-on-capital leg (Phelps test 2) — a Baron name earns high returns on what it reinvests; the reinvestment leg (GM−OM ≥15pp) is any SG&A-heavy business — R&D/revenue or growth capex tells reinvestment from bloat (A2 §C on expensed_growth_value); capex ≥8% of sales is any capital-intensive business regardless of growth intent.
- Methodology issues: window shortening on fill; controlled-sub inflation; P/S ≤6 escape loose for 12% growth; the reinvestment OR is nearly always satisfiable.
- FMP augmentations: `key-executives` Founder title (global; A2 A.3 N); `financial-growth` tenY/fiveYRevenueGrowthPerShare (global, per-FY; A2 A.1 N; endpoint matrix: ten-year per-share revenue ≥+159%); income-statement R&D (already `fq` `rnd` flow) → R&D/revenue; `owner-earnings` growthCapex (global) for the capex leg; `historical-employee-count` (US) revenue/employee.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] decade duration from financial-growth per-share windows (A2 B.2 #14 / matrix LEG, open). 2 [spirit] founder leg via key-executives (A2 open) and cap insider at 60% (A1 open). 3 [spirit] evidence the reinvestment: R&D/revenue ≥10% (fq) or growth capex (owner-earnings) instead of the bare GM−OM gap. 4 [method] a capital-productivity floor (gross profit / total assets, fq) so the suppressed earnings sit on a productive base.

### arch_xr_bigbath_rebound  (line 4408)
- Spirit: a one-off non-cash writedown polluted trailing EBITDA/NI; CFO healthy; cheap on the normalized base; beaten-down. Human sources: Wolf/Oak "Archetype 9: Turnaround" (non-recurring charges, first improved report); Pernas "Low-multiple operational inflection" (depressed earnings + a measurable normalisation mechanism); master ref I.C(19) "Temporarily worse than it looks".
- As coded: mid-cycle EBITDA (5-yr avg margin × current revenue) >0; EBITDA_ttm <0.6× mid-cycle OR NI<0; cfo_yield>0; EV/mid-cycle ≤6; beaten_down_any(0.30); shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 652 firers (132 US), median mcap $127M.
- Lineage: normalized_ebitda/revenue = Yahoo 5-yr averages (46%/64%); EBITDA_ttm, cfo_yield, EV master; global. EV (listing ccy) over margin×revenue_ttm (reporting ccy) relies on `_fx_coherent`.
- Spirit gaps: the BATH is never identified — nothing distinguishes an impairment/restructuring from a cyclical trough or a margin collapse (the non-cash test is only cfo_yield>0); the roll-off timing is unmeasured; the NI<0 branch fires on interest/tax/discontinued causes with EBITDA intact.
- Methodology issues: overlaps double_trough/cyclical_trough/latent_bath on the same mid-cycle signal (grouped in one confluence family; fine); a 5-yr average that contains the bath year is depressed (conservative); `cfo_yield>0` is not tested against NI or the EBITDA gap; no evidence of the charge's size.
- FMP augmentations: cash-flow `otherNonCashItems` and income-statement `otherExpenses` (global, dated) → charge magnitude vs the EBITDA shortfall; `fqx_ebit_ttm_g` (already computed) → rebound evidence (endpoint matrix LEG, open); `earnings-calendar` (global) → the roll-off print date. EDGAR impairment tags (ImpairmentOfLongLivedAssets, GoodwillImpairmentLoss) for US.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] identify the bath: non-cash charge (EDGAR impairment tags; FMP otherNonCashItems) ≥50% of the EBITDA/NI shortfall — trough ≠ bath. 2 [method] move the bare `NI<0` branch to a watch tier unless EBITDA ≥0.6× mid-cycle (so the pollution is below EBITDA and explained by a charge). 3 [data] fqx_ebit_ttm_g rebound leg (matrix LEG open). 4 [spirit] surface roll-off: quarter in which the charge exits the TTM window (fq panel).

### arch_xr_cannibal_below_cash  (line 4486)
- Spirit: market cap below net cash while management buys back — every share bought below its cash backing (Teledyne / 2022 China ADR class). Human sources: Wolf/Oak Archetype 4 catalysts ("share buybacks below NAV"); master ref III capital-structure (net cash, buybacks; Korea Value-Up treasury cancellation).
- As coded: net_cash_pct ≥1.0; buyback signal (yield ≥2% or net_buyback>0) corroborated by net_buyback>0 or shares_yoy<0; shares_yoy ≤2%; NI>0 or ni_avg>0 or cfo_yield>0; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 80 firers (24 US; KR 22, HK 12, JP 10), median mcap $89M.
- Lineage: net cash = broad cash+investments − debt (Yahoo/EDGAR, global; may include restricted/long-term investments — the engine's own G2 note calls >100% "shell/holdco artifact" elsewhere); buyback_yield EDGAR-only (US), so non-US rows pass on Yahoo net_buyback_ttm>0 (a $1 net repurchase qualifies); shares_yoy master.
- Spirit gaps: accretion magnitude (buyback% × (net cash/mcap − 1)) unmeasured; treasury vs cancellation (Korea) only via shares_yoy<0; cash accessibility (onshore China cash / VIE — the very 2022 ADR problem) untested; cash burn vs cash pile (cfo_yield>0 is the only guard).
- Methodology issues: the first OR is redundant given the corroboration leg — effective rule is net_buyback>0 OR (yield ≥2% & shrinkage); no buyback materiality; broad cash in the numerator.
- FMP augmentations: `splits` (global; A2 A.4 N; matrix SPL PROXY) to prove shares_yoy<0 is not a reverse split; cash-flow `commonStockRepurchased` (already fq `buyback` flow) → global buyback yield through the fx bridge (materiality floor); balance-sheet `cashAndShortTermInvestments` vs `longTermInvestments` → narrow-cash coverage; `historical-market-capitalization` (global) → buyback-at-discount timing.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] buyback materiality ≥1-2% of mcap from fq buyback (global) instead of net_buyback>0. 2 [data] splits guard (A2 §C open). 3 [method] narrow cash (cash+STI − debt) ≥ mcap as core, broad cash as watch. 4 [spirit] surface per-share accretion as the ranking score.

### arch_xr_cannibal_below_tbook  (line 4941)
- Spirit: buying back below TANGIBLE book while earning — Teledyne one step out. Human sources: as above plus compendium Part I #6 (Marston's 0.37x NAV, Wetherspoon unrevalued) and master ref III (Japan TSE PBR reform, Korea Value-Up).
- As coded: 0<p_tb<1; buyback signal + corroboration (as XR17); shares_yoy ≤2%; NI>0 or ni_avg>0; nde<3 or net cash; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 681 firers (111 US; JP 353, KR 78), median mcap $175M.
- Lineage: p_tb EDGAR (25% US) → `fq_p_tb` fill = listing mcap / ((totalStockholdersEquity − goodwillAndIntangibleAssets) × fx); 641 of 681 firers FMP-filled (parent-only equity confirmed in fmp_quarterly.py). Buyback legs as XR17 (non-US relies on Yahoo net_buyback>0).
- Spirit gaps: no returns floor — buying below TB a 2%-ROE business accretes book, not value (Teledyne bought a high-return business); no accretion magnitude; no cancellation test; Japan dominance (353) reflects TSE-reform buybacks generally, which is the source thesis but makes 681 members a policy cohort, not a rarity.
- Methodology issues: `ni_avg>0` admits a current loss; no buyback materiality; `nde<3` is 99-filled when EBITDA ≤0 so only net cash rescues loss-makers (intended).
- FMP augmentations: `splits` guard (open); fq buyback level for materiality; `ratios`/`key-metrics` ROE / fmp_roic (global) for a returns floor; `dividends` history for total payout.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] buyback materiality ≥1% of mcap (fq buyback, global). 2 [spirit] returns floor (ROE ≥8% or fmp_roic ≥8%) — accretion on a business worth owning. 3 [data] splits guard (A2 open). 4 [spirit] rank by (1/p_tb − 1) × buyback yield (accretion to TB/share).

### arch_xr_cash_leads_book  (line 5314)
- Spirit: the accruals anomaly inverted and proven — FCF ≥1.2× NI now and ≥1.1× a year ago, CFO growing ≥10pp faster than NI, not a working-capital release or SBC add-back; cheap on the understated E. Human sources: Liger "clean cash conversion" and "incremental margins above historical averages" (Earnings/backlog/conservative guidance); master ref II (Yartseva: FCF/P the strongest driver). Engine-native construction (Sloan 1996 inverted).
- As coded: revenue ≥$10M; `fq_cash_leads_earnings_flag`==1; P/E ≤15 or FCF yield ≥6%; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 549 firers (177 US), median mcap $723M.
- Lineage: the flag is fully global (fmp_quarterly: PIT filing dates, one-currency, cadence-aware, dimensionless); P/E and FCF yield from master. Well built — the trajectory test is observed, not inferred.
- Spirit gaps: the MECHANISM is not identified — conservative provisioning, deferred-revenue float, front-loaded expensing and plain heavy D&A all pass; the last one is amortization_mask/depreciation_cliff wearing this flag. "CFO growing faster than NI" is satisfied by NI FALLING with CFO flat.
- Methodology issues: `fq_cfo_growth_minus_ni_growth ≥0.10` has no floor on CFO growth itself; P/E (master NI) vs flag (fq NI) are different bases but the same sign; the archetype is a thin wrapper around one fq flag — its quality is the flag's quality.
- FMP augmentations: the fq flows already carried (`da`, `sbc`, `chg_wc`, `defrev`) plus cash-flow `deferredIncomeTax` (global) → decompose CFO−NI into D&A / deferred tax / WC / SBC / other and tag the mechanism; `key-metrics` incomeQuality (already used in F2) as cross-check; `earnings-surprises-bulk` for the "market prices the GAAP line" half (beats on EPS while cash leads).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] decompose the wedge and tag mechanism; treat "wedge ≈ D&A" as a sibling (amortization/depreciation) not this class. 2 [method] require CFO TTM growth >0 as well as faster-than-NI. 3 [method] keep as is otherwise — the proven-trajectory design is the right one; the D&A-only case is the only leak worth closing.

### arch_xr_cash_tax_advantage  (line 5211)
- Spirit: cash taxes paid materially below the book provision (≥40% wedge) in a normal book-tax year, uplifting owner earnings ≥10% over GAAP NI; a deferred-tax-liability cushion the EPS crowd ignores. Engine-native (gap G7); human cousins: master ref III NOL shells/§382, compendium III assembly checklist (NOL, TRA), Buffett DTL float.
- As coded: EDGAR path — pretax>0, book tax>0, book rate 10-45%, cash tax >0 and ≤60% of book, NI>0, wedge/NI ≥10%; FMP path only where EDGAR tax is NaN — same-FY fq rates, cash ≤0.6×book, median wedge ≥40% over ≥2 FYs, rate-form uplift ≥10%, NI>0; cheap = P/E ≤20 or EY ≥5% or FCF yield ≥3%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 278 firers, 270 US, 8 non-US, only 10 via the FMP path; median mcap $3.1B.
- Lineage: EDGAR IncomeTaxesPaid TTM (29% US); `fq_cash_tax_rate` covers 9% US / 1% non-US because FMP `incomeTaxesPaid` is blank for most non-US filers — the "global" path is nominal. In practice US-only and large-cap skewed.
- Spirit gaps: the shield's TYPE is not classified — accelerated depreciation (DTL, reverses when capex slows), NOL usage (finite; `nol_usd` exists in master), SBC deductions (a dilution cost, should be excluded), foreign-rate arbitrage; the "other half" (when the wedge closes) is unmeasured; EDGAR path is a single TTM window (persistence only on the FMP path).
- Methodology issues: two paths with different persistence standards; cheapness leg loose (FCF yield ≥3%); no DTL/NOL balance materiality check.
- FMP augmentations: cash-flow `deferredIncomeTax` (the deferred-tax accrual — the wedge's mirror image, global, far better filled than incomeTaxesPaid) → second global lens: deferredIncomeTax/NI ≥10% persistent; balance-sheet `deferredTaxLiabilitiesNonCurrent` and `taxAssets` (global) → DTL/mcap materiality and growth with capex; `fq_sbc_to_cfo` to exclude SBC-driven wedges.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] use FMP `deferredIncomeTax` as the global second lens (the FMP incomeTaxesPaid path reaches 10 firers). 2 [spirit] classify the shield (NOL: negative RE / nol_usd → finite; DTL: rising DTL with capex ≥ D&A → durable while investing; SBC-driven → exclude via fq_sbc_to_cfo). 3 [method] give the EDGAR path the same ≥2-FY persistence the FMP path has (prior-FY cash tax from the EDGAR annual cache).

### arch_xr_clean_net_net  (line 4296)
- Spirit: Graham net-net (NCAV net of preferred and NCI) covering the whole price, positive earnings power (5-yr average or latest), management paying owners while you wait. Human sources: compendium Part I #6 (scrapyard, Japanese net-nets), master ref III net-nets/net cash, Wolf Archetype 4 (buybacks below NAV), Cundill.
- As coded: ncav_pct ≥1.0; ni_avg>0 or NI>0; dividend yield ≥2% or buyback_yield>0; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 308 firers (15 US; JP 86, KR 84, HK 34), median mcap $56M.
- Lineage: NCAV — EDGAR and Yahoo (yartseva_db) paths are both net of NCI + preferred (verified); ni_avg EDGAR/fmp_st fill; dividend_yield Yahoo-fresh; buyback_yield is EDGAR-only (0% non-US), so for 293 of 308 firers the "paying" leg is dividend-only — the buyback half is dead outside the US.
- Spirit gaps: NCAV quality (inventory/receivable haircuts — `compute_nnwc.py` exists but is unused here); NCAV trend (a floor that shrinks 20%/yr is not a floor); dividend continuity (paid, not cut); the realisation catalyst (tender, activist, TSE/Value-Up reform) is absent; Graham's 2/3 entry margin is not the exceptional tier.
- Methodology issues: `buyback_yield>0` is uncorroborated (the XR17 creation/redemption-artifact lesson is not applied); ni_avg>0 admits a current loss-maker (Graham-consistent, but combine with NCAV trend).
- FMP augmentations: fq `buyback` flow (global) → restores the buyback leg for non-US; `dividends` (already `ev_div_raise_streak`, `ev_div_cut_2y`) → paid-and-not-cut; balance-sheet totalCurrentAssets/totalLiabilities/minorityInterest/preferredStock (fq, global, dated) → NCAV now vs year-ago; `key-metrics` grahamNetNet as an independent second opinion (A2 §C).
- Suggestions: 1 [data] non-US buyback leg from fq buyback via the fx bridge (95% of firers currently cannot satisfy it). 2 [method] apply XR17's corroboration (net_buyback>0 or shares_yoy<0) to the buyback leg. 3 [spirit] NCAV trend ≥ −10% YoY (fq) and ev_div_cut_2y==0 so the floor and the coupon both hold. 4 [data] NNWC (compute_nnwc) ≥ mcap as the exceptional tier.

### arch_xr_compounding_deployer  (line 4312)
- Spirit: high audited ROIIC on capital actually deployed, internally financed, revenue still compounding, ordinary multiple. Human sources: compendium Part I #1 (100x: high ROIC, reinvestment runway, low start valuation); master ref II (asset growth covered by EBITDA growth).
- As coded: roic_lindy ≥12%; roiic_lindy ≥20%; financing_cf_ttm ≤0; rev streak ≥4 or rev_yoy ≥10%; ev_ebit ≤14 or P/E ≤18 or evsg ≤0.40; shares ≤2%; not melting (no fx guard needed — ratios and a sign). Lenses: none. 437 firers (117 US; IN 95, JP 51), median mcap $1.4B.
- Lineage: roic/roiic lindy EDGAR (edgar_roic_roiic: median rolling ROIIC with ≥$1M deployment) → `fmp_st_roiic_lindy` fill (320 of 437 firers non-US, so most members ride the FMP definition; A2 A.1 warns rolling ROIIC on small deltas is noisy); financing_cf EDGAR → fq fill (329 of 437 filled); NaN financing fails the `<=0` test silently (unknown = excluded).
- Spirit gaps: no evidence of deployment NOW (a harvester with a great ROIIC history passes); financing ≤0 can be a dividend, which is a payout not self-funding of growth; per-share compounding (the 100-bagger literature's actual measure) absent; the evsg escape admits any EV/EBIT if growth is fast.
- Methodology issues: two ROIIC definitions (EDGAR vs fmp_st) mixed across rows without a basis tag; silent NaN exclusion undocumented.
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth` fiveY/tenY per-share revenue & OCF (global, per-FY; A2 B.2 #14, matrix EXC) → exceptional tier; `fq_capex_to_da` ≥1 / `fq_acq_pct_assets` / total-asset growth (already carried) → "deploying now"; cash-flow `netCashUsedForInvestingActivites` sign.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] add a deploying-now leg (capex/D&A ≥1.0 or asset growth ≥5% or acquisitions >0, fq). 2 [data] FG per-share growth exceptional tier (A2 open). 3 [method] tag the ROIIC basis (EDGAR vs fmp_st) and require the fmp_st version to clear a minimum invested-capital delta. 4 [method] document or fall back the NaN-financing exclusion (fmp_st financing-outflow years).

### arch_xr_confluence  (line 6133)
- Spirit: the historic outliers were a floor AND an engine AND a forensic tell AND a dislocation at once. Human source: compendium "Cross-Archetype Convergence" (score on ≥3 archetypes; rank by count × asymmetry ratio × liquidity tier); Pernas (dislocation + strengthening motor + catalyst + large upside).
- As coded: `xr_family_count ≥3` over four families (floor 10 gates, dislocation 4, forensic 22, engine 15). Lenses: none (a graded conviction score follows at 6135+: secular-decline and thin-delta down-weights, fortress up-weight). 1,568 firers (389 US; 1,229 at 3 families, 339 at 4), median mcap $134M — ~6% of INV, not "the rarest flag in the book".
- Lineage: inherits members. Breadth comes from broad members: gaap_profit_crossover 3,200 (engine), audited_streak 2,331 (engine), asset_owner_catalyst 1,169 (floor), floor_inflection 720 (floor).
- Spirit gaps: the compendium ranks by asymmetry and liquidity — neither is in the count; within-family strength is flattened (5 forensic gates = 1); no tradeability term.
- Methodology issues: the drawdown is shared across families — `beaten_down_any` sits in floor_inflection and asset_owner_catalyst (floor), bigbath (forensic), double_trough / quality_crisis / insider_capitulation (dislocation), so one crash can light three families; harvest_distribution, paydown_yield and cyclical_trough are classed "engine" though they are asset/forensic theses; gaap_profit_crossover (13% of INV) makes "engine" nearly free for any first-profit microcap.
- FMP augmentations: none new — `ts_dvol26_usd` (panel) for the liquidity factor; member upgrades flow through.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] count a family only through gates that are not driven by the same drawdown leg as another counted family (or drop beaten_down_any from the floor family's members for counting). 2 [method] exclude or down-weight the two broadest gates (gaap_profit_crossover, audited_streak) in the count, or require at least one member with ≤300 firers. 3 [spirit] surface (family count) × (asymmetry proxy: net cash + NCAV) × (ts_dvol26_usd tier) as the compendium's ranking; keep count-4 as exceptional.

### arch_xr_contracted_backlog  (line 5006)
- Spirit: remaining performance obligations ≥1 year of revenue while the market prices the trailing tape. Human sources: Wolf/Oak "Archetype 3: Earnings/Backlog Inflection with Conservative Guidance" (ADF Group: backlog +95%, stock halved, 5× EV/EBITDA; reject vague/MOU or one-drop backlogs); Liger ("a backlog increase without corresponding investor attention"; backlog conversion); master ref VIII Contract/Inflection.
- As coded: rpo>0; rpo/revenue_ttm_usd ≥1.0; cheap = EV/S ≤4 or EV/EBITDA ≤15 or FCF yield ≥3%; rev_yoy ≥0; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 105 firers, 103 US, median mcap $8.8B.
- Lineage: `rpo` EDGAR-only (7% US, 0% non-US; ASC 606 disclosure) in USD vs revenue_ttm_usd (same currency, correct). No non-US substitute (FMP has no RPO; A1 A.6 open: transcripts). The median member is a large-cap SaaS/aerospace/defense filer whose RPO is fully visible — the opposite of the sources' <$500M neglected name.
- Spirit gaps: backlog must be GROWING (Wolf: +95%; Liger: "increase") — no RPO YoY, no book-to-bill; "unpriced" is asserted by cheapness, never tested against expectations; RPO ≥1× revenue is industry-NORMAL for subscription and long-cycle defense, so the level is a sector property, not a mispricing; the 12-month portion of RPO (disclosed under ASC 606) is the convertible part; one-drop vs recurring backlog untested.
- Methodology issues: EV/EBITDA ≤15 is broad at large-cap; rev_yoy ≥0 too weak for "converting"; no industry frame.
- FMP augmentations: `analyst-estimates` next-FY revenue (global, snapshot; A2 A.2 U*) → "unpriced" = consensus growth below what 12-month RPO implies; `earning-call-transcript` (targeted; A1 A.6 / A2 A.2 N) → backlog / book-to-bill for non-US; profile `industry` (global) for the peer frame; `fq_defrev_to_rev` (global) as the explicit sibling fallback.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] require RPO growth: RPO YoY ≥ revenue YoY + 10pp (prior-context RPO is in the EDGAR cache). 2 [spirit] test "unpriced": consensus forward revenue growth < implied backlog conversion (analyst-estimates). 3 [method] normalise RPO coverage against the FMP industry median so structural-backlog industries do not pass on level. 4 [data] transcripts for non-US reach (A1 A.6 open).

### arch_xr_cyclical_trough  (line 4798; reframed at 4822)
- Spirit: a cyclical at a genuine asset discount with earnings depressed against its own mid-cycle, where the physical base floors the downside and the cycle mean-reverts. Human sources: compendium Part II D Capital Cycle (buy at maximum pessimism, asset-heavy below replacement cost, survivors); master ref I.E "Surviving thing" three-layer test; Pernas "cyclically depressed".
- As coded (reframed): asset_heavy measured (fq PP&E/assets ≥30% else capex intensity ≥5% else the cyclical-sector label); 0<pb<1; mid-cycle >0 and (EBITDA <0.75× mid-cycle OR op margin ≤ tc_med_opm − 5pp with tc_years ≥5); EV/mid-cycle ≤7; shares ≤5%; positive-EBITDA branch: (nde<3.5 or net cash) & op_viable & not melting; negative-EBITDA branch: net cash ≥20% OR (−90<nde≤1.0). Lenses: tc_med_opm − opm (+), pb (−), ts_dist_hi260 (−). 338 firers (51 US), median mcap $119M.
- Lineage: mid-cycle = Yahoo 5-yr avg margin × current revenue (global); tc_med_opm FMP 8-FY (global); fq PP&E includes ROU (documented); EV vs revenue_ttm relies on `_fx_coherent`. Old rule kept as `_watch`.
- Spirit gaps: the supply-side half (industry capex cuts, consolidation, "weakest competitor throws in the towel") is absent; "survived the downcycle" is a balance-sheet snapshot, not a through-cycle record (tc_min_opm / tc_fcf_pos exist); no turn evidence (A2 B.5 #31 exceptional open); replacement cost proxied by pb<1.
- Methodology issues: the negative-EBITDA "very low leverage" alternative is DEAD CODE — `nde` is forced to 99 whenever ebitda_ttm ≤0 (line 1075), so `(nde>-90)&(nde<=1.0)` can never hold in that branch and only net cash ≥20% admits deep troughs (same defect in double_trough's `_deep_surv18`); the 5-yr mean margin is not a full cycle (a 2021-22 super-cycle year inflates "mid-cycle"); asset intensity admits capital-intensive non-cyclicals (telecom-like) once the sector label is bypassed.
- FMP augmentations: none new needed — industry aggregate capex/D&A trend from the cached fq statements grouped by profile industry (capital-cycle leg, zero calls); `tc_min_opm`/`tc_fcf_pos` (present) for survival-through-cycle; `fqx_ebit_ttm_g`, `ts_r13` for the turn.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] fix deep-trough survivability: measure low leverage as fq total_debt/total_assets or net debt/equity (nde is unusable at EBITDA ≤0) — shared with double_trough. 2 [spirit] capital-cycle leg: industry capex/D&A falling YoY (cached fq, grouped by industry). 3 [method] mid-cycle sanity: prefer the FY-median margin (tc_med_opm) over the 5-yr mean where the window holds an outlier year. 4 [data] exceptional tier per A2 B.5 #31 (turning up, ts_r13>0) open.

### arch_xr_deferred_revenue_lead  (line 5163)
- Spirit: a collected-but-unrecognised forward book (deferred revenue ≥15% of revenue) that is BUILDING, while the market prices the cheap trailing tape. Human sources: Wolf/Oak "Archetype 3: Earnings/Backlog Inflection" (screen: check filings for "backlog, WIP, inventory stockpile, deferred revenue"; EV/EBITDA <5×; reject one-drop or vague backlog); Liger three-layer thesis (observed fact → economic translation → recognition mechanism); master ref VIII Contract/Inflection. Sibling of contracted_backlog (RPO), mirror of float_compounding.
- As coded: defrev/revenue_ttm ≥0.15; rev_yoy ≥5% OR (fq_defrev_growth_minus_rev ≥5pp & rev_yoy ≥0); cheap = EV/S ≤3 OR EV/EBITDA ≤12 OR FCF yield ≥3%; fx-coherent; not melting. No dilution, profitability or size guard (unlike its XR siblings). Lenses: none. 635 firers (247 US; CN 80, JP 59, HK 33), median mcap $1.29B.
- Lineage: deferred_revenue EDGAR (22%/1%) → `fq_defrev` fill; 490 of 635 firers are FMP-filled, so the class is global. EDGAR aliases can carry the TOTAL contract liability (current + non-current) while FMP `deferredRevenue` is the current line (non-current is a separate field) — the fill changes the ratio's meaning across rows [data]. The book-build lens `fq_defrev_growth_minus_rev` (fmp_quarterly.py 432-433, only where the prior balance ≥5% of revenue) is present for 477 firers but is the SOLE qualifier for 65: 570 of 635 pass on rev_yoy ≥5% alone. Cheap legs: EV/S 453, FCF yield 460, EV/EBITDA 255. 118 firers have NI ≤0. Median defrev/rev 0.29; 137 firers ≥0.5 (prepaid-heavy education/membership/airline models where the level is structural).
- Spirit gaps: (a) "BUILDING" is satisfied by revenue growth, not by the book — the source thesis is the book leading the tape, yet the book's own growth is optional; (b) "unpriced" is asserted by an absolute cheapness OR (FCF yield ≥3% is loose) and never tested against expectations; (c) customer prepayments (SaaS float) and progress billings of long-cycle contractors (which reverse with cost — Wolf's "one big drop") are not distinguished; (d) the docstring's mechanism ("a transition or conservative recognition depresses reported revenue") is never observed — rev_yoy ≥5% means the tape is not depressed; (e) prepayments at a loss-maker (118) are a liability to deliver, not a forward book — no CFO/NI or dilution guard.
- Methodology issues: the ≥15% level is industry-normal for subscription software, education and travel — a sector property, the same critique A3 gave contracted_backlog; the 12-month (current) vs total deferred split is unmeasured, and the fill mixes the two; the mirror with float_compounding is enforced only by the P/E leg (≥20 there, cheap here), so the float evidence differs between the two for no thesis reason; acquisitions inflate the balance (acquired deferred revenue) — `fq_acq_pct_assets` exists and is unused.
- FMP augmentations: balance-sheet `deferredRevenue` + `deferredRevenueNonCurrent` (global, dated; already fq flows) → current-only ratio on both sources plus the book's YoY; cash-flow `deferredRevenue` change line (global) → the collected-cash proof (its CFO contribution); `analyst-estimates` next-FY revenue (snapshot; A2 A.2 U*) → "unpriced" = consensus growth below what the book implies; `earning-call-transcript` bookings/RPO for non-US (A1 A.6 / A2 S14); profile `industry` (global) for a peer frame.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] make the book-build the core leg (fq_defrev_growth_minus_rev ≥5pp or fq_defrev_build_flag); revenue-growth-only members → `_watch`. 2 [method] normalise the level against the FMP industry median (as A3 contracted_backlog §3) so structural-prepayment industries pass on build, not level. 3 [spirit] add the sibling guards: shares_yoy ≤5% and (NI>0 or CFO>0). 4 [data] use the current-only deferred line on both EDGAR and FMP paths — the fill currently changes meaning. 5 [data] "unpriced" via analyst-estimates (A2 A.2 open); transcripts for bookings (A1 A.6 open).

### arch_xr_depreciation_cliff  (line 4426)
- Spirit: an almost fully depreciated asset base (large D&A against a small remaining net PP&E) with low replacement spend — reported earnings are a coiled spring that releases as charges roll off, and the cash was real all along. Human sources: Pernas idea-generation screen "high D&A-to-capex and low EV/EBITDA" (Pernas doc, "Pitch extraction"); master ref II (Yartseva: FCF/P, not EPS); compendium III assembly block (3) fresh-start heavy D&A understating year-1/2 cash earning power (the post-reorg cousin); Wolf Archetype 8 "low or no growth capex requirement".
- As coded: D&A>0; D&A/ppe_net ≥0.35; 0 ≤ capex_ttm ≤ 0.6×D&A; OE (= NI + D&A − max(capex_ttm, capex_avg)) >0 and mcap/OE ≤10; P/E ≥12 or NaN; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 22 firers, ALL US, median mcap $1.18B.
- Lineage: `ppe_net` is EDGAR-only (30% US / 1% non-US) and deliberately NOT FMP-filled (FMP PP&E carries lease ROU; archetype_tags.py 497) → the class is US-only by construction; `da_ttm` audited for all 22; capex_avg fmp_st-filled. Maintenance capex uses the audit-#2 max(TTM, 5-yr) basis — good. 8 of 22 pass on the P/E NaN escape (GAAP loss-makers with OE>0).
- Spirit gaps: (a) the numerator is TOTAL D&A (depreciation + amortisation of acquired intangibles) over net PP&E ONLY — median D&A/PPE is 2.1× and the 75th percentile 16×; VTRS, CLVT, ADEA, PSFE, WLY, IMMR are amortisation-heavy asset-light names, i.e. amortization_mask wearing the cliff's label, not a depreciation roll-off (a cliff needs a physical base); (b) no roll-off DATE or asset-age evidence (accumulated depreciation / gross PP&E is computed for XR44 as `_accum_ratio_x44` and unused here); (c) "the cash was real all along" is untested (no CFO ≥ NI+D&A check); (d) capex ≤0.6×D&A also describes an under-investing melter — A2 nuance #4 pairs the ratio with revenue direction, absent here.
- Methodology issues: D&A/PPE ≥0.35 is a unit mismatch when amortisation is in the numerator; the NaN-P/E escape admits 8 loss-makers whose "polluted E" is a real loss; no non-US path at all although the canonical instances (old-plant JP/DE/KR industrials) are non-US; near-duplicate of `arch_overdepreciated_assets` (same capex ≤0.6×D&A leg) and forensic_floor_growth's third leg.
- FMP augmentations: `fq_da_to_ppe` (already emitted, global, quarterly; ROU in the denominator biases the ratio DOWN, so a pass is conservative) → global reach; `key-metrics.capexToDepreciationTTM` (A2 nuance #4, universe-wide); FMP standardized statements do not expose gross PP&E / accumulated depreciation (EDGAR only — A2 "cannot fill"); EDGAR `AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets` (US) for a depreciation-only numerator (same split A3 asked of amortization_mask §2).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] depreciation-only numerator where the EDGAR amortisation tag exists; else require goodwill+intangibles ≤15% of assets (`fq_gw_pct_assets`) so the ratio is a PP&E statement — 5-6 of the 22 current firers are amortisation cases and belong to amortization_mask. 2 [spirit] roll-off evidence: accumulated depreciation/gross PP&E ≥0.7 (`_accum_ratio_x44`, US) or fq D&A falling YoY with capex flat (global). 3 [data] global reach via `fq_da_to_ppe` ≥0.35 & `fq_capex_to_da` ≤0.6 (document the ROU bias) — 0 non-US firers today. 4 [method] keep the P/E-NaN escape only with CFO>0 and fq_rev_growth ≥ −5% (a cliff, not a melt; A2 nuance #4 open). 5 [method] cash-real check: fq_cfo_to_ni ≥1.2 or cfo_yield ≥ OE yield × 0.8.

### arch_xr_discops_mask  (line 5276)
- Spirit: consolidated NI depressed or negative BECAUSE of a discontinued / held-for-sale unit while continuing operations are solidly profitable and cheap on their own; the re-rate comes when the drag is divested. Human sources: Wolf/Oak "Archetype 9: Turnaround / Structural Improvement" (screen: "focus shift: disposal of unprofitable unit"; catalyst: "release of the first improved earnings report"; reject: "temporary restructuring charges", contingent liabilities off balance sheet) and the consolidated table row "Turnaround / Costs Cut → disposal of loss-making unit"; master ref I.C(6) hidden segment inside a bad consolidated screen / (19) temporarily worse than it looks; Pernas "Low-multiple operational inflection" (cost reset).
- As coded: continuing income >0; mask = (NI ≤0 & cont>0 & row not FMP-filled) OR (discops loss ≥20% of continuing) OR (assets held for sale / mcap ≥15%); continuing yield (cont / listing-ccy mcap) ≥6%; fx-coherent; not melting. No dilution, leverage or cash guard. Lenses: none. 104 firers (76 US; UK 5, IN 4), median mcap $154M.
- Lineage: `income_continuing_ops_ttm` EDGAR (11%/0%) → fq fill (36 of 104 firers FMP-filled; FMP continuing income is pre-NCI vs parent-only NI — the code correctly bars leg A on those rows); `income_discontinued_ops_ttm` → `fq_ni_disc` fill (present for 100 of 104); `assets_held_for_sale` EDGAR-only (5%/0%; present for 25) so leg C is US-only with no substitute (A2: held-for-sale is a footnote item FMP drops). Legs: A 56, B 46, C 10. Currency: cont in master ccy over listing-ccy mcap (the documented ~150× fix). Median continuing yield 14%, 75th pct 27%.
- Spirit gaps: (a) the divestiture is never dated or confirmed — a "discontinued" line can be years old (drag already gone) or an open-ended wind-down still consuming cash; (b) leg A proves a consolidated loss with a profitable core but not its CAUSE (interest, tax, impairments inside continuing ops, NCI on EDGAR rows) — the comment calls it "a clean mask", it is not; (c) no quality test on continuing income (one-off gains in continuing ops pass; no CFO>0); (d) retained liabilities are the trap Wolf names, and there is no leverage guard; (e) a 14-27% continuing-earnings yield is distress pricing — the market is pricing something beyond the mask; (f) Wolf's catalyst (first clean print post-disposal) is absent although `fqx_eps_turned` / `fmp_dyn_*_turned_positive` exist.
- Methodology issues: on FMP-filled rows the yield uses pre-NCI continuing income over parent mcap (overstates for majority-owned subs); legs A and B overlap without a basis tag; AHFS (USD, EDGAR) over USD mcap is coherent but US-only; `fq_disc_ops_share` (global, present for 89 of 104) is the natural global form of leg B and is unused.
- FMP augmentations: fq `ni_disc` trend across two periods (global, dated) → drag shrinking toward zero = divestiture progressing; `sec-filings-search` 8-K item 2.01 (completion of disposition) and `mergers-acquisitions-latest` (both US) → dated divestiture; `fqx_eps_turned` → the first clean print; `fq_disc_ops_share` → leg B globally; no FMP source for held-for-sale (A2 "cannot fill", open).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] require the mask to be CAUSED by disc ops: leg A only where disc<0 or AHFS>0 is also observed; bare NI≤0 & cont>0 → `_watch`. 2 [method] quality on the core: continuing income ≤1.1× EBITDA (the earnings_oneoff_flag logic) and CFO>0; leverage guard nde<3 or net cash. 3 [spirit] dated realisation tier: 8-K 2.01 / M&A completion ≤12m (US) or the disc line shrinking ≥50% over two fq periods (global), plus fqx_eps_turned. 4 [method] on FMP-filled rows scale continuing income by (NI_parent / NI_consolidated) where NCI is material, or route them through leg B only. 5 [data] state plainly on the book that leg C (held for sale) has no non-US path.

### arch_xr_double_trough  (line 4525)
- Spirit: a trough multiple on trough earnings — price near multi-year lows, EV cheap against the mid-cycle base, current earnings below that base, survival assured; the multiple re-rates AND earnings mean-revert. Human sources: compendium Part II D Capital Cycle ("the turn occurs during maximum pessimism as the weakest competitor throws in the towel"; survivors); master ref I.E "Surviving Thing" three-layer test (priced for old-cycle economics, survived the downcycle, next cycle tighter); Pernas "cyclically depressed" / staffing-trough pitches; Wolf "Archetype 10" (crash >50% from highs, P/B or P/cash <0.5, cash >20% of mcap, a plausible path).
- As coded: trough price = pct_off_52w_high ≤−50% | 5y-range ≤15% | ts_dd52 ≤−50% | ts_dist_hi260 ≤0.5; EV / mid-cycle EBITDA (5-yr avg margin × current revenue) ≤5; at-trough = EBITDA ≤0.85× mid-cycle OR (op margin ≤ tc_med_opm −5pp, tc_years ≥5); positive-EBITDA branch: (net cash ≥0 | IC ≥4 | nde ≤1.5) & not melting; negative-EBITDA branch: net cash ≥20% OR (−90<nde ≤1.0); shares ≤5%; fx-coherent. Lenses: none. 494 firers (93 US; KR 70, HK 60, TW 34, CN 33), median mcap $97M.
- Lineage: `normalized_ebitda` Yahoo 5-yr average (46%/64%; RNO.PA basis repair) — present for all 494; EV (listing ccy) over margin × revenue_ttm (reporting ccy) via `_fx_coherent`. Trough-price lenses: ts_dist_hi260 carries 399, 5y-range 344, the 52w lenses 77-98 (A1 A.4 panel swap done). tc leg fires for 197. Negative-EBITDA branch: 40 firers, ALL via net cash ≥20% — the `(nde>-90)&(nde<=1.0)` alternative is DEAD CODE because `nde` is forced to 99 wherever EBITDA ≤0 (line 1075); identical defect to cyclical_trough (A3 XR14 §1).
- Spirit gaps: (a) "survived the downcycle" is a balance-sheet snapshot, not a through-cycle record (`tc_min_opm`, `tc_fcf_pos` present, unused); (b) the valuation base is a 5-yr MEAN margin that can hold a 2021-22 super-cycle year — the median-margin alternative (`tc_med_opm`) sits only in the at-trough leg, never in the EV/mid-cycle leg, so a fat peak year can manufacture "cheap"; (c) no turn evidence (A2 B.5 #31 exceptional, open) and no capital-cycle supply leg; (d) no secular-decline test: at median mcap $97M in KR/HK/TW, many "troughs" are permanent (master ref failure mode: "the new mean is just the old mean"); (e) the deep branch has no runway test — net cash ≥20% of a small mcap can be a few quarters of burn.
- Methodology issues: dead leverage alternative (above); the negative-EBITDA branch skips `_not_melting` by design but also `_op_viable`; a name 50% below a 5-yr high after a 10× run is not "maximum pessimism" — no 52-week/starting-multiple context; 5y-range ≤15% and ts_dist_hi260 ≤0.5 are near-duplicates (harmless); 101 firers have EBITDA >0.85× mid-cycle and qualify only through the tc-median leg (two different "trough" definitions without a basis tag).
