- FMP augmentations: `tc_med_opm` (present) in the VALUATION leg — EV / (tc_med_opm × revenue) as a second mid-cycle basis; `tc_min_opm`, `tc_fcf_pos` (present) → survived-through-cycle; `fqx_ebit_ttm_g`, `ts_r13` (present) → the turn; fq `total_debt`/`total_assets`, fq TTM CFO/capex → deep-branch leverage and runway; industry capex/D&A trend from the cached fq statements grouped by profile industry (zero calls) → capital-cycle leg; `ts_dist_hi260` already wired.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] replace the dead deep-trough leverage alternative with fq total_debt/total_assets ≤0.25 or net debt/equity ≤0.3 (nde is unusable at EBITDA ≤0) — shared fix with cyclical_trough (A3 XR14 §1). 2 [method] deep-branch runway: net cash ≥2× TTM burn (fq_cfo − fq_capex), not merely ≥20% of mcap. 3 [spirit] mid-cycle sanity: base = min(5-yr mean margin, tc_med_opm) × revenue for the EV leg, so a super-cycle year cannot fake cheapness. 4 [spirit] survived-through-cycle: tc_min_opm > −5% or tc_fcf_pos ≥5 of 8 (the assembly checklist's financial-vs-economic-distress gate). 5 [data] exceptional tier = A2 B.5 #31 (ts_r13>0, fqx_ebit_ttm_g>0, insider or company buying) — open.

### arch_xr_float_compounding  (line 4387)
- Spirit: customers prepay (deferred revenue / negative working capital) so cash collections run ahead of GAAP revenue; bookings accelerating; headline P/E dear or meaningless, yet on collected cash the price is ordinary (SaaS / Ryanair / insurance float). Human sources: Liger "clean cash conversion"; Pernas "Fallen platform with improving motor" (subscription/platform, improving unit economics, route to operating leverage); master ref VI (per-share cash, Fisher/Cassel). Buffett-float construction is engine-native.
- As coded: float exists = defrev/revenue ≥0.10 OR NWC<0; CFO/NI ≥1.3 (NI>0) OR cash_conversion ≥1.2; rev_accel>0 OR rev_yoy ≥15%; P/E ≥20 or NaN; mcap/CFO ≤15; roic_after_sbc not negative (EDGAR-only, soft); shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 371 firers (120 US; CN 68, JP 22, KR 15, UK 15), median mcap $1.29B.
- Lineage: deferred_revenue fq-filled (global); NWC master → fq fill. Among firers only 130 clear the deferred-revenue leg; 241 (65%) qualify on NWC<0 ALONE — the proxy the docstring itself calls weaker ("also flags stretched payables") is the majority path. 62 firers have NI ≤0 (CFO/NI NaN) and ride cash_conversion ≥1.2; 57 use the P/E-NaN escape. rev_accel>0 carries 342 vs rev_yoy ≥15% for 116 — "bookings engine turning" is a one-point second derivative (A2 §C fragile). The SBC guard reaches 38 of 371 (EDGAR `roic_after_sbc`). `fq_ccc` <0 (the direct float measure) holds for 144 firers and is not in the gate — A1 A.6 called this archetype "fine: fq CCC already wired", which is true of customer_float, not here.
- Spirit gaps: (a) FLOAT ≠ negative NWC — stretched payables, a distressed retailer's deposits and a one-off working-capital release (`fq_wc_release_flag`) all pass; (b) "collections ahead of revenue" needs the deferred balance to GROW — only the level is tested (`fq_defrev_growth_minus_rev` / `fq_defrev_build_flag` exist); (c) CFO/NI ≥1.3 is met by any D&A-heavy business, so the wedge is not attributed to float; (d) the SBC guard misses 90% of firers, precisely the non-US SaaS it targets, while `fq_sbc_to_cfo` is global and unused; (e) no per-share test although the thesis is per-share cash compounding.
- Methodology issues: "dear on E / cheap on cash" (P/E ≥20 & mcap/CFO ≤15) explained by CFO/NI ≥1.3 is the depreciation family's signature as much as float's; the mirror with deferred_revenue_lead differs only in the P/E leg, so identical float evidence should back both; `cash_conversion` (master) and CFO/NI are different bases ORed.
- FMP augmentations: `fq_ccc` and `fq_op_nwc_to_rev` (global, present) → measured float with magnitude; `fq_defrev_build_flag` / `fq_defrev_growth_minus_rev` (global) → the growing book; `fq_sbc_to_cfo` (global) → SBC guard (A2 nuance #11); cash-flow `deferredRevenue` change (global) → wedge attribution (float vs D&A vs WC), as A3 cash_leads_book §1; `key-metrics.cashConversionCycleTTM` (A2 nuance #2).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] core float leg = (defrev/rev ≥0.10 AND fq_defrev_growth_minus_rev ≥0) OR fq_ccc ≤ −20 days; bare NWC<0 → `_watch` (A2 nuance #2 open). 2 [method] attribute the wedge: ΔdeferredRevenue + non-D&A accruals ≥50% of (CFO − NI), else route to the depreciation/amortisation siblings. 3 [data] SBC guard via fq_sbc_to_cfo ≤0.3 globally (A2 nuance #11 open). 4 [method] growth on fq TTM (fq_rev_growth ≥10%) or fqx TTM instead of rev_accel>0 (A2 §C open). 5 [spirit] rank by CFO per share growth (fqx_fcf_ps_g / fq_shares_yoy) — the compounding is per share.

### arch_xr_floor_inflection  (line 4172)
- Spirit: Graham-and-catalyst — priced at or below a HARD asset floor (net-net, deep net cash, hidden-asset gap) exactly as operating inflection appears (a first-positive print, or acceleration with operating leverage) on a beaten tape. Human sources: Wolf/Oak "Archetype 4: Asset-Backed Floor + Optional Growth" (mcap ≤ cash + assets; reject "core business still burning cash with no path to profitability"); Wolf "Archetype 10" (crash >50%, cash >20% mcap, a near-term realisation event); compendium Part II B footprint (first profitable quarter + accelerating revenue, 1-2 quarters before the run); master ref I.G(20) "Operating KPI crosses a threshold".
- As coded: floor = NCAV ≥80% OR net cash ≥50% OR hidden ≥40%; inflect = any annual first-positive (EBITDA / CFO / FCF / NI) OR (rev_accel>0 & oper_lev_any); beaten_down_any(0.30); shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 720 firers (104 US; KR 131, HK 107, IN 92, CN 59), median mcap $33M.
- Lineage: NCAV (EDGAR / Yahoo, net of NCI + preferred, global); net cash global; hidden_pct = associates + net cash (associates present for 42 of 720 → for 94% the "hidden" leg IS net cash ≥40%, strictly weaker than the ≥50% leg beside it — the effective floor is net cash ≥40% or NCAV ≥80%). Inflection: rev_accel & oper_lev_any carries 561 (78%); first-positive flags are annual (FCF 189, the noisiest per A1 kpi_threshold; 58 fire on FCF alone); `fqx_eps_turned` is present for 61 firers and unused. beaten_down_any already reads ts_dist_hi52 first (A1 A.4 done). 231 firers have NI ≤0 and 217 EBITDA ≤0.
- Spirit gaps: (a) the inflection is mostly a snapshot second derivative (rev_accel × the six-way `oper_lev_any` OR, which includes raw sequential moves — A2 §C), not a "first-positive print"; (b) floor durability is untested: a third of members are burners, and a net-cash floor at a burner shrinks — Wolf's explicit reject; (c) "hidden-asset gap" is nominal (associates observed for 6%); (d) no realisation path (asset sale, buyback below NAV, activist) — the beaten tape is the only "why now", and Wolf's Archetype 4 catalysts are all absent; (e) median mcap $33M with KR/HK dominance is the governance-trap cohort (master ref G failure mode) — no controlled-sub or float check.
- Methodology issues: three net-cash formulations ORed; `hidden ≥0.40` dominates nothing and is dominated by `net cash ≥0.50` on non-associate rows; the melt gate's `_returns_improving` escape (op_margin_delta>0 etc.) overlaps `oper_lev_any`, so a melting name with one positive delta passes both; annual first-positive vs the quarterly `fmp_dyn_*_turned_positive` (itself a single-quarter test — see XR41) are not reconciled.
- FMP augmentations: `fqx_eps_turned` (present, dated TTM) and `fmp_dyn_opinc_turned_positive` confirmed by fq TTM EBIT>0 → a dated inflection; `fqx_inc_ebit_margin` → replaces oper_lev_any (A2 §C open); fq TTM burn (fq_cfo − fq_capex) and NCAV / net-cash YoY from the fq balance sheet → floor durability; `evt_sc13d_date` (US) / net_buyback>0 (global) / `dividends` → realisation; `shares-float` (global, snapshot) → controlled-float flag (A1 C#12).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] tier the inflection: core = dated turn (fqx_eps_turned, or fmp_dyn opinc turn confirmed on fq TTM) or first-positive corroborated on ≥2 lines (EBITDA & CFO); rev_accel & oper_lev_any → `_watch` (A1 kpi_threshold / A2 §C open). 2 [spirit] floor durability for NI ≤0 members: net cash ≥3× TTM burn and NCAV or net cash not down >10% YoY (fq). 3 [method] count the hidden-asset floor only where associates are observed (else it duplicates net cash) — same as A3 asset_owner_catalyst §4. 4 [spirit] a realisation lens for ranking: net_buyback>0, dividend paid, 13D ≤365d (US), with `controlled_sub_flag` as a down-weight (A1 C#12 open).

### arch_xr_forced_seller  (line 4545)
- Spirit: the price collapsed ≥40% in a year in which the business GREW on both lines with no dilution — a seller-driven, not business-driven, mark (index deletions, fund liquidations, spin-off orphans). Human sources: compendium Part II A ("buy securities being sold by non-economic holders… build screens around the forced-seller calendar and the filing signal, not the story"), Part II C (index/style funds dump the stub), appendix ("the bottom is usually after the technical selling clears"); master ref III technical/flow-driven (index deletions, ETF rebalancing, tax-loss selling, margin calls); Pernas "Fallen platform with improving motor" (down heavily on a temporary threat, unit economics improving).
- As coded: revenue ≥$10M; ts_r52 (fallback price_yoy) ≤−40%; rev_yoy ≥5%; ebitda_yoy ≥0 OR NI>0; shares ≤2%; nde<3 or net cash ≥0; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: fmp_inst_own_chg_q0 (−), ts_r52 (−), fq_rev_growth (+). 318 firers (82 US; CN 56, IN 43, HK 34, KR 26), median mcap $297M.
- Lineage: ts_r52 present for 296 of 318 (A1 §0.3 swap done; 22 still on the noisy price_yoy fallback); rev_yoy is the master annual/TTM point — 59 of 318 firers show fq TTM revenue growth <0, contradicting "the business grew"; the "both lines" leg is ebitda_yoy ≥0 (238) OR NI>0 (75 pass on NI>0 with EBITDA falling — a positive level is not growth); the 13F lens covers 52 of 318 (US-only) and ≤−5pp for 13; `ev_sp500_removed_12m` fires for 0 firers. ts_r52 among firers clusters at the threshold (90th pct −41%, median −49%).
- Spirit gaps: (a) NO SELLER IS IDENTIFIED for any member — "fell 40% while growing" is also the profile of a fraud suspicion, a China/HK governance discount, a pre-announced customer loss, or a de-rating from an absurd starting multiple (no starting-valuation test: 30× sales to 18× "grew and collapsed"); (b) the compendium's calendar (index deletion, spin distribution, CEF wind-down, lock-up expiry, tax-loss season) is entirely absent; (c) no "selling has cleared" test (the source's entry rule); (d) no cheapness leg; (e) CN/IN/HK make up 42% of firers — the markets where growth + collapse most often means a governance mark, not a non-economic seller.
- Methodology issues: the NI>0 escape breaks the "both lines" claim; master rev_yoy vs fq TTM disagree on 59 rows; the 13F lens sign (exit = good) is consistent with the thesis but unbounded (a total exodus is a red flag); dual lines are not deduped (ERO/ERO.TO-style duplicates seen in sibling gates); the class is "index orphan" in the docstring but the index leg is lens-only and US-only (A2 B.4 #26 open).
- FMP augmentations: `historical-sp500-constituent` (done: 0 hits) + `historical-nasdaq/dowjones-constituent` (A2 C4, 4 calls, US); `etf/asset-exposure` (global, snapshot; A2 S8) → passive-share drop; `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` N-PORT (global holdings by US funds; A2 S9) → a named fund exit for non-US names; `institutional-ownership/extract-analytics/holder` closedPositions (US); profile `ipoDate` + `symbol-change` → spin orphans (A2 B.4 #23); `grades-news` absence of downgrades (the mark was not fundamental, US); `ts_vol_spike4`, `ts_r13` (panel) → "selling cleared".
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] tier by seller evidence: exceptional = a NAMED seller (index removal ≤12m, 13F −5pp or closedPositions spike, N-PORT fund exit, spin ≤18m, ETF passive-share drop) — A2 B.4 #26 open; unnamed → `_watch`. 2 [method] "business grew" on the date-matched panel: fq_rev_growth ≥5% AND fqx_ebit_ttm_g ≥0; drop the NI>0 escape (59 contradictions today). 3 [spirit] "selling cleared": ts_r13 ≥ −5% and ts_vol_spike4 <1.5, so entry follows the calendar, not the collapse. 4 [method] starting-valuation sanity: EV/S ≤ own 3-yr median (fmp_dyn EV history) or P/E ≤25 now — a de-rating from bubble multiples is not forced selling. 5 [data] non-US seller evidence only via N-PORT / ETF exposure (A2 S8/S9 open); the 13F and index legs stay US-only — label them so on the book.

### arch_xr_forensic_floor_growth  (line 4225)
- Spirit: a floor INVISIBLE to standard screens — off-EV securities (hidden-asset gap), retained earnings above 1.5× the price, or an over-depreciated asset base — under a business that GROWS. Human sources: compendium Part I #6 Kerrisdale/Scrapyard (assets at historical cost, property unrevalued for decades, stakes worth more than the cap); master ref I.C "Hidden Thing"; Pernas screen "high D&A-to-capex". The three-way combination is engine-native.
- As coded: floor = hidden ≥50% OR 1.5 ≤ RE/mcap ≤5 OR (D&A>0, 0 ≤ capex_ttm ≤0.5×D&A, D&A/revenue ≥5%); rev_yoy 10-100%; profit present (EBITDA>0 | op margin>0 | FCF yield>0); shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 839 firers (271 US; KR 94, JP 88, CN 74, HK 62), median mcap $330M.
- Lineage: hidden = associates + net cash over local mcap — associates present for 54 of 839, so for the rest the "invisible" leg is net cash ≥50%, the most screened floor there is (246 pass it); RE/mcap 1.5-5 = 84 (RE fq-filled for 588; the IFRS-reserve guard applies); over-depreciated = 510 (61%) — the dominant leg, and it uses `_capex_loc` (a single TTM window) rather than `_maint_capex` (max of TTM and 5-yr average) that the sibling overdepreciated_assets adopted after audits #2/#4/#5 — a momentarily collapsed capex window qualifies here. `da_ttm` is TOTAL D&A. 149 firers have NI ≤0 (profit_present admits EBITDA>0 or FCF yield>0 loss-makers).
- Spirit gaps: (a) 29% of the floor is visible net cash and 61% is "capex < half D&A", which is a harvest / under-investment tell, not a FLOOR — an over-depreciated base is a floor only if the assets have realisable value (owned property: XR44's `_owns_x44` and accumulated-depreciation ratio), otherwise the leg catches amortisation-heavy roll-ups (total D&A) exactly as A3 found for depreciation_cliff; (b) retained earnings ≥1.5× mcap says the firm once earned it, not that the value remains (RE is not an asset — the retained_earnings_discount critique); (c) growth ≥10% while spending half of depreciation is self-contradictory for an asset-heavy business, so the leg selects asset-LIGHT growers whose "floor" is nothing; (d) growth is one rev_yoy point (A2 §C fragile); (e) no realisation catalyst for the hidden value (the scrapyard thesis needs a Keisei/Elliott-type event).
- Methodology issues: inconsistent maintenance-capex basis vs the sibling (TTM only); D&A/revenue ≥5% admits amortisation; the hidden leg collapses to net cash (as in floor_inflection and asset_owner_catalyst); profit_present admits 149 loss-makers; RE/mcap on fq-filled rows mixes reporting and listing currency unless the fx bridge is applied (A2 E4 currency map open).
- FMP augmentations: `fq_capex_to_da`, `fq_da_to_ppe`, `fq_ppe_net/fq_total_assets` (global, present) → an over-depreciated leg that requires a physical base (ROU-biased, documented); `owner-earnings` maintenanceCapex (global, cached; A2 A.1 U*) → capex vs maintenance rather than vs D&A; gross PP&E / accumulated depreciation are EDGAR-only (XR44, US) — the actual "unrevalued asset" evidence has no FMP substitute; `fqx` TTM growth; `fq_gw_pct_assets` to exclude amortisation cases.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] make the third leg a FLOOR: require fq_ppe_net/total_assets ≥0.25 with fq_da_to_ppe ≥0.2 (global) or XR44's owned-property + accumulated-depreciation ≥0.5 (US); capex < half D&A on an asset-light name is not a floor. 2 [method] use `_maint_capex` (max of TTM and 5-yr avg) as overdepreciated_assets does — the audit-#2 lesson was not propagated here. 3 [method] count the hidden leg only where associates are observed; net-cash-only rows belong to the visible-floor siblings (triple_floor / neg_ev_growth). 4 [spirit] exclude goodwill+intangibles ≥30% of assets from the D&A leg (amortization_mask territory). 5 [data] growth on fq TTM (A2 §C open); RE/mcap through the fx bridge (A2 E4 open).

### arch_xr_forensic_multiple_gap  (line 4242)
- Spirit: the market prices the ACCOUNTING multiple (headline P/E) while forensic owner earnings (latest or 5-yr average) imply ≤6×; the spread pays out when accounting catches up with cash. Human sources: master ref II (Yartseva: FCF/P is the strongest driver, EPS growth statistically insignificant); Pernas "high D&A-to-capex, low EV/EBITDA" screen; compendium III assembly block (3) heavy D&A understating cash earning power. Engine-native.
- As coded: 1.5 ≤ mcap/OE_best ≤6 (OE_best = OE_loc if >0 else oe_avg>0; OE_loc = NI + D&A − max(capex_ttm, capex_avg)); P/E NaN OR (P/E>0 & P/E ÷ forensic multiple ≥2.5); NI>0; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. Lenses: none. 396 firers (185 US; HK 24, IN 21, ID 13, SG 12), median mcap $721M.
- Lineage: D&A audited (`da_ttm`) for 321 of 396, else implied EBITDA−EBIT under the op_margin>0 guard; capex_avg fmp_st-filled (359) and oe_avg fmp_st-filled (360) → global, but with NI>0 and D&A>0 the OE_loc path is almost always >0, so the 5-yr-average path is nearly unused. P/E-NaN escape: 2 (moot under NI>0). P/E median 16; 173 of 396 pass with P/E <15 through the ratio route — at P/E 11 a 2.5× gap needs OE ≥2.75× NI, i.e. D&A − maintenance capex ≥1.75× NI. earnings_oneoff_flag set on 12.
- Spirit gaps: (a) the wedge's SOURCE is unidentified — acquired-intangible amortisation, depreciation of old plant, or a 5-yr-average capex that understates a growing firm's replacement needs (the wedge widens with growth); (b) "accounting catches up with cash" has no mechanism (amortisation roll-off, payout, buyback) — a wedge that persists is the market's steady-state view, not a mispricing; (c) OE is an accounting reconstruction, never confirmed in observed cash (no CFO ≥ NI + D&A − maint capex check); (d) SBC is not deducted (fq_sbc_to_cfo global; sbc_polluted_flag EDGAR-only); (e) the ratio route admits P/E 8 with a 3× forensic multiple — a cheap D&A-heavy stock, not "the market pricing the accounting multiple".
- Methodology issues: the docstring's own claim (headline ≥15 or meaningless) was replaced by a ratio with no headline floor; the 1.5× lower guard is a currency heuristic where the `financial-statement-symbol-list` map would be exact (A2 E4); the OE family (owner_earnings_power, amortization_mask, depreciation_cliff, cash_leads_book) overlaps heavily — fine under confluence, but each should carry the same wedge attribution; A2 matrix marks this "NONE / no material gain", which understates the owner-earnings endpoint and the cash-confirmation gap.
- FMP augmentations: `owner-earnings` ownersEarnings / maintenanceCapex (global, cached; A2 A.1 U*, A1 §6) → second OE basis; `fq_cfo_to_ni` and `fq_sbc_to_cfo` (global, present) → cash confirmation and SBC deduction; EDGAR `AmortizationOfIntangibleAssets` (US) → wedge attribution; `dividends` / fq buyback → the payout mechanism; `ratios.priceToFairValueTTM` as a "look twice" column (A2 nuance #14).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] cash-confirm the wedge: fq_cfo_to_ni ≥0.8 × (OE/NI) — owner earnings must be visible in CFO, not only reconstructed. 2 [spirit] attribute and route: amortisation-driven → amortization_mask; old-plant depreciation → depreciation_cliff; wedge relying on a filled 5-yr capex average → `_watch` unless capex_avg is observed. 3 [method] restore a headline floor for the CORE tier (P/E ≥15 or NaN) and keep the ratio route as `_watch` — at P/E <12 there is no headline multiple to be fooled by. 4 [data] owner-earnings cross-check (A2 A.1 U* open) and SBC deduction via fq_sbc_to_cfo (A2 nuance #11 open). 5 [spirit] exceptional tier: a payout or buyback ≥3% of mcap (the cash is being handed over, so the market must reprice it).

### arch_xr_gaap_profit_crossover  (line 5134)
- Spirit: the first GAAP net profit unlocks latent demand (index eligibility, profitability-screened mandates, simple quant screens) — the multiple was capped by WHO could not own it. Human sources: compendium Part II B footprint ("first profitable quarter is the strongest footprint"; discovery cycle: catalyst → retail → institutional threshold); Wolf consolidated table "Turnaround / Costs Cut → first profitable quarter post-turnaround"; master ref I.G(20) KPI crossing (KORU first positive operating margin); compendium appendix ("treat index inclusion as exit liquidity, not a fresh thesis").
- As coded: revenue ≥$20M; crossover = annual NI first-positive OR fqx_eps_turned OR fmp_dyn_ni_turned_positive; NI_ttm >0; op_margin >0 OR EBITDA >0; not price-ghost / OTC; not melting. No dilution, valuation, tape or size guard (fire-broad by design). Lenses: (1 − evt_sp500_member), evt_free_float, market_cap_usd, op_margin (all +). 3,200 firers (803 US; CN 412, JP 411, TW 233, KR 209), median mcap $391M; 917 <$100M, 1,098 ≥$1B — 13% of INV, the broadest XR gate (A3 confluence §).
- Lineage: annual first-positive (TTM vs prior TTM) 1,052; fqx_eps_turned (date-matched TTM EPS ≤0 a year ago → >0 now) 1,170; fmp_dyn_ni_turned_positive 2,321 — and this is a SINGLE-QUARTER test (fmp_dynamics.py 109-116: latest quarterly NI >0 and the minimum of the prior four quarters ≤0), so any seasonal business with one loss quarter a year "crosses" every year; NI_ttm>0 does not repair it (a name TTM-profitable throughout still qualifies). `evt_sp500_member` (fmp_events, renamed on merge) is US-only; `evt_free_float` is a global snapshot. 298 firers carry earnings_oneoff_flag; 404 have NI margin <1%; 341 pass on EBITDA>0 with a GAAP operating loss; 59 have fq TTM NI ≤0.
- Spirit gaps: (a) the mandate mechanism is US-specific (S&P profitability rule) and 75% of firers are non-US, where the lens's "not a member" is trivially true and no analogue is wired — for them the spirit is "loss-maker stigma shed", a weaker claim; (b) no persistence: the S&P rule is the sum of four quarters >0 AND the latest quarter >0 (A2 B.4 #25 spec) — one quarter above zero is not a crossover; (c) no "not yet re-rated" leg — names that already ran on the print are included; (d) "operational profit" is claimed on EBITDA for 341 rows with an operating loss; (e) the A2 index-candidate tier (size band via HMC, float ≥10%, RS) is unwired.
- Methodology issues: three heterogeneous crossover definitions ORed without a basis tag (annual TTM, TTM EPS, single quarter); 404 zero-crossings within rounding; 3,200 members make the confluence "engine" family nearly free for any first-profit microcap (A3 confluence §2); no dilution guard although the first profit of a serial issuer is the compendium's cardinal-sin case.
- FMP augmentations: the quarterly panel (`fq_ni`, present) → the S&P-rule crossover (4-quarter sum >0 now and ≤0 four quarters earlier, latest quarter >0); `historical-sp500/nasdaq-constituent` (US) → the addition event; `shares-float` (done) → float ≥10%; `historical-market-capitalization` (A2 S5) → the eligibility band; `earnings-surprises-bulk` (global) → the crossing quarter was a beat; `ts_r13`, `ts_dist_hi52` → not yet re-rated; `fmp_dyn` EV history → P/S vs own median. No FMP endpoint for non-US index families (TOPIX / KOSPI / CSI eligibility) — say so.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] define the crossover once on the quarterly panel (TTM NI >0 now, TTM NI ≤0 four quarters ago, latest quarter >0); retire the single-quarter fmp_dyn trigger as a gate input (keep as `_watch`). 2 [method] materiality: NI margin ≥2% or NI ≥2% of mcap, op_margin >0 (not EBITDA), shares_yoy ≤10% — 404 firers today sit within rounding of zero. 3 [spirit] the unpriced half: ts_r13 ≤ +15% and P/S ≤ own 3-yr median, so the unlock is still ahead. 4 [data] wire the A2 B.4 #25 index-candidate exceptional tier (US only; open) and label non-US members "stigma shed" on the book. 5 [method] count toward confluence only from the core tier (A3 confluence §2).

### arch_xr_gross_margin_lead  (line 5110)
- Spirit: gross margin inflects first (mix / pricing / scale) while SG&A has not yet scaled, so operating margin lags and the trailing op margin understates the earnings power that is coming (Monster-class backward induction). Human sources: Pernas Xometry pitch (marketplace gross margin rising from 30.8% as the fundamental validation); master ref VI Andreola-Deden gate ("gross margin stable/rising, operating income positive or inflecting", revenue growth >25%); compendium Part II B ("gross margin high and stable/expanding"); Wolf Archetype 3 (ADF: revenue +79%, gross margin boosted, stock halved).
- As coded: revenue ≥$20M; GM delta YoY ≥2pp; rev_yoy ≥5%; GM ≥20%; op-margin delta < 0.5 × GM delta; not melting. No valuation, dilution or profitability guard. Lenses: none. 478 firers (IN 221, US 88, CN 31, JP 20, KR 16), median mcap $765M.
- Lineage: `gross_margin_delta_yoy` / `op_margin_delta_yoy` from yartseva_db (coherent TTM pairs from Yahoo quarterlies, else annual; yartseva_db.py 549-576), not FMP-filled (0 of 478); coverage 38% US / 60% non-US — the Yahoo quarterly feed is thinner for US, which is why 46% of firers are .NS/.BO (a source artefact, not a spirit cluster). The engine already holds three unused GM-trend measures: `fq_gm_inflection_flag` (58 firers), `fq_gm_yoy_streak_m` ≥6 (135), `fmp_dyn_gross_margin_trend` >0 (164). 49 firers have op_margin ≤0.
- Spirit gaps: (a) the "coil" leg `opmd < 0.5 × gmd` is satisfied by a FALLING operating margin: 207 of 478 have op-margin delta <0 and 85 are ≤ −2pp — gross margin up while operating margin DOWN means opex outrunning gross profit (or a one-off), the opposite of latent leverage unless SG&A is a deliberate, evidenced growth investment; (b) no persistence — one YoY print (A2 matrix: "GM lead measured on 2 consecutive TTM quarters", open); (c) the source thesis pairs GM expansion with fast revenue growth (≥25%) and a route to operating leverage — rev_yoy ≥5% is weak; (d) no unpriced leg (Monster's re-rate was an unpriced GM; a name at 40× has priced it); (e) mix attribution exists in XR39 margin_mixshift and is not linked; (f) GM recovering to its own norm after a bad year is recovery, not a lead (`tc_min_gm` / `tc_med_gm` present, unused).
- Methodology issues: the 0.5× rule is scale-free and sign-blind (GM +2pp with op −5pp passes); GM ≥20% admits distributors where +2pp is input-cost pass-through; 46% Indian firers reflect data-source coverage; no dilution guard although its XR siblings carry one.
- FMP augmentations: `fq_gm_yoy_streak_m` / `fq_gm_inflection_flag` (global, dated) → persistence; `tc_min_gm`, `tc_med_gm` (present) → above own norm; `financial-growth` sgaexpensesGrowth vs revenueGrowth (global, per-FY; A2 A.1 N) → SG&A growing slower than revenue = real coil; `key-metrics.researchAndDevelopementToRevenueTTM` (A2 nuance #3) → expensed growth vs bloat; `revenue-product-segmentation` (done) → mix attribution; `fmp_dyn_gross_margin_trend` (present).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] split the coil leg: core = 0 ≤ opmd < 0.5 × gmd (operating margin up, capturing less than half); opmd <0 → `_watch` unless the SG&A growth is explained (R&D/revenue ≥10% or sgaexpensesGrowth ≤ revenueGrowth + 10pp). 2 [spirit] persistence: fq_gm_yoy_streak_m ≥6 or fq_gm_inflection_flag (two consecutive TTM prints) — A2 matrix item open. 3 [spirit] add an unpriced leg (EV/S ≤ own 3-yr median, or ts_r52 ≤ market median) and the sibling dilution guard (shares ≤5%). 4 [data] GM now ≥ tc_med_gm (a rise from below norm is recovery, not lead). 5 [data] surface source coverage (Yahoo-quarterly skew) on the book rather than changing the rule; fill the delta from the fq panel (fq gross margin TTM vs prior TTM) for US names where Yahoo is thin.

### arch_xr_growth_capex_masked  (line 4904)
- Spirit: capex far above depreciation is VOLUNTARY growth investment that depresses reported FCF while the base business earns high returns; maintenance owner earnings sit far above FCF and the market prices the suppressed FCF. Human sources: master ref II (Yartseva: aggressive asset growth is good ONLY when covered by EBITDA growth — the CMA contradiction); compendium Part I #1 Phelps test (3) long reinvestment runway; Wolf Archetype 8's mirror reject ("heavy maintenance capex reduces free cash"); the Greenwald maintenance-capex split is engine-native.
- As coded: capex/D&A ≥1.5 (EDGAR levels, else `fq_capex_to_da`); CFO>0; ROCE ≥12% OR EBITDA margin ≥20%; rev_yoy ≥10%; FCF yield <4%; maintenance yield ≥7% = min(CFO − D&A, CFO − Greenwald maint capex) / local mcap, with a fallback of FCF yield + growth-capex yield from fq ratios; shares ≤5%; not financing-fragile; not melting; fx-coherent. Lenses: none. 24 firers (1 US; IN 6, CA 3, JP 2, KR 2), median mcap $464M — includes RELIANCE.NS and the dual line ERO / ERO.TO.
- Lineage: da_ttm / capex_ttm levels for 16-17 of 24; 9 ride the fq fallback. The fallback (`_maint_y31 = fcf_yield + gcx_y`, growth capex = capex − D&A) is lens (a) ONLY — it never applies the Greenwald lens (b), so fallback rows face a looser test than level rows: the fallback changes meaning [method]. The Greenwald growth-capex term uses the master rev_yoy point: a revenue dip zeroes "growth capex" (all capex becomes maintenance — conservative), a spike makes almost all capex "growth" — the min() protects only when D&A is the larger deduction. ROCE route 20 of 24; only 2 firers have negative FCF yield.
- Spirit gaps: (a) "returns justify it" is a snapshot ROCE on the OLD base — the thesis needs the INCREMENTAL return on the new capital (`roiic_lindy` / `fmp_st_roiic_lindy` / `fqx_roic_ttm` exist, unused) and Yartseva's cover (asset growth ≤ EBITDA growth); (b) nothing shows the capex is growth rather than a mandatory replacement wave (asset age: `fq_da_to_ppe`, accumulated depreciation untested); (c) no capex-peak / harvest timing (a `_capex_rolloff` helper exists at line 7242 for the mb_ family, not here) — the FCF release is undated; (d) 24 firers globally with 1 US: the EDGAR level path and ROU-biased fq PP&E both restrict reach, and A2's named upgrade (owner-earnings growthCapex, matrix REACH) is still unwired; (e) SBC add-backs inflate CFO and thus both maintenance lenses.
- Methodology issues: two maintenance definitions min'd, then a third quantity as fallback; EBITDA margin ≥20% is not a return; rev_yoy single point drives the Greenwald split (A2 §C fragile); "FCF suppressed" (<4%) vs "maintenance fat" (≥7%) is a 3pp gap, within the noise of a TTM capex window; dual lines double-count (ERO / ERO.TO).
- FMP augmentations: `owner-earnings` growthCapex / maintenanceCapex (global, cached; A2 A.1 U*, matrix REACH — open) → third lens and global reach; `fq_capex_to_da` + `fq_da_to_ppe` (present) → asset age; `fmp_st_roiic_lindy` / `fqx_roic_ttm` → return on the new capital; `financial-growth` assetGrowth vs ebitdaGrowth and fiveYRevenueGrowthPerShare (global; A2 A.1 N) → the Yartseva cover; `key-metrics.capexToDepreciationTTM` (A2 nuance #4); `fq_sbc_to_cfo` for the SBC deduction.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] wire owner-earnings maintenanceCapex as lens (c) inside the min(), and make the fq fallback compute the same Greenwald quantity from fq_capex / fq_revenue / fq_rev_growth (present) instead of FCF + growth-capex yield (A2 A.1 U* open; the fallback currently changes meaning). 2 [spirit] returns ON the capex: roiic (EDGAR or fmp_st) ≥12% or fqx TTM EBIT growth ≥ revenue growth — retire the EBITDA-margin escape. 3 [spirit] Yartseva cover: asset 3-yr CAGR ≤ EBITDA 3-yr growth; otherwise it is capex without return. 4 [method] dedupe dual lines and use fq TTM revenue growth for the Greenwald split (A2 §C open); deduct SBC (fq_sbc_to_cfo) from CFO in both lenses. 5 [spirit] exceptional tier: capex peak passing (fq capex < prior period with capex/D&A still >1 — the mb_ `_capex_rolloff` logic), so the FCF release is dated.

---

## Slice summary — the ten most valuable augmentations (all 26 archetypes; suggestions only, tags as above)

1. [method] **Dead survivability code at EBITDA ≤0.** `nde` is forced to 99 (line 1075), so the "very low leverage" alternative in cyclical_trough and double_trough's deep branch can never fire; only net cash ≥20% admits deep troughs. Replace with fq total_debt/total_assets or net debt/equity, plus a burn-runway test (net cash ≥2-3× TTM burn) — shared with floor_inflection's loss-making members.
2. [method] **gaap_profit_crossover's dominant trigger is a single-quarter test** (`fmp_dyn_ni_turned_positive`: latest quarter >0, any of the prior four ≤0 — 2,321 of 3,200 firers). Define the crossover once on the quarterly panel with the S&P rule (TTM >0 now, ≤0 four quarters ago, latest quarter >0), add materiality (NI margin ≥2%, op margin >0) and count toward confluence only from that core tier — this also shrinks the 13%-of-INV "engine" family that makes confluence nearly free.
3. [method] **Total D&A is conflated with depreciation across the OE family** (amortization_mask, depreciation_cliff — 75th pct D&A/PPE 16×, forensic_floor_growth's 61% "over-depreciated" leg, forensic_multiple_gap). Split via the EDGAR amortisation tag (US) or a goodwill-share guard (`fq_gw_pct_assets`, global); use `_maint_capex` (max of TTM and 5-yr) everywhere; cash-confirm OE with `fq_cfo_to_ni` and deduct SBC with `fq_sbc_to_cfo`.
4. [spirit] **Float and forward-book evidence is level-only or proxy-only.** float_compounding qualifies 65% of members on bare NWC<0; deferred_revenue_lead qualifies 90% on revenue growth alone. Make `fq_ccc` ≤ −20d or (defrev ≥10% AND fq_defrev_growth_minus_rev ≥0) the core float leg, the book-build the core lead leg, normalise the level by FMP industry, and use current-only deferred revenue on both EDGAR and FMP paths (the fill changes meaning).
5. [spirit] **forced_seller names no seller.** Tier by a named seller (index removal ≤12m, 13F −5pp / closedPositions, N-PORT fund exit for non-US, spin ≤18m, ETF passive-share drop — A2 B.4 #26 open), require "business grew" on fq TTM (59 current contradictions) without the NI>0 escape, and add a "selling cleared" leg (ts_r13 ≥ −5%, ts_vol_spike4 <1.5).
6. [method] **The "hidden-asset" floor collapses to net cash** wherever associates are unobserved (asset_owner_catalyst, floor_inflection — 94% of members, forensic_floor_growth). Count it only where associates exist; route net-cash-only rows to the visible-floor siblings; add NCAV / net-cash trend ≥ −10% YoY so the floor holds.
7. [method] **Dated inflection replaces annual first-positive flags and `oper_lev_any`** (floor_inflection 78% on rev_accel × oper_lev_any; asset_owner_catalyst; audited_streak's magnitude-free streak; discops_mask's missing first-clean-print). Core = `fqx_eps_turned` or a TTM-confirmed opinc turn, `fqx_inc_ebit_margin` for operating leverage, corroboration on ≥2 lines for first-positives (A1 kpi_threshold / A2 §C open).
8. [data] **Global reach where the class is US-only by construction**: depreciation_cliff (0 non-US; `fq_da_to_ppe` + `fq_capex_to_da` with the ROU bias documented), cash_tax_advantage (10 FMP-path firers; cash-flow `deferredIncomeTax` as the global lens), clean_net_net / cannibal_* (buyback leg dead outside the US; fq `buyback` through the fx bridge + `splits` guard), growth_capex_masked (owner-earnings maintenanceCapex, and a fallback that computes the same Greenwald quantity rather than a different one).
9. [spirit] **Coil-sign and persistence fixes for the margin/growth tells**: gross_margin_lead admits 207 members whose operating margin FELL (split the coil leg on sign; two consecutive TTM prints; GM above own `tc_med_gm`; explain SG&A growth via R&D/revenue or `financial-growth` sgaexpensesGrowth); contracted_backlog needs RPO growth ≥ revenue growth + 10pp and an industry-normalised level; baron_compounder needs evidenced reinvestment (R&D or growth capex) and a decade window (`financial-growth` per-share).
10. [spirit] **Ownership and realisation legs the human sources insist on**: founder / 13D-IN leg with a 60% controlled-sub cap (baron, asset_owner, floor_inflection; A1 B.1 / A2 A.3 open); a dated realisation tier (8-K 2.01 divestiture for discops_mask, tender / 13D / buyback for the net-net and floor classes, capex-peak passing for growth_capex_masked, payout ≥3% for forensic_multiple_gap); and an "unpriced" leg (own 3-yr EV/S median from `enterprise-values`, ts_r13 / ts_r52 context) for the crossover, gross-margin, forced-seller and streak classes so the re-rate is still ahead. All of these are suggestions for tiering (core / watch / exceptional), none a veto, and none carries a data-tuned threshold.
