# Slice xr_b: Forensic / hidden-value / exceptional-return, slice B (26)

## Audit 3 — slice XR-B (26 forensic / hidden-value / exceptional-return archetypes)

Read-only audit of `archetype_tags.py` (line numbers = current file). Firer counts are from `archetype_tags.csv` joined to
`asymmetry_global.csv` (46,526 rows; 16,945 US). Coverage figures are measured on the master and side files (US / non-US).
Shared context used throughout: `mcap` = `market_cap_usd`; `s()` fills NaN with a default (0 or 99), `_ncol()` keeps NaN;
`_not_melting` = not (op margin < 0 or ROCE < −5%) AND no cash-on-cash lens positive AND not improving (so it is vacuous on
data-empty rows); `_fx_coherent` = mcap-USD twin fx within 10% of the revenue-USD twin, and never `.F` / `9xxxxx.SS` lines;
`_dna_loc` = audited `da_ttm` (55%) else bounded implied EBITDA−EBIT; `_maint_capex` = max(capex_ttm, EDGAR 5-yr `capex_avg`
(10% cov, US 26%)); `_oe_loc` = NI + D&A − maint capex (local); `_midcyc_ebitda` = (normalized_ebitda/normalized_revenue) ×
revenue_ttm; `_hidden_pct` = (associates + cash − debt)/mcap (local); `beaten_down_any(d)` = any of ts_dist_hi52/hi260/r52,
5y-range, roc12, price_yoy, mom12 with a contradiction veto from the panel 52w lens; `data_quality_flag` = balance-sheet
identity breaks (equity>assets, cash>assets, tangible>equity, EBITDA>2×revenue).
EDGAR-derived hidden-value columns (associates, em_*, inv_remark_*, nol_usd, segments, ppe_gross/accum-dep/ROU, ETR,
retained_earnings, streaks, Form-4 flags, unlock language) exist essentially only for US filers (0.1–1% non-US coverage).

### arch_xr_harvest_distribution  (line 4264)
- Spirit: Capital-cycle harvest paid out — "falling capex/depreciation ratios, share-count reduction, depressed valuations after years of poor returns" (Compendium Part II-D Capital Cycle Screen) crossed with Wolf/Oak Archetype 8 "Cash-Flow Deleveraging & Yield" (dividends/buybacks >3–5% of mcap, low growth capex). Engine-native framing XR7.
- As coded: D&A>0 and maintenance capex (max of TTM capex and 5-yr avg) in [0, 0.5×D&A]; combined payout = dividend_yield + buyback_yield (NaN→0) ≥ 6%; 0<P/B<1; owner earnings (NI+D&A−maint capex) >0 or EDGAR oe_avg>0; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: da_ttm (EDGAR audited 55%; implied fallback gated to op_margin>0) → EDGAR/Yahoo; capex_ttm 56% global, capex_avg EDGAR 5-yr **US-only** (26% US / 0.8% non-US) so the "5-yr avg" protection is absent for ~90% of the universe; dividend_yield 41% (Yahoo primary, FMP fill; provider TTM yield, FX-corruptible per A2 §C); buyback_yield 8% (US 22%, non-US ≈0) → non-US members qualify on dividend alone; oe_avg EDGAR US-only. 249 firers (103 US / 146 non-US), median mcap $256m, 50 sub-$50m.
- Spirit gaps: the thesis is a CONTROLLED harvest of a still-producing base; the code has no revenue-holding guard (sibling `arch_overdepreciated_assets` requires rev_yoy ≥ −5%), so a decaying business that cut capex and still pays a soon-to-be-cut dividend passes. No payout persistence / no-cut test (matrix row: "payout persistence from DIV", still open). No share-count guard, so a buyback_yield paired with net dilution counts as distribution.
- Methodology: `_payout_x7` fillna(0) is fine directionally but a 6% yield on a sub-book stock is the classic yield-trap band without cover evidence; owner-earnings leg is NI-based (a loss year fails even when CFO is fine); the maint-capex max() is only conservative where capex_avg exists (US).
- FMP augmentations: `owner-earnings` maintenanceCapex/growthCapex (global, H) as second maintenance-capex lens where capex_avg is missing (A2 A.1 U*, open); `dividends` history (global, H): paid in each of last 3 FYs, no cut, yield recomputed in listing currency (A2 §C open); wired `tc_uncov_payout_3y` (through-cycle payout not covered by FCF) as cover test; wired `fq_capex_to_da` (fmp_quarterly, global) as a same-currency capex/D&A fallback.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] use `fq_capex_to_da` / owner-earnings maintenanceCapex when capex_avg is NaN so the collapsed-TTM-window fix reaches non-US names (A2 A.1 open). 2 [spirit] add rev_yoy ≥ −5% (or tc revenue not in multi-year decline) — harvest, not decay. 3 [method] payout persistence + cover: ≥3 FY of payments from `dividends` and `tc_uncov_payout_3y`==0 (matrix LEG open). 4 [method] `~(shares_yoy > 0.02)` when the buyback leg is what lifts payout over 6%.

### arch_xr_hidden_segment_compounder  (line 5032)
- Spirit: Master Reference I-C "The Hidden Thing — (6) Hidden Segment Inside a Bad Consolidated Screen" (Wavelock, Food Empire, Everus); Pernas DFIN ("printer" label hiding a software business). Failure mode named in the source: "the hidden segment is actually small, shrinking, or structurally dependent on the bad wrapper".
- As coded: revenue ≥ $20m; segment_count ≥2; fastest_segment_yoy ≥20%; segment margin inflecting (fastest_seg_opmargin_delta_yoy>0 | seg_margin_inflect_flag | seg_oplev ≥0.10); fastest_segment_share_delta ≥2pp; fastest_segment_share ≤60%; cheap whole (EV/S ≤3 | EV/EBITDA ≤12 | P/B<2 | FCF yld ≥3%); fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: all segment legs from `edgar_segment_signals.csv` (EDGAR dimensional harvest of OperatingIncomeLoss by segment axis: 4,745 filers, 2,542 multi-segment, margin-delta 983, seg_oplev 722) → **US-only by construction** (38 of 39 firers US; median mcap $1.8bn). `fmp_segments.csv` (revenue-product-segmentation, global) is merged into df but unused here. Cheapness legs from the master (global).
- Spirit gaps: the "bad wrapper" is never tested — consolidated growth/margin may already be good; only cheapness is required. No relative test that the segment outgrows the consolidated line. `segment_rot_flag` (core segment shrinking ≥10%) is computed two lines later but is neither a lens nor a veto, though the source's failure mode is exactly "dependent on the bad wrapper".
- Methodology: cheapness OR includes P/B<2, weak for a compounder claim; no `data_quality_flag` gate although sibling hidden-value archetypes (stake_fv_gap, remark, lookthrough) carry one; segment data is annual 10-K (staleness vs current EV unchecked); seg_oplev ≥0.10 semantics (segment operating leverage) undocumented in the gate.
- FMP augmentations: `revenue-product-segmentation` / `revenue-geographic-segmentation` (global, H, wired as fmp_segments): fastest-segment growth / share / share-delta legs globally; segment EBIT/margin is NOT available from FMP (map §6/§11), so the margin-inflection leg stays EDGAR. `earning-call-transcript` (targeted, A2 A.2 N) for segment margin commentary is the only non-US route to the margin leg.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] build the revenue-side legs from fmp_segments for non-US names and emit a `*_watch` where the margin leg is unobservable (FMP map #3 open). 2 [spirit] require the wrapper to look bad: consolidated rev_yoy ≤ fastest_segment_yoy − 10pp, or consolidated op margin ≤ segment margin. 3 [method] add `data_quality_flag == 0` and a segment fiscal-year freshness check (≤15 months). 4 [method] surface `segment_rot_flag` as a negative lens (dependent-on-wrapper risk) rather than leaving it orphaned.

### arch_xr_insider_capitulation  (line 4618)
- Spirit: Compendium Part II-A filing-signal cheat sheet — "Form 4: screen open-market purchases, clusters of 3+ within ~10 days"; Master Reference III — "insider buying clusters (≥3 unique insiders, code P, within ~2 weeks) … cluster buys roughly double excess returns of lone buys (Lakonishok & Lee)"; VIII "≥1 independent director, $25k+ size, at sentiment troughs".
- As coded: insider_cluster_buy_flag ≥1; beaten_down_any(0.40); rev_yoy ≥ −10%; economics intact (GM delta ≥ −3pp | owner earnings >0 | oe_avg>0); EBITDA margin > −10% | FCF yld>0; nde<3 | net cash; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: `sec_insider_buys.py` — SEC Form 3/4/5 bulk, code P open-market, **last 4 quarters (~12 months)**, cluster = **≥2 distinct buyer CIKs** over that whole window, no size floor, no role requirement → SEC/US-only (84 of 88 firers US; sec_insider_signals.csv 4,459 names, 1,372 flagged). Drawdown from the ts panel (global). Non-US substitute: none catalogued (insider endpoints are SEC-derived).
- Spirit gaps: "cluster" in the sources is 3+ insiders inside ~2 weeks; the code's 2 buyers across 12 months is not a cluster. No timing link between the buys and the crash — the capitulation claim ("buying their own crash") is unproven when the buys predate the drawdown. No $ size floor, no officer/independent-director requirement (`insider_officer_buy_flag`, `insider_net_buy_value`, `insider_distinct_buyers` are produced and merged but unused). No sentiment-trough evidence beyond the drawdown.
- Methodology: the economics OR is permissive (oe_avg>0 is a 5-yr average — stale); EBITDA > −10% | FCF>0 floor is fine; 88 firers is plausible but the definition admits token buys.
- FMP augmentations: `insider-trading/statistics` (US, H, quarterly totalPurchases/totalSales/acquiredDisposedRatio since 2007) → buys in the crash quarter (PIT), purchases in ≥2 of last 4 quarters (A2 B.28, matrix PROXY — open); `insider-trading/search` (US, H) transaction dates/prices for a true ≤14-day, ≥3-buyer cluster with price-at-buy ≤0.6×52w high; `key-executives` (global, S) to tag CEO/CFO buyers.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] redefine cluster = ≥3 distinct buyers within 14 days, each ≥$25k (Form-4 rows already parsed). 2 [spirit] date the capitulation: latest buy within 180 days AND ts_dist_hi52 ≤0.6 at the buy date (A2 B.28 open). 3 [method] add an officer/director leg (`insider_officer_buy_flag` or `insider_net_buy_value` ≥ 0.1% of mcap) as core, bare flag as watch. 4 [data] IST quarterly purchases as the PIT corroboration column (A2 A.3 open).

### arch_xr_investment_remark  (line 5375)
- Spirit: Compendium I-6 Kerrisdale/Scrapyard mismarked balance sheet ("cross-holdings carried at cost … strategic stakes worth more than the whole market cap"); Master Reference I-G Unlocked Thing, catalyst "portfolio mark-up". Engine-native LanzaTech/SGLT example (a step-up to fair value on an investee's listing).
- As coded: inv_remark_pct ≥50% and jump>0; jump/market_cap_usd ≥10%; unrealized_inv_gain>0 | nonop_gain_ttm>0; inv_carry_now/market_cap_usd ≥15%; data_quality_flag==0; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: `compute_investment_remark.py` on the EDGAR companyfacts cache, USD-only series, carrying line = first concept with data from a **broadest-first** list (Investments, LongTermInvestments, EquitySecuritiesWithoutRDFV…, EquityMethodInvestments, MarketableSecuritiesNoncurrent); nonop from NonoperatingIncomeExpense. investment_remark.csv: 3,196 symbols, 1,382 with positive remark_pct → US-only. 11 firers, all US, median mcap **$57m**, 5 sub-$50m.
- Spirit gaps: nothing tests that the market has NOT repriced (no tape leg vs the remark date); no cheapness on the consolidated business (sibling stake_fv_gap uses `_stake_cheap`); the source's mismark is a stake worth more than book — a carrying-value jump only proves a remeasurement occurred.
- Methodology: the broadest concept ("Investments"/LongTermInvestments/MarketableSecuritiesNoncurrent) also moves when cash is swept into treasuries or a bond portfolio is bought — an allocation change, not a remark; the gain confirmation is any positive nonop_gain_ttm (interest income qualifies) with no magnitude link to the jump; `inv_carry_concept` is recorded by the producer but not used to discriminate. Tiny median cap makes a $10m securities purchase a "remark".
- FMP augmentations: no FMP equivalent for equity-method fair value (map §6). `historical-market-capitalization` / ts panel (global, H) for "not repriced since the remark": price change since `inv_carry_now_end` < jump/mcap. `balance-sheet-statement.longTermInvestments` (global, H) gives only the same ambiguous line for non-US — watch tier at best.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] require the gain to be commensurate: (unrealized_inv_gain or nonop_gain_ttm) ≥ 0.5 × inv_remark_jump. 2 [method] tier by concept: EquityMethod/EquitySecuritiesWithoutRDFV hits = core; Investments/LongTermInvestments/MarketableSecuritiesNoncurrent = watch (allocation-change risk). 3 [spirit] add an unpriced leg: ts_r26 (or return since the carrying date) < 0.5 × jump/mcap. 4 [method] add `_stake_cheap` for consistency with the sibling stake archetypes.

### arch_xr_latent_bath_floor  (line 4775)
- Spirit: Wolf/Oak Archetype 4 "Asset-Backed Floor + Optional Growth" and Liger "Asset-backed optionality" (net cash or monetisable assets cover much of market value; operating business near break-even; "liquidation value should not be treated as equivalent to cash"), crossed with the big-bath rebound (a non-cash charge depressing the trailing line). Engine XR28 = pre-rebound XR13 with a hard floor instead of a beaten-down tape.
- As coded: mid-cycle EBITDA (normalized margin × current revenue) >0; EBITDA_ttm < 0.7×mid-cycle | NI<0; EV/mid-cycle in (0, 8]; cfo_yield>0; hard floor (net cash ≥30% | NCAV ≥60% | hidden ≥40% | gross cash ≥50% of mcap); rev_yoy ≥ −10%; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: normalized_ebitda / normalized_revenue (master, 57–62%, Yahoo/FMP multi-year) × revenue_ttm local, EV local → same-currency; ncav_pct_mcap 52%, net_cash_pct 76%, cash_pct_mcap (gross); associates for `_hidden_pct` US-only (so non-US floors are cash/NCAV). 587 firers (465 non-US), median $144m, 181 sub-$50m — the broadest "hard-floor" XR in the slice.
- Spirit gaps: no evidence of a ONE-OFF at all — EBITDA < 0.7×mid-cycle also describes a plain cyclical or secular decline (that is `xr_cyclical_trough`'s job); the only non-cash test is cfo_yield>0. The Liger caveat (liquidation ≠ cash) is ignored by the gross-cash leg: cash_pct_mcap ≥50% admits levered names whose net cash is negative.
- Methodology: `NI<0` alone (with EBITDA at mid-cycle) is admitted — an interest-eaten name is "depressed" only by leverage; the rev_yoy floor is one year, so a three-year revenue slide passes with a mid-cycle EBITDA rebuilt on the lower revenue; no DQ gate although the floor leans on balance-sheet identities; `_hardfloor_28` deliberately excludes P/B but keeps gross cash, which is looser.
- FMP augmentations (wired): `tc_med_opm` / `tc_years` → `_below_midcyc_opm` (used by double_trough, not here) as a through-cycle trough lens; `tc_min_opm`>0 as an exceptional tier (never a loss FY); fmp_quarterly `fq_cfo` vs `fq_ni_cf` (global) → non-cash-charge share (CFO − NI)/|NI| as the bath tell; `fq_disc_ops_share` for a discontinued drag. FMP `cash-flow-statement.otherNonCashItems` (global, H) is the standardized impairment/write-down proxy.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] require an observable bath: (fq_cfo − fq_ni) ≥ 0.5×|NI| or otherNonCashItems ≥ 20% of revenue or fq_disc_ops_share>0 — else route to cyclical_trough. 2 [method] drop gross `cash_pct_mcap ≥0.5` from the hard floor or pair it with net_cash_pct ≥0. 3 [method] add `_below_midcyc_opm` as an alternative trough lens and `tc_min_opm>0` as exceptional (A2 B.31 pattern). 4 [method] add `data_quality_flag == 0`.

### arch_xr_latent_inflection_floor  (line 4752)
- Spirit: MicroCap footprint — "profitable or at a clear inflection … 1–2 quarters before the run" (Compendium II-B, Andreola), Liger "Earnings, backlog and conservative guidance" (reported improvement, incremental margins above history, net cash, low expectations), floored per Wolf Archetype 4. Engine XR27 = the pre-crossing version of XR3.
- As coded: not financing-fragile; improving (ROCE delta>0 & ROCE ≤5% | op-margin delta >2pp & op margin ≤6% | EBITDA-margin delta in (2pp, 30pp] & EBITDA margin ≤10% | fcf_eta ≤5 quarters & FCF yld<0); not yet crossed (no annual *_first_positive flag); floor (net cash ≥20% | NCAV ≥50% | P/B<0.8 | hidden ≥30% | gross cash ≥50%); rev_yoy ≥ −5%; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: single-year deltas roce_delta_yoy 60%, op/ebitda margin deltas 52% (Yahoo primary, FMP fill); fcf_eta_quarters from a linear run-rate extrapolation (fill_asymmetry_gaps, 22%, clipped 0–40); first_positive flags annual (s() → 0 when absent); floors as XR28. 622 firers (517 non-US), median mcap **$56m**, 289 sub-$50m — the smallest-cap member of the slice.
- Spirit gaps: "latent" is not enforced — the ROCE leg (ROCE ≤5% & any positive delta) admits already-profitable names with a +0.1pp move; no magnitude on any improvement; the source wants incremental margins above the company's own history — no own-history frame (tc_*) is used.
- Methodology: `_not_yet_crossed` is built from annual flags that are 0-filled when missing, so for most non-US rows it is vacuously true — a name that crossed on the quarterly TTM (fqx_eps_turned==1) still counts as "not yet crossed"; P/B<0.8 as a "hard" floor contradicts the XR28 comment ("sub-book alone is ubiquitous among depressed cyclicals"); gross cash leg as in XR28.
- FMP augmentations: wired `fqx_ebit_ttm_g`, `fqx_inc_ebit_margin` (fmp_quarterly_ext, global, H) as the TTM improvement lens (matrix LEG "improvement measured on TTM" — open); wired `fqx_eps_turned` / `fmp_dyn_opinc_turned_positive` to make "not yet crossed" an observed fact; `tc_med_opm` (global) for own-history framing; `analyst-estimates` forward EBIT/EPS (global-ish, S) crossing zero next FY as an exceptional tier (FMP map §7).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] make latency observed: require current level still ≤0 on at least one line (op margin ≤0 or FCF<0) AND ~(fqx_eps_turned==1 | fmp_dyn_opinc_turned_positive==1) in addition to the annual flags. 2 [method] align the floor with `_hardfloor_28` (drop bare P/B<0.8; pair gross cash with net cash ≥0). 3 [data] TTM improvement via fqx_* (matrix open) with a minimum magnitude (ROCE delta ≥2pp, EBIT TTM growth >0). 4 [spirit] own-history lens: op margin ≥5pp below `tc_med_opm` (the improvement heads back toward a margin it has earned before).

### arch_xr_leverage_detonation  (line 4568)
- Spirit: Master Reference I-G (20) "Operating KPI crosses a threshold" (KORU first positive operating margin); Compendium II-B "the first profitable quarter is the strongest footprint"; Liger "incremental margins above historical averages"; Wolf Archetype 9 catalyst "release of the first improved earnings report (breakeven)". Engine XR20.
- As coded: not financing-fragile; crossing (ebitda_first_positive | fcf_first_positive | fcf_eta ≤2 quarters); drop-through (annual incremental_ebitda_margin ≥35% | TTM fqx_inc_ebit_margin ≥35%, domain ≤1.0); rev_yoy 20–150%; P/S ≤3 | EV/S ≤3; revenue ≥$10m; shares ≤10%; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: incremental_ebitda_margin derived in derive_missing_columns (ΔEBITDA/ΔRevenue rebuilt from yoy rates; requires ΔRev>0; 34% cov, global); fqx_inc_ebit_margin (16k names, global, date-matched); first_positive flags annual (Yahoo/EDGAR/FMP fill); fcf_eta linear extrapolation. 93 firers (70 non-US), median $368m.
- Spirit gaps: the "crossing" is annual and FCF-based (fcf_first_positive is a working-capital-noise flag per A2 §C); no operating confirmation (EBIT/op margin positive on TTM); the "trailing screens still price the loss-maker" claim is only P/S ≤3; no dated crossing (A2 B.32: first positive TTM EBIT inside the last 2 quarters — open).
- Methodology: the annual incremental-margin leg has NO ≤1.0 domain limit (only the fqx leg does), so a shrinking loss on a negative base (EBITDA −10 → −2 on +20% revenue) prints a >100% "drop-through" and passes — the engine's own comment calls that a different fact; ΔEBITDA is rebuilt as ebitda×yoy/(1+yoy), unstable near yoy = −1; shares_yoy ≤10% is looser than the 5% used by every sibling growth gate; fcf_eta ≤2 quarters is a forecast, not a print.
- FMP augmentations: `fmp_dyn_opinc_turned_positive` / `fqx_eps_turned` (wired, global) for a dated crossing (A2 B.32 open); `splits` (global, H) to verify the flat share count (A2 §C); ts `ts_weinstein_stage` ∈ {1,2} (wired) as tape context; `analyst-estimates` forward EBITDA >0 next FY (global, S) as the forward confirmation (FMP map §7).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] apply the same ≤1.0 domain to `incremental_ebitda_margin` or require ebitda_ttm>0 now so 35% is a true drop-through, not a loss unwinding. 2 [spirit] add the dated crossing lens (opinc/EPS turned positive within 2 quarters) as core; annual-flag-only as watch (A2 B.32 open). 3 [method] tighten shares_yoy to ≤5% (or require financing_cf ≤0). 4 [data] forward-consensus EBITDA >0 as the exceptional tier.

### arch_xr_look_through_value  (line 4925)
- Spirit: Compendium I-6 Kerrisdale/Scrapyard — "cross-holdings carried at cost … strategic stakes worth more than the whole market cap (Keisei/Oriental Land ~2× its own cap, Samsung C&T)"; Wolf Archetype 5 Holding-company/NAV discount ("look-through audit: confirm who owns what").
- As coded: investments_associates / mcap ≥30%; profit present (EBITDA>0 | op margin>0 | FCF yld>0); cheap (P/B<1.5 | EV/EBITDA ≤10); nde<3 | net cash; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: investments_associates = EDGAR EquityMethodInvestments (5.7% cov; US 15%, non-US 0.3%) → US-only in practice (16 of 17 firers US, median $306m). Denominator is `mcap` = market_cap_usd: fine for 10-K filers (USD), but a 20-F filer reporting in home currency would mix currencies (`_fx_coherent` tests mcap vs revenue, not this line).
- Spirit gaps: the source's edge is a stake worth MORE than its carrying value (Keisei); the code gates on the carrying value itself, which is by definition the understated number — 30% of mcap at book proves size, not mismark. No listed-investee lookup, no em_income growth, no catalyst. Heavy overlap with `arch_hidden_assets` (associates + net cash ≥25% of mcap, P/B<1.5) — most members are double-counted.
- Methodology: EV/EBITDA ≤10 on a holdco-like parent can be flattered by equity-method income inside "EBITDA" (the engine's Porsche SE note); `_profit_present` with FCF-only is thin. 17 firers is fine for the data, but the archetype is essentially hidden_assets restricted to the associate line.
- FMP augmentations: none for equity-method fair value (map §6). `balance-sheet-statement.longTermInvestments` (global, H) is a coarse non-US proxy (includes securities) — watch tier only, and divide by local `market_cap`, not USD mcap.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] tier by evidence of mismark: core = em_fv_gap>0 or em_income/mcap ≥3% (the stake earns/marks more than book); bare carrying-value ≥30% = watch. 2 [method] use local `market_cap` (or an fx-coherent mcap) as the denominator when the filer is a 20-F home-currency reporter. 3 [method] cheapness ex-associate income: EV/(EBITDA − em_income) or note the double count with `arch_hidden_assets` in the book. 4 [data] longTermInvestments (FMP, global) as a watch fallback for non-US holdco parents.

### arch_xr_lookthrough_earner  (line 5409)
- Spirit: Buffett look-through earnings applied to Compendium I-6 (Keisei: the Oriental Land stake's earnings are the parent's real earnings); Wolf Archetype 5 look-through audit.
- As coded: em_income>0; em_income/pretax_income ≥25% (pretax>0) | em_income/market_cap_usd ≥5%; `_stake_cheap` (EV/EBITDA ≤12 | P/B<2 | FCF yld ≥3% | EV/S ≤3); data_quality_flag==0; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: em_income = EDGAR IncomeLossFromEquityMethodInvestments (investment_remark.csv: 1,704 observed, 839 positive), pretax_income_ttm EDGAR → US-only (18 of 18 firers US, median mcap $2.0bn). No non-US substitute.
- Spirit gaps: "compounds inside the consolidated numbers, unpriced" — a single-period em_income (can be a one-off gain on an associate: dilution gain, disposal) with no persistence or growth; no test that the market prices the parent WITHOUT the associate.
- Methodology: em_income/pretax ≥25% is a small-denominator artifact when pretax income is near zero (a $1m associate share on $3m pretax fires) — no floor on pretax or on em_income/mcap in that branch; `_stake_cheap` EV/EBITDA leg is flattered where provider EBITDA already includes the equity income (double count: the "hidden" engine is also inflating the cheapness lens); P/B<2 is a weak cheapness lens for this thesis.
- FMP augmentations: none — FMP standardized income statements have no equity-method line (map §6). The EDGAR producer already has the history in companyfacts and keeps only the latest value, so persistence is a producer change, not an endpoint.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] require em_income/mcap ≥2% in BOTH branches (kills the small-pretax artifact) or pretax_income ≥3% of mcap. 2 [spirit] persistence: em_income>0 in the prior FY as well (producer keeps prior period — trivial). 3 [method] cheapness ex-associate: (mcap)/(NI − em_income) ≤15 or EV/(EBITDA − em_income) ≤10 so the stake is not paying for its own cheapness. 4 [method] keep as is otherwise — well built for what EDGAR gives.

### arch_xr_margin_mixshift  (line 5081)
- Spirit: Master Reference I-C Hidden Thing (6) (segment mix inside a bad wrapper); Pernas DFIN "business mix shifted towards software" and the "Fallen platform with improving motor" recipe (improving unit economics/margins); Compendium II-B "gross margin high and stable/expanding".
- As coded: revenue ≥$20m; segment_count ≥2; seg_mix_uplift (fastest segment op margin − blended) ≥5pp; fastest_segment_share_delta >0; cheap (EV/S ≤3 | EV/EBITDA ≤12 | P/B<2 | FCF yld ≥3%); fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: seg_mix_uplift from `segment_valuation.csv` (698 observed; EDGAR segment OperatingIncomeLoss; USD EBIT guarded by a 0.5–2.0× revenue reconciliation, the margin ratio is not); segment_count / share_delta from edgar_segment_signals → US-only (80 of 82 firers, median $2.0bn). fmp_segments (global revenue) unused.
- Spirit gaps: no growth requirement on the fastest segment — share_delta>0 can come from the legacy segment shrinking (mix by attrition), which raises the blended margin but is not the compounder the source describes; no test that the consolidated margin has NOT yet expanded (the "market can't yet see it" claim); no minimum share of the richer segment.
- Methodology: share_delta >0 of any size (0.1pp) — sibling XR37 requires ≥2pp; segment margins exclude unallocated corporate costs, so the "uplift" is against a segment-level blend, fine, but a segment with 3% share and 40pp uplift moves the consolidated margin by ~0.1pp per year — no materiality (uplift × share_delta); no DQ gate; annual segment data vs current EV.
- FMP augmentations: `revenue-product-segmentation` (global, H) for the share-delta and growth legs; segment margin not fillable (map §11). No further catalogued endpoint.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] materiality: share_delta ≥2pp AND fastest_segment_yoy ≥5% (growth, not attrition), and rank by uplift × share_delta (implied consolidated margin expansion). 2 [spirit] "unpriced" leg: consolidated op_margin_delta_yoy < 0.5 × (uplift × share_delta) — the mechanical expansion hasn't printed yet. 3 [data] revenue-side legs from fmp_segments for non-US as a watch tier (FMP map #3 open). 4 [method] add `data_quality_flag == 0` and segment-year freshness.

### arch_xr_monetization_trifecta  (line 4987)
- Spirit: Compendium Part III Assembly checklist block (2) "NOL tax assets — quantum, §382(l)(5) survival, valuation allowance, watch for a TRA"; Part II-A technique (7) post-bankruptcy orphaned equities with NOLs; Master Reference III canonical WMIH ($6.0bn NOL, no annual limitation, monetised via Mr. Cooper). Engine XR35 = net cash + NOL + inflection.
- As coded: net cash ≥20% of mcap; nol_usd/mcap in [0.30, 20]; inflecting (roce_first_positive | fcf_first_positive | roce_inflection | cfo_first_positive); EBITDA margin >3%; FCF yld > −5%; not melting. No `_fx_coherent` (nol USD / mcap USD is coherent). No lenses.
- Lineage: nol_usd = EDGAR companyconcept OperatingLossCarryforwards, **USD-unit only** (2,861 positive; a deliberate guard against RMB/JPY shells) → US-only (5 firers, all US, median $534m); net_cash_pct global; inflection flags annual (Yahoo/FMP fill).
- Spirit gaps: §382 is the whole game and is unmodelled — no shares_yoy guard (an equity raise both creates the net cash and can trigger the ownership change that limits the NOL); `deferred_tax_valuation_allowance` (used by arch_dta_reversal) is not consulted, though an allowance release is the board's own statement that the NOL will be used; no TRA check; "monetizable" needs taxable income — EBITDA margin >3% is not pretax profit.
- Methodology: nol_usd is the GROSS carryforward; its tax value is ~21% (federal) — a "≥30% of mcap" NOL is ~6% of mcap in cash-tax value, a modest asset; the 20× cap still admits §382-impaired shells (a $10m cap with $200m NOL). OperatingLossCarryforwards can mix federal + state and is the latest annual value (staleness unchecked). FCF > −5% while "inflecting" tolerates burn.
- FMP augmentations: none for the NOL (map §5: EDGAR-only). Wired `fq_cash_tax_rate` (fmp_quarterly, global) — a low cash-tax rate on positive pretax income shows the shield in use; wired `fmp_dyn_ni_turned_positive` / `fqx_eps_turned` for a dated inflection.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] tax-effect the NOL (×0.21) and gate on ≥10–15% of mcap in tax value, ranked by it. 2 [spirit] add `~(shares_yoy > 0.05)` and a §382 proxy (no >50% cumulative share-count change over 3 years from fq share history). 3 [spirit] require pretax_income_ttm>0 or NI>0 (taxable income exists) instead of EBITDA margin >3%. 4 [data] corroborate with `fq_cash_tax_rate` ≤0.6×book rate where observed.

### arch_xr_neg_ev_growth  (line 4132)
- Spirit: Compendium II-B MicroCap screen (net cash preferred; ≥25% growth; profitable), Flyover "TVE Co — below net cash, 0 analysts", Wolf Archetypes 4/10 (cash >20% of mcap as the floor). Engine XR1 "paid to grow".
- As coded: cash_gt_ev_flag>0 | net cash ≥80% of mcap; rev_yoy 15–100% | rev_yoy_streak_q ≥8; profit present (EBITDA>0 | op margin>0 | FCF yld>0); shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: cash_gt_ev_flag / net_cash_pct_mcap (master; Yahoo primary, FMP fill; 76%); rev_yoy (Yahoo/FMP); rev_yoy_streak_q EDGAR **US-only** (12%, non-US 0.5%) — the "audited streak" substitute never reaches the 176 non-US firers; shares_yoy 55%. 221 firers (176 non-US), median mcap **$85m**, 82 sub-$50m.
- Spirit gaps: "cash covers the price" is asserted from a provider net-cash number: restricted cash, customer deposits and minority interests are not netted, and the cash's existence is never corroborated (the G2 comment in the engine already calls >100% cash "a shell/holdco artifact") — the member set is dominated by small Asian listings where trapped/unverifiable cash is the known failure mode. Profitability is presence-only; Yartseva (Master Ref II) wants the LEVEL.
- Methodology: `_profit_present` passes on FCF>0 alone (working-capital cash on a loss-making line); no DQ gate (cash>assets is a DQ leg but only used elsewhere); the streak leg is a US-only OR that silently narrows meaning by geography; no cheapness on earnings (a 15% grower at 80% net cash with a 60× P/E on the residual still qualifies — by design, but unranked).
- FMP augmentations: `balance-sheet-statement` (global, H) cashAndShortTermInvestments − totalDebt − minorityInterest for a stricter net cash; wired fmp_quarterly net-debt path (`fq_netdebt_*`) → net cash stable/rising over ≥3 balance sheets (cash that is real accumulates); `income-statement.interestIncome` (global, H) → interest yield on cash ≥0.5% as an existence test; wired `fq_rev_yoy_streak_m` (fmp_quarterly, global) as the streak substitute (matrix LEG "use TTM growth as the growth lens", open).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] cash-existence test: fq net cash non-decreasing across ≥3 quarters AND interest income ≥0.5% of cash where the line exists. 2 [data] replace the US-only streak leg with `fq_rev_yoy_streak_m ≥24` (global) — matrix open. 3 [spirit] profitability LEVEL: op margin >0 or FCF yield ≥3% (not bare presence), and add `data_quality_flag == 0`. 4 [method] rank members by ex-cash P/E (`arch_cash_adjusted_pe` machinery already exists) so the residual business is priced.

### arch_xr_nol_shield  (line 4850)
- Spirit: Compendium II-A (7) post-bankruptcy equities with NOLs; Assembly block (2); Tillinghast certainty of cash flows. Engine XR30 (deferred-tax nuance): a loss deficit shielding new profits so cash earnings exceed GAAP after-tax.
- As coded: retained_earnings <0; loss-deficit (~(ROE>20%) | P/B<2); NI>0; cfo_yield>0; observed effective_tax_rate in [0, 15%]; rev_yoy ≥5%; P/E ≤25 | FCF yld ≥5%; shares ≤8%; not financing-fragile; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: retained_earnings EDGAR (14%; US 37%, non-US 1%); effective_tax_rate EDGAR (5%; US 14.5%) = book tax / pretax; NI, cfo_yield master → 21 firers, all US, median $817m. FMP `ratios-ttm` effective tax exists universe-wide but is not coalesced into this leg (FMP map §2 note).
- Spirit gaps: a negative retained-earnings balance is a BOOK deficit, not a tax-loss bank (goodwill impairments, SBC, non-deductible charges create deficits with no NOL) — while the engine already holds the direct measure (`nol_usd`, 2,861 names) and never uses it here; §382 unmodelled; the observed BOOK ETR excludes the strongest cases (a company that has released its valuation allowance books a full tax charge while paying no cash tax — the shield is then visible only in cash tax, i.e. `xr_cash_tax_advantage`'s lens), and admits low-tax-jurisdiction or SBC-deduction cases that are not shields.
- Methodology: `_re_is_loss30` is a weak discriminator (P/B<2 passes almost everyone); rev_yoy ≥5% is a growth wish, not a shield test; `_etr30 >= 0` excludes negative rates (tax benefits) — acceptable; the archetype is US-only by data although the thesis is global (IFRS filers also carry unrecognised tax losses).
- FMP augmentations: wired `fq_cash_tax_rate` / `fq_book_tax_rate_fy` (fmp_quarterly, global) — cash tax is what a shield saves; `balance-sheet-statement.retainedEarnings` + `income-statement.incomeTaxExpense`/`incomeBeforeTax` (global, H) give the deficit and book rate universe-wide; nol_usd (EDGAR) as the direct corroboration for US names.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] corroborate the deficit with `nol_usd` ≥ 0.5×mcap (gross) where present; deficit-only members become watch. 2 [method] add `fq_cash_tax_rate ≤ 0.15` as an alternative to (or requirement alongside) the book ETR leg — the shield is a cash fact. 3 [data] extend the deficit + low-rate test globally from FMP balance sheet / income statement (21 US-only firers today). 4 [method] add `data_quality_flag == 0`.

### arch_xr_oneoff_loss_mask  (line 4963)
- Spirit: Wolf Archetype 9 Turnaround ("significant non-recurring charges in history — impairments, writedowns … low current valuation"); Master Reference I-C (19) "Temporarily Worse Than It Looks"; Pernas UPWK/META "cyclical weakness mistaken for structural AI impairment". Engine XR34 = the inverse of `earnings_oneoff_flag`.
- As coded: NI<0; EBITDA margin >15%; gross margin >15%; FCF yld >3%; 0 < EV/EBITDA ≤9; nde ≤3 | net cash; fx-coherent. No `_not_melting` (by design: FCF>3% stands in), no share guard, no DQ gate. No lenses.
- Lineage: all master-level (Yahoo primary, FMP fill; global) → 85 firers (47 non-US), median $458m. `da_ttm` (55%) is available but unused.
- Spirit gaps: the one-off is never identified. NI<0 with a 15% EBITDA margin and positive FCF also describes (a) a capital-intensive business whose D&A is a REAL cost with capex temporarily below D&A (shipping, telecom, rentals — a harvest, not a mask), (b) an interest-eaten name at nde ≈3, (c) minority-interest leakage, and (d) a business that has lost money for three years. No recurrence test (loss in only the latest period), no charge tell (impairment, restructuring, discontinued ops), no evidence that pre-charge EBIT is positive.
- Methodology: EV/EBITDA is "D&A-immune" but not impairment-immune in some provider builds (EBITDA = EBIT + D&A carries the impairment when it sits in EBIT) — the cheapness lens can be the very number the charge depressed; the missing shares_yoy guard lets a diluting name pass; absent DQ gate.
- FMP augmentations: wired `tc_*` (fmp_throughcycle, global, 8 FY): loss years count / `tc_min_opm` — a mask is a FIRST loss after profitable years; wired fmp_quarterly `fq_cfo`, `fq_ni_cf`, D&A → non-cash charge = CFO − NI − D&A as the observable charge; `fq_disc_ops_share` for discontinued drags; FMP `cash-flow-statement.otherNonCashItems` (global, H) as the standardized write-down proxy.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] require the charge to be observable: (CFO − NI − D&A) ≥ 0.5×|NI| or otherNonCashItems ≥ |NI| or fq_disc_ops_share>0. 2 [spirit] recurrence: NI>0 in ≥2 of the prior 3 FYs (tc) — a first loss, not a chronic one. 3 [method] pre-charge EBIT test with audited D&A: ebitda_margin − da_ttm/revenue ≥5pp, so real depreciation is not read as a mask. 4 [method] add `~(shares_yoy > 0.05)` and `data_quality_flag == 0`.

### arch_xr_owned_realestate_value  (line 5233)
- Spirit: Compendium I-6 Scrapyard "property unrevalued for decades (J D Wetherspoon 25+ years, Marston's 0.37× net assets)"; Wolf Archetype 4 (appraised real estate as the floor); Liger asset-backed caveat "property may take years to sell, be operationally essential, or realise less than asking". Engine XR44 / gap G3.
- As coded: ppe_gross>0 & accumulated depreciation>0; accum/gross ≥0.5; ppe_gross/mcap ≥0.75; ppe_net/assets ≥25%; owns (operating-lease ROU / ppe_net ≤0.5, or ROU missing); P/B<2 | EV/S ≤2; op margin>0 | FCF>0; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: PropertyPlantAndEquipmentGross, AccumulatedDepreciation…PPE, OperatingLeaseRightOfUseAsset from EDGAR (10–11% cov; US 26–30%, non-US 0.7%) → US-only (86 of 86 firers, median $1.6bn). ppe_gross (USD) / mcap (USD) coherent for 10-K filers. FMP does not expose gross PP&E (map §6).
- Spirit gaps: the tag is ALL PP&E — machinery, vehicles, fixtures — not land and buildings; a 50%-depreciated machinery base is old plant, not hidden real estate (that is `arch_overdepreciated_assets`' thesis, which this duplicates for industrials). No land share, no acquisition vintage (the source's edge is decades-old cost), no location/appraisal proxy. "Owner" is tested only against operating-lease ROU (finance leases sit inside PP&E).
- Methodology: P/B<2 is generous for "hidden value" — at P/B 1.9 the market already pays double book; EV/S ≤2 has nothing to do with property; ROU-missing counts as owner (permissive by construction); no DQ gate despite the leg resting on balance-sheet identities.
- FMP augmentations: none for gross PP&E / land (map §6). `balance-sheet-statement.propertyPlantEquipmentNet` (global, H) covers only the ppe_net/assets leg for non-US — insufficient alone. EDGAR tags `Land`, `LandAndLandImprovements`, `BuildingsAndImprovementsGross` are the data fix (producer-side, US).
- Suggestions: 1 [data] add Land / Buildings gross tags to `edgar_universe_extract` and gate on (land + buildings)/ppe_gross ≥40% or land/mcap ≥15% — property, not plant. 2 [method] tighten cheapness to P/B<1.2 or (mcap − net cash)/ppe_gross ≤1.0 (the market pays less than original cost). 3 [method] drop the EV/S branch and add `data_quality_flag == 0`. 4 [spirit] pair with `arch_xr_value_unlock` language (sale-leaseback, "real estate value") as the confirmed tier — already wired in `_vu_forensic`; surface this archetype as watch until then.

### arch_xr_paydown_yield  (line 4280)
- Spirit: Wolf/Oak Archetype 8 "Cash-Flow Deleveraging & Yield Plays" — ΔEquity = ΔAssets − ΔNetDebt + buyback accretion; FCF yield >10%; ND/EBITDA 1.5–3× and trending down; falling interest expense; reject if "paydown funded by equity issuance", "faked FCF via receivable build", "heavy maintenance capex", "debtholders in control". Master Reference VII Akre/AMT (debt panic + visible FCF inflection). Engine XR8 "Weschler transfer".
- As coded: −financing_cf_ttm / market_cap (local/local) ≥10%; nde 2–90; EBITDA>0; EBITDA yoy ≥ −5% | ebitda_inflection; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: financing_cf_ttm EDGAR + Yahoo statement gap-fill (26%; US 47%, non-US 14%); nde master (99 when EBITDA ≤0). 833 firers (557 non-US), median $390m, 182 sub-$50m — the second-largest member set in the slice.
- Spirit gaps: a financing OUTFLOW is not a paydown: it nets dividends, buybacks, debt repayment, share/debt issuance and — for IFRS filers — lease principal repayments (a retailer's rent reads as "deleveraging"); a refinancing nets to zero; nothing requires net debt to actually fall (matrix PROXY: "require net debt actually falling (fq)" — open), nothing requires the paydown to be FCF-funded (Wolf: FCF>10%; reject equity- or cash-drawdown-funded); no share-count guard; nde upper bound 90 admits solvency-is-the-thesis names (8–10×), against the sibling XR47's own 4× cap.
- Methodology: the 10% is a flow/mcap ratio dominated by low mcap — a distressed stub with a collapsed cap "yields" 10% on a token repayment; EBITDA yoy ≥ −5% is the only earnings-stability leg. Sibling `arch_xr_verified_deleveraging` already implements the right measurement; this archetype is its proxy and fires 12× as often.
- FMP augmentations: wired `fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets` ≤ −5% and `fq_netdebt_decline_months` ≥9 (fmp_quarterly, global) = the matrix fix; FMP `cash-flow-statement` (global, H) `debtRepayment`, `netDebtIssuance`, `commonDividendsPaid`, `commonStockRepurchased` to split the transfer by channel and exclude lease principal where the filer reports it separately.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] require net debt actually falling: `fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets ≤ −0.03` where observed (matrix PROXY, open) and treat financing-outflow-only as watch. 2 [spirit] operations-funded: FCF>0 and cfo_yield ≥ 0.5 × paydown yield, plus `~(shares_yoy > 0.02)`. 3 [method] cap nde ≤6 (beyond that solvency, not transfer, is the thesis) and floor mcap or revenue (≥$10m) against the low-cap ratio artifact. 4 [data] split the financing line by channel from FMP cash-flow (debt vs dividends vs buybacks) so "paydown yield" is a debt number.

### arch_xr_peer_margin_gap  (line 5351)
- Spirit: A1 gap G9 "comparable-normalised forensic lens"; Wolf Archetype 9 Turnaround ("shedding low-margin divisions, cutting SG&A … release of the first improved earnings report"; Zoomd SG&A cuts); Compendium I-4 Tillinghast profitability normalization. Engine XR46 / N5.
- As coded: revenue ≥$20m; peer norm = industry median op margin where ≥20 operating peers (margin in [−50%, 60%], revenue ≥$20m) else sector median, norm >5%; gap ≥5pp; op margin ≥ −5%; turn (op-margin delta>0 | GM delta>0 | buyback_yield>0 | shares_yoy < −1% | insider ownership ≥10%); cheap (EV/S ≤2 | EV/EBITDA ≤10 | FCF yld ≥4%); 3y revenue CAGR not < −5%; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: op_margin / deltas master (global); industry/sector labels from profile; the median is computed across the whole universe (all countries and sizes) — a Japanese micro-cap is normalised against US software peers. 1,755 firers (1,437 non-US, 552 sub-$50m), median $143m — the loosest gate in the slice.
- Spirit gaps: the source's "turn" is self-help EVIDENCE (new management, disposal, cost cuts, first improved print); the code's turn OR contains two non-turns — a static insider-ownership level and any positive buyback_yield — which alone explain the count. "Structurally inferior vs latent" is never adjudicated with the company's OWN history: a margin it earned before (`tc_med_opm`, 8 FY, global, wired) is the strongest evidence that the gap is latent, and it is unused.
- Methodology: peer medians mix regions/sizes and include the firer; a 5pp gap against a >5% median is often within normal dispersion for the industry (no dispersion frame — e.g. gap vs the industry IQR); the ≥20-peer floor is good; secular-decline guard is 3y CAGR only.
- FMP augmentations: `peers-bulk` (1 call; A2 C2 open, matrix PROXY) for company-specific peer sets; wired `tc_med_opm` for the own-history norm; wired `fqx_ebit_ttm_g` >0 as the observed TTM turn; `insider-trading/statistics` (US, H) for insider BUYING (a turn signal) instead of ownership level; `key-executives` (global, S) / `sec-filings-8k` item 5.02 (US) for a management change.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] remove `insider ≥0.10` and bare `buyback_yield>0` from the turn OR; require an observed margin turn (op-margin delta ≥1pp or fqx_ebit_ttm_g>0 or `fq_gm_inflection_flag`) or a dated management change (A1 §b / A2 open). 2 [spirit] add an own-history lens: `tc_med_opm − op_margin ≥5pp` as an alternative norm (and the core when both peer and own gaps agree). 3 [method] compute the industry median within region (or use peers-bulk sets) and express the gap in units of industry IQR (A2 C2 open). 4 [method] publish core/watch: today's rule = watch.

### arch_xr_pre_scale_margin  (line 4714)
- Spirit: Compendium II-B (high, stable/expanding gross margin; "$5–10m revenue businesses that are slightly profitable"); Master Reference VI red flag "revenue growth without margin improvement"; Pernas "Fallen platform with improving motor — improving unit economics/margins and a plausible route to operating leverage" (XMTR marketplace GM 30.8→33.5%); Liger "incremental margins above historical averages". Engine XR26.
- As coded: not financing-fragile; GM ≥40%; op margin ≤12%; GM − OM ≥30pp; scaling (annual inc. EBITDA margin ≥30% & > EBITDA margin | op-margin delta >2pp | EBITDA-margin delta in (2pp, 30pp]); rev_yoy 15–150%; EV/GP ≤8 | EV/S ≤4; revenue ≥$15m; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: gross/op margin and deltas master (global); incremental_ebitda_margin derived (34%); ev_gross_profit master. 167 firers (100 non-US), median $234m.
- Spirit gaps: the gap is ASSUMED to be a fixed base being absorbed; the same gap is SG&A bloat, sales-and-marketing-bought growth (CAC) or expensed R&D (FMP map note: R&D/revenue tells them apart). The direct test — opex growing slower than revenue — is not made; no headcount productivity (revenue/employee) leg; the transcript leg (matrix LEG) is open.
- Methodology: op margin ≤12% has no lower bound (a −60% margin "pre-scale" burner passes if not financing-fragile; sibling `expensed_growth_value` floors at −15%); a +2pp EBITDA-margin delta is thin proof of scaling against a 30pp gap; annual incremental margin has no ≤1.0 domain (see leverage_detonation).
- FMP augmentations: `income-statement` (global, H) sellingGeneralAndAdministrativeExpenses + researchAndDevelopmentExpenses vs revenue growth (or `financial-growth` `sgaexpensesGrowth`, `rdexpenseGrowth`, A2 A.1 N) → opex growth < revenue growth by ≥5pp = fixed base absorption observed; `employee-count` (US-only, H; A2 A.1 N) → revenue/employee rising; `earning-call-transcript` "operating leverage"/"scale" (A2 A.2 N, matrix LEG open).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] add the direct test: TTM (SG&A + R&D) growth ≤ revenue growth − 5pp from fq/FMP statements (global) as core; margin-delta-only as watch. 2 [method] floor op margin at ≥ −15% (match expensed_growth_value) and apply the ≤1.0 domain to the annual incremental margin. 3 [data] revenue-per-employee trend (US) as exceptional tier (A2 A.1 open). 4 [data] transcript leg (A2 open).

### arch_xr_quality_crisis  (line 4203)
- Spirit: Templeton "maximum pessimism" (Compendium II-D/E), Tillinghast Four Elements (profitability sustained ~10 years, single-digit P/E for average businesses), Yartseva screener (positive operating profitability, high FCF/P, price near 12-month low), Master Reference I-E Surviving Thing. Engine XR4.
- As coded: quality (n_yrs_positive_fcf ≥4 | equity_cagr_5y ≥10% | roic_lindy ≥12% | (oe_avg>0 & ni_avg>0) | NI streak ≥8q | rev streak ≥8q); crisis (pct_off_52w ≤ −40% | 5y-range position ≤0.30 | ts dd52 ≤ −40% | ts_dist_hi260 ≤0.6); cheap (P/B<1.2 | P/E ≤10 | FCF yld ≥10%); EBITDA margin > −5%; shares ≤5%; not melting. Spirited: lenses tc_min_opm (+), ts_dist_hi260 (−), fq_rev_growth (+), roic_lindy (+).
- Lineage: quality legs are EDGAR-derived (US-only: oe/ni_avg 10–12%, streaks 12%, roic_lindy) or Yahoo (equity_cagr_5y, n_yrs_positive_fcf with FMP fill); crisis from ts panel (global) with quote-time fallbacks; cheap from master. 3,676 firers (2,866 non-US, 1,223 sub-$50m), median $138m — a screen, not an XR gate; the spirit score carries the discrimination.
- Spirit gaps (A1 §c / A2 B.30, still open): ≥2 independent multi-year lenses; `oe_avg>0 & ni_avg>0` is merely "profitable on average"; crisis should be a drawdown from a former high — the 5y-range ≤0.30 leg fires on a stock that has been flat-low for five years (no crisis); the "unusual for this name" drawdown (prior 5y max drawdown shallower) is not modelled; TTM revenue ≥0 not required.
- Methodology: cheap OR with P/B<1.2 alone is a value screen (a low-ROE industrial at 1.1× book is not "quality at a crisis price"); for non-US members the quality leg that fires is almost always the Yahoo equity_cagr or FCF-years count (the weakest lenses), so geography changes the meaning of "quality"; 5y-range and pct_off_52w are snapshot lenses (A2 §C fragile).
- FMP augmentations (wired): `tc_min_opm` ≥0 / `tc_fcf_years` ≥7 (8 FY, global) as the global multi-year quality lens the non-US members lack; `ts_dist_hi260` ≤0.5 as the crisis lens; `ts_maxdd_5y` for the unusual-drawdown test (A2 B.30); `financial-growth` five/tenY per-share growth (A2 A.1 N, global) for the "audited compounding" leg globally.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] require ≥2 quality lenses with `tc_min_opm ≥0` or `tc_fcf_years ≥6` eligible as one (A1 §c open) — gives non-US names a real quality bar. 2 [method] crisis = drawdown from a former high only (`ts_dist_hi260 ≤0.6` or dd52 ≤ −40%); drop the bare 5y-range leg. 3 [spirit] "unusual for this name": prior-5y max drawdown shallower than −30% (A2 B.30 open). 4 [method] split core/watch — 3,676 firers cannot be an XR count.

### arch_xr_reusable_assembler  (line 4649)
- Spirit: Compendium Part III Assembly Theory (reuse of a built substrate) applied to the Baron terminal phenotype; Master Reference IV Flyover blueprint (durable moat, high ROIC >15%, strong FCF), Harpin/Den Fujita capital-light recurring models. Engine XR24.
- As coded: not financing-fragile; reuse (annual inc. EBITDA margin ≥30% & > EBITDA margin | TTM fqx inc. EBIT margin ≥30% & > op margin); asset-lite (capex_intensity ≤6% | goodwill+intangibles ≥25% of assets); self-funded (financing_cf_ttm ≤0 **or missing**); EBITDA margin ≥15%; durable (rev streak ≥6q | 3y CAGR ≥12% | rev_yoy ≥12%); nde ≤2 | net cash; EV/S-to-growth ≤0.40 | EV/EBIT ≤22; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: incremental margins (derived 34% / fqx 16k, global); capex_intensity, goodwill_intangibles_pct_assets master; financing_cf_ttm 26% (US 47%, non-US 14%); rev_yoy_streak_q US-only; evsg/ev_ebit master. 463 firers (301 non-US), median $1.6bn.
- Spirit gaps: the substrate's signature is RETURN on capital (Flyover ROIC >15%) — no ROIC/ROIIC leg at all; "recurring" is unmeasured; the goodwill/intangibles branch of asset-lite flags SERIAL ACQUIRERS (bought substrate, which the engine elsewhere calls the amortization-mask class) — the opposite of organic reuse; durability accepts a single rev_yoy ≥12% print.
- Methodology: `_selffund_x24` passes when financing_cf_ttm is MISSING — 74% of the universe, so for most non-US members the self-funded leg is vacuous; inc margin > average margin is a one-year comparison (A2 §C); EV/EBIT ≤22 is a growth-adjusted price only if growth is present, which the OR-durability does not ensure.
- FMP augmentations: wired `fqx_roic_ttm` (global) ≥15% as the returns leg; wired `tc_min_opm` ≥10% / `tc_years ≥5` for durability; FMP `cash-flow-statement.netCashUsedProvidedByFinancingActivities` (global, H; fmp_quarterly panel holds the CF flows) to make self-funding observed (FMP map gap #1 open); `financial-growth` fiveYRevenueGrowthPerShare (A2 A.1 N, global) for duration; `employee-count` (US) revenue/employee.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] make self-funded observed: fill financing_cf from fq/FMP cash-flow and remove the `.isna()` pass-through (FMP map #1 open). 2 [spirit] add a returns leg: `fqx_roic_ttm ≥0.15` or `roic_lindy ≥0.15` — reuse must show in ROIC. 3 [method] restrict the intangible-substrate branch to non-acquisitive names (goodwill growth ≤ revenue growth, shares flat) or drop it. 4 [data] duration via financial-growth 5y per-share revenue growth ≥ 10% (A2 open).

### arch_xr_segment_justifies_whole  (line 5058)
- Spirit: Compendium II-A technique (9) sum-of-the-parts / holdco discount collapse; Master Reference III stub stocks / SOTP; Wolf Archetype 5 (discount-on-discount; catalysts: asset sale, tender, wind-up). Failure mode named: "the discount is a governance trap". Engine XR38.
- As coded: revenue ≥$20m; segment_count ≥2; seg_best_ebit_usd>0 & enterprise_value_usd>0; best segment EBIT × 12 ≥ EV; seg_total_ebit > seg_best (other segments net-positive); fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: `segment_valuation.csv` (576 names with reconciled USD segment EBIT = segment revenue × segment op margin from EDGAR OperatingIncomeLoss; nulled unless segment-total revenue is within 0.5–2.0× consolidated) → US-only (72 of 73 firers, median $1.3bn); segment_count from a different producer (edgar_segment_signals). Annual 10-K segment data vs a live EV.
- Spirit gaps: segment EBIT is BEFORE unallocated corporate cost (the classic SOTP error — corporate overhead must be capitalised and deducted), so "best segment alone covers EV" overstates by the corporate line; 12× is not conservative for a cyclical or peak-margin segment; no catalyst or tape leg (the source's failure mode); no minority-interest/pension claims senior to the equity in the EV bridge beyond the provider EV.
- Methodology: `seg_total > seg_best` guards only the aggregate of the others (one big positive segment can hide a burning third); no DQ gate; segment fiscal year may be 12–18 months stale against the current EV; the 0.5–2.0× reconciliation band still allows a 2× mis-scale in the USD EBIT.
- FMP augmentations: none for segment EBIT (map §11). Wired `value_unlock` machinery already uses this archetype in `_vu_forensic` (SOTP language + numbers) — that is the right confirmed tier. Consolidated operating income (master `op_margin × revenue`) is available to test overhead.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] deduct corporate overhead: require consolidated EBIT ≥ 0.7 × seg_total_ebit (overhead ≤30% of segment profit) or use best_ebit − (seg_total − consolidated EBIT). 2 [method] margin-quality multiple: 10× where the best segment's op margin <10% or its revenue fell YoY; 12× otherwise. 3 [spirit] core = members that also carry unlock language / 13D (`value_unlock_confirmed`), bare SOTP = watch. 4 [method] tighten the reconciliation band to 0.8–1.25× for the `_usd` figures and add `data_quality_flag == 0` plus a ≤15-month segment-year freshness check.

### arch_xr_stake_fv_gap  (line 5394)
- Spirit: Compendium I-6 Scrapyard (Keisei/Oriental Land: the stake's quoted value exceeds the parent's own cap); ASC 323 requires the fair value of an equity-method stake to be disclosed when a quoted price exists — the company itself publishes the mismark. Engine XR44 "the sharpest LATENT tell".
- As coded: em_carry>0; em_fair_value ≥1.3×carry; (FV − carry)/market_cap_usd ≥10%; `_stake_cheap` (EV/EBITDA ≤12 | P/B<2 | FCF yld ≥3% | EV/S ≤3); data_quality_flag==0; fx-coherent; not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: EquityMethodInvestmentsFairValueDisclosure (188 observed, 49 positive gaps) and EquityMethodInvestments from the EDGAR cache (USD) → US-only; 4 firers (median $195m). Well built and correctly gated; tiny by disclosure, not by design.
- Spirit gaps: the gap is pre-tax (realisation is taxed) and no catalyst is required; the disclosed FV is annual and can be stale against the investee's current quote; nothing checks whether the consolidated price already reflects the stake (P/B<2 includes the stake at carry, so a P/B of 1.9 may already price it).
- Methodology: 1.3× and 10% are reasonable; `_stake_cheap` includes the EV/EBITDA branch that may carry equity income (see lookthrough_earner); no other issues.
- FMP augmentations: none (map §6). Producer-side: the older us-gaap element `EquityMethodInvestmentQuotedMarketValue` (used by many filers before the FairValueDisclosure tag) is not in EM_FV_CONCEPTS — worth verifying in the taxonomy; it would raise the 188-name base.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] add `EquityMethodInvestmentQuotedMarketValue` (and any `…AggregateFairValue` variant) to EM_FV_CONCEPTS after checking the taxonomy — the only lever on 4 firers. 2 [method] tax-effect the gap (×~0.79) and gate on ≥10% net, or raise the gross bar to 15%. 3 [spirit] rank by gap/mcap and keep `value_unlock_confirmed` as the catalyst tier (already wired). 4 (none) — otherwise well built.

### arch_xr_triple_floor  (line 4150)
- Spirit: Compendium preface "downside is structurally bounded (asset value … valuation floor) while the upside is open"; Wolf Archetype 4 Asset-Backed Floor (Oak's "hard floor (cash + property)") crossed with Archetype 8's PAID yield ("buyback or dividend ≥5%") and the MicroCap net-cash/growth footprint (Compendium II-B). Engine XR2: net cash + real earnings + a paid dividend while revenue still grows.
- As coded: net_cash_pct (clipped ±2) ≥40% of mcap; NI_ttm>0 | ni_avg>0; dividend_yield ≥3%; rev_yoy ≥8%; 0<P/B<1.5; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. No DQ gate. No lenses (spirit_spec.md row proposes net cash / earnings yield / dividend / fq_rev_growth / P/B lenses; not wired).
- Lineage: net_cash_pct_mcap master (Yahoo primary, FMP fill, 76%); ni_avg EDGAR 5-yr with the fmp_st_ni_avg bridge fill — 116 of 139 members carry fmp_filled_ni_avg=1 (fill flag), so the Graham-average leg IS global here; dividend_yield provider TTM (blanked >40%; FX-corruptible, A2 §C open; 2 members FMP-filled); rev_yoy single year. 139 firers, **2 US / 137 non-US** (Japan 39, Korea 25, HK 10, China 8, Taiwan 8), median mcap $100m, 36 sub-$50m; 48 also fire neg_ev_growth, 22 clean_net_net; 4 members carry data_quality_flag=1.
- Spirit gaps: the three floors are not independent — net cash ≥40% of mcap and P/B<1.5 are the same balance sheet (the cash IS the book), and dividend and NI are the same P&L; the dividend floor is a TTM yield (14 members ≥8%: special/one-off payouts read as a floor) with no persistence or cover test (matrix DIV LEG "paid in each of the last 3 FYs", A2 §C open); ni_avg>0 admits a currently loss-making name (6 members) — a floor should be current earnings; growth is one print (fq_rev_yoy_streak_m median 7.5 months among members).
- Methodology: the member set is the same Asian small-cap net-cash cohort as neg_ev_growth, where trapped/unverifiable cash is the known failure mode — but here the paid dividend is a genuine cash-existence test, the archetype's real strength; no DQ gate although the floor rests on balance-sheet identities; no payout-cover leg (dividend_yield × P/E median 0.48, but unbounded); the ±2 clip on net cash is harmless.
- FMP augmentations: `dividends` history (global, H; A2 A.1 U, matrix DIV open) → paid in ≥3 consecutive FYs, no cut, yield recomputed in listing currency; wired `evt_div_raise_streak` / `evt_div_cut_2y` (fmp_events) as the persistence lens; wired fq net-debt path (`fq_netdebt_decline_months`) → net cash non-decreasing across ≥3 balance sheets (real cash accumulates); `income-statement.interestIncome` (global, H) interest on cash ≥0.5% as an existence corroboration; wired `fq_rev_yoy_streak_m` for growth persistence.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] dividend floor = persistence + cover: ≥3 FY paid and no cut (`dividends` / `evt_div_cut_2y==0`) and dividend ≤ NI (payout ≤100%); TTM-yield-only members become watch (matrix DIV open). 2 [spirit] earnings floor must be current: NI_ttm>0 required; ni_avg>0 as an additional (exceptional) lens, not a substitute. 3 [method] add `data_quality_flag==0` and the cash-accumulation test (fq net cash not falling over ≥3 snapshots, or interest income ≥0.5% of cash). 4 [spirit] growth persistence: `fq_rev_yoy_streak_m ≥12` or rev_yoy ≥8% in 2 of the last 3 FY (tc) — one 8% print is not "still grows".

### arch_xr_value_unlock  (line 5447)
- Spirit: Master Reference I-G "Discount + catalyst = value realisation … asset sale, tender, buyback, liquidation … activist pressure, spin"; VIII scanner categories ("exploring strategic alternatives", 13D filings, proxy fights, self-tenders); Wolf Archetype 9 "new management or strategic review announced" and Archetype 5 holdco-discount catalysts. Engine XR46: cheap + fresh distinctive language or a structured event in motion.
- As coded: `_vu_cheap` (0.05<P/B<1 | NCAV ≥100% | net cash ≥30% | 0<EV/EBITDA ≤6 | forensic_hidden_pct ≥20%); (`_vu_language`: unlock_days_ago ≤400 & (distinctive regex | ≥2 distinct phrases)) | `_vu_event` (spin/tender/merger/going-private flag | SC 13D ≤180d & mcap<$2bn); data_quality_flag==0; fx-coherent; not melting. Ranked by value_unlock_score; `value_unlock_confirmed` = member & `_vu_forensic`. Lens: value_unlock_score (existing composite).
- Lineage: language from `compute_value_unlock.py` — EDGAR EFTS full-text, 27 exact phrases, forms 8-K/DEF 14A/SC 13D/10-K/10-Q/6-K, window from 2025-01-01, **≤300 filings per phrase**, ticker parsed from every entity in `display_names`; event flags from `edgar_event_signals.py` (US CIKs, **730-day** window); 13D date from fmp_events (`SC 13D` and `SC 13D/A`, header: "activist OR strategic"). 270 firers (160 US / 110 non-US reached via 6-K/20-F: China 17, UK 6, Greece 5), median $163m, 89 sub-$50m; 101 confirmed; the matrix count was 153 before the 13D leg. 156 members have NI<0; 57 have rev_yoy < −10%.
- Spirit gaps: the source's catalyst is a process on a business worth more than its price; the modal micro-cap use of "exploring strategic alternatives" is a failed biotech / cash shell seeking a reverse merger, and P/B<1 with NI<0 (58% of members) is exactly that population — there is no operating-business or earnings leg, so the "cheap" gate is book-cheap, not discount-to-value; the acquirer side of a deal carries the same phrases ("entered into a definitive agreement", "as its financial advisor") and EFTS display_names list every entity on a filing, so a BUYER (or a listed 13D filer) is tagged as unlocking value.
- Methodology: `unlock_days_ago` is computed against a hard-coded scrape date (2026-09-14) and is not re-aged in the engine (unlike `_evt_age_days`, which uses the panel as-of), so the ≤400-day freshness drifts with the CSV's age; the 300-hit cap makes the boilerplate phrases ("enhance/maximize shareholder value") an arbitrary sample, so the ≥2-phrase branch is not universe-complete even for US; EDGAR event flags use 730 days while the fmp_events fallback in special_situation uses 270 — a DEFM14A/tender 18–24 months ago on a still-listed name is a BROKEN deal, not a catalyst; the 13D leg counts amendments (13D/A — often the exit) and CO/HC strategic holders (A2 B.27 open); "return of capital to shareholders" is REIT/MLP tax-characterisation boilerplate yet sits in the distinctive regex.
- FMP augmentations: `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US, H; A2 A.3 U, B.27 open) → first 13D ≤12m, percentOfClass ≥5, type IA/IN not CO/HC; wired `evt_ma_target_date` (fmp_events feed) to require the name be the TARGET when the hit is deal-side; `sec-filings-8k` items 1.01/2.01/5.02 (US) for a dated 8-K catalyst (A1 §213 c, open); `earning-call-transcript` (targeted, A2 A.2 N) as the only non-US language route; `holder-performance-summary` for the activist's record (A2 B.27 exceptional).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] require an operating business under the discount: revenue ≥$10m and (EBITDA>0 | NCAV ≥100% | forensic_hidden_pct ≥20%); loss-making sub-book names without a hard asset floor → biotech_deep_value / watch. 2 [method] re-age freshness in the engine from `unlock_latest_date` against the panel as-of; cut the EDGAR event window to ≤270d (align with special_situation); raise the EFTS per-phrase cap or drop the two "shareholder value" phrases from the ≥2-phrase count. 3 [method] side-of-deal: for "definitive agreement"/"financial advisor" hits require `evt_ma_target_date` or merger/tender flag on the same name; exclude "return of capital" from the distinctive set unless a tender/buyback flag is present. 4 [data] 13D from `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` with type IA/IN and first filing ≤12m as the core activist leg; 13D/A-only = watch (A2 B.27 open).

### arch_xr_verified_deleveraging  (line 5295)
- Spirit: Wolf/Oak Archetype 8 "Cash-Flow Deleveraging & Yield" — FCF yield >10%, ND/EBITDA 1.5–3× trending down, falling interest expense, static/falling share count; reject "debtholders in control", "heavy maintenance capex", "faked FCF via receivable build", "paydown funded by equity issuance"; Pernas ACH "highly leveraged home-health turnaround at very low FCF multiple" (+70%); Master Reference VII Akre/AMT. Engine XR47 = the verified version of XR8 paydown_yield.
- As coded: fq_deleveraging_flag==1 (net debt down across ≥9 months of consecutive balance sheets, by ≥5% of assets, fq_shares_yoy ≤2%, latest CFO>0, fq_disc_ops_share ≤20%); nde 1.5–4.0; 0<EV/EBITDA ≤8; EBITDA>0; not melting. No `_fx_coherent` (all legs are ratios or same-currency panel — acceptable). No lenses (spirit_spec.md proposes decline months, change pct, EV/EBITDA, FCF yield; not wired).
- Lineage: `fmp_quarterly.py` net-debt path = FMP `totalDebt − cashAndShortTermInvestments` over the last ≤5 balance sheets (contiguous; half-yearly filers need 2 declines = 12 months); `fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets` = (nd[0] − nd[−1])/assets over the WHOLE window, not the decline run; nde and EV/EBITDA from the master (provider debt definition). 70 firers (7 US / 63 non-US: Japan 8, France 5, UK 5, Canada 4, HK 4), median $380m, 17 sub-$50m; 27 also fire paydown_yield, 29 oak_deleveraging. Median fcf_yield 23%, decline 12 months, change −9% of assets.
- Spirit gaps: Wolf's FCF >10% is not gated (8 members below, 1 negative) and neither is falling interest expense (`int_exp` is parsed in the panel and `fq_interest_cover` emitted; master interest_coverage covers 9% of members); no revenue guard — 17 of 70 members have rev_yoy < −5% and 6 carry fq_wc_release_flag, i.e. deleveraging by liquidating working capital in a shrinking business (the engine's own wc_release caveat); "operations-funded" is only CFO>0 in the latest period, not CFO−capex ≥ the paydown — an asset sale booked in investing CF (not discontinued ops) or a sale-leaseback (Pernas: "sale-leaseback deleveraging") funds the whole decline and passes.
- Methodology: two debt definitions — the fq path uses FMP totalDebt (IFRS-16 lease liabilities included inconsistently across filers) while the 1.5–4× band uses the master nde, so the "path" and the "level" can disagree; the ≥5%-of-assets change is measured over up to 5 snapshots, so a large decline four periods ago plus two token declines satisfies both legs; disposal guard sees only discontinued ops; SBC-inflated CFO not excluded (`fq_sbc_to_cfo`, 27% cov). Otherwise the best-built deleveraging gate in the engine (A2 §671 "no material gain" stands for data, not for logic).
- FMP augmentations (wired panel): Σ(`fq_cfo` − |`fq_capex`|) vs the net-debt decline for a quantified operations-funded test; `int_exp` YoY (interest expense falling, Wolf leg); `fq_interest_cover ≥2` (debtholders not in control); FMP `cash-flow-statement` (global, H) `debtRepayment`, `salesMaturitiesOfInvestments` / `otherInvestingActivites` for asset-sale proceeds; `balance-sheet-statement.capitalLeaseObligations` to put path and level on one debt definition.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] quantify the funding: Σ(fq_cfo − |fq_capex|) over the decline span ≥ 0.7 × net-debt decline, else watch (asset-sale / sale-leaseback funded). 2 [spirit] add rev_yoy ≥ −5% and `fq_wc_release_flag==0` (or CCC not shortening >30 days) — paydown from operations, not liquidation. 3 [method] Wolf legs as core: fcf_yield ≥10% (or ≥5% with interest expense falling YoY) and `fq_interest_cover ≥2`. 4 [method] measure `fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets` over the decline run only (nd[0] − nd[dec]) and compute nde on the same FMP totalDebt the path uses (producer change).

### arch_xr_wc_normalization  (line 4442)
- Spirit: Pernas CELH "Pepsi destocking masking strong underlying sell-through" (+45%) and SWBI "channel inventory normalisation" (+21%); Compendium red flag "receivables/inventory growing faster than sales" — the archetype deliberately inverts it as a ONE-cycle glut with demand and margins intact (the Kohl's-cycle tell). Engine XR15: broken FCF optics, unbroken business.
- As coded: fcf_yield <2%; earnings_yield ≥8%; (cash_conversion <0.7 | ΔDIO ≥15 days | ΔDSO ≥10 days | fq_inv_vs_cogs ≥15pp); EBITDA>0; rev_yoy ≥ −5%; GM delta ≥ −3pp; nde<3; shares ≤5%; fx-coherent; not melting. No DQ gate, no forensic veto. No lenses (spirit_spec.md proposes EY−FCF gap, ΔDIO, ΔDSO, cash_conversion; not wired).
- Lineage: `cash_conversion` = **CFO/EBITDA** (master; the enrich_asymmetry_global comment states the definition) — NOT CFO/NI; fcf_yield / earnings_yield provider (FX-corruptible; the A2 §C note names earnings_yield); fq_dso/dio/inv_vs_cogs from fmp_quarterly (global, date-matched YoY, ≥5%-of-sales base; 91% of members). 350 firers, **4 US / 346 non-US** (Japan 88, Korea 50, India 40, China 22, Taiwan 18), median $114m, 102 sub-$50m; P/E median 8.7, CFO/EBITDA median 0.28.
- Spirit gaps: the source's glut sits in the CUSTOMER's inventory (Pepsi destocking, channel inventory) — the code measures the company's own DIO/DSO, which is the Beneish channel-stuffing tell: 54 members trip fq_beneish_risk_flag, 45 fq_high_accruals_flag, 28 fq_receivables_divergence_flag, 35 fq_inventory_build_flag, and none is a veto; nothing shows the glut is one cycle (CCC normal before, then up) rather than chronic — 198 of 350 members (57%) fire on cash_conversion alone with no observed build; no unwind or demand evidence beyond rev_yoy ≥ −5%.
- Methodology: CFO/EBITDA <0.7 is the NORMAL state of any taxed, interest-paying company (CFO is after tax and interest, EBITDA before), so the outcome proxy does not identify working capital at all — a capex-heavy or high-tax name with no WC build passes; fcf_yield <2% can be a capex surge rather than WC (95 members have cfo_yield ≥ earnings_yield, i.e. the operating cash IS there and the drag is capex); earnings_yield ≥8% concentrates members in Japan/Korea/India/China small caps where receivable build is the known failure mode (the neg_ev_growth cohort); no DQ gate.
- FMP augmentations: wired `fq_chg_wc` (fmp_quarterly `changeInWorkingCapital`, global) → WC absorption as the direct mechanism (FMP_DEEP_EXAMINATION names it for this archetype); `key-metrics.cashConversionCycleTTM` + day metrics (global, S; map nuance #2) for CCC vs its own prior level; `key-metrics.incomeQualityTTM` (CFO/NI, global) as the right outcome proxy (map nuance #1); wired `fq_beneish_risk_flag` / `fq_receivables_divergence_flag` / `fq_high_accruals_flag` as vetoes; wired `fq_ccc_p` for the "normal before" frame.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] replace `cash_conversion <0.7` with a WC-specific test: fq_chg_wc ≤ −0.3×NI, or (cfo_yield < earnings_yield − 3pp AND one of ΔDIO/ΔDSO/inv_vs_cogs observed); cash_conversion-only members → watch. 2 [spirit] one cycle, not chronic: ΔCCC ≥ +20 days off a prior CCC ≤120 days, and prior-year CFO ≥ 0.8×NI (tc / fq_cfo_p) so the conversion loss is new. 3 [spirit] veto the fraud reading: `fq_beneish_risk_flag==0` and `fq_receivables_divergence_flag==0` — a DSO-led glut is the red flag, not the setup; an inventory-led glut with GM intact is the admissible form. 4 [method] add `data_quality_flag==0` and route capex-driven FCF weakness (cfo_yield ≥ earnings_yield) out of this archetype.

## 10-line summary of the slice's most valuable augmentations
1. Global TTM/quarterly substitutes for US-only EDGAR legs — `fq_rev_yoy_streak_m`, `fqx_ebit_ttm_g`, `fqx_inc_ebit_margin`, `fqx_eps_turned` (neg_ev_growth, latent_inflection_floor, leverage_detonation, reusable_assembler): the largest reach gain in the slice, all matrix-open.
2. Make the one-off OBSERVABLE — (fq_cfo − fq_ni − D&A), `otherNonCashItems`, `fq_disc_ops_share`, tc loss-year counts — for latent_bath_floor and oneoff_loss_mask, which today never identify the charge they are named for.
3. Net debt actually falling (`fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets`, `fq_netdebt_decline_months`) as the core of paydown_yield (833 firers on a financing-outflow proxy), and a QUANTIFIED operations-funded test (Σ(fq_cfo − |capex|) ≥ 0.7 × decline, rev_yoy ≥ −5%) for verified_deleveraging.
4. Cash-existence tests for the Asian net-cash cohort (neg_ev_growth, triple_floor): fq net cash non-decreasing over ≥3 snapshots, `interestIncome` ≥0.5% of cash, `data_quality_flag==0`.
5. Dividend persistence and cover from `dividends` history / `evt_div_cut_2y` (harvest_distribution, triple_floor) — the matrix DIV leg, still open.
6. Working-capital mechanism instead of CFO/EBITDA — `fq_chg_wc`, CCC vs its prior level, `incomeQualityTTM`, with the Beneish / receivables-divergence flags as vetoes (wc_normalization: 57% of members fire on a proxy that reads normal taxation as a glut).
7. Own-history frame `tc_med_opm` / `tc_min_opm` / `tc_fcf_years` (peer_margin_gap, quality_crisis, latent_inflection_floor, oneoff_loss_mask) — the strongest evidence that a gap is latent rather than structural, wired but unused.
8. Dated, role-filtered catalysts — a true insider cluster (≥3 buyers / 14 days / $25k, IST crash-quarter PIT) for insider_capitulation; `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` first-13D IA/IN ≤12m, `evt_ma_target_date` side-of-deal and engine-side re-aging for value_unlock; EDGAR event windows cut from 730 to ≤270 days.
9. Segment revenue legs from `fmp_segments` globally as a watch tier (hidden_segment_compounder, margin_mixshift), materiality (share_delta ≥2pp × growth) and corporate-overhead deduction for segment_justifies_whole.
10. Producer-side fixes that cost no endpoint: `EquityMethodInvestmentQuotedMarketValue` (stake_fv_gap), Land/Buildings tags (owned_realestate_value), carrying-concept tiering (investment_remark), tax-effected NOL with a §382 share-count proxy (monetization_trifecta, nol_shield), and core/watch splits for the 1,755- and 3,676-firer screens (peer_margin_gap, quality_crisis).
