# Slice rest_a: Single archetypes, slice A (37)

## Audit 3 — slice rest_a (37 archetypes)

Read-only audit of `archetype_tags.py` (line numbers from `dumps2/`), cross-referenced against `sources/archetype_compendium.txt` (Compendium), `sources/master_archetype_reference.txt` (Master Ref), the Pernas / Liger-Byron / Wolf-Oak practitioner notes, `docs/archetype_upgrade_audit.md` (A1), `docs/fmp_catalog_upgrade_audit.md` (A2), `docs/archetype_endpoint_matrix.csv` and `docs/FMP_ENRICHMENT_MAP.md`. Lineage shorthand: master = `asymmetry_global.csv` (EDGAR/Yahoo/FMP-filled, `fmp_filled_*` = fill flag); `fmp_st_*` = fmp_statements (annual, global); `fq_*` = fmp_quarterly; `fqx_*` = fmp_quarterly_ext; `tc_*` = fmp_throughcycle (8 FY); `ts_*` = ts_snapshot (weekly panel); `bs_*` = base_snapshot; `sent_*` = fmp_sentiment (grades, US-centric); `evt_*` = fmp_events (2,000-row partial run); `usf_*` = SEC-only.

State-of-play notes that change what is "still open": the four layers (`ts_*`, `tc_*`, `fqx_*`, `evt_*`) ARE now merged (archetype_tags.py 264–276), so A2 §0.1's "not wired" is closed; the A1 P0 coiled-base currency bug is closed (`event_study_base.latest_row` converts dvol to USD, NaN when currency unknown); `asset_heavy()` (1152) implements A1 A.4 for fixed_cost/regime. Items below marked "(A1/A2 … open)" were re-checked in the code and remain unimplemented.

---

### arch_analyst_awakening  (line 6624)
- Spirit: Compendium Part I #8 "First-Time Coverage & Earnings Divergence" (fresh initiation / behaviour change not yet priced) + Master Ref VI Andreola discovery cycle (retail → institutional threshold). Engine-native in its "screaming buy, re-rating just begun" form.
- As coded: mcap>0; n_analysts (Yahoo, pew fallback) ≥3; rating present 0<rec≤3.0; conviction = (≥1 of [rec≤2.2, target upside≥25%, n≥8] with ≥half of PRESENT lenses agreeing) OR ≥2 sentiment-turn lenses (buy-share d12≥+0.10, net upgrades≥2, PT revision q≥+5%, initiations≥1); not extended (roc_12m≤+50%, momentum fallback); not freefall (≥−35% off 52w high or momentum/roc_6m>0); live tape. `_tier`: core = ≥1 turn lens or (ts_mrs rising & ts_r13>0); exceptional = evt_pt_lead_flag or ≥2 turn lenses; measured = ts_mrs or sent_buy_share present. Lenses: sent_buy_share_d12, net upgrades, sent_pt_rev_q, ts_mrs−ts_mrs_13ago, evt_pt_rev_90d.
- Lineage: yf_recommendation_mean / analyst_target_upside_pct / n_analysts → Yahoo snapshot (yartseva_db; US-tilted, stale for small caps); sent_* → grades-historical/grades (G-partial, JP covered); evt_pt_* → price-target-news (US/CA only, partial run); roc_12m / momentum_12m / pct_off_52w_high → snapshot (A1 §0.3 noisy vs panel); ts_* panel.
- Spirit gaps: "awakening" = perception CHANGING; the level route survives wherever ts_mrs and sent_buy_share are both absent (the `measured` escape), i.e. exactly the non-US small caps where the level is stalest. Initiation (the Compendium's first signal) is one of four turn lenses, never a path on its own. No pairing with the beats-not-rewarded fact (Compendium #8's "behaviour change high, reaction low"). Clinical biotech not excluded (A2 Z2 open, 90 firers) — binary-event names dominate "target upside".
- Methodology: `_confirm` scores only PRESENT lenses, so a name with rating alone scores 1.0 and passes on one level (the original degeneration A1 §0.6 measured: 2,046/2,102). n≥8 as a conviction lens rewards DEEP coverage, the opposite of a discovery thesis. Upside normalisation (`abs≤5 → fraction, else /100`) silently rescales a genuine +600% upside to 6%. sent_pt_rev_q (last-quarter avg target / last-year avg) rises mechanically with price — it is not price-independent. `_not_extended`/`_not_freefall` read snapshot roc/momentum (A1 §0.3 / A2 Z3 open).
- FMP augmentations: `grades-news` priceWhenPosted (US) → upgrade lead-vs-chase; `analyst-estimates` numAnalystsEps + (epsHigh−epsLow)/|avg| dispersion (global, snapshot, cached) → conviction with low disagreement; `grades-consensus` bulk; `earnings-surprises-bulk` (global) → beat + no reaction pairing; `price-target-news` universe-wide (US/CA).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] make initiations≥1 (or sent_n_analysts_d12≥2) a stand-alone core path and drop the `measured` escape for the pure-level route (A1 A.2 open). 2 [method] replace roc_12m / pct_off_52w_high with ts_r52 / ts_dist_hi52 in `_not_extended`/`_not_freefall` (A2 Z3 open) and remove n≥8 from the conviction stack. 3 [spirit] `& ~_is_clinical_biotech` → biotech_awakening_watch (A2 Z2 open). 4 [data] grades-news lead/chase for US firers (A2 S15 open).

### arch_analyst_rerating_confirmed  (line 6675)
- Spirit: engine-native companion — the awakening CONFIRMED by a fresh 52-week high (O'Neil "N" / Weinstein Stage 2 with analyst conviction). Master Ref II notes the O'Neil-vs-Yartseva contradiction (highs vs lows); this is the "highs" side.
- As coded: same conviction stack as awakening & (is_52w_high or rel_is_52w_high from the lynch tape) & not blown off (roc_12m≤+150%) & live tape. `_tier`: core = ts_dist_hi52≥0.97 or (ts_rs_at_hi & ts_above_ma30); exceptional = ts_dist_hi260≥0.98 & ts_rs_at_hi; elite ts_mrs; measured = ts_dist_hi52 present. Lenses: ts_dist_hi260, ts_mrs, sent_buy_share_d12, sent_pt_rev_q.
- Lineage: is_52w_high / rel_is_52w_high → per-name lynch price enricher (Yahoo, freshness-tracked); ts_* panel (82% INV); conviction inputs as above.
- Spirit gaps: "confirmed" should tie the rating change to the breakout in TIME (upgrade within ~13 weeks of the high; A2 B.8 open). Base freshness (base_depth_12m) is only in the score, so a name mid-uptrend for two years with a standing Buy fires — that is "already re-rated", not "re-rating confirmed". Biotech not excluded (30).
- Methodology: conviction is a LEVEL here too (rec≤2.2 with no change lens required), so a long-standing consensus Buy at a new high qualifies. Fires alongside awakening and the high_52w flags on the same name → one fact counted 2–3× in archetype_count. `_not_blownoff` at +150% is generous for "early". Names without ts_dist_hi52 keep the rel-high-only leg A1 flagged (345/390 passed only through it).
- FMP augmentations: `grades-news` priceWhenPosted (US) → upgrade ≤13w after breakout and evt_pt_chase_flag==0; ts_ma30_slope13 (panel); `earnings-surprises-bulk` → a beat inside the breakout window.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] require ≥1 change lens (sent turn or evt_pt_rev_90d>0) as the awakening core does — a static Buy at a high is not a re-rating event. 2 [method] make it exclusive with awakening (fire one or the other) or demote to a confirmation flag so archetype_count is not inflated. 3 [data] upgrade-timing exceptional tier from grades-news (A2 B.8 open). 4 [spirit] biotech exclusion (A2 Z2 open).

### arch_asleep_at_wheel  (line 5942)
- Spirit: Compendium #8 earnings divergence; Liger-Byron "Earnings, backlog and conservative guidance" (a consistently conservative guidance practice + investors underreact to earnings); Wolf Archetype 3 "guidance vs reality: frequent beats even when guidance is nominal".
- As coded: OR of three branches. (a) beats: earnings_beat_rate≥0.75 & ≥2 of [rate≥0.75, avg surprise>+2%, streak≥3 or surprise inflecting]. (b) EPS: eps_yoy_positive_share≥0.75 & yoy growth streak≥3 & operating & rev_yoy≥0 & revenue≥$20M. (c) forward: fmp_dyn_fwd_underestimate_gap≥0.10 & beat_rate≥0.60 & same validity. `_tier`: core = evt_beats_8q≥7 or (rate==1.0 & surprise≥2%); exceptional = beats≥7 & evt_ignored_beats_2y≥3; measured = evt_beats_8q or beat_rate present. Lenses: evt_beat_share_8q, evt_surprise_4q, avg_earnings_surprise, earnings_beat_streak, evt_ignored_beats_2y.
- Lineage: earnings_beat_rate / avg surprise / streak / inflecting → Yahoo `earnings_history` (yartseva_db, ~4 quarters, unwinsorised), NaN-filled by fmp_earnings_beat_rate (fmp_enrich, 210 names, winsorised ±100%); eps_yoy_positive_share → Yahoo quarterly EPS (8 YoY pairs); fmp_dyn_fwd_underestimate_gap → analyst-estimates (current consensus, not PIT); evt_* → per-symbol `earnings` (fmp_events, 1,432 INV names).
- Spirit gaps: the market's non-reaction (what makes it "asleep") is only in the exceptional tier; revenue beats absent (EPS beats via buybacks/tax are not "the business beating"); no analyst-count floor — a beat against one estimate is noise. Branch (b) contains no estimate at all (it is EPS growth, a different thesis) yet wears the asleep label.
- Methodology: branch (a) double-counts: beat_rate≥0.75 is both the gate and one of the "≥2 legs", so the real bar is rate + ONE more. Streak counts any surprise>0 (a +0.1% beat) with no materiality. The `measured` escape means branch (b)/(c) names with no beat data bypass the tightened core. Coverage: only 367 of 2,445 firers have evt_* (A2 §0.1).
- FMP augmentations: `earnings-surprises-bulk` (global, ~13 calls) → evt_beats_8q/median surprise universe-wide (A2 C1 open); per-symbol `earnings` revenueActual/Estimated for firers (A2 S12); `analyst-estimates` numAnalystsEps ≥2 as validity; `key-metrics` incomeQualityTTM as an accrual guard on the beaten EPS.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] ESB bulk to close the 367/2,445 hole (A2 C1 open). 2 [method] gate = beat_rate≥0.75 & ≥1 of (median surprise≥2%, streak≥3 with ≥1% each); remove the double count. 3 [spirit] require ≥2 estimates behind a beat (numAnalystsEps) and add a revenue-beat lens. 4 [spirit] move branch (b) to `asleep_eps_watch` — no estimate, no "wheel".

### arch_asleep_unrerated  (line 6020)
- Spirit: user/engine spec — told repeatedly (beats) and still no multiple expansion; Compendium #8 "behaviour-change score high vs reaction score low". Engine-native.
- As coded: asleep_at_wheel==1 & no-rerate (ANY of 6: ev_sales_change_yoy≤+10%; price_yoy≤median(rev_yoy, ebitda_yoy); implied P/E expansion≤10%; fmp_dyn_unrerated_gap≥0.15; bs_coil_rev≥log1.25; bs_coil_ebit≥log1.40) & not rich (P/E≤25 or EV/EBITDA≤14 or both NaN) & not melting. `_tier`: core = 1y coil (log1p(fq_rev_growth)−log1p(ts_r52))≥0 or the 2y coils; exceptional = evt_react_beats_4q≤0; elite = max(1y coil, bs_coil_rev). Lenses: unrerated_gap_1y, bs_coil_rev, evt_ignored_beats_2y, −evt_react_beats_4q.
- Lineage: ev_sales_change_yoy → derive (reconstructed from price_yoy; A2 §C fragile); price_yoy Yahoo snapshot; fmp_dyn_unrerated_gap → enterprise-values history (global, ~16k/30k done); bs_coil_* → base_snapshot (panel + quarterly panel TTM); fq_rev_growth, ts_r52.
- Spirit gaps: inherits asleep's coverage hole. "Unrerated" is measured on sales vs price; a re-rating on EARNINGS (P/E) is a different multiple, and the P/E lens uses median(rev, EBITDA growth) as "fundamental growth". The multiple's own history (EV/Sales now vs its 3-year median on the dated EV series; A2 B.5 open) is the direct claim and is absent.
- Methodology: six ORed lenses, any one suffices; `_pe_exp_au≤0.10` is algebraically the same fact as lens (b), so two "independent" lenses are one. The 1y coil core is NOT per share (narrative_lag subtracts share growth; this does not) — a diluter passes. `_not_rich_au` passes when both multiples are NaN (loss-makers), which is where beats are least meaningful. `_pyw_au` snapshot price_yoy remains in the base rule (A2 Z3 open).
- FMP augmentations: `enterprise-values` history (fmp_dyn, global) → EV/Sales ≤ own 3y median; `earnings-surprises-bulk` + panel → evt_react_beats_4q universe-wide; `historical-market-capitalization` (from/to) for a PIT multiple path; fq_shares_yoy.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] per-share 1y coil: subtract log1p(fq_shares_yoy) as narrative_lag does. 2 [method] require ≥2 of the 6 no-rerate lenses (A1 A.2's rule for the xr sibling applies here too). 3 [spirit] dated "multiple did not expand": EV/Sales now ≤ 3y median from the EV series as core (A2 B.5 open). 4 [data] ESB for reactions (A2 C1 open).

### arch_asymmetric_assembly  (line 5702)
- Spirit: engine-native PSIX causal system (six reinforcing engines). Nearest practitioner sources: Wolf Archetype 8 "Cash-Flow Deleveraging & Yield" (FCF yield>10%, ND/EBITDA 1.5–3× trending down, falling interest, buybacks) and Pernas "Low-multiple operational inflection" (measurable normalisation mechanism). Not the Compendium's assembly theory (post-reorg) despite the name.
- As coded: operating & mcap<$5B & rev_yoy≤+5% & oper_lev_any & strong improvement (oper_lev_score≥0.40 or EBITDA yoy≥15% or GP up while revenue down) & heavy_debt (not known net cash & [nde 3–30 or (EV/mcap 1.75–30 & nde≥1.5)]) & EBITDA>0 & (FCF>0 or CFO>0) & (EBITDA yoy>0 or inflection) & (EV/EBITDA≤7 or robust cash yield≥15%) & beaten_down_any(0.35) & capex intensity soft <15%. No lenses.
- Lineage: rev_yoy / ebitda_yoy / gross_profit_yoy / oper_lev angles → master (Yahoo + EDGAR Tier-B deltas + `*_qoq_ttm`); nde → master net_debt_ebitda (snapshot; 99 when EBITDA≤0); ev_over_mcap; robust_cash_yield → derive; beaten_down_any panel-first. Ratios are currency-neutral; mcap USD.
- Spirit gaps: engine (3) "actively DELEVERAGING" is proxied by EBITDA rising (the comment admits it); the direct proof — net debt falling — exists in fmp_quarterly (`fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets`, `fq_deleveraging_flag`) and is unused (A1 A.4 open). No debt-service survivability (Holmström-Tirole "pledgeable" ≈ CFO>0 only). Wolf's falling interest / refinancing and PSIX's controlling owner (Weichai) are absent.
- Methodology: `oper_lev_any` admits raw-sequential moves (A2 §C fragile) inside a deliberately strict conjunction. `_not_known_netcash` vetoes on net_cash_pct>0.10 — a levered firm with a large gross cash pile can read "net cash" on a stale snapshot. nde 99-fill (EBITDA≤0) fails heavy_debt — correct here. robust_cy is levered (equity) cash while EV/EBITDA is enterprise; the OR is acceptable.
- FMP augmentations: fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets<0 / fq_deleveraging_flag (global, wired); `ratios-ttm` interestCoverageRatioTTM, debtServiceCoverageRatioTTM (global snapshot; FMP_ENRICHMENT_MAP #13); `insider-trading/statistics` (US) buying at the stub; `key-executives` / 13D for a controlling operator.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] add net debt falling (fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets<0 or fq_deleveraging_flag) as a core leg (A1 A.4 open). 2 [method] use `season_robust` (drop raw-seq) for the improvement leg in this strict conjunction. 3 [data] DSCR/IC soft guard from ratios-ttm (<1 = solvency countdown). 4 [spirit] insider/owner buying as an exceptional tier (IST, US).

### arch_balance_sheet_return  (line 2003)
- Spirit: engine-native "cash-rich runoff": capital returned that operations did not earn, plus negative-EV names. Loosely Wolf Archetype 4/5 (asset floor, buybacks below NAV). No Compendium/Master Ref entry.
- As coded: operating & mcap>0 & (uncovered | neg_ev). uncovered = returns_any (capital_return_yield≥2% or div+buyback yield≥2%) & (tc_uncov_payout_3y≥2 or [tc NaN & fcf_yield<0]); neg_ev = enterprise_value<0 or cash_gt_ev. No lenses.
- Lineage: capital_return_yield EDGAR (~4%); dividend_yield / buyback_yield master (provider TTM yield, FX-corruptible per A2 §C); tc_uncov_payout_3y → fmp_throughcycle (global 8 FY; A1 B.1's tc swap IS implemented here); enterprise_value provider/reconstructed; cash_gt_ev_flag derive.
- Spirit gaps: "returning cash from the balance sheet" needs a balance sheet to return it from — no net-cash cushion on the uncovered branch, so a company paying an uncovered dividend from DEBT (the Ponzi payout) qualifies. The neg-EV branch needs no payout at all and duplicates negative_ev_value (A1 B.1: 48% of firers; still open). No survivability guard (no _not_melting), no size floor.
- Methodology: provider dividend_yield in the wrong currency can manufacture a 2% "return"; a 2% yield is not a "return" thesis; neg-EV on reconstructed EV with stale cash. Fires 3,287 (A1 B.1 very broad).
- FMP augmentations: `dividends` (global) same-currency yield and actual cash paid (fmp_st commonDividendsPaid); `scores-bulk` altmanZScore as a distress guard; `balance-sheet` cash trend (squatter vs returner, as cluseau).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] uncovered branch must show the balance sheet: net_cash_pct_sane ≥ 2× total yield (or nde≤0) — otherwise it is a debt-funded payout, not this thesis. 2 [method] neg-EV-without-payout → negative_ev_value only (A1 B.1 open); keep neg-EV here only with returns_any. 3 [data] same-currency yield from `dividends` (A2 §C open). 4 [method] mcap≥$10M and an Altman/not-melting guard.

### arch_base_ignition  (line 6886)
- Spirit: engine theory + base-breakout event study — first buyers arriving at a coiled base; Master Ref VI discovery cycle "catalyst/inflection → retail → institutional"; Kullamägi/O'Neil volume on the breakout. Engine-native.
- As coded: coiled_base==1 & ≥2 of 7 ignition lenses [bs_dvol_trend≥1.6; bs_updown_vol≥1.8; bs_rs26≥+10% & bs_r26≥+10%; institutional_accumulation or insider_buy_flag; sent warming or discovery; change-point volume (bs_cp_dvol_z13 or CUSUM ≥ 80th pct of current bases); slope break ≥80th pct] & at least one volume lens (dvol_trend, updown_vol or cp volume). Not spirited/tiered.
- Lineage: bs_* → base_snapshot (weekly panel; dvol now USD — A1 P0 closed); bs_cp_* → event_study_analyse change-points; institutional_accumulation → 13F (US-listed only); insider_buy_flag → SEC Form 4 (US); sent_* → grades (US-centric). Quintile cuts are computed at run time on the current base population.
- Spirit gaps: no DATED trigger — the Kullamägi episodic pivot (earnings-week gap ≥+8% on ≥3× volume), a new 13D/G, or N-PORT initiations (A2 B.2 open). No trend confirmation (above 30w MA, MRS>0; A2 exceptional). Ex-US names can ignite only on price/volume because the informed-buyer and perception lenses are US feeds — by doctrine acceptable, but the "who is buying" half is structurally blank for ~60% of the universe.
- Methodology: `_q80` thresholds float with the universe each run (a weak week lowers the bar; membership is non-stationary). bs_dvol_trend and cp z13/CUSUM measure the same dollar-volume expansion, so the "≥2 lenses" can be satisfied by one fact. bs_rs26 is r26 minus the market median (not an index) — fine.
- FMP augmentations: `earnings-surprises-bulk` + panel → evt_react_last (earnings-week reaction, global); `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` new 13D/G ≤90d (US); `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` N-PORT change>0 across ≥3 US funds (non-US reach; A2 S9); `institutional-ownership/extract-analytics/holder` isNew; `earnings-calendar` to avoid firing on the eve of a print.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] add a dated trigger lens (evt_react_last≥+8% on a beat, or new 13D/G ≤90d) (A2 B.2 open). 2 [method] collapse dvol_trend + cp_volume into one family (max) so the ≥2-lens rule counts independent evidence. 3 [method] freeze the change-point cuts as absolute thresholds taken from the event study, not run-time quantiles. 4 [data] N-PORT initiations as the non-US informed-buyer lens (A2 S9 open).

### arch_biotech_deep_value  (line 7919)
- Spirit: Master Ref I.F "Option mispriced as dead" (NKTR, BIOA, STOK: failed/forgotten asset, TAM intact, new data changes odds, downside capitulated, balance sheet survives to the catalyst) + the below-cash floor (Wolf 4, Liger asset-backed optionality: "dilution risk limited").
- As coded: clinical biotech (drug developer & not commercial) & mcap≥$2M & (net cash 0.5–3.0× mcap or cash>EV or NCAV 0.8–3.0×) & runway≥1y (fq_cash_sti / −fq_fcf with a 3× scale check vs master FCF; else net-cash USD / USD burn; non-burners=99) & shares_yoy not >50%. Score: net cash, cash>EV, runway 1–4y, NCAV, dilution.
- Lineage: net_cash_pct_mcap / ncav_pct_mcap → derive / compute_nnwc (FX-corruptible, band-clamped); fq_cash_sti, fq_fcf → fmp_quarterly (reporting currency, global) — A1 A.6 runway IS implemented; fcf_ttm_usd; classifier at 7810 (industry/name + commercial flag).
- Spirit gaps: nothing measures "priced as dead": no drawdown (ts_dist_hi260), no coverage collapse, no insider/activist buying (the engine's own mb comment at 7850 cites BIOA insiders 36%, activist 59%, price 22% of the 5y high), no catalyst proximity, no burn trend. A 1-year runway below cash is the classic dilution trap the Liger note warns about; practitioners want ≥2 years or a financing already done.
- Methodology: `shares_yoy>0.50` is the only dilution veto — 49% dilution passes; mcap≥$2M admits nano shells; score double-weights the cash floor (net cash 0.35 + cash>EV 0.20 are one fact). Net-cash band 0.5–3.0 is sane. Runway from net cash × USD mcap ÷ USD burn is coherent.
- FMP augmentations: ts_dist_hi260 (wired column) for capitulation; `insider-trading/statistics` (US) and `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (activist, US); `earnings-calendar` / 8-K stream for catalyst dates; quarterly panel fq_fcf vs fq_fcf_p → burn accelerating/decelerating; `key-executives` (new CEO = "new management changes odds").
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] capitulation core: ts_dist_hi260≤0.5; exceptional: insider/13D buying (US) or a dated catalyst ≤6 months. 2 [method] runway≥2y, or ≥1y only with shares_yoy≤15% (financed). 3 [data] burn-trend flag from the quarterly panel (runway shrinking vs a year ago). 4 [method] mcap≥$10M like the sibling floors.

### arch_blindspot  (line 1689)
- Spirit: Master Ref I.G "(14) Regional blind-spot alpha" and IV Flyover hunting grounds (Japan "the most inefficient market", Mittelstand, AIM, Nordics, India, TASE, B3, ASX, KOSDAQ); engine comment: markets the Yellowbrick corpus under-weights.
- As coded: src country in BLINDSPOT_COUNTRIES (KR, GR, ID, TH, ZA, MX, BR, CO, AR, PH, VN, HU, CZ, EE, LV, LT, PL, TR, SA, RO, ML, PE, CL, IL) & mcap<$400M & (pew ADV missing or <$500k). `_tier`: core = operating & weekly USD dvol (ts_dvol26_usd, pew×5 fallback) $50k–$2.5M & sent_n_analysts not >1; exceptional = no analysts & dvol≤$500k; elite fcf_yield; measured = dvol present. Lenses: −sent_n_analysts, −ts_dvol26_usd, +fcf_yield, −ev_ebitda.
- Lineage: country = listing domicile (`src`), not economic geography; pew avg_dollar_volume (8% coverage, 0% on firers per A1 — dead leg); ts_dvol26_usd panel; sent_n_analysts grades-historical (US-centric → missing reads as neglected via `~(x>1)`).
- Spirit gaps: the country set is static and omits the Master Ref's named deepest pools (JP, DE, SE/NO/DK/FI, IN, AU, TW). "Blind spot" is a coverage-of-coverage idea (relative to a corpus); the proxy is domicile, so a US-listed ADR of a Korean company is out and a Korean-listed US-revenue company is in. No business-quality floor in the base rule (financials, REITs and shells fire; only the tier adds is_operating).
- Methodology: missing analysts pass in core AND exceptional (A2 §C fragile "missing ≠ neglected"); names without panel dvol keep the dead-ADV rule; no mcap floor. A1 B.1 (707 of 3,244) is implemented as the tier; the observed-coverage half is open.
- FMP augmentations: `analyst-estimates` numAnalystsEps (cached, zero-call, global; A2 Z5); `grades-consensus` bulk counts; `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` (no US fund holds it) and `etf/asset-exposure` passive share <2% (A2 S8/S9); `revenue-geographic-segmentation` for economic geography.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] observed neglect: numAnalystsEps≤1 where present, missing → watch only (A2 Z5 open). 2 [spirit] base rule gets is_operating, mcap≥$10M and profit_present — a blind spot on a shell is not alpha. 3 [spirit] derive the country set from measured coverage density per market (share of names with any estimate) instead of a hand list, or at least add the Master Ref IV markets. 4 [data] N-PORT / ETF passive share as the "no US institution owns it" exceptional (A2 S8/S9 open).

### arch_book_compounder_discount  (line 4072)
- Spirit: forensic F14 (engine): audited book compounding ≥8%/yr priced below book — Buffett's dollar-retained test; Compendium 4 Tillinghast "profitability (ROE>10–12% sustained) + certainty"; Master Ref II Yartseva "high B/M + positive profitability".
- As coded: (operating or financial) & not utility & mcap>0 & equity_cagr_5y≥8% & pb<1.0 & (NI_ttm>0 or ni_avg>0) & not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: equity_cagr_5y = EDGAR TOTAL-equity CAGR (US) NaN-filled by fmp_st_equity_cagr = BVPS CAGR (global) — two definitions in one column (the fill comment acknowledges it); pb master snapshot; ni_avg EDGAR; net_income_ttm master.
- Spirit gaps: no dividend adjustment (a 60%-payout compounder's book grows slowly though its total book return is high); no reason the discount exists or will close (buyback, insider, catalyst); the "hardest series to fake" claim breaks on the EDGAR path where a rights issue lifts total equity — no dilution guard there.
- Methodology: mixed total-vs-per-share definitions mean EDGAR names can pass on issuance while FMP names cannot; financial members have no ROE floor (financials_value has 10%) and `_not_melting` is ill-defined for banks (op_margin NaN → permissive). pb<1.0 on the 99-default is safe.
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth` bookValueperShareGrowth and fiveYShareholdersEquityGrowthPerShare per FY (global, per share, consistency; A2 S4); `dividends` history → BVPS + cumulative DPS compounding; `splits` for the share path.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] add ~(shares_growth_5y>0.05) so the total-equity path cannot pass on issuance, or compute BVPS on both paths. 2 [spirit] compound BVPS + DPS (total book return) using dividends history. 3 [data] FG per-FY consistency (BVPS up in ≥4 of 5 FYs) as exceptional (A2 matrix open). 4 [method] roe≥8% floor for financial members.

### arch_bottleneck  (line 7946)
- Spirit: Master Ref I.B "The Constraint" (STX, FTAI, Okano Valve, PILLAR, Koppers, SGL Carbon, Tokyo Ohka) + VII Akre bottleneck asset; Pernas "Policy/resource bottleneck" (LEU, ARQ: own the scarce capability before the catalyst; the asset must exist first).
- As coded: operating & not melting & revenue≥$5M & GM≥40% & (GM delta not <−3pp, or tc_min_gm≥40% over ≥7 FY) & (roce≥15% not one-off, or roic_lindy≥15%) & op margin>5% & capex intensity not >10% (missing passes). Lenses: tc_min_gm, gross_margin, roic_lindy, −capex_intensity.
- Lineage: gross_margin master; gross_margin_delta_yoy Tier-B; tc_min_gm / tc_years → fmp_throughcycle (global 8 FY; A2 B.17's core input is wired as a lens/escape, not the core); roce master snapshot; roic_lindy EDGAR + fmp_st fill; capex_intensity derive.
- Spirit gaps: the code screens capital-LIGHT quality economics (fat GM, high ROIC), which also describes software and brands; the Master Ref/Pernas constraints are mostly capital-HEAVY physical chokepoints (enrichment, poles, graphite, photoresist, engine shops) that the capex guard excludes. Missing the constraint signatures: pricing power RISING (GM up), forced demand (backlog, allocation, "capacity constrained"), scarcity (few listed peers). Two theses conflated.
- Methodology: roce≥15% is a single period (A2 §C fragile); GM erosion leg "missing ⇒ pass"; capex intensity "missing ⇒ pass" — for ex-US names both guards are often absent, so the gate reduces to GM≥40% & profitable. Well built as a quality-economics tag; mis-named as a constraint.
- FMP augmentations: `earning-call-transcript` NLP ("sole source", "allocation", "capacity constrained", "price increase") for firers (A2 S14/B.17 open); quarterly panel TTM GM rising; `peers-bulk` peer count (scarcity proxy, one call); `revenue-product-segmentation` (single-product concentration as a chokepoint tell).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] split: keep this as bottleneck_light and add a capital-heavy constraint variant (asset_heavy & tc_min_opm>0 & GM rising & fq_rev_growth≥10%) so the physical chokepoints are not excluded by design. 2 [spirit] add a "pricing power rising" core lens (TTM GM up YoY or fqx_inc_ebit_margin ≥ current op margin). 3 [method] tc_min_gm≥0.40 as core where tc_years≥5 (A2 B.17 open). 4 [data] transcript-language exceptional tier.

### arch_buyback_compounder  (line 2235)
- Spirit: Compendium D capital-cycle BUY signal "share-count reduction"; Master Ref III "Buybacks (esp. ASRs)"; Wolf 8 "dividends/buybacks material (>3–5% of mcap)"; Greenblatt cannibals.
- As coded: operating & roce_now_ok & shrink [5y≤−5% (and ≥−30% or buyback_yield>0) | 3y≤−3% (same) | buyback_yield≥3% & shares_yoy not >2%] & shares_yoy not >2% & roic_lindy≥8% & n_yrs_roic_pos≥4 & nde≤1.5. Lenses: −shares_growth_5y, −shares_growth_3y, roic_lindy, buyback_yield.
- Lineage: shares_growth_3y/5y EDGAR + fmp_st fill (global, audited diluted shares); buyback_yield EDGAR only (~4%); shares_yoy master; roic_lindy EDGAR + fill; nde snapshot (99 when EBITDA≤0 → fails; fine).
- Spirit gaps: "compounder" = per-share value growth — no FCF/share or EPS/share leg; no price-paid discipline (bought below value / counter-cyclical, A2 B.13); the −30% reverse-split heuristic (A2 §C fragile) with `buyback_yield` as the only rescue means a genuine >30% five-year cannibal OUTSIDE EDGAR is excluded — exactly the best non-US members.
- Methodology: roic_lindy≥8% is modest for a compounder; nde≤1.5 does not stop a shrink financed by new debt inside the window; no dividend context. Otherwise coherent.
- FMP augmentations: `splits` (global) replaces the −30% heuristic (A2 S7); `financial-growth` weightedAverageSharesDilutedGrowth per FY (shrink in ≥4 of 5 FYs); `historical-market-capitalization` + `cash-flow` commonStockRepurchased → buyback/mcap by FY vs price-off-high (counter-cyclical); fqx_fcf_ps_g for per-share compounding (wired).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] replace the −30% veto with the splits feed and let fmp_st repurchases stand in for buyback_yield ex-US (A2 S7 open). 2 [spirit] add fqx_fcf_ps_g>0 (or FG per-share NI growth) as the compounding proof. 3 [data] FG per-FY shrink consistency as the exceptional tier (A2 B.13 open). 4 [spirit] counter-cyclical execution score from HMC + repurchases.

### arch_cannibal_at_discount  (line 4032)
- Spirit: forensic F12 — retiring stock below book, "the strongest insider signal"; Wolf 4/5 "share buybacks below NAV"; Cluseau (cheapness is a screen, action is the thesis).
- As coded: operating & mcap>0 & pb<1.0 & shrink (shares_yoy≤−2%, or buyback_yield≥3% corroborated by net_buyback_ttm>0 or shares_yoy<0) & shares_yoy not <−30% & (NI>0 or ni_avg>0 or fcf_yield>0) & not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: pb master; shares_yoy master (Yahoo/FMP, split-contaminated possible); buyback_yield / net_buyback_ttm EDGAR (US); ni_avg EDGAR.
- Spirit gaps: "discount" is to BOOK, while Cluseau/Wolf want realizable book (cash, securities, NAV) — buying back below a goodwill-heavy book is not the same accretion (xr_cannibal_below_tbook covers tangible; overlap). Persistence: one year; no acceleration into the discount (cluseau_buyback_accel covers). No funding check — a debt-funded buyback at 0.8× book in a levered name is not the signal.
- Methodology: −30% veto (splits, A2 §C open); ex-US names can only pass via shares_yoy≤−2% because the corroborated buyback path is EDGAR-only; overlaps three siblings on one fact → archetype_count inflation.
- FMP augmentations: `splits`; fmp_st commonStockRepurchased / mcap as a global buyback_yield; `historical-market-capitalization` for the average price paid vs book (buyback at a discount, dated).
- Suggestions: 1 [data] global buyback_yield from fmp_st repurchases so the corroborated leg works ex-US. 2 [method] splits guard (A2 S7 open). 3 [spirit] require nde≤2 or net_buyback ≤ FCF_ttm (self-funded cannibal). 4 [method] count cannibal_at_discount / xr_cannibal_below_tbook / cluseau_buyback_accel as one family in archetype_count.

### arch_capex_famine_harvest  (line 3973)
- Spirit: forensic F9 (engine); Compendium D capital cycle "falling capex/depreciation ratios … capacity withdrawal → rising returns" (Marathon); Wolf 8 "low or no growth capex requirement".
- As coded: operating & mcap>0 & capex_avg>0 & capex_ttm≥0 & capex_ttm≤0.6×capex_avg (FMP pair fq_capex / fmp_st_capex_avg where fmp_filled_capex_avg==1) & rev_yoy≥−5% & op margin>0 & pb<2.0 & not melting. No lenses.
- Lineage: capex_ttm / capex_avg EDGAR (US) or same-source FMP pair (global; correctly paired — good); rev_yoy snapshot; pb master.
- Spirit gaps: the capital-cycle thesis is INDUSTRY-level (capacity leaving the sector); this is company-level and cannot tell harvest from starvation: FMP_ENRICHMENT_MAP #4 — capex/D&A<1 with stable revenue = harvester, with falling revenue = melting under-investor — and rev_yoy≥−5% is a one-year check. No capex-vs-D&A leg (the true harvest signature) and no FCF-inflection proof ("loaded before it prints" is asserted, not measured).
- Methodology: 0.6× a 5-year MEAN — one big project year inflates the average so every later year is a "famine"; capex sign conventions differ between EDGAR capex_ttm and fq_capex (abs'd) but are handled. A1 A.6: "no material upgrade" — disagree on the D&A pairing.
- FMP augmentations: `key-metrics` capexToDepreciationTTM (global snapshot) or fq D&A → capex≤D&A; `owner-earnings` maintenanceCapex / growthCapex (global, cached) → growth capex collapsing while maintenance holds = harvest, maintenance cut = starvation; `*-statement-bulk` for an industry-level capex aggregate (the Marathon lens, new flag).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] core corroboration capex ≤ D&A (fq or capexToDepreciation<1). 2 [method] use the MEDIAN of the FY capex series for capex_avg (peak-robust). 3 [spirit] Greenwald split from owner-earnings: growthCapex↓ with maintenanceCapex flat — the harvest-vs-starvation discriminator. 4 [data] industry capex trend flag from statement-bulk (Marathon capital cycle, industry frame).

### arch_cheap_per_roiic  (line 1815)
- Spirit: engine-native PEG-on-ROIIC (Mauboussin); Master Ref II Yartseva "asset growth >0 but < EBITDA growth" (reinvestment covered by returns).
- As coded: operating & roce_now_ok & not melting & roic_lindy≥5% & cheap_per_roiic (EV/EBITDA ÷ (ROIIC×100)) in (0, 1.5] & roiic_lindy>10%. Lenses: −cheap_per_roiic_lindy, cash_roiic_lindy, asset_3y_cagr.
- Lineage: cheap_per_roiic_lindy EDGAR or recomputed from fmp_st_roiic_lindy fill (A1: 92% FMP-filled, rolling-3y median ROIIC — noisy on small deltas); ev_ebitda master snapshot (provider; currency-invariant).
- Spirit gaps: paying little per unit of reinvestment return only matters if reinvestment is HAPPENING (A1 B.1 open: asset_3y_cagr≥5% and cash ROIIC≥8% keep 1,530/4,147). No "still paying now" leg (roiic_1y_positive_flag / fqx_ebit_ttm_g).
- Methodology: the ratio is not a cheapness floor at high ROIIC — ROIIC 60% at EV/EBITDA 90× gives 1.5 and "cheap"; a 20% ROIIC admits up to 30× EV/EBITDA. Snapshot EV/EBITDA vs a multi-year lindy denominator mixes horizons. 4,147 firers (A1 B.1 very broad).
- FMP augmentations: fmp_st_cash_roiic_lindy (cash corroboration, wired as lens); fqx_roic_ttm / fqx_ebit_ttm_g (wired); `financial-growth` per-share OCF growth; `enterprise-values` history for a smoothed EV/EBITDA.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] add an absolute EV/EBITDA cap (≤20×) so the ratio cannot certify a 90× name. 2 [method] require asset_3y_cagr≥5% and cash_roiic_lindy≥8% as core (A1 B.1 open). 3 [spirit] add fqx_ebit_ttm_g>0 (the reinvestment is still returning now).

### arch_cheap_sales_scaler  (line 5789)

The A2 B.1 exceptional (coil_n≥2, observed coverage≤2, fqx_eps_pos_share_8≥0.75, ts_weinstein_stage==1) is not implemented — no `_tier` call. Coils are not per share (narrative_lag subtracts share growth; here a diluter in a base "coils"). Clinical biotech not excluded (A2 Z2 open).

The fall may be dilution-driven (bs_prior_dd is price; share count can have tripled) — a bust equity, not a base.

- Suggestions: 1 [method] require the fall to be per share: shares_growth_3y≤+20% or HMC mcap drawdown within 15pp of the price drawdown. 2 [spirit] "survived" leg: nde≤3 & tc_min_opm>−0.10 (never a deep-loss year), and a former-coverage lens (sent_n_analysts_d12<0) as exceptional. 3 [data] as coiled_base.

### arch_concentrated_segments  (line 2089)
- Spirit: engine-native NEGATIVE transparency flag (one segment ≥70% of revenue = single-segment risk). Nearest sources are risk checklists, not a buy archetype: Tillinghast "certainty" (Compendium 4), MCC red flag "one customer driving all growth" (Master Ref VI). The positive archetypes on the same fact run the other way — Master Ref I.B "(16) Small monopoly in a non-sexy niche" (Okano, PILLAR) is concentrated BY DESIGN.
- As coded: is_operating & (segment_hhi ≥0.70 | largest_segment_share ≥0.70) & segment_count ≥2. Not tiered/spirited. 529 firers; counted 1:1 in archetype_count (8807, no dedup cluster, nothing flips the sign) and zeroed for double cash-burners by the segment scrub (8509).
- Lineage: segment_count / segment_revenue_hhi / largest_segment_share = edgar_segment_signals (US dimensional 10-K, ~10% of US filers) NaN-filled by fmp_seg_count / fmp_seg_hhi_rec / fmp_seg_largest_share_rec (revenue-product-segmentation: global, revenue-only, latest FY, totals/eliminations skipped by _SKIP_PAT). Two taxonomies in one column — EDGAR reportable operating segments vs FMP product lines — so "70%" means different things by source. One FY snapshot; segment_hhi_delta (451) is wired and unused.
- Spirit gaps: a NEGATIVE flag that ADDS archetype density (and so ETA / convergence) is the opposite of its stated sign; the "downstream can flip" comment is implemented nowhere (only the pretty-name map and the scrub reference it). Pure plays (segment_count==1, the most concentrated) are excluded, so it marks 70/30 two-segment firms and misses 100% single-segment ones. customer_concentration_flag (2069, EDGAR) — the concentration the sources actually name — is unused. The cash-burner scrub REMOVES the risk flag exactly where the risk bit.
- Methodology: hhi ≥0.70 implies largest share ≥~0.84, so the HHI leg is redundant (one lens, not two). No trend: "concentrating" (hhi_delta>0) and "a segment growing INTO dominance" (the fastest_segment thesis) are indistinguishable. Mixed-source HHI is not comparable across US/non-US.
- FMP augmentations: revenue-geographic-segmentation (wired as fmp_geo_*) → single-country ≥70% as a second concentration axis; segment_hhi_delta (wired) for the trend; `earning-call-transcript` "largest customer" share for non-US names (10-K Item 1A is US-only).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] take it out of archetype_count (surface as `concentration_risk_flag`, or subtract) — a negative signal must not add density. 2 [spirit] include segment_count==1 pure plays or rename it "multi-segment concentration"; add customer_concentration_flag as the material leg. 3 [method] drop the redundant HHI leg; use hhi_delta>0 to separate "concentrating" from "hidden engine emerging". 4 [method] do not scrub cash-burners from a negative flag.

### arch_crisis_asset_backed_recovery  (line 3559)
- Spirit: Cundill RECOVERY-fund punt (engine: Sibir Energy 1999, −79% on Russia's default with reserves intact) — a hard-asset OPERATOR smashed by a macro/country/commodity crisis, bought below tangible assets as returns begin to inflect. Sources: Wolf Archetype 4 "Asset-Backed Floor + optional growth" (mcap ≤ cash + appraised assets, low debt); Liger "Asset-backed optionality" (liquidation value ≠ cash: discount gross assets, deduct every senior claim, dilution limited); Master Ref I.E three-layer test; Compendium D "asset-heavy balance sheets below replacement cost".
- As coded: is_operating & mcap ≥$20M & asset-heavy (sector ∈ Energy/Materials/Industrials/Utilities OR ppe_gross/assets ≥0.40) & crash (beaten_down_any(0.55) | price_pct_of_5y_range ≤0.15 | ts_dist_hi260 ≤0.45) & below assets (pb<0.8 | p_tb<0.8) & _not_melting. Not tiered; spirited from docs/spirit_spec only. 623 firers.
- Lineage: ppe_gross / assets EDGAR (US; no fmp fill found for ppe_gross — only p_tb ← fq_p_tb), so the MEASURED asset leg is US-only and the sector label carries every non-US name (fixed_cost, by contrast, uses asset_heavy() on fq_ppe_net); p_tb EDGAR + fq_p_tb (global); pb master; ts_dist_hi260 panel (A2 matrix "crash = ts_dist_hi260≤0.5" is half-implemented: 0.45 inside an OR with the local-currency snapshot 5y-range proxy). beaten_down_any is panel-first.
- Spirit gaps: the CRISIS is unmeasured — nothing separates a macro/country/commodity washout from an idiosyncratic collapse (fraud, write-down, secular decline); Cundill's point is the assets are intact BECAUSE the cause was external. No breadth test (peers/market also down), no impairment check (a −55% stock AFTER a reserve write-down is priced correctly). No leverage cap at all: pb<0.8 proves net assets survive senior claims at BOOK, not at market — a levered driller at 0.7× book with nde 6 is the case Cundill only touched via a senior convertible. The "returns begin to inflect" half is only _not_melting (benign to any name with one positive cash lens). Country risk = listing domicile (Enrichment Map nuance 8).
- Methodology: three ORed crash lenses on different bases (52w, 5y range, 5y high); price_pct_of_5y_range is a local-currency snapshot (A2 §C). ppe_gross/assets uses GROSS PP&E — a fully-depreciated fleet reads asset-heavy. Utilities are in the sector set while is_operating excludes utilities → dead label member.
- FMP augmentations: asset_heavy() on fq_ppe_net/fq_total_assets (wired, global); crisis breadth = share of `peers-bulk` peers (or same-suffix market) with ts_dist_hi260 ≤0.6 (zero calls once peers-bulk is on disk); `revenue-geographic-segmentation` for economic geography; `insider-trading/statistics` (US) buying in the crash quarter (matrix: score); fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets<0 / fqx_ebit_ttm_g>0 for the inflection half; `scores-bulk` Altman as a solvency guard.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] crisis-breadth leg: ≥50% of peers/market also ≥40% off the 5y high — the external cause is the thesis. 2 [method] use asset_heavy() so the measured leg is global; drop the dead Utilities label. 3 [spirit] senior-claims guard: nde ≤4 or fq total_debt/total_assets ≤0.5 (a levered asset owner below book is Cundill's convertible case, not his equity case). 4 [spirit] inflection half as core: fqx_ebit_ttm_g>0 or ts_r13>0; insider buying (IST, US) as exceptional.

### arch_customer_float  (line 3948)
- Spirit: forensic F8 (engine): negative working capital read INVERTED — customers fund the business, cash arrives before earnings. Sources: Compendium 2 Den Fujita "contracts-at-a-loss-then-monetise / time-as-inventory", Harpin subscription (5); Wolf 8 anti-pattern "operating cash flow misreported via big receivable build"; insurance-float lineage.
- As coded: is_operating & mcap>0 & (net_working_capital<0 | fmp_cash_conversion_cycle<0 | fq_op_nwc_to_rev<0 | fq_ccc<0) & op_margin>3% & rev_yoy ≥0 & CFO/NI ≥1.1 (fmp_income_quality fallback) & _not_melting. Not tiered/spirited. 1,692 firers. A1 A.6 / A2 matrix: "fine, already wired".
- Lineage: net_working_capital EDGAR (US) filled by fq_conv_nwc (global; unclassified balance sheets NaN'd — good); fmp_cash_conversion_cycle key-metrics-ttm (provider snapshot, global); fq_ccc = DSO+DIO−DPO (fmp_quarterly, global — NO deferred revenue); fq_op_nwc_to_rev = (rec+inv−pay−defrev)/rev (global, includes defrev); CFO/NI master with the FMP income-quality fallback.
- Spirit gaps: the FLOAT must GROW with the business to be the thesis — every leg is a level; fq_defrev_to_rev (9424) is wired and unused, so the direct customer-float measure is absent while three flags (customer_float, xr_deferred_revenue_lead, xr_float_compounding) share the fact. No distinction between CUSTOMER float (prepayments, deferred revenue) and SUPPLIER float (stretched payables): a company paying suppliers late reads as "customer-funded" — the Wolf anti-pattern inverted.
- Methodology: net_working_capital<0 includes short-term DEBT in current liabilities (a maturing term loan = "float"); fq_ccc and the FMP CCC are DSO/DIO/DPO only, so a negative CCC is mostly a long DPO. CFO/NI ≥1.1 on one TTM is one working-capital swing (understated_earnings requires the year-ago TTM too). op_margin>3% is the only quality floor; rev_yoy ≥0 one year.
- FMP augmentations: fq_defrev_to_rev and its prior (wired) — disagree with the matrix "NONE": the defrev path is the direct measure; fq_dpo vs fq_dpo_p (wired) to separate supplier stretch from customer prepayment (nuance 2); fq_cfo_p / fq_ni_cf_p (wired) for CFO/NI persistence.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] core = float exists AND grows: fq_op_nwc_to_rev below its prior or defrev/rev rising. 2 [method] read the operating-NWC / defrev legs first and total NWC only as fallback (short-term debt out); veto DPO-driven "float" where fq_dpo − fq_dpo_p ≥ +20 days with receivables/inventory flat. 3 [method] CFO/NI persistence on the year-ago TTM as understated_earnings does. 4 [method] count customer_float / xr_float_compounding / xr_deferred_revenue_lead as one family in archetype_count.

### arch_dead_option  (line 1633)
- Spirit: Master Ref I.F "(12) Option mispriced as dead" (NKTR, BIOA, STOK, First Nordic): failed/forgotten story, downside emotionally capitulated, balance sheet survives, one proof point restores probability mass — "convexity, not value". The engine renders it as a beaten-down CASH COW, i.e. the Wolf 8 / Pernas "low-multiple operational inflection" cousin rather than the optionality thesis.
- As coded: is_operating & mcap ≥$10M & beaten_down_any(0.40) & cash yield >5% on any of four lenses (fcf / owner-earnings / robust / cash_return_ev) & ebitda_margin>0 & _op_viable(0) & _roce_now_ok & nde ≤3. Spirited: −ts_dist_hi260, +ts_r13, tc_fcf_pos/tc_fcf_years, fcf_yield. 1,617 firers (A1 watch band).
- Lineage: beaten_down_any panel-first (ts_dist_hi52 / ts_dist_hi260 / ts_r52, snapshot fallbacks) — A1 A.4 "≥40% below the 5y high" is one OR lens among eight; "no longer collapsing (ts_r13>−0.10)" is a lens only. owner_earnings_yield EDGAR-tilted; robust_cash_yield / cash_return_ev derive; nde snapshot (99 on EBITDA ≤0 → fails, fine); tc_fcf_* global 8 FY.
- Spirit gaps: the OPTION is absent — no dormant asset, remaining TAM, catalyst (new data / partner / management) and no test that the market prices it at zero (EV ≤ core FCF × ~6–8 would say the stub is free). "Capitulated" is depth, not exhaustion (no volume dry-up, ts_wks_since_lo260, coverage collapse). A required 5% cash yield with positive EBITDA describes a profitable value stock in a drawdown — the same names as templeton_pessimism / xr_quality_crisis / oak_deep_value; the genuinely "dead" optional things (NKTR/BIOA-type, no FCF) are excluded by construction and land only in biotech_deep_value if biotech.
- Methodology: beaten_down_any(0.40) admits a 40% 52-week fall from a 5-year high — "dead" needs the long horizon (ts_dist_hi260 ≤0.5, A2 B.31) as core; cash_return_ev is enterprise while fcf_yield is equity, and four cash lenses are one fact (a levered name passes on either); three profitability gates (_roce_now_ok, _op_viable, ebitda_margin>0) on a "dead" thesis.
- FMP augmentations: ts_dist_hi260 (wired) as core; ts_wks_since_lo260 / ts_vol_spike (wired) for exhaustion; `insider-trading/statistics` (US) buying in the drawdown (A2 B.31 exceptional); `institutional-ownership` newPositions vs closedPositions (nuance 7: fleeing vs entering); `earnings-calendar` / 8-K stream for a dated proof point; `analyst-estimates` numAnalystsEps decline = "forgotten".
- Suggestions: 1 [method] core = ts_dist_hi260 ≤0.5 & ts_r13 > −0.10; current rule → watch (A1 A.4 open). 2 [spirit] price the option at zero explicitly: EV / core FCF ≤8 (or cash_return_ev ≥12%) so the cash cow alone covers the price. 3 [spirit] "forgotten" leg (sent_n_analysts_d12<0 or observed ≤2) and insider/13F re-entry as exceptional. 4 [method] merge the four cash-yield lenses (max) and split the family: `dead_option` (cash cow in a drawdown) vs an optionality variant without the FCF requirement.

### arch_derate_through_growth  (line 1497)
- Spirit: Compendium 3 Gardner Sub-B "Already re-rated: the multiple compressed 50%+ through earnings growth, not price decline — grew into and past its valuation" (Nvidia P/E 131→40×); engine: SNAP/MNDY/HUBS from 2021, the complement of narrative_lag (no sector cheapness anchor). Compendium D "value in growth" variant is the nearest other source.
- As coded: _dg_any = any horizon ≥2y (sales_2y/ebit_2y via bs_coil; sales/ebit/fcfps 3y & 5y via fmp_st per-share growth vs ts_r156/ts_r260) where the RAW outrun ≥ threshold (log 1.2…1.6) AND the advance is plausible (_adv: 15–300% over 2y; 5–60%/yr on long spans; sales only where opm_chg ≥ −2pp); & is_operating & mcap ≥$10M. Surfaces derate_growth_extent/years/horizons/froth/share/absorbed. Tiered via spirit_spec lenses (per-share 5y/3y growth, op_margin, share, absorbed, years, −ev_sales).
- Lineage: fmp_st_*_ps_{3,5}y_g (fmp_statements, global, 2-year-averaged endpoints, per share); revenue_3y/5y_cagr fallback less _sh3/_sh5 (5y shares only where years_of_history ≥6); bs_coil_* base_snapshot (82% INV); ts_r156/ts_r260 total return. Per share and single-currency throughout — sound.
- Spirit gaps: Sub-B needs the multiple to compress THROUGH GROWTH, not price decline; the rule accepts any raw outrun, including a −70% price on +30% sales (destruction, the "man in a hole"). derate_growth_share measures exactly this discrimination but is a score term, not a gate. No "rich multiple deflated" leg: a name that outran from 0.5× to 0.3× sales is a value stock lagging, not froth normalising — the froth column is "for reference" and uses today's SECTOR norm, not the name's own starting multiple. No forward test that growth continues (Sub-B names keep compounding).
- Methodology: 3y and 5y lenses share fmp_st endpoints (correlated horizons counted as independent); sales_2y prorates a 3-year share change to 2 years; the only quality gates are is_operating and mcap — a −40% op-margin grower at a collapsed price fires (op_margin is a lens).
- FMP augmentations: `enterprise-values` history (fmp_dyn, global, ~16k done) → EV/Sales at t−3y/t−5y vs now (the actual ≥50% compression, and whether the start was above the sector norm); `financial-growth` per-FY per-share growth consistency (compounded every year, not one step); `analyst-estimates` forward revenue (still compounding); `historical-market-capitalization` (from/to) as the PIT mcap path where EV history is absent.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] gate on derate_growth_share ≥0.5 — at least half the compression came through growth, otherwise it is the fall. 2 [spirit] require a prior premium: EV/Sales at the horizon start ≥1.5× sector norm (fmp_dyn EV history) or froth>0. 3 [method] profitability floor (_profit_present or fqx_inc_ebit_margin>0) — "grew into profitability" is a lens but a burner can fire. 4 [data] read the compression from the dated EV series rather than inferring it as growth − return.

### arch_discounted_vehicle  (line 1538)
- Spirit: Master Ref I.G "(8) Discounted vehicle with a catalyst to prove NAV" (Molten, Seraphim, HG Capital, Zegona) — value is there, needs a MECHANISM (asset sale, tender, buyback, liquidation, activist); failure mode "governance trap: the controller has no incentive to close it". Wolf holdco recipe: price/NAV<0.7, (cash+quoted stakes)/mcap>0.5, overhead<3%.

A1 A.6 "no material upgrade"; A2 matrix LEG "surface evt_sc13d_date ≤365d as a catalyst score term" — not done (only the mb_* recipes read evt_sc13d_date).
- Lineage: pb master; cash_gt_ev_flag / net_cash_pct derive (level-over-mcap, FX-corruptible per A2 §C, sane-capped ≤100%); nde snapshot (99 unknown → permissive). Every input is a snapshot.
- Spirit gaps: the CATALYST — the defining half of I.G — is absent: no buyback / tender / asset-sale / activist / liquidation leg; the rule is "net-cash operator below 0.85× book", which negative_ev_value, tangible_value, oak_asset_floor and liger_asset_backed also fire on (five flags, one fact). "Vehicle" (holdco / trust / fund with a NAV) is what is_operating EXCLUDES — every Master Ref example lands in oak_nav_discount instead. No governance-trap guard (controller ≥60% with no buyback).
- Methodology: the discount is to BOOK, not to realisable NAV — plant and goodwill pass (cluseau_realizable_book covers). nde 99 permissive lets EBITDA-negative names skip the debt contradiction. $2B cap and mcap>0 fine.
- FMP augmentations: evt_sc13d_date (wired, US) ≤365d; `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` CO/HC ≥50% → controlled_sub_flag (the trap); fq_shares_yoy ≤ −2% or fmp_st repurchases (global buyback = self-catalyst); `mergers-acquisitions-latest` / sec-filings tender (US); investments_associates (wired) for realisable NAV; `historical-market-capitalization` for whether the discount is narrowing.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] catalyst leg as core: (fq_shares_yoy ≤ −0.02 | buyback_yield ≥2%) | 13D ≤365d | tender/M&A event | dividend initiation — no mechanism, no vehicle thesis (A2 matrix LEG open). 2 [spirit] governance-trap veto or watch: insider ≥60% with no buyback. 3 [method] cluster with negative_ev_value / tangible_value / oak_asset_floor in archetype_count. 4 [method] discount to REALISABLE assets: (cash + investments_associates − debt)/mcap ≥0.5 (Wolf's recipe) rather than pb alone.

### arch_diversified_segments  (line 2073)
- Spirit: engine-native resilience label (4+ segments, HHI ≤0.40). No practitioner source treats diversification as alpha; the Compendium/Master Ref run the OTHER way — Master Ref I.C "hidden segment inside a bad consolidated screen", Compendium A technique (9) "sum-of-the-parts / holdco discount collapse": diversification is the SOURCE of mispricing, not a quality. Tillinghast "certainty" is the only positive reading.
- As coded: is_operating & segment_count ≥4 & segment_hhi ≤0.40 & (fcf>0 | ebitda_margin>5%) & _roce_now_ok. Not tiered/spirited; 235 firers; counted in archetype_count; cash-burner scrub applies.

- Spirit gaps: carries no mispricing thesis; where the sources use multi-segment structure it is as a SOTP / hidden-value setup (xr_segment_justifies_whole, xr_hidden_segment_compounder already cover that). Segment count without segment margins cannot tell diversification from a roll-up of unrelated low-margin lines (Deden's "acquisition-dependent growth" red flag). Guards are byte-identical to geographic_global.
- Methodology: FMP product-line segmentation inflates count (six SKUs = "diversified"); no materiality floor per segment (55/25/15/5 clears HHI 0.40); a positive label with an FCF-or-margin floor is a quality assertion at base rate.
- FMP augmentations: none that change the thesis; segment_hhi_delta (wired) for diversifying vs concentrating; segment count consistent across FYs (a structure, not a reclassification).
