### arch_dividend_verified_value  (line 3992)
- Spirit: forensic F10 (engine): a fat payout covered by BOTH earnings and FCF at a sub-book price proves the earnings are cash — the global, non-EDGAR forensic lens. Sources: Wolf 8 "dividends/buybacks material (>3–5%)" and the Wolf table "dividend ≥5%"; Cundill point 5; Enrichment Map nuance 9 (a high yield on a recent CUT is a yield trap — the path matters).
- As coded: is_operating & mcap>0 & _fx_coherent & dividend_yield ≥6% & NI>0 & div_paid (= yield × local mcap) ≤70% NI & FCF>0 & div_paid ≤70% FCF & 0<pb<1 & _not_melting. Spirited: evt_div_raise_streak (+), evt_div_cut_2y (−), tc_uncov_payout_3y (−), dividend_yield (+). 153 firers, 4 exceptional.
- Lineage: dividend_yield provider TTM (Yahoo / FMP fill) — FX-corruptible (A2 §C: Σ adjDividend / price in listing currency); the payout is RECONSTRUCTED as yield × mcap, not read from the cash-flow line (fmp_st commonDividendsPaid / fq dividends exist); NI/FCF master local, _fx_coherent guards the basis. evt_div_* from fmp_events (2,000-row partial run); tc_uncov_payout_3y global 8 FY, lens only (A2 Z7 open).
- Spirit gaps: "hardest item to fake" is the CASH PAID, yet it is inferred from a provider yield; a special / one-off dividend passes as "fat, real" (frequency unknown); persistence (paid and covered in ≥3 FYs, the matrix exceptional) is absent from the gate; the yield-trap path (cut in 2y) is a lens, not a veto. The forensic proof is COVERAGE, but 6% AND pb<1 turns it into a 153-name deep-value screen.
- Methodology: 70% of NI and 70% of FCF on ONE TTM — a cyclical peak covers what the trough will not (tc_uncov_payout_3y answers this and sits only in the score); a 6% yield is ordinary in BRL/TRY and extreme in JPY (no market-relative frame); yield × mcap can mix an ADR price basis with a local mcap.
- FMP augmentations: `dividends` (global, deep; A2 §0.2) → Σ adjDividend 12m / price, frequency, raise streak, cuts, specials (irregular amounts); fmp_st commonDividendsPaid (cash-flow line, global) for div_paid; tc_uncov_payout_3y (wired) into the gate; `ratios` dividendPayoutRatioTTM as a cross-check.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] measure the payout from the cash-flow line and the yield from `dividends` history in listing currency; exclude specials (A2 §C dividend_yield open). 2 [spirit] persistence as core: tc_uncov_payout_3y==0 and evt_div_cut_2y==0 where observed (A2 Z7 open). 3 [method] market-relative yield: ≥1.5× the listing market's median payer yield instead of a flat 6%. 4 [spirit] widen to pb<1.5 with yield ≥5% and keep pb<1 as exceptional — coverage is the thesis, sub-book is the bonus.

### arch_double_inflect  (line 2315)
- Spirit: engine-native (Mauboussin): NOPAT-ROIC and cash-ROIC both crossed zero from below in the latest FY — a real cash inflection, not accruals. Sources: Compendium B/8 Andreola "first profitable quarter is the strongest footprint"; Master Ref VI "operating income positive or inflecting"; Master Ref I.G (20) KPI crosses a threshold (KORU first positive operating margin); Yartseva profitability LEVEL present.
- As coded: is_operating & roic_inflection_flag==1 & cash_roic_inflection_flag==1 & rev_yoy>0 & _not_melting & not shares_yoy>15% (missing passes). Not tiered/spirited. 187 firers. A1 A.6 "no material upgrade"; A2 matrix LEG "confirm with fqx_roic_ttm>0" — open.
- Lineage: both flags = EDGAR (edgar_roic_roiic: prior FY ≤0 & latest >0) NaN-filled by fmp_st_roic_inflection_flag / fmp_st_cash_roic_inflection_flag (global annual). FY-dated — up to ~15 months stale against the quarterly turns already on disk (fmp_dyn_*_turned_positive, bs_ebit_turned, fqx_eps_turned). shares_yoy master (split-contaminated possible).
- Spirit gaps: an ANNUAL crossing is late for a "1–2 quarters before the run" footprint; no magnitude (−0.1% → +0.1% is a crossing; no ROIC ≥5% / op margin ≥3% floor); no "not yet priced" leg (Compendium 8: behaviour change high, reaction low) — nothing tests ts_r52 or the earnings-week reaction. Overlaps roic_inflect (which ORs the same flags plus the quarterly turn) and kpi_threshold on the same names.
- Methodology: the cash flag corroborates the NOPAT flag only if the cash measure excludes working-capital releases (A1 B.1: FCF-first-positive is the noisiest, 52% of kpi_threshold); no check that the prior-year negative was an impairment year (a big-bath year then a normal one "inflects" — xr_bigbath_rebound territory); rev_yoy>0 one print; a 15% dilution cap is generous for "not funded by dilution".
- FMP augmentations: fmp_dyn_opinc_turned_positive / fqx_eps_turned / bs_ebit_turned (wired, dated) as the timely leg; fqx_roic_ttm>0 (matrix); fq_chg_wc/fq_cfo (wired) to veto a working-capital-release "cash" inflection; ts_r52 / evt_react_last for "unpriced"; `earnings-surprises-bulk` for the first-clean-quarter beat.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] quarterly path: (annual double cross) OR (fqx_roic_ttm crossed >0 on TTM & CFO-based cash ROIC >0), and require fqx_roic_ttm>0 NOW where present (A2 matrix LEG open). 2 [method] magnitude floor (ROIC ≥5% or op margin ≥3%) and an impairment-year veto (op_margin_delta not > +30pp). 3 [spirit] "unpriced" exceptional: ts_r52 ≤ market median or evt_react_beats_4q ≤0. 4 [method] WC-release veto fq_chg_wc/fq_cfo ≤0.5 (as understated_earnings); cluster with roic_inflect / kpi_threshold in archetype_count.

### arch_dta_reversal  (line 4117)
- Spirit: forensic F17 (engine): a large deferred-tax VALUATION ALLOWANCE in a business that has turned profitable — the allowance reverses (one-off equity/EPS boost) and future earnings compound tax-free; "the data-precise cousin of the NOL shell". Sources: Compendium III assembly block (2) "NOL tax assets — quantum, §382(l)(5) survival, valuation allowance"; Master Ref III "NOL shells (Section 382)"; Compendium A technique (7).
- As coded: is_operating & real sector & mcap>0 & VA>0 & VA/mcap ≥0.15 & (op_margin>0 | roce>0) & _not_melting. Not tiered/spirited. 213 firers. A1 A.6 "no material upgrade"; Enrichment Map: FMP cannot fill (XBRL footnote); A2 matrix NONE.
- Lineage: deferred_tax_valuation_allowance = EDGAR DeferredTaxAssetsValuationAllowance (US SEC filers only, annual, latest 10-K/20-F — up to ~15 months stale); VA is in the filer's reporting currency over a LOCAL market_cap with NO _fx_coherent (the sibling forensic gates have it; a 20-F IFRS filer's EUR VA over a USD mcap passes unguarded). op_margin / roce snapshot.
- Spirit gaps: a VA reverses on SUSTAINED profitability (ASC 740 "more likely than not", in practice ~3 years of cumulative income) — one positive op-margin year, or a positive roce on a negative op margin (asset-sale gain), is not that; ni_avg / n_yrs_positive_* / fqx_eps_pos_share_8 exist and are unused. The shield's size vs FUTURE earnings (VA / NI_ttm = years tax-free) is unmeasured; VA/mcap is a price ratio. No §382 guard (equity raise / change of control limits the NOL) though nol_shell and xr_nol_shield check shares_yoy. The already-reversed case (VA fell sharply YoY = the one-off already printed and flattered EPS) is not excluded — the prior-context VA is in the EDGAR cache. Three flags on one tax asset (dta_reversal / nol_shell / xr_nol_shield).
- Methodology: no cheapness leg (the shield can already be in the price); no dilution veto; stale by a year during exactly the period the VA releases.
- FMP augmentations: none for the VA itself (footnote); fq cash-tax vs book-tax wedge (wired for xr_cash_tax_advantage) to see the shield ACTIVE; fqx_eps_pos_share_8 ≥0.75 as sustained profitability; `sec-filings-search` 8-K / 10-Q text "release of valuation allowance" (US) as the dated event; fq_shares_yoy for §382.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] sustained profitability as core: ni_avg>0 & fqx_eps_pos_share_8 ≥0.75 (or n_yrs_positive_roic ≥3). 2 [method] add _fx_coherent and a shares_yoy ≤8% guard as the NOL siblings do. 3 [spirit] exceptional = VA / NI_ttm ≥3 with observed cash-tax rate ≤10%; surface VA down ≥50% YoY as `dta_released_flag` (already happened). 4 [method] cluster dta_reversal / nol_shell / xr_nol_shield in archetype_count.

### arch_durable_reinvestment  (line 1775)
- Spirit: Mauboussin ROIIC / Mayer 100-bagger "reinvest at high returns over a cycle"; Compendium 1 Phelps/Mayer tests (2) sustained ROIC >15% and (3) a long reinvestment runway; Master Ref II Yartseva "asset growth >0 but < EBITDA growth" (aggressive investment only when covered).
- As coded: is_operating & _roce_now_ok & roic_lindy ≥10% & n_yrs_positive_roic ≥4 & roiic_lindy 15–100% & (asset_3y_cagr>5% | roiic ≥20%). Spirited: roiic_lindy, roic_lindy, ts_maxdd_5y (price-lindy), fqx_ebit_ttm_g. 1,805 firers (A1 watch band; the price-lindy tier A1 A.3 named — 1,151 — is implemented as a lens, not a tier).
- Lineage: roic_lindy / roiic_lindy / n_yrs_positive_roic / asset_3y_cagr EDGAR (US) NaN-filled by fmp_st_* (global annual; rolling-3y median ROIIC — A1: 92% FMP-filled, noisy on small deltas). ts_maxdd_5y panel; fqx_ebit_ttm_g global TTM. All FY-annual.
- Spirit gaps: "durable" = 4 positive ROIC years (the base rate of profitable firms, A1 §0.8) plus a lindy MEDIAN — no through-cycle minimum (tc_min_opm>0) and no per-share compounding CONSISTENCY (FG per-FY per-share growth; A2 B.14 / matrix EXC open): a compounder that compounded in 2 of 5 years qualifies. No runway leg (Phelps test 3). The asset-light escape (roiic ≥20%) is right in spirit but lets a no-growth cash cow with a noisy 20% ROIIC on a tiny capital delta fire without reinvesting anything.
- Methodology: ROIIC on small capital deltas explodes (banded ≤1.0, but a 60% print on a 1% delta passes); asset_3y_cagr is TOTAL assets (acquisitions, leases, cash piles count as reinvestment); nothing is date-matched; overlaps cash_reinvest / qarp / reinvest_inflect / cheap_per_roiic on the same lindy columns.
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth` fiveY/threeY RevenueGrowthPerShare and OperatingCFGrowthPerShare on each FY row (global, per share — A2 B.14); tc_min_opm (wired) never a loss year; fq capex + fq_ppe_net path (capex/D&A>1.2 or net PP&E growth) for REAL reinvestment; `owner-earnings` growthCapex (global, cached) = the reinvestment dollars directly.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] exceptional tier (A2 B.14 open): FG fiveYRevenueGrowthPerShare ≥61% and fiveYOperatingCFGrowthPerShare ≥61% on each of the last 5 FY rows & tc_min_opm>0. 2 [method] measure reinvestment as growthCapex/D&A or net PP&E + working-capital growth, not total assets. 3 [method] ROIIC validity: capital delta ≥5% of invested capital over the window, else treat ROIIC as unmeasured (no pass via the ≥20% escape). 4 [method] cluster the lindy-ROIIC siblings in archetype_count.

### arch_evsales_derating  (line 6345; tier 6361)
- Spirit: engine-native "EV/Sales compressing while sales rip — a coiled spring". Pernas "fallen platform with improving motor" (XMTR: the price says impairment, primary operating data says the marketplace is strengthening; require improving unit economics and a plausible route to operating leverage; 8/9 hit rate); Compendium 8 earnings divergence; Master Ref I.A (15) narrative lag. Distinct from derate_through_growth by the cheapness cap (EV/Sales ≤6) and the sales-only frame.
- As coded: mcap $50M–$20B & revenue ≥$5M & rev_yoy ≥15% & 3y CAGR not <0 & derate_any (1y rev_yoy − snapshot stock return ≥15% | 3y sales CAGR − annualised roc_3_5y ≥15% | bs_coil_rev ≥0.15 | exceptional evsg with confirmed growth and a non-negative gap) & 0.10<EV/Sales ≤6 & (GM ≥20% | EBITDA>0 | FCF>0) & not (EBITDA<0 & FCF<0 & GM<40%) & is_operating. `_tier`: core = fq_rev_growth ≥15% & (derate_gap_1y = log1p(fq_rev_growth) − log1p(ts_r52) ≥0.15 | bs_coil_rev ≥0.15); exceptional = either ≥ log 1.5; elite = derate_gap_1y; measured = fq_rev_growth present. Lenses derate_gap_1y, bs_coil_rev, fq_rev_growth, fqx_inc_ebit_margin. 1,735 watch → 824 core, 76 exceptional.
- Lineage: base-rule _stk_ret = price_yoy → momentum_12m → roc_12m (snapshot; A1 §0.3 noisy; A2 Z3 swap → ts_r52 still open in the BASE rule — a name without fq_rev_growth keeps the snapshot path via `measured`); roc_3_5y lynch tape; bs_coil_rev base_snapshot; ev_sales master snapshot; fq_rev_growth date-matched TTM; fqx_inc_ebit_margin TTM.
- Spirit gaps: "EV/Sales compressing" is never read from EV/Sales — it is inferred as growth minus PRICE return, which ignores net-debt changes and DILUTION: a company that issued 30% more shares with a flat price and +30% sales has an unchanged per-share sales multiple, yet derate_gap_1y ≈ log 1.3 (narrative_lag's sales_1y subtracts _sh1; this tier does not). Pernas's "improving motor" is a lens only; the A2 B.6 spec (TTM ≥25%, FG 3y per-share ≥52%, EV/Sales down ≥30% on the dated EV series, inc margin ≥15%) is open. Clinical biotech not excluded (first-product sales ripping at 5× EV/Sales).
- Methodology: the exceptional tier at log 1.5 on a 1-year gap rewards a 50% outrun, which is mostly price collapse (the anti-thesis the deep-audit note fought at the gate); the 1y snapshot gap pairs an annual rev_yoy window with a price year; GM ≥40% burners pass by design (documented), lower-GM burners are vetoed — fine. EV/Sales ≤6 with a $20B cap fine.
- FMP augmentations: `enterprise-values` history (fmp_dyn, global, partial) → EV/Sales now vs 1y/2y ago directly (A2 B.6; matrix H); fq_shares_yoy (wired) for the per-share gap; `financial-growth` threeYRevenueGrowthPerShare (durable, per share); `analyst-estimates` forward revenue (the spring is loaded only if growth continues); `historical-market-capitalization` + fq net debt where EV history is absent.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] make derate_gap_1y per share (subtract log1p(fq_shares_yoy)) and swap _stk_ret → ts_r52 in the base rule (A2 Z3 open). 2 [spirit] core corroboration from the dated EV series: EV/Sales_now ≤0.8× EV/Sales_1y (or ≤0.7× 2y) where fmp_dyn covers the name (A2 B.6 open). 3 [spirit] exceptional = fqx_inc_ebit_margin ≥0.15 & FG 3y per-share ≥52% (the improving motor), not a deeper 1-year gap. 4 [spirit] `& ~_is_clinical_biotech` (A2 Z2 pattern).

### arch_exceptional_evsg  (line 5814)
- Spirit: engine-native EV/Sales-to-growth (capital-structure-neutral PSG); Compendium B "room to expand (<3× sales)"; Master Ref VI Andreola "25%+ revenue growth PER SHARE"; Yartseva LEVEL of profitability (the _profit_present gate cites it). Companion of cheap_sales_scaler (above) and lynch_evgy.
- As coded: 0<mcap<$20B & revenue ≥$5M & (evsg 0.002–0.05 | psg 0.0025–0.06 when evsg missing) & TTM growth (fq_rev_growth, rev_yoy_c fallback) ≥20% & (≤100% | 3y CAGR ≥15%) & 0.15 ≤EV/Sales ≤4 & _profit_present & is_operating. Not tiered/spirited. 1,144 firers.
- Lineage: evsg = ev_sales / (rev_yoy × 100) in derive (ANNUAL snapshot rev_yoy) while the growth gate reads the date-matched fq TTM — the ratio and the gate use different growth prints (A2 matrix PROXY "growth on date-matched TTM" is half done); ev_sales master; _profit_present = EBITDA>0 | op_margin>0 | fcf_yield>0 (one lens suffices).
- Spirit gaps: growth is TOTAL revenue, not per share — no shares guard at all (cheap_sales_scaler's audited gap, tenbagger_credible's stable_share_count; MCC: "dilution is the cardinal sin"); acquired growth is not organic (the comment concedes it) and there is no M&A tell (goodwill growth, acquisitions line); no unit-economics leg — a 0.03 EVSG at 1× sales on 8% gross margin is a distributor, not a scaler; no attention leg though the thesis is "the market is not paying for growth".
- Methodology: EVSG ≤0.05 at EV/Sales 4 needs ≥80% growth, so the band is dominated by base-effect growers below 1× sales, and the 3y-CAGR corroboration only bites above 100% YoY; the psg fallback compares equity price to EV thresholds; fcf_yield>0 alone satisfies _profit_present (working-capital FCF on negative EBITDA); no cash-burn veto (evsales_derating has one).
- FMP augmentations: fq_shares_yoy (wired) for per-share growth; fq goodwill/intangibles delta or `cash-flow` acquisitionsNet (global) as the M&A tell; `key-metrics` grossProfitMargin; `analyst-estimates` numAnalystsRevenue ≤2 (unnoticed growth); `financial-growth` threeYRevenueGrowthPerShare for durability.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] per-share growth: fq_rev_growth − fq_shares_yoy ≥20% and ~(fq_shares_yoy>0.10). 2 [spirit] organic tell: veto where acquisitions ≥25% of the revenue increase (or goodwill grew faster than revenue). 3 [method] compute EVSG on the same date-matched TTM growth the gate uses; _profit_present via EBITDA>0 or op_margin>0 (fcf-only → watch). 4 [method] GM ≥25% or GM rising as the scaler floor; cluster with cheap_sales_scaler in archetype_count.

### arch_expensed_growth_value  (line 3838)
- Spirit: forensic F3 (engine, Novy-Marx gross-profitability lens): fat gross margin + thin operating margin = intangible investment expensed through the P&L; cheap on gross earnings power while the P/E screens it out. Sources: Master Ref I.C "(19) Temporarily worse than it looks"; Enrichment Map nuance 3 (R&D intensity separates expensed growth from SG&A bloat); MCC/Deden "TAM first, unit economics later" as the anti-pattern.
- As coded: is_operating & _fx_coherent & revenue ≥$5M & 40% ≤GM ≤98% & GP/mcap ≥0.50 & −15%<op_margin<10% & 10% ≤rev_yoy ≤100% & not shares_yoy>5% (missing passes) & _not_melting. Not tiered/spirited. 217 firers. A1 A.6 "no material upgrade"; A2 matrix LEG "optional: R&D/SG&A growth > revenue growth".
- Lineage: gross_margin master; revenue_ttm and market_cap local (fx_coherent guarded — GP/mcap is single-currency, correct); op_margin snapshot; rev_yoy annual; shares_yoy master (EDGAR/Yahoo-tilted; fq_shares_yoy is wired and unused here). fmp_quarterly caches `researchAndDevelopmentExpenses` (as "rnd") and SG&A but emits neither as an fq column (SG&A feeds only the Beneish index).
- Spirit gaps: the "expensed growth" is ASSUMED from the margin gap — no R&D or S&M intensity leg, so SG&A bloat, a sub-scale cost base and a low-volume manufacturer fire identically (nuance 3). No test that the spend BUYS growth (opex growth tracking revenue growth — the matrix's own LEG) or that incremental margins are rising (the spend is leverageable). Overlaps xr_pre_scale_margin on the same names.
- Methodology: the −15% lower bound admits burners with negative FCF as long as one _not_melting cash lens is positive; no cash-burn veto; the dilution guard is missing-permissive for non-EDGAR names; GM ≤98% artefact guard is right; rev_yoy one annual print (A2 §C).
- FMP augmentations: fq_rnd / fq_sga from the CACHED quarterly payload (zero calls) → R&D/rev, (R&D+SG&A)/rev and their growth vs revenue; `key-metrics` researchAndDevelopementToRevenueTTM (global snapshot) as the cross-check; `financial-growth` rdexpenseGrowth / sgaexpensesGrowth (matrix LEG); fqx_inc_ebit_margin (wired) rising; fq_shares_yoy for the dilution guard globally.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] require the spend to be identifiable: R&D/revenue ≥10% or (R&D+S&M)/revenue ≥25% where the lines exist; missing → watch (nuance 3). 2 [spirit] the spend buys growth: opex growth ≤ revenue growth + 10pp AND fqx_inc_ebit_margin ≥ current op margin. 3 [method] dilution guard on fq_shares_yoy (global) and a cash-burn veto (fcf_yield ≥ −10%). 4 [method] cluster with xr_pre_scale_margin in archetype_count.

### arch_fastest_segment  (line 2149)
- Spirit: Master Ref I.C "(6) Hidden segment inside a bad consolidated screen" (Wavelock, Food Empire, Everus inside a legacy label); Liger "historical revenue and gross margin by segment … later segment results provide financial confirmation"; Pernas "growing green-energy segment" tells.
- As coded: is_operating & revenue ≥$20M & (_not_melting | segment engine: seg op-margin delta>0 or seg_margin_inflect_flag, with ebitda_margin>−20%) & rev_yoy>−10% & 3y CAGR not <−5% & segment_count ≥2 & seg_inflect_any (≥1 of 9 _confirm lenses: q YoY 25–200%, FY YoY, legacy YoY, accel ≥5%, seg op-margin delta ≥2pp, seg oplev ≥10%, share delta ≥2pp, dispersion ≥30%, _adv_breadth ≥3) & fastest-segment growth ≥10%. seg_inflect_score surfaced. Not tiered/spirited. 611 firers; segment scrub applies.
- Lineage: growth / share / dispersion EDGAR dimensional (US) NaN-filled by fmp_seg_* (revenue-product-segmentation, global, FY + quarterly-matched YoY with _q_confirm / consec_growth guards); the segment MARGIN lenses (_fs_omd, seg_oplev, seg_margin_inflect_flag) are EDGAR-only (FMP segments are revenue-only — Enrichment Map limits), so the "engine escape" and two of nine lenses are US-only; _adv_breadth consolidated annual/TTM.
- Spirit gaps: "hidden" is the thesis and is unmeasured — nothing tests that the consolidated screen is BAD (consolidated growth well below the segment's, or the parent on a legacy multiple: EV/Sales below the sector norm) or that the segment is MATERIAL (fastest_segment_share ≥15%: a 3%-of-revenue segment growing 60% moves nothing — the Master Ref failure mode). The whole-company corroboration lens (_adv_breadth ≥3) is the OPPOSITE of hidden (admits names whose consolidated numbers already show it). No price-lag leg (an engine the market already priced is not hidden).
- Methodology: nine lenses, any one suffices; q YoY / FY YoY / legacy YoY are one fact on three time bases; dispersion ≥30% fires on a shrinking laggard as much as a growing leader; FMP product lines reclassify year to year (a renamed line reads as +100%; the q-confirm guard covers the q lens only). $20M floor and the wind-down guard are sound.
- FMP augmentations: fmp_seg_fastest_share_rec and fmp_seg_core_declining (both wired) for materiality and the Wavelock shape (legacy core shrinking while the engine grows); the narrative_lag _below_norm machinery for the "bad wrapper" multiple; ts_r52 / bs_coil_rev for the unpriced test; `earning-call-transcript` segment commentary (matrix TR).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] materiality core: fastest_segment_share ≥15% or share_delta ≥3pp. 2 [spirit] "hidden": consolidated rev_yoy ≤ fastest − 15pp or seg_core_declining==1, AND EV/Sales ≤ sector norm. 3 [method] collapse the three YoY lenses (max), drop dispersion as a stand-alone trigger, move _adv_breadth to the score. 4 [method] tier it: core = revenue-side (global), exceptional = segment margin inflecting (EDGAR) — so the US-only margin legs are a bonus, not a hidden bias.

### arch_financials_value  (line 2948)
- Spirit: engine-native home for banks/insurers excluded from EV-based screens: cheap on book with real returns. Sources: Master Ref II Yartseva high B/M + positive profitability; Compendium 4 Tillinghast ROE >10–12% sustained; Master Ref III/VIII Korea Value-Up / Japan PBR reform (sub-book financials with a mandated catalyst); Compendium preferreds sleeve (Korean 우선주, US bank preferreds).
- As coded: is_financial & ~is_reit & ~fund vehicle (closed-end / BDC / trust / fund / asset manager) & mcap ≥$50M & 0.15 ≤pb<1.0 & roe ≥10% & (0<P/E ≤15 | P/E NaN). Not tiered/spirited. 285 firers. A1 A.6 "no material upgrade"; A2 matrix LEG: exceptional = BVPS compounding ≥8%/yr in ≥4 of 5 FYs (FG) & no dividend cut — open.
- Lineage: pb / roe / p_e master snapshot (Yahoo / FMP fill; roe provider, one period); industry strings for the vehicle exclusion; no lindy/tc columns apply (financials are outside the EDGAR ROIC harvest; FMP `ratios` annual is the only multi-year source — Enrichment Map §1).
- Spirit gaps: a bank at 0.9× book on 10% ROE is fairly priced (ROE ≈ cost of equity); the mispricing is ROE well ABOVE what the P/B implies, or a CATALYST (Value-Up / PBR-reform disclosure, buyback, tender) — the reason the Master Ref's sub-book financials re-rate. No asset-quality / leverage lens (equity/assets, NPLs — the bank analogue of Tillinghast's "debt/EBITDA >4× is scary"); no capital-return leg (how a book discount closes); ROE is one period (a reserve-release year). Banks, insurers, brokers and lending holdcos share one P/B rule — insurers on book are a different animal (combined ratio, float).
- Methodology: pb / roe = P/E, so roe ≥10% & pb<1 implies P/E <10 — the P/E ≤15 leg is redundant except as the NaN pass-through; pb ≥0.15 ADR guard fine; mcap ≥$50M fine.
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth` bookValueperShareGrowth per FY (global; matrix LEG) for BVPS + DPS compounding; `dividends` (global) streak / no cut; `ratios` annual ROE persistence (3 of 4 FYs ≥8%); `balance-sheet` totalEquity/totalAssets (leverage floor, global); fmp_st repurchases / fq_shares_yoy (buyback below book = the bank cannibal); a JP/KR sub-book × reform-catalyst surfaced flag.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] price the return: core = pb ≤0.8 & roe ≥12% (or pb ≤ 0.8 × roe/0.10); keep pb<1 & roe ≥10 as watch. 2 [spirit] capital-return leg (dividend ≥3% or shares shrinking) and the reform catalyst (JP/KR sub-book) as exceptional (A2 matrix LEG open). 3 [method] ROE persistence (FG/ratios) and a leverage floor (equity/assets ≥6% for lenders). 4 [method] drop the redundant P/E leg (or P/E ≤10 only where roe is NaN); note insurers as an unmeasured sub-pool.

### arch_fixed_cost_demand_shock  (line 1501; reframed 1529)
- Spirit: Master Ref I.D "(5) Fixed-cost asset meeting a demand shock" (the Forced Thing: regulation / defence / grid demand hits a fixed-cost base — APi, Mueller Water, Cabot); Compendium D "how to find the torque: fixed costs → incremental revenue drops through; asset-heavy below replacement cost"; Wolf commodity recipe (high operating leverage, reasonable balance sheet).
- As coded (reframed; old rule → fixed_cost_demand_shock_watch): not financial/REIT & asset_heavy (fq net PP&E/assets ≥30%, else capex intensity ≥5%, else HEAVY_ASSET_SECTORS label) & rev_accel>0 & rev_yoy>0 & _not_melting & not (6<nde<90) & (EBITDA-margin delta 2–20pp | margin_shock_any | fqx_inc_ebit_margin_dt ≥30%). Spirited: fqx_inc_ebit_margin_dt, fq_rev_growth, tc_med_opm − op_margin (headroom), ebitda_margin_delta_yoy. 2,975 watch; A1 A.4 "measured asset intensity" IS implemented (asset_heavy(), 1152); the "+≥10% TTM growth" half (1,223) is not.
- Lineage: fq_ppe_net (includes lease ROU — documented) / fq_total_assets fmp_quarterly (global); capex_intensity derive; rev_accel master (annual YoY minus prior YoY, one FY step; Yahoo/EDGAR); rev_yoy snapshot; margin_shock_any = ORed YoY/TTM angles each held to a 2pp standard, incl. operating_leverage_ratio ≥1.5 (A2 §C fragile near zero growth); nde snapshot (99 unknown permissive by design); tc_med_opm through-cycle (global).
- Spirit gaps: the DEMAND SHOCK is inferred from one year of acceleration — no magnitude (rev_accel>0 is +0.1pp), no forced-demand signature (regulatory date, backlog, book-to-bill, "capacity constrained"), no industry frame (a shock hits a sector; one name accelerating is company-specific). "Below replacement cost" (Compendium D torque) unmeasured — pb / p_tb absent. The drop-through proof is one of three ORs, so a 2pp gross-margin move on 0.5% acceleration qualifies; "shock-sized" 2pp is drift for a 40%-margin business and a shock for a 5% one (no scaling).
- Methodology: rev_yoy>0 has no floor (A1 A.4: ≥10% TTM keeps 1,223 of 2,975); operating_leverage_ratio ≥1.5 with EBITDA yoy ≥5% still explodes at ~1% revenue growth; the leverage cap skips nde 99 (EBITDA ≤0), i.e. the most levered loss-makers are unchecked (mostly caught by _not_melting). Utilities stay in the pool by design (the reframed rule uses _not_fin_re, not is_operating), consistent with the comment.
- FMP augmentations: fq_rev_growth ≥10% (wired) as the shock floor; tc_med_opm − op_margin (wired) as the A2-matrix exceptional (op margin ≤ tc_med_opm − 5pp with TTM rev ≥ +10% = headroom to mid-cycle); industry-group revenue acceleration from the cached fq statements grouped by profile industry (zero calls) = the shock is sectoral; fq_defrev_to_rev rising as an order-book proxy; `earning-call-transcript` "backlog / capacity constrained / price increase" (targeted); p_tb / fq_p_tb for below-replacement.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] shock floor: fq_rev_growth ≥10% with rev_accel ≥ +3pp — a 0.1pp acceleration is not a shock (A1 A.4 open half). 2 [spirit] sectoral-shock leg: ≥40% of same-industry names accelerating, or a defrev/backlog build — the Forced Thing is exogenous. 3 [spirit] exceptional = headroom & drop-through: op margin ≤ tc_med_opm − 5pp & fqx_inc_ebit_margin ≥0.30 (A2 matrix EXC open). 4 [method] scale "shock-sized" to the margin base (delta ≥ max(2pp, 25% of the prior margin)).

### arch_flyover  (line 7968; tier 7982)
- Spirit: Master Ref IV Todd Wenning flyover / quiet compounders: durable moat (ROIC >15%) + boring essential business + excellent capital allocation + LOW coverage (<5, ideally 0) + family/insider control + strong FCF; hunting grounds JP (deepest pool), Mittelstand, AIM, Nordics, IN, TASE, B3, ASX, KOSDAQ (Okano Valve, TVE, Schaffer, Plasson, B&C Speakers — all 0 analysts).
- As coded: is_operating & not (n_analysts>5; missing passes) & insider ≥20% & (roce ≥15% not one-off | roic_lindy ≥15%) & (FCF>0 | n_yrs_positive_fcf ≥3) & op_margin>0 & _not_melting & nde ≤2. `_tier`: core = insider 20–60% | insider_buy_flag | buyback_yield ≥1%; exceptional = not (sent_n_analysts>2) & tc_fcf_pos==tc_fcf_years; elite roic_lindy; measured = insider or buyback_yield present. Lenses roic_lindy, −sent_n_analysts, insider_ownership_pct, tc_fcf_pos/tc_fcf_years. 2,631 watch → 1,538 core, 95 exceptional. A1 A.3 insider band IS implemented.
- Lineage: n_analysts Yahoo (+pew, US-tilted; missing → 0 → passes); sent_n_analysts grades-historical (US-centric; missing passes `~(x>2)` in the EXCEPTIONAL tier too); insider_ownership_pct Yahoo (corporate parents included — the 20–60% band mitigates); roce snapshot / roic_lindy EDGAR + fmp_st fill; tc_fcf_* global 8 FY; buyback_yield EDGAR-only and insider_buy_flag Form 4 — the two core ESCAPES are US-only.
- Spirit gaps: "unwatched" is proven by ABSENCE in both core and exceptional (A2 §C / Z5 open) — every non-US name with no grades feed is an "exceptional flyover", i.e. the tier equals "no data" in exactly the markets Wenning names. No geography / institutional-absence leg though flyover is the archetype's name (blindspot holds the country set; neither references the other). "Boring essential" and "excellent capital allocation" unmeasured (tc_uncov_payout_3y, fq_shares_yoy exist). Family control ≠ founder (A2 S2 key-executives open).
- Methodology: roce ≥15% single period (A2 §C fragile) OR lindy — acceptable; the US-only escapes make the core US-friendlier than the thesis; nde ≤2 fails on 99 (EBITDA ≤0) — fine. Overlaps owner_operator (same band) and quiet_compounder; the ONLY distinguishing leg (coverage) is the weakest-measured.
- FMP augmentations: `analyst-estimates` numAnalystsEps (cached, zero-call, global; A2 Z5) as observed coverage; `grades-consensus` bulk counts; `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` (no US fund holds it) and `etf/asset-exposure` passive <2% (A2 S8/S9) as the institutional-absence leg; `key-executives` founder titles (global, A2 S2); `shares-float` (float <50% = family block); fmp_st repurchases for a global buyback escape.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] observed neglect: numAnalystsEps ≤2 (or grades-consensus total ≤2) where present as core; missing → watch, never exceptional (A2 Z5 open). 2 [spirit] flyover leg: listing in the Master Ref IV markets OR (no US-fund N-PORT holder & passive share <2%) (A2 S8/S9 open). 3 [spirit] capital-allocation leg: tc_uncov_payout_3y==0 & (fq_shares_yoy ≤0 | dividend ≥2%). 4 [data] founder / family from key-executives + shares-float rather than the Yahoo insider level; replace the EDGAR-only escapes with fmp_st repurchases (A2 S2 open).

### arch_forensic_payout_confirmed  (line 5574)
- Spirit: engine "BOOST leg": a forensic / hidden-value member that is ALSO returning capital earns an extra archetype count (revealed preference: management monetising hidden value for owners). Sources: Cluseau "cheapness is a screen, action is the thesis"; Compendium D BUY signals "insider buying; share-count reduction"; Wolf 8 "dividends/buybacks material (>3–5% of mcap)".
- As coded: _forensic_any (OR of 14 forensic flags) & _payout_any (dividend_yield ≥0.5% | buyback_yield>0 | shares_yoy < −1%). Not tiered/spirited. 7,751 firers — the third-broadest flag in the engine (A1 B.1); counted 1:1 in archetype_count with no dedup cluster.
- Lineage: dividend_yield provider (FX-corruptible, A2 §C); buyback_yield EDGAR (US ~4%); shares_yoy master (split-contaminated); the 14 member flags. tc_uncov_payout_3y is wired and unused here (A2 Z7 open); fmp_st repurchases / commonDividendsPaid (global cash-flow lines) unused.
- Spirit gaps: A1 A.6 TAUTOLOGY open — dividend_verified_value, cannibal_at_discount and self_funded_returner are DEFINED by a payout, so confirmation is automatic for them; a 0.5% dividend is not "monetising hidden value" (Wolf: >3–5%); no cover (an uncovered payout from a forensic-cheap name is the Ponzi anti-pattern, not a boost); the action is neither dated nor accelerating (Cluseau's acceleration lives in cluseau_buyback_accel). As a meta-flag it double-counts every member's thesis: three forensic flags plus a 1% yield = +1 density for the same facts.
- Methodology: three ORed payout lenses of unequal materiality (0.5% yield, any buyback, 1% shrink); shares_yoy < −1% has no split guard (A2 §C splits open); no FCF cover; forensic_any breadth (9,521 union, A1) × a base-rate payout ≈ 7,751.
- FMP augmentations: tc_uncov_payout_3y (wired) for cover; fmp_st repurchases + commonDividendsPaid (global, same currency) for a material total yield; `dividends` history (global) for raises/cuts; `splits` for the share-drop guard; fq_shares_yoy (dated) as the shrink measure.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] exclude payout-defined members (dividend_verified_value, cannibal_at_discount, self_funded_returner) from _forensic_any so the flag is "non-payout forensic member + payout" (A1 A.6 open; 3,458 of 7,751 measured there). 2 [spirit] materiality and cover: total yield (div + repurchases/mcap) ≥3% or fq_shares_yoy ≤ −1%, AND tc_uncov_payout_3y==0 (A2 Z7 open). 3 [method] take it OUT of archetype_count — it is a modifier on other counts, not an independent thesis; surface as `forensic_payout_flag` or a score term. 4 [data] global buyback from fmp_st repurchases so the confirmation is not EDGAR-tilted.

---

## 10-line summary (slice rest_a, 37 archetypes)

1. Coverage: all 37 archetypes in slice_rest_a are audited (18 by the first pass, 19 in this resumption); the state-of-play note at the top still holds (layers merged, coiled-base currency bug closed, asset_heavy() live).
2. Biggest cross-cutting finding — archetype_count hygiene: a NEGATIVE flag (concentrated_segments) and a tautological modifier (forensic_payout_confirmed, 7,751) both ADD density; diversified_segments is a label with no thesis; and same-fact siblings (cannibal / cash-below-book / float / lindy-ROIIC / NOL families) are counted as independent evidence. Fix in the count, not in the rules.
3. "Missing = neglected" is the most repeated flaw: flyover (core AND exceptional), blindspot, coiled_base perception, awakening's `measured` escape — every one makes non-US names look unwatched for want of a feed (A2 Z5 open).
4. Multiples are inferred, never read: evsales_derating, asleep_unrerated and derate_through_growth infer compression as growth − price return while the dated `enterprise-values` history is on disk (A2 B.5/B.6 open).
5. Per-share discipline exists in narrative_lag but not in its siblings (evsales derate_gap_1y, coiled_base coils, asleep 1y coil, exceptional_evsg, fixed_cost) — dilution reads as a lag or a derate.
6. The catalyst / mechanism half is absent across the "unlocked" family: discounted_vehicle (no mechanism), dead_option (no option), dta_reversal (no sustained-profit test, no §382 guard), crisis_asset_backed (no crisis breadth, no senior-claims guard).
7. Snapshot → panel swaps are still open in BASE rules even where the tier reads the panel: evsales _stk_ret, asleep_unrerated _pyw_au, awakening roc/pct_off (A2 Z3) — the `measured` escapes route unmeasured names back to the noisy path.
8. US-only legs shape tiers silently: buyback_yield / insider_buy_flag core escapes (flyover, cannibal), segment-margin lenses (fastest_segment), DTA VA and ppe_gross (EDGAR), 13F/Form 4 ignition lenses; exceptional tiers built on them are US tiers.
9. Payouts are measured as yield × mcap from an FX-corruptible provider yield with no cover or persistence (dividend_verified, forensic_payout, balance_sheet_return) while tc_uncov_payout_3y and the cash-flow lines are wired (A2 §C / Z7 open).
10. Priority: (a) count hygiene (negatives, modifiers, clusters); (b) observed-neglect swap (numAnalystsEps, zero calls); (c) dated EV/Sales compression + per-share gaps; (d) catalyst legs from the wired evt_* / fq_shares / tc_* columns; (e) global buyback/dividend from fmp_st cash-flow lines.

# Slice rest_b: Single archetypes, slice B (37)

## Audit 3 — slice rest_b (37 archetypes)

Read-only audit of `archetype_tags.py` (line numbers = the dump's first assignment; where the engine later overrides the rule via `_reframe` / `_tier` / a second assignment, the override line is given too). Firer counts are from the current `archetype_tags.csv`. Sources: compendium (`archetype_compendium.txt`), master reference (`master_archetype_reference.txt`), Pernas, Liger/Byron Street, Wolf/Oak Bloke; FMP catalogue (`fmp_catalog.md`, A2 §A); prior audits A1 (`archetype_upgrade_audit.md`), A2 (`fmp_catalog_upgrade_audit.md`), matrix CSV. "US-only" legs are SEC/EDGAR-derived or FMP feeds A2 §A marks US.

Lineage shorthand: MASTER = `asymmetry_global.csv` (EDGAR / Yahoo / FMP-filled; `fmp_filled_<col>`=1 marks a fill), ST = `fmp_statements.csv` (`fmp_st_*`, FY history, global), FQ = `fmp_quarterly.csv` (`fq_*`, reporting currency, global), FQX = `fmp_quarterly_ext.csv`, TC = `fmp_throughcycle.csv`, TS = `ts_snapshot.csv` (weekly panel, total return), BS = `base_snapshot.csv`, EVT = `fmp_events.csv` (2,000-row partial; `ev_`→`evt_`), SENT = `fmp_sentiment.csv`, USF = `fmp_us_filings.csv` (SEC only), INST = `fmp_institutional.csv` (13F, US-listed), ENR = `fmp_enrichment.csv` (key-metrics/ratios TTM, global; insider/exec-comp US), SEG = `fmp_segments.csv`, F4 = `sec_insider_signals.csv` (Form 4, US), EES = `edgar_event_signals.csv` (US).

### arch_geographic_global  (line 2097)
- Spirit: engine-native descriptor ("AF — 4+ geographies reporting: currency + market diversification"). No source entry; nearest is the compendium Den Fujita "civilisation gap" (EM brand exporting to DM) and enrichment-map nuance 8 (true economic geography vs listing domicile).
- As coded: operating & `_roce_now_ok` & `geographic_region_count >= 4` & (FCF>0 | EBITDA margin >5%). No lenses (not spirited). 667 firers.
- Lineage: `geographic_region_count` ← EDGAR `edgar_segment_signals.py` (US dimensional harvest, ~10% of filers), NaN-filled from SEG `fmp_geo_count` (`revenue-geographic-segmentation`, global, FY). Default 0 when absent (missing = not global: correct sign for a positive claim). `fcf_ttm`, `ebitda_margin` MASTER.
- Spirit gaps: a count of reporting regions measures disclosure granularity, not diversification (a US filer reporting "US / non-US" scores 2; a JP filer with 5 regions scores 5 on 90% domestic revenue). No share-of-revenue test, no domestic-vs-listing-currency mismatch, no thesis of mispricing (nothing says the market misprices geographic breadth).
- Methodology issues: FMP geo schemas differ per filer (`fmp_seg_schema`), so "≥4" is not comparable across markets; no HHI / largest-region share (product segments have `segment_hhi`, geography has none); `_roce_now_ok` NaN-permissive is fine.
- FMP augmentations: `revenue-geographic-segmentation` (U, global) already wired; SEG already emits `fmp_geo_em_share`, `fmp_geo_china_share(_delta)` — unused here; `financial-statement-symbol-list` (N, 1 call, global) reporting vs trading currency for the "currency diversification" claim; `profile` country for domestic share.
- Suggestions:
  1. [spirit] Replace the count with a share test: largest-region share ≤0.6 and non-domestic share ≥0.4 (from SEG rows); keep the count as a surfaced descriptor.
  2. [method] Add a geographic HHI beside `segment_hhi` so "global" means dispersion, not disclosure.
  3. [spirit] Pair with a translation-drag lens (revenue geography in weak currencies vs a strong listing currency) so the flag carries a why-it-is-cheap; otherwise demote to `geographic_global_flag`.

### arch_growth_algo  (line 5878)
- Spirit: engine-native ("DLO"-style growth algorithm: gross-profit growth + operating leverage + shrinking share count compounding FCF/share, bought cheap on P/FCF). Master ref II (Yartseva: FCF/P the single strongest driver; profitability level, not growth rate) and VI (MCC: growing revenue and earnings, not diluting).
- As coded: mcap <$50bn; USD revenue ≥$20M; `rev_yoy` ≥15%; `oper_lev_any`; FCF>0; FCF growth ≥20% on any of (fcf_yoy | fcf_per_share_yoy | fqx_fcf_ps_g | cfo_yoy when both FCF lenses NaN); P/FCF 2–15 (USD mcap / USD FCF); `not_diluting` (soft: shares_yoy >2% excluded, missing passes); operating. 598 firers. No lenses.
- Lineage: `rev_yoy`, `fcf_yoy`, `cfo_yoy`, `fcf_per_share_yoy` MASTER (annual/TTM snapshot); `fqx_fcf_ps_g` FQX (date-matched TTM, global); `fcf_ttm_usd` MASTER (USD-converted); `oper_lev_any` = 6 YoY angles | 4 TTM-sequential | 3 raw-sequential via `_confirm` (MASTER derived). Nothing US-only.
- Spirit gaps: the comment says gross-profit growth but the leg reads revenue (`gross_profit_yoy` exists as `_gpy` elsewhere); the DLO "−5% share count" leg is absent (A1 A.2 open: `fq_shares_yoy <= -0.02`); no persistence (one year of +20% FCF is a base effect: FCF 1→1.2).
- Methodology issues: the `cfo_yoy` fallback changes meaning (CFO ≠ FCF) exactly where capex is unknown; `oper_lev_any` includes raw sequential moves (A1 §C fragile); no FCF-margin floor, so a 0.5% FCF-margin name with +20% FCF qualifies; `not_diluting` missing-passes contradicts the shrinking-count thesis.
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth` (N, global, ~25k calls) `weightedAverageSharesDilutedGrowth`, `fiveYOperatingCFGrowthPerShare` per FY → per-share persistence; `splits` (N, global) to guard the share-count leg; `owner-earnings` (U*, cached) maintenance capex for a truer FCF.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Use `_gpy` (gross_profit_yoy ≥15%) for the GP leg and `fqx_inc_ebit_margin >= 0.20` for operating leverage (A1 A.2 open).
  2. [spirit] Add the count leg: `fq_shares_yoy <= -0.02` or FG diluted-share growth <0 in ≥2 of 3 FYs as core; missing → `_watch`.
  3. [method] FCF-margin ≥3% (or FCF/mcap ≥3%) floor so +20% growth is off a real base; require the FCF lens present (drop the CFO fallback into `_watch`).

### arch_hidden_assets  (line 3724)
- Spirit: compendium Part I #6 "Kerrisdale / Scrapyard mismarked balance sheet" — cross-holdings at cost, property unrevalued, pension surpluses, strategic stakes worth more than the market cap; Wolf/Oak Archetype 5 holdco NAV discount (price/NAV <0.7, catalysts: realisation, tender, buyback, activist).
- As coded: operating & `_fx_coherent` (USD/local twin agreement, no `.F` / B-share lines) & `_hidden_pct` = (investments_associates + cash − total_debt)/mcap ≥0.25 & (net cash ≥10% | cash ≥30% mcap) & 0<pb<1.5 & `_not_melting` & (op margin>0 | FCF yield>0 | EBITDA>0). 1,995 firers. No lenses.
- Lineage: `investments_associates` EDGAR (US only) — NaN filled with 0, so for ~29k non-US names `_hidden_pct` collapses to net cash / mcap; `cash`, `total_debt`, `market_cap` MASTER local currency (same-currency ratio, good); `pb` MASTER.
- Spirit gaps: the scrapyard thesis is assets carried at COST worth multiples (carrying value understates); the code reads carrying value only, so the mismark itself is invisible (only `xr_stake_fv_gap` sees fair value, EDGAR, 4 firers). No property-at-cost lens, no catalyst (activist, parent-child unwind, TSE PBR reform), no "controlling shareholder incentive" failure-mode test (master ref I.G).
- Methodology issues: outside EDGAR this is `negative_ev_value`/`net_cash_returner` under another name (net cash ≥25% of mcap with pb<1.5); pb<1.5 "the portfolio is not being paid for" does not follow when the portfolio is inside book at cost; minority interest / preferred not netted; `_fx_coherent` needs both USD twins present (silently drops names with a missing `revenue_ttm_usd`).
- FMP augmentations: `balance-sheet-statement` (U, global) `longTermInvestments` / `otherNonCurrentAssets` as the non-EDGAR associate proxy (FQ layer); `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (U event-study, US) new 13D ≤365d as the catalyst; `financial-statement-symbol-list` (N, 1 call) currency map to replace the twin inference (A2 E4).
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Split into `hidden_assets_associates` (associate/LT-investment stakes ≥10% of mcap, any source) and let pure net-cash cases fall to `negative_ev_value`; today 90%+ of the pool is the latter.
  2. [data] Fill the associate line from FQ `longTermInvestments` (reporting currency, same basis as cash/debt) for non-US names — the compendium's cases (Keisei, Sapporo, Great Eagle) are all non-US.
  3. [spirit] Catalyst tier: `evt_sc13d_date <= 365d` (US) or a buyback/tender (`buyback_yield >= 0.02`) as `hidden_assets_exceptional`; surface `controlled_sub_flag` as the governance-trap warning.

### arch_ignition_fallen_angel  (line 6894)
- Spirit: compendium Part II E narratology ("man in a hole" — fall-then-rise is the most contagious arc) crossed with the base-ignition setup (Cassel discovery cycle: pre-discovery → catalyst → retail; Yartseva "buy near 12-month lows"). Engine-native composite.
- As coded: `arch_base_ignition == 1` & `bs_prior_dd <= 0.60` (a ≥40% drawdown before the base). Lenses: −bs_prior_dd, bs_cp_dvol_z13, bs_dvol_trend, bs_updown_vol, bs_rs26, bs_coil_rev. 40 firers.
- Lineage: all BS (weekly panel snapshot; `bs_prior_dd` in price ratio terms, currency-free). Inherits base_ignition's validity gate — A1 §0.1 P0 (dvol in listing currency) was to move to `ts_dvol26_usd`; confirm the ignition gate now reads the USD column.
- Spirit gaps: no test that the business survived the fall (a fallen angel whose sales also halved is just a base); no dated trigger (earnings reaction, new 5% holder, first profitable quarter — the "catalyst" stage of the discovery cycle); "fallen" is measured on price only, never on the narrative (coverage dropped, holders exited).
- Methodology issues: a fixed 0.60 cut on `bs_prior_dd` is absolute where own-history is meant (a 40% fall is normal for a high-beta name); the lens list double-counts volume (dvol_z13, dvol_trend, updown_vol) so ranking is volume-dominated.
- FMP augmentations: `earnings-surprises-bulk` (N, global, ~13 calls) → EVT `evt_react_last` ≥+8% as the dated trigger; `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` new 13D ≤90d (US); `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` (N, non-US reach) ≥3 US funds initiating; `historical-market-capitalization` (N) for a PIT prior-drawdown by mcap (survives share-count changes).
- Suggestions:
  1. [spirit] Add "business intact": `bs_coil_rev >= 0` or FQ TTM revenue ≥ −10% vs the pre-fall level — the arc needs a surviving thing (master ref I.E).
  2. [spirit] Exceptional = a dated trigger (A2 B.2): earnings-week reaction ≥+8% on a beat, or a new 13D/13G ≤90d, or N-PORT initiations.
  3. [method] Express "fallen" relative to the name's own 5-year drawdown history (`ts_maxdd_5y`): prior_dd deeper than 1.5× its median drawdown, rather than a flat 0.60.

### arch_insider_conviction  (line 5758)
- Spirit: compendium Part II A filing-signal sheet (Form 4 open-market purchases; clusters of 3+ within ~10 days; best >$500m cap) and master ref III (≥3 unique insiders, code P, ~2 weeks, ≥1 independent director, $25k+, at sentiment troughs; Lakonishok & Lee: purchases in smaller firms, clusters double the excess return).
- As coded: (cluster | 10%-owner | officer buy flag) & net-buyer flag & mcap <$20bn & `_not_melting` & value leg (financials/REITs pb<1; operating: EV/EBITDA ≤15 | pb<2.5 | FCF yield ≥3% | `cheap_any`). Lenses: −ts_dist_hi52, insider_distinct_buyers, fmp_insider_alignment_ratio. 574 firers.
- Lineage: all insider flags F4 (`sec_insider_buys.py`, SEC bulk Form 3/4/5 datasets, code P only) — **US-only; no non-US substitute exists in the catalogue** (FMP `insider-trading/*` is also SEC). `QUARTERS` is a hard-coded list (2025q3–2026q2): the "last 12 months" ages until someone edits it. Value legs MASTER; `ts_dist_hi52` TS.
- Spirit gaps: no purchase size vs mcap or vs pay (alignment ratio is a lens, not a gate; $25k floor absent); no independent-director test; "at sentiment troughs" is only a lens; no persistence (buying in ≥2 of the last 4 quarters).
- Methodology issues: the value OR is loose (pb<2.5 or EV/EBITDA ≤15 is most of the operating universe) so the archetype is essentially "any Form-4 cluster/officer buy"; a static quarter list makes signal age drift; `cheap_any` is a further OR.
- FMP augmentations: `insider-trading/statistics` (U event-study, US, PIT since 2007) `totalPurchases` by quarter → persistence; USF already holds `usf_ins_buy_quarters_4q`, `usf_ins_net_buy_4q` (zero-call, unused here); `insider-trading/search` `typeOfOwner` / `key-executives` to tag CEO/CFO buyers; `governance-executive-compensation` (U, US) for purchase ÷ salary.
- Suggestions:
  1. [data] Gate on `usf_ins_buy_quarters_4q >= 2` (already on disk) as core; keep the F4 window flags as `_watch` (A2 B.28 open).
  2. [method] Size floor: purchases ≥0.1% of mcap or ≥1× salary (`fmp_insider_buy_usd_12m`, `fmp_exec_comp_total`); derive the F4 quarter list from the run date.
  3. [spirit] Move "into a drawdown" from lens to exceptional: `ts_dist_hi52 <= 0.7` and ≥3 distinct buyers (195 of 392 in A2).

### arch_institutional_accumulation  (line 6749)
- Spirit: compendium Part II B discovery cycle (institutional threshold → rapid multiple expansion) and O'Neil "I"; master ref VII/III smart-money entry. Engine comment: 13F adding above the quarter's cross-sectional median while the tape is flat or down.
- As coded: adding (own-chg >0 & shares-chg >0 & both above the cross-sectional median) & persistent (`fmp_inst_accum_quarters >= 2` of 3) & sane (|own-chg| ≤15pp, shares-chg ≤50%) & flat/down (`roc_12m`→momentum_12m→price_yoy ≤10% & roc_6m ≤10%, or bs base with bs_r26 ≤10%) & valid (holders ≥15, mcap ≥$50M, 12m ≥ −70%, not ghost, not a fund). Score `inst_accum_score` surfaced. 523 firers.
- Lineage: INST (`institutional-ownership/symbol-positions-summary`, 3 quarters, **US-listed only**, 45-day filing lag); tape from MASTER snapshots (Yahoo) — A1 §A.2 swap to `ts_r52`/`ts_r26` is **still open** in the gate (only the BS branch reads the panel).
- Spirit gaps: aggregate 13F cannot tell passive/index rebalancing from active discovery, nor who is buying (patient, skilled holders vs quant); non-US names get nothing; no link to the "why now" (a new 13D, an initiation).
- Methodology issues: cross-sectional demeaning is over whatever rows carry INST data (US), fine; `_ip12 <= 0.10` on noisy snapshots (A1: 29–55% differ by >15pp from the panel); `fmp_inst_accum_quarters` counts net buying in raw terms while `_inst_adding` is demeaned — the persistence leg is on a different baseline than the current-quarter leg.
- FMP augmentations: `institutional-ownership/extract-analytics/holder` (N, US) `isNew`, `holdingPeriod`, `avgPricePaid`; `holder-performance-summary` (N) 3y relative performance, turnover; `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` N-PORT (N, **global holdings**, rate-limited) `change`>0 across ≥3 US funds for non-US reach; `etf/asset-exposure` (N, global) to net out passive share; 8-quarter history (+5 calls per US name).
- Suggestions:
  1. [data] `_ip12`→`ts_r52`, `_ip6`→`ts_r26` (A1 A.2 open, zero-call).
  2. [data] Non-US reach via N-PORT (S9): ≥3 US funds with `change`>0 and none selling.
  3. [spirit] Exceptional (A2 B.9): ≥3 `isNew` holders with positive 3y relative performance and turnover <0.3, while `ts_r26 <= 0`; demean persistence on the same baseline as the current quarter.

### arch_kpi_threshold  (line 1676)
- Spirit: master ref I.G "The Unlocked Thing" (20) *Operating KPI crosses a threshold* — Brooks Macdonald (first positive net flows in 2+ years), KORU (first positive operating margin): a crossing that changes who can own the name. Compendium #8 first clean quarter.
- As coded: operating & `_not_melting` & `_kpi_turn` (any annual first-positive of EBITDA/CFO/FCF/NI/ROCE | `fqx_eps_turned` | `bs_ebit_turned`) & (any margin delta ≥1pp | roce ≥5%) & mcap ≥$10M. Lenses: fqx_inc_ebit_margin_dt, fqx_eps_pos_share_8, evt_surprise_4q, ebitda_margin_delta_yoy. **5,289 firers** (second broadest in the slice).
- Lineage: `*_first_positive` MASTER (derived annual, global where FMP statements exist); `fqx_eps_turned` FQX; `bs_ebit_turned` BS; margin deltas MASTER; `evt_surprise_4q` EVT (partial run). Nothing US-only.
- Spirit gaps: the thesis's KPI is *operational* (flows, subscribers, utilisation, op margin) — the code substitutes five financial zero-crossings including FCF/CFO (working-capital noise; A1: 52% of firers fire on FCF-first-positive); no "threshold that changes demand" (dividend initiation, GAAP profit for index eligibility, uplisting); no "priced on the old side of the threshold" leg.
- Methodology issues: adding `fqx_eps_turned`/`bs_ebit_turned` as further ORs broadened the gate (A1 B.1 asked for quarterly *confirmation*, 1,946 of 4,123); `roce_today >= 0.05` as an alternative to margin confirmation lets a first-positive FCF print through with no margin evidence; 1pp margin drift counts as confirming.
- FMP augmentations: `fmp_dyn_*_turned_positive` (on disk; author rejects as seasonal — agreed, prefer TTM turns); `earnings-surprises-bulk` (N, global) → a beat in the turning quarter; `dividends` (U events, global) first payment ≤12m as a genuine threshold; `earnings-calendar` (N) for freshness.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Core = an operating-line crossing (op margin / EBIT / NI first-positive) AND a dated quarterly turn where FQX is measured; FCF/CFO first-positives only with a same-year op-line turn; old rule → `_watch` (A1 B.1 open).
  2. [spirit] Add ownership-changing thresholds: first dividend (DIV), 4-quarter GAAP NI>0 (S&P eligibility, `xr_gaap_profit_crossover`), exchange uplisting (`profile.exchange` change).
  3. [method] Drop the `roce_today` escape; require `margin_confirming` at ≥2pp or `fqx_inc_ebit_margin >= 0.30` (A2 EXC).

### arch_large_cap_quality  (line 2346)
- Spirit: engine-native ("a home for quality giants the small-cap-tilted screen never surfaces"). Compendium Tillinghast four elements (profitability ROE >10–12% sustained ~10 years, life span, growth, certainty; P/E teens for better businesses) and master ref II (level of profitability drives returns).
- As coded: operating & mcap ≥$10bn & EBITDA margin (sane, <60%) ≥15% & (FCF yield>0 | ≥3 FCF years) & nde <3 & returns floor (roce ≥10% not one-off | roic_after_sbc ≥15% | roic_lindy ≥12%) & (capital_return_yield ≥2% | div yield ≥1.5% | FCF yield >2%). 811 firers. No lenses.
- Lineage: `roce`, margins, `nde` MASTER; `roic_after_sbc` EDGAR (US); `roic_lindy` EDGAR, ST-filled (`fmp_filled_roic_lindy`); `capital_return_yield` EDGAR (4% covered) with ST payout decomposition; `dividend_yield` provider TTM snapshot (FX-corruptible per A2 §C). Global.
- Spirit gaps: no durability (worst-year margin, `tc_min_opm`), no growth (Tillinghast element 3; FG per-share), no valuation ceiling (FCF yield >2% is the only price term — a 50x P/E passes), no price-lindy (A1 A.3: 625 of 818). It describes "large and profitable", not a mispricing.
- Methodology issues: an ROE/ROCE floor cannot see whether returns are levered (nuance 5: interest burden); `nde <3` reads the 99-fill as fail (fine) but negative-EBITDA names are already out; the payout OR is trivially met by any large cap.
- FMP augmentations: TC `tc_min_opm` (zero-call); `financial-growth` (N, global) `tenYRevenueGrowthPerShare`; `key-metrics` `interestBurdenTTM`/`taxBurdenTTM` (ENR holds `fmp_interest_burden`, `fmp_tax_burden`, unused); `dividends` (U events) `ev_div_raise_streak`; `historical-market-capitalization` (N) for counter-cyclical buyback timing.
- Suggestions:
  1. [spirit] Exceptional per A2 B.18: `tc_min_opm >= 0.20` and FG ten-year revenue/share ≥+97%; elite by `ts_maxdd_5y` (price-lindy).
  2. [spirit] Add a "reasonable price" leg (EV/EBIT ≤20 or FCF yield ≥4%) so the archetype carries a thesis, or rename it `large_cap_quality_flag`.
  3. [method] Use `fmp_interest_burden >= 0.8` (operating, not levered, returns) beside the ROCE floor.

### arch_levered_inflection  (line 5728)
- Spirit: Wolf/Oak Archetype 8 "Cash-flow deleveraging & yield" (FCF yield >10%, ND/EBITDA 1.5–3× trending down, falling interest expense, payouts >3–5%; reject if debtholders control the sweep); master ref VII Akre/AMT (debt panic + unit-economics machine + visible FCF inflection + Davis double play); Pernas failure mode (COMM −57%, RRGB −43%: "fragile optionality — too many things must go right").
- As coded: operating & mcap <$5bn & `oper_lev_any` & `strong_op_improvement` (oper_lev_score ≥0.4 | EBITDA +15% | GP up with revenue down) & `heavy_debt` (not known net cash; nde 3–30 or EV/mcap 1.75–30 with nde ≥1.5) & EBITDA>0 & (FCF>0 | CFO>0) & `_not_melting` & (EBITDA yoy>0 | ebitda_inflection) & (EV/EBITDA ≤8 | robust cash yield ≥12%) & `beaten_down_any(0.25)` & capex intensity <15% (soft). 471 firers. No lenses.
- Lineage: all MASTER (nde, EV, EBITDA, `robust_cash_yield` derived median of FCF/CFO/E yields); `beaten_down_any` reads TS first (`ts_dd52`, `ts_dist_hi260`, `ts_r52`) then snapshots. Global.
- Spirit gaps: "deleveraging" is proxied by rising EBITDA, never by net debt falling (A1 A.4 open: `fq_deleveraging_flag`, `fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets` are on disk); no interest-expense trend; no interest-coverage / debt-service floor (nde up to 30× admits solvency countdowns — the Pernas COMM class); the maturity wall is unscreenable (honest limit).
- Methodology issues: four gates read the same trailing window (inflecting, cheap, beaten, EBITDA rising) so precision comes from coincidence, not from proof; `heavy_debt` on `ev_over_mcap` is FX-corruptible for cross-listed lines (the comment acknowledges); `(FCF>0 | CFO>0)` — CFO>0 with FCF<0 is "cash" only before capex, the wrong side for a levered stub.
- FMP augmentations: FQ `fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets` (zero-call); ENR `fmp_debt_service_coverage`, `fmp_interest_coverage` (ratios-ttm, global, on disk, unused here — A2 nuance 13); `cash-flow-statement` `debtRepayment` (global) for a paydown line; `company-notes` (N, US) listed-notes existence (low).
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Core = net debt actually falling (`fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets < 0` or `fq_deleveraging_flag`) — the EBITDA-rising proxy becomes `_watch` (A1 A.4 open).
  2. [method] Survivability floor: `fmp_debt_service_coverage >= 1` or `fmp_interest_coverage >= 1.5`; cap core nde at 8×, 8–30× as `_watch` (Pernas: balance sheet first).
  3. [spirit] Surface a `levered_stub_tier` (nde band) so sizing follows the probability of zero (Pernas 1% speculative vs core).

### arch_lifo_hidden_reserve  (line 4092)
- Spirit: engine-native forensic (F15): a LIFO reserve understates inventory and book; compendium #6 assets at historical cost.
- As coded: operating & real sector & mcap>0 & `lifo_reserve` >0 & reserve/mcap ≥10% & (1/pb + reserve/mcap) ≥1 (price ≤ LIFO-adjusted book) & `_not_melting`. 7 firers. No lenses.
- Lineage: `lifo_reserve` EDGAR `LIFOInventoryAmount`/reserve tag (MASTER, 258 rows) — **US-only by construction** (IFRS prohibits LIFO); `mcap` USD vs reserve in filing currency (USD for domestic filers; foreign private issuers on US GAAP rare). Snapshot.
- Spirit gaps: no tax haircut (a LIFO reserve reverses through taxable income; ≈21–25% goes to the state); no inflation regime (reserves swelled 2021–23 and shrink when input prices fall — the "hidden asset" unwinds with deflation); no catalyst (LIFO liquidation, method change, takeout at FIFO book).
- Methodology issues: `1/pb + reserve/mcap ≥ 1` is the right arithmetic; `_adj_book_f15` is NaN when pb ≤0 (fine); the 10% materiality is absolute but the reserve is a level over a USD mcap (fine for US).
- FMP augmentations: none — LIFO reserve is an XBRL footnote FMP's standardised statements drop (A2 §A.1, enrichment map "cannot fill"). Only `historical-market-capitalization` (N) for a PIT denominator.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] After-tax reserve: reserve × (1 − max(ETR, 0.21)) before the 10% and adjusted-book tests.
  2. [spirit] Require the reserve to have risen over the last 2 FYs (rising input prices) — otherwise the asset is melting; surface `lifo_reserve_trend`.
  3. [spirit] Keep as a surfaced forensic flag feeding `tangible_value` / `cluseau_realizable_book` rather than a standalone archetype (7 firers).

### arch_low_sbc_quality  (line 2014)
- Spirit: engine-native quality descriptor (SBC <2% of revenue, profitable, not diluting). Master ref VI Deden ("rejects companies in the business of their common stock"), MCC dilution discipline (15 of 100 points).
- As coded: operating & `_roce_now_ok` & not (shares_growth_3y >10%) & ((0 ≤ sbc_pct_revenue < 2%) | (roic_after_sbc >10% & |roce − roic_after_sbc| <2pp)) & EBITDA margin >5% & (roce ≥8% | roic_after_sbc ≥10%). 1,825 firers. No lenses.
- Lineage: `sbc_pct_revenue` EDGAR, NaN-filled from ST `fmp_st_sbc_pct_revenue_valid` (FY cash-flow SBC / revenue, global — the A2 REACH item is implemented; `fmp_filled_sbc_pct_revenue` marks it); `roic_after_sbc` EDGAR only (US); `shares_growth_3y` EDGAR/ST-filled. ENR also holds `fmp_sbc_to_revenue` (key-metrics TTM), unused.
- Spirit gaps: not a mispricing thesis — SBC <2% is the base rate outside US tech (JP/EU industrials pay ~0), so the flag mostly says "old economy or non-US"; no peer frame (a 4% software name in a 12% peer group is "low"); no valuation leg.
- Methodology issues: the ST fill is valid only where `fmp_st_sbc_pct_revenue > 0` (line 571): filers reporting SBC = 0 exactly get NaN → missing fails → the cleanest names are excluded unless EDGAR covers them — the inverse of the thesis; the alternate branch is EDGAR-only anyway; `shares_growth_3y >10%` missing-passes.
- FMP augmentations: ENR `fmp_sbc_to_revenue` (global, on disk) as the TTM cross-check; `peers-bulk` (N, 1 call) for a peer-relative SBC; `financial-growth` `weightedAverageSharesDilutedGrowth`.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Accept SBC = 0 in the fill when the cash-flow line is reported (non-null) and revenue >0; keep the (0,1) band only as a units guard.
  2. [spirit] Peer-relative core: SBC/revenue ≤ 0.5× the industry median (peers-bulk / profile industry), so the flag means "unusually clean", not "non-US".
  3. [spirit] Demote to `low_sbc_flag` feeding `owner_earnings_power`, `cash_quality`, `understated_earnings` (nuance 11) — it is a veto/lens, not a setup.

### arch_micro_activist_inflect  (line 1734; 13D exceptional at 1751)
- Spirit: compendium Part II A (13D Item 4; the amendment carries the escalation) and Part II B footprint (a new accomplished management team 1–2 quarters before the run); master ref III activist situations and management changes. The engine's own name: microcap inflection **plus** a new capital-allocation board member.
- As coded: operating & rev_yoy >0 & `_not_melting` & mcap <$250M & EBITDA margin >0 & `inflection_now` (any of 5 annual first-positives | EBITDA margin Δ ≥2pp | rev_accel ≥5%) & clean balance sheet (cash>EV | net cash >5% | nde ≤0) & cheap (EV/EBITDA ≤8 | P/E ≤8 | pb ≤0.8). Exceptional = `evt_sc13d_date <= 365d` & mcap <$2bn. 769 firers. No lenses.
- Lineage: MASTER annual flags; `evt_sc13d_date` EVT (`sec-filings-search`, **US**, 2,000-row partial run). Everything else global.
- Spirit gaps: the defining half (activist / new board member) is only an exceptional tag, so the core is "cheap inflecting microcap" (overlaps liger/wolf); 8-K item 5.02 (director appointment) not wired; no activist quality; non-US has no substitute in the catalogue (RNS holdings notices, Japanese large-holding reports are not in FMP) — say so rather than let missing read as absent.
- Methodology issues: `rev_accel >= 0.05` (drift) counts as an inflection; `inflection_now` ORs seven annual lenses; `cheap_on_ebitda` P/E ≤8 on a $30M microcap is often a stale-print artefact; the `_13d_recent` symbol lookup catches 13Ds the company FILED (comment acknowledges; the <$2bn cap only partly fixes it).
- FMP augmentations: `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (U event-study, US) `typeOfReportingPerson` IA/IN not CO/HC, `percentOfClass ≥5`, first filing ≤12m; `sec-filings-search/symbol` (U, US) 8-K 5.02; `holder-performance-summary` (N) activist skill; `insider-trading/statistics` post-appointment buying; `splits` (N) reverse-split veto (nuance 10).
- Suggestions:
  1. [spirit] For US names make the 13D/5.02 leg the core (`_core`) and demote quant-only to `_watch`; non-US stays `_watch` with an explicit `board_leg_unmeasured` flag (A2 B.27 open).
  2. [method] Inflection must be shock-sized (`margin_shock_any` or a first-positive) — drop `rev_accel >= 0.05`.
  3. [data] Reverse-split ≤12m veto from `splits`; use BO reporting-person type to exclude corporate parents.

### arch_midcap_garp  (line 2703)
- Spirit: engine-native GARP (reinvestment quality on a growing earnings stream at a fair E/P); compendium Tillinghast valuation rule (single-digit P/E average, teens better, low-20s exceptional); master ref II (profitability level).
- As coded: operating & mcap ≥$2bn & ROIIC quality (`roiic_lindy` ≥15% | `roiic_acceleration` >0 | cash ROIIC ≥15%; else, where all three are absent, proxy: ROE ≥15% with NI>0, or EBITDA margin 18–60% with roce ≥10%/ROE ≥10%, AND a margin delta >0 or ROE ≥20%) & value (E/P ≥5% | EBITDA/EV ≥8% in a sane EV band) & growing (`ebit_growth_yoy` >0 | rev_yoy ≥8% | `fqx_ebit_ttm_g` >0). 1,516 firers. No lenses.
- Lineage: ROIIC family EDGAR, ST-filled (global where FMP FY history exists); proxy legs MASTER; `fqx_ebit_ttm_g` FQX (A2 PROXY implemented as an extra OR). Global.
- Spirit gaps: no growth-vs-multiple ratio (the "G" of GARP is any positive growth; PEG absent); no forward growth; no durability (a one-year ROE ≥15% on the proxy branch); "reinvestment quality" for non-EDGAR names is really "high ROE and margin up".
- Methodology issues: `roiic_acceleration > 0` alone satisfies ROIIC quality with no floor (−20%→−15% qualifies); growth ORs at zero thresholds; E/P ≥5% on trailing E; the EV-yield band guard is good.
- FMP augmentations: FQX `fqx_ebit_ttm_g` (done); `financial-growth` (N, global) `threeYNetIncomeGrowthPerShare`; `analyst-estimates` (U*, cached, snapshot, global) forward EBIT/EPS growth for a true forward PEG (nuance 6); ENR `fmp_interest_burden` (DuPont, nuance 5).
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Floor the acceleration leg (`roiic_lindy >= 0.05` with `roiic_acceleration > 0`); growth ≥8% on `fqx_ebit_ttm_g` (date-matched) rather than any positive print.
  2. [spirit] Add PEG-style pairing: P/E ≤ 1.2× TTM EBIT growth (capped 8–40%) — the thesis is price *relative to* growth.
  3. [spirit] Exceptional: FG 3y per-share NI ≥+26% and forward consensus EPS growth >0 (analyst-estimates, global where covered).

### arch_narrative_lag  (line 1290; rebuilt at 1431; tiers `narrative_lag_exceptional/_elite`)
- Spirit: master ref I.A (15) *Narrative Lag After Fundamental Inflection* (categorisation neglect — screens anchor the old label; re-rating needs the narrative to shift); compendium #8 First-Time Coverage & Earnings Divergence (high "behaviour change" vs low "reaction").
- As coded (final rule 1431): `narrative_lag_lenses >= 1` & `_adv_breadth >= 2` & operating & mcap ≥$10M & `_roce_now_ok` & (FCF>0 | EBITDA>0 | first-positive) & (pb<3 | FCF yield ≥3%). Lenses: per-share fundamental outrun of total-return price on 1/2/3/5-year horizons (fq_rev_growth, fqx_ebit/ni/fcf_ps vs `ts_r52`; bs coils vs 2y; ST per-share 3y/5y vs `ts_r156/r260`), each capped by log(sector-norm multiple / current multiple) and only where the advance is plausible (5–60%/yr), plus relative lag (`ts_rs_pct_mkt <= 40` & rev ≥15%) and ignored beats (`evt_ignored_beats_2y >= 2`); `narrative_lag_extent` sums horizon gaps. Exceptional: ≥2 lenses & extent ≥ log 1.5; elite top 10%. 5,359 firers (watch = old rule).
- Lineage: FQ/FQX/BS/ST/TS all global; `evt_ignored_beats_2y` EVT (partial: 1,432 INV names); `ts_rs_pct_mkt` TS; sector norms from `sector`. Nothing US-only. A1 A.2 / A2 B.3 "lag measured against the advance" is implemented and extended (per-share, multi-horizon, multiple-anchored) — well built.
- Spirit gaps: the label shift itself (what the market calls the company) has no proxy; `_adv_breadth` still counts drift lenses (`interval_inflect_any`, rev ≥10%) so "≥2" is easy; the multiple anchor uses `sector` (A1 A.6: industry); ignored-beats is silent for most names until ESB lands.
- Methodology issues: the core still fires on ≥1 lens with pb<3 (22% of INV) — the exceptional tier is the usable core; the relative-lag lens has no valuation anchor; 5y share growth is dropped when `years_of_history < 6` (silently permissive).
- FMP augmentations: `earnings-surprises-bulk` (N, global, ~13 calls) → `evt_ignored_beats_2y` universe-wide (C1); `peers-bulk`/profile industry for the norm; `enterprise-values` history (U) for the name's own multiple history as a second anchor.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Promote to core: lenses ≥2 across horizons or extent ≥ log 1.3; old rule stays `_watch` (A2 B.3 open in spirit, 362 core measured).
  2. [method] `_adv_breadth` must include ≥1 shock-sized lens (`margin_shock_any` or a first-positive) — drift lenses count toward breadth only alongside a shock.
  3. [data] ESB C1 (13 calls) so the ignored-beats lens covers the universe; anchor on industry medians with ≥20 peers.

### arch_negative_ev_value  (line 5845)
- Spirit: master ref III net-nets / net cash; Wolf/Oak Archetype 10 distressed deep value (P/B <0.5, cash >20% of mcap, a realisation path); Liger asset-backed optionality (liquidation value ≠ cash; deduct claims senior to equity; dilution risk limited).
- As coded: operating & mcap <$5bn & ((EV<0 | cash>EV | net cash 75–100% of mcap) & (FCF>0 | EBITDA>0 | net cash ≥50%)) OR (0<pb<0.7); `_not_melting`. 4,685 firers. No lenses.
- Lineage: `enterprise_value`, `cash_gt_ev_flag`, `net_cash_pct_mcap` MASTER (USD mcap vs cash — A2 §C currency-mix class), `pb` MASTER. Global.
- Spirit gaps: the sub-book branch (~3,262 firers in A1) is not "negative EV" and duplicates `tangible_value`/`cundill` — **A1 B.1 still open**; no runway/burn test (Liger: the option is worthless if losses consume the cash), no senior claims (minorities, preferred, leases), no catalyst (buyback below cash, tender, 13D), no "why the market pays you to own it".
- Methodology issues: the survivability OR is satisfied by `net_cash_pct_sane >= 0.5` — a burner with 80% of mcap in cash and negative EBITDA/FCF fires; net cash uses USD mcap against a local cash/debt level; EV<0 on a stale EV snapshot.
- FMP augmentations: ENR `fmp_graham_net_net`, `fmp_ncav`, `fmp_altman_z` (on disk, global; A2 nuance 12/§1 distress gate) — unused here; FQ `fq_cash_sti` / TTM `fq_fcf` for a same-currency runway; `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US) / `dividends` special payments as catalysts; `financial-statement-symbol-list` currency map.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Drop the pb<0.7 branch (route it to `tangible_value`); keep the negative/low-EV branch only (A1: 1,420 kept).
  2. [method] Survivability = FCF>0 or runway ≥3 years (FQ cash ÷ TTM burn, reporting currency) — net cash alone is not survivability; add `fmp_altman_z` as the distress gate already shipped for Cluseau.
  3. [spirit] Exceptional: a realisation catalyst (buyback ≥2%, tender, new 13D ≤365d, special dividend in DIV) — the compendium's "paid to own it" only pays when something forces the cash out.

### arch_net_cash_returner  (line 2965)
- Spirit: master ref III "net-nets / net cash" plus VIII "Capital Return / Tender" (self-tenders, Dutch auctions, special dividends, capital reductions); compendium II.A technique (1) odd-lot tenders; Wolf/Oak NAV-discount catalysts ("wind-up, tender offers, buybacks below NAV"). Engine: the rewarded form of cash-exceeds-EV — a surplus being handed back, vs the dormant hoard (`balance_sheet_return`).
- As coded: operating & mcap>0 & `net_cash_pct_mcap >= 0.30` & `_returning` (buyback_yield>0 | dividend_yield>1% | shares_3y_cagr < −1% | net_buyback_ttm>0) & `_not_melting`. Lenses: −tc_uncov_payout_3y, net_cash_pct_mcap, capital_return_yield. 1,900 firers (279 US).
- Lineage: `net_cash_pct_mcap` MASTER (USD mcap vs local cash/debt: the A2 §C currency-mix class; **unclamped** here — `net_cash_pct_sane` is used by nol_shell/strong_coverage but not by this gate); `buyback_yield` EDGAR + ST fill (8% pre-fill), `net_buyback_ttm` 31%, `dividend_yield` provider TTM snapshot (41%, FX-corruptible per A2 §C), `shares_3y_cagr` MASTER; `tc_uncov_payout_3y` TC (72%). Global.
- Spirit gaps: no size of return (a 1.01% dividend qualifies as "actively returning" 30% of mcap in cash); no persistence (one buyback print); no valuation leg (a 40x P/E net-cash name fires); no catalyst class the sources name (tender, special dividend, capital reduction); no "the surplus is genuinely surplus" test (runway / working-capital need). The spirit-spec lens list (capret_yield_eff, shares_growth_3y, fcf_yield, evt_div_raise_streak) differs from the engine's — the spec and the code have drifted.
- Methodology issues: `net_cash_pct > 1.0` shells pass (tangible_value vetoes exactly these); `_netcash_not_contradicted` (nde known ≥1) is not applied, so stale-snapshot "net cash" with real debt fires; the `shares_3y_cagr < −0.01` leg has no reverse-split guard (no_dilution's `_not_split_3y` exists 1,000 lines earlier); dividend_yield is in provider currency against a USD-based net-cash ratio.
- FMP augmentations: `dividends` (U events, global) — `frequency`/irregular rows as special dividends, `ev_div_raise_streak`; TC `tc_uncov_payout_3y` (on disk, lens only — A2 EXC open); `splits` (N, global) for the shrink leg; ENR `fmp_ncav` (on disk) as the same-currency cushion cross-check; `sec-filings-search` SC TO-I (US) for issuer tenders.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Read `net_cash_pct_sane` with `_netcash_not_contradicted` and the `_not_split_3y` guard; missing payout lenses should fail, not pass silently.
  2. [spirit] Core = total payout ≥3% of mcap (div + buyback, same currency) in ≥2 of 3 FYs with `tc_uncov_payout_3y == 0` **or** net cash ≥3× the annual payout (A2 §B "returns in ≥2 of 3 FYs FCF-covered" open); exceptional = a tender / special dividend / capital reduction ≤12m (DIV irregular row or SC TO-I).
  3. [spirit] Add a price term (P/E ex-cash ≤12 via `cash_adjusted_pe`'s `_adj_pe`, or FCF yield ≥5%) so the archetype is a mispricing, not a description.

### arch_no_dilution  (line 1916; tier at 1929: `no_dilution_watch/_exceptional/_elite`)
- Spirit: compendium II.B "the cardinal sin: dilution" (losers diluted ~61%, winners ~22%; flat or shrinking count, ideally <10m shares); master ref VI Deden ("rejects companies in the business of their common stock"), MCC dilution discipline (15/100 points). Docstring: Mayer/Mauboussin owner-operator "reinvesting at high returns without constantly tapping equity holders".
- As coded: operating & `_roce_now_ok` & `_not_melting` & ((shares_growth_3y ≤ +2% & `_not_split_3y`) | (3y NaN & shares_yoy ≤ 1% & `_not_split_yoy`)) & ~(shares_yoy > 2%) & n_yrs_fcf_pos ≥4 & n_yrs_roic_pos ≥4. Tier: core adds `roic_lindy >= 0.10` (measured where roic_lindy present); exceptional = shares_growth_5y ≤0 & roic_lindy ≥15% & fqx_fcf_ps_g >0; elite = top decile of roic_lindy. Watch 7,972 → core 2,636 (838 US) → exceptional 180 → elite 11. Lenses: −shares_growth_5y, −shares_growth_3y, roic_lindy, cash_roic_lindy, fqx_fcf_ps_g.
- Lineage: `shares_growth_3y/5y`, `n_yrs_positive_fcf/roic`, `roic_lindy` EDGAR, ST-filled (39–41k rows filled: the REACH item is done, global); `shares_yoy` MASTER (55%); `_buyback_corrob` reads `buyback_yield` (8% pre-fill) so the −30% split heuristic is what usually binds; `fqx_fcf_ps_g` FQX (25%). `n_yrs_*` default 0 when absent → missing fails (right sign for a positive claim).
- Spirit gaps: no reinvestment or per-share growth in the core (the docstring's "reinvesting" is only in the exceptional tier via fqx_fcf_ps_g); no valuation; flat shares + 4/5 FCF + ROIC ≥10% is the base rate of mature profitable firms (A1: 29% of INV fired on the old rule), so the core describes "mature and clean", not a setup; the <10m-share / small-cap half of the MCC thesis is absent.
- Methodology issues: the elite metric is `roic_lindy`, i.e. elite ranks quality, not the archetype's defining axis (least dilution / per-share compounding); `~(shares_yoy > 0.02)` missing-passes; the ≤+2% test on a cumulative 3-year figure is strict where the 1-year fallback (≤1%) is looser than 2%/yr would imply — the two branches are not the same bar; the −30% guard still stands in for `splits` (A2 B.13 open).
- FMP augmentations: `splits` (N, global, ~2k names with a ≥20% drop) replaces the −30% heuristic (A2 B.13 open); `financial-growth` (N, global) `weightedAverageSharesDilutedGrowth` per FY (≤0 in ≥4 of 5 FYs) and `fiveYOperatingCFGrowthPerShare`; `cash-flow-statement` `commonStockIssued/Repurchased` for the SBC-offset check (spirit_spec's TTEC class).
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Elite on `shares_growth_5y` (most negative) or `fqx_fcf_ps_g`, keep roic_lindy as the core floor; make the 1-year fallback bar consistent (≤ +0.7%/yr).
  2. [spirit] Core adds a per-share compounding leg: FG 5y OCF/share growth ≥+28% (5%/yr) or `fqx_fcf_ps_g > 0`; names failing it stay `_watch` (the "reinvesting" in the docstring is otherwise unmeasured).
  3. [spirit] Demote the archetype to a `no_dilution_flag` feeding sustainable_scaler / buyback_compounder / owner_operator as a veto, unless a price leg (EV/EBIT ≤15 or FCF yield ≥5%) is added; the sources treat dilution as a filter, not a thesis.

### arch_nol_shell  (line 8242)
- Spirit: master ref III "NOL shells (Section 382 mechanics)", WMIH (~$6.0B federal NOLs, no annual limitation, monetised via the Nationstar acquisition); compendium II.A technique (7) post-bankruptcy orphaned equities with NOLs; Assembly Theory block (2): quantum, §382(l)(5) survival, valuation allowance, and a TRA that pipes savings to old creditors.
- As coded: operating & mcap>0 & 0.5 ≤ nol_usd/mcap ≤ 20 & (net_cash_pct_sane ≥10% | `_excellent_value` | cash>EV) & op_margin > −30% & `_not_melting` & ~(fcf_yield < −25%). No lenses. 66 firers (63 US).
- Lineage: `nol_usd` EES (EDGAR `OperatingLossCarryforwards`, 2,965 rows) — **US-only by construction**, gross (not tax-effected), federal/state/foreign mixed, no expiry; mcap USD (coherent). Everything else MASTER.
- Spirit gaps: no §382 test (an ownership change caps the annual usable NOL at equity value × the long-term tax-exempt rate — the single fact that decides whether a big ratio is worth anything); no monetisation path (sponsor / acquirer / reverse-merger — WMIH had KKR); no valuation-allowance read (that lives in `dta_reversal`); the "shell" half is unmeasured, so a profitable operating company with a legacy NOL (a tax-shield story) and a cash shell with no business (an acquisition-vehicle story) fire on the same rule.
