- Methodology issues: the ratio is on the gross NOL (worth ~21% federal); 0.5–20 band is arbitrary and the 20× cap is a units guard doing thesis work; `op_margin > −0.30` is meaningless on a shell with near-zero revenue (margin explodes); the `_excellent_value` OR admits ordinary cheap profitable US names (tax_efficient territory); missing fcf stays permissive on the burn veto.
- FMP augmentations: none for the NOL itself (A2 "NONE", enrichment map "cannot fill"); `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (U event-study, US) a sponsor ≥10% holder as the monetisation leg and a >50% change as the §382 trigger; `sec-filings-search/symbol` (US) 8-K 5.01 change-of-control; `income-statement` `incomeTaxExpense/incomeBeforeTax` (global) cash-tax rate ≈0 as evidence the shield is in use.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Tax-effect the NOL (×0.21) and cap it at a §382 proxy (mcap × ~3.5% × 20y ≈ 0.7× mcap) when a >50% holder change is on record; surface `nol_expiry_years` if the tag is harvestable.
  2. [spirit] Split into `nol_shell` (revenue ≤10% of mcap, net cash ≥50%, a ≥10% sponsor) and `nol_shield_operating` (pretax >0, cash-tax rate <5%, NOL ≥1× pretax income) — the second feeds tax_efficient / dta_reversal; today's OR blends them.
  3. [data] Honest limit: state in the tag that non-US names cannot be measured (IFRS "unrecognised tax losses" is a footnote FMP drops) rather than letting absence read as "no NOL".

### arch_overdepreciated_assets  (line 3784)
- Spirit: compendium #6 scrapyard ("assets carried at historical cost or impaired values"; JD Wetherspoon "unrevalued 25+ years"); Pernas idea screen "high D&A-to-capex and low EV/EBITDA" (spirit_spec); engine F1 "harvest mode": economic vs accounting depreciation, so P/B understates the real asset backing.
- As coded: operating & mcap>0 & D&A>0 (audited `da_ttm`, else implied EBITDA−EBIT bounded to op_margin>0, ≤EBITDA, ≤35% of revenue) & revenue>0 & D&A/rev ≥5% & `_maint_capex` (max of capex_ttm and capex_avg) ≤ 0.6×D&A & op_margin>0 & rev_yoy ≥ −5% & 0<pb<1.2 & `_not_melting`. No lenses. 637 firers (250 US).
- Lineage: `da_ttm` EDGAR (55%, **no ST fill**) → non-US names ride the implied fallback from MASTER margins; `capex_avg` EDGAR (10%) ST-filled via the FX bridge (`fq_conv_capex_avg`, ~27k rows) — this rule then takes max(master `capex_ttm`, converted FMP average), the mixed-basis pairing the sibling F9 explicitly avoids (`_f9_fmp` swaps in `fq_capex`); `pb` MASTER (book incl. goodwill). Global.
- Spirit gaps: capex < D&A with flat revenue is *also* the signature of an under-investing melter (enrichment map §241: same number, opposite meaning) — one year of rev_yoy ≥ −5% is the only guard; no asset-age lens (old base = high accumulated depreciation; `fq_da_to_ppe` is on disk), no property/land-at-cost lens, no catalyst (sale-and-leaseback, revaluation, takeover); the priced-below-book leg uses P/B not P/TB, so goodwill-heavy names can qualify.
- Methodology issues: implied D&A for non-US names is a margin rebuild, not a level; the 0.6 ratio against a 5-year average that includes growth capex understates the gap (conservative, acceptable); D&A/rev ≥5% is a low bar for "fixed-asset business"; spirit_spec's lenses (`fq_capex_to_da`) are not the gate's lenses.
- FMP augmentations: `owner-earnings` `maintenanceCapex` (U*, cached, global) as the third lens (A2 §B LEG open); FQ `fq_capex_to_da` (65%, on disk) and `fq_da_to_ppe` (asset age) — date-matched, same currency; `balance-sheet-statement` `propertyPlantEquipmentNet` (global) for a P/(TB + PPE uplift) view; `ratios-ttm` `tangibleBookValuePerShareTTM` for P/TB reach.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] For ST-filled rows compare FMP's own current capex (`fq_capex`) with `fmp_st_capex_avg`, as F9 does; add `fq_capex_to_da <= 0.6` as the global date-matched lens and FMP maintenanceCapex as the second (A2 LEG open).
  2. [spirit] Require an old base and a held top line: `fq_da_to_ppe >= 0.10` and `rev_3y_cagr >= -0.02`; otherwise route to a `harvest_underinvest_watch` flag — the melter must not wear the scrapyard label.
  3. [spirit] Price on P/TB (<1.0) and surface the owner-earnings yield; consider tiering with `owner_earnings_power` (same mechanism, different price term) instead of two overlapping archetypes.

### arch_owner_earnings_power  (line 3902)
- Spirit: Buffett owner earnings (NI + D&A − maintenance capex ± working capital) as a forensic lens — the P&L understates what the owner keeps when depreciation overstates asset consumption; Pernas screen "high D&A-to-capex" (spirit_spec); Graham/Templeton 5-year averaging in the price leg.
- As coded: operating & local mcap>0 & `_fx_coherent` & NI>0 & D&A>0 & capex ≥0 & OE/NI ≥1.4 (OE = NI + D&A − max(capex_ttm, capex_avg)) & (1.5 ≤ mcap/OE ≤ 10 | 1.5 ≤ mcap/oe_avg ≤ 10 with OE>0) & ~(shares_yoy > 5%) & `_not_melting`. No lenses in the engine (spirit_spec lists owner_earnings_yield, fmp_st_owner_earnings_yield, oe_avg/ni_avg, fq_capex_to_da). 1,110 firers (383 US).
- Lineage: `net_income_ttm`, `market_cap`, `capex_ttm` MASTER local; `da_ttm` EDGAR (55%, no fill) → non-US names use the implied EBITDA−EBIT rebuild; `capex_avg`, `oe_avg` EDGAR ST-filled through the FX bridge (~27k rows; the max(master TTM, converted FMP average) pairing F9 avoids); `fmp_st_owner_earnings_yield` ST (74%, on disk, **unused by the gate**). `_fx_coherent` needs both USD twins.
- Spirit gaps: the OE surplus is by construction the D&A ≫ capex gap, i.e. the same fact as `overdepreciated_assets` / `xr_depreciation_cliff` / `xr_forensic_multiple_gap` under a different price term; no working-capital adjustment (Buffett's third term); no test that the low capex is not under-investment (revenue trend absent here); no destination for the cash (payout or reinvestment at a return) — "power" that is neither paid out nor compounded is stranded; no persistence beyond the oe_avg OR.
- Methodology issues: the 1.5× floor on mcap/OE is a units guard, fine; the implied-D&A fallback makes OE a margin rebuild for most non-US firers; the A2 verdict "no material gain, owner-earnings already used" is wrong for this gate — the FMP owner-earnings series is on disk but the rule reads only the EDGAR/derived pair; `~(shares_yoy > 0.05)` missing-passes.
- FMP augmentations: `owner-earnings` (U*, cached, global) `ownersEarningsPerShare`, `maintenanceCapex` → a second, date-matched OE; FQ `fq_capex_to_da` (65%) for the mechanism over time; `financial-growth` for OE/share persistence; ENR `sbc_polluted_flag` (on disk) where the cash yield is SBC-propped (enrichment map §262).
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Core = cheap on **both** sources where both exist (mcap/OE ≤10 and `fmp_st_owner_earnings_yield >= 0.10`); where only one exists keep the old rule as `_watch`; for ST-filled rows pair FMP capex with FMP average (as F9).
  2. [spirit] Require the mechanism to be durable and benign: `fq_capex_to_da < 0.8` in ≥3 FYs (TC) and `rev_3y_cagr >= -0.02`; otherwise the "surplus" is deferred maintenance.
  3. [spirit] Add a use-of-cash leg: `capret_yield_eff >= 0.03` or ROIC ≥10% with assets growing — and consider tiering this with overdepreciated_assets (same mechanism) rather than running two overlapping gates.

### arch_owner_operator  (line 2262; tier at 2272: `owner_operator_watch/_exceptional/_elite`; `controlled_sub_flag` at 2269)
- Spirit: compendium Meyer Phelps test (4) founder/owner-operator alignment; II.B "insider ownership 20–58% in the best cases", intelligent-fanatic checklist (below-market salary, large founder ownership **bought on the open market**); master ref IV flyover "family/insider control", VI Deden "owner-managed"; Russo "capacity to suffer". The thesis is a *manager who owns*, not a register entry.
- As coded: `_oo_common` (operating & `_roce_now_ok` & `_not_melting` & rev_yoy > −15% & insider ≥20%) & (US path: ROIC 4/5 & FCF 4/5 & shares_growth_3y ≤2% & history ≥5y | global path: (country≠US | roic_lindy NaN) & roce ≥12% & not one-off & FCF yield>0 & ~(shares_yoy>2%)). Tier: core = insider 20–60% or `_aligned` (fmp_insider_aligned_flag [7 rows] | insider_buy_flag [F4, US] | buyback_yield ≥1% | 3y shrink); exceptional = insider 30–60% & (insider_buy_flag | aligned | fmp_insider_net_usd_12m>0 [213 rows]); elite = top decile roic_lindy. Watch 5,707 → core 4,296 (461 US) → exceptional 338 → elite 14; `controlled_sub_flag` 8,570. Lenses: insider_ownership_pct, fmp_insider_alignment_ratio, −shares_growth_3y, roic_lindy.
- Lineage: `insider_ownership_pct` Yahoo snapshot (A1: includes corporate parents; definitions differ by market — JP/KR "insiders" are major holders); `insider_buy_flag` F4 (US); `fmp_insider_*` ENR (US, nearly empty); lindy/streak fields EDGAR, ST-filled (global, so the "US path" also runs for filled non-US names). The A1 20–60% core is implemented; the A2 B.16 founder leg is **open**.
- Spirit gaps: ownership is not operatorship — a 35% family trust, state holding, PE sponsor or founder-emeritus passes as "owner-operator"; no founder-in-title, no tenure, no salary-vs-stake, no open-market buying outside the US; no neglect leg (flyover carries it) and no price leg, so the archetype is a shareholder-register filter.
- Methodology issues: 40–60% parent-controlled subsidiaries remain in the core (the flag only marks ≥60%); `_aligned` via buyback ≥1% is weak; exceptional depends on US-only inputs (338 ≈ US); elite ranks roic_lindy, not alignment; the global path's `fcf_yield > 0` is stricter than the US path's history test (asymmetric bars by domicile).
- FMP augmentations: `key-executives` (N, global, ~9k firers) title contains "founder" / family surname match (A2 B.16 open); `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US) `typeOfReportingPerson == 'IN'` matched to an executive; `shares-float` (done) — insider% + freeFloat > 110% flags a strategic-holder double count; `governance-executive-compensation` (US) salary ≤ 1% of stake value; `insider-trading/statistics` (US) net buying ≥1 of 4 quarters.
- Suggestions:
  1. [spirit] Core requires an operator link: founder/family in an executive title (KE, global) **or** a 13D/G individual matching an executive (US) **or** F4/IST buying; ownership-only names go to `_watch` with `controlled_sub_flag` extended to ≥50% (A2 B.16 open).
  2. [method] Elite on the alignment axis (`fmp_insider_alignment_ratio` or stake value ÷ salary), not roic_lindy; make the two domicile paths the same bar (both demand FCF>0 and a 4/5 ROIC record where ST history exists).
  3. [spirit] Add the flyover legs so the tag carries a mispricing: `sent_n_analysts <= 3` and EV/EBIT ≤15 (or FCF yield ≥5%) — otherwise fold into flyover as its ownership component.

### arch_pension_overfunded  (line 4104)
- Spirit: compendium #6 "pension surpluses ... UK DB pension-surplus plays" (the source's examples are UK schemes); a positive funded status is a hidden asset the market prices at zero.
- As coded: operating & real sector & mcap>0 & `pension_funded_status` >0 & surplus/mcap ≥10% & (fcf_yield >3% | 0<pb<2) & `_not_melting`. No lenses. **9 firers, all US.**
- Lineage: `pension_funded_status` EDGAR footnote tag (`DefinedBenefitPlanFundedStatusOfPlan`) — **US-only by construction**; FMP cannot fill (enrichment map "cannot fill"); mcap USD (coherent). Snapshot at the last 10-K.
- Spirit gaps: a surplus is only an asset if extractable — US reversion pays a 50% excise tax plus income tax (~15–25 cents on the dollar), UK reversion 25% with trustee consent — so the economic value is a contribution holiday / pension income, not the gross number; no discount-rate sensitivity (a surplus at a 5.5% discount rate can vanish at 4%); no catalyst (plan termination, annuity buy-out, merger of schemes); the UK cases the source names are unmeasurable here.
- Methodology issues: gross surplus over mcap with no tax haircut; the "not dear" OR (fcf_yield >3% or pb<2) is most of the universe; 9 names is too thin to be an archetype (spirit_spec: "thin cores, usable but noisy"); the `_real_sector_f` guard exists only because an ETN inherited JPM's pension (a mapping artifact, not a thesis guard).
- FMP augmentations: none for funded status; `sec-filings-search/symbol` (US) 8-K for plan termination / annuity purchase as the catalyst; `income-statement` has no net periodic pension line (no help). Honest limit: SEC-only.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Haircut the surplus (US ×0.25 after excise and corporate tax; treat the remainder as ≤3 years of avoided service cost) before the 10% test.
  2. [spirit] Fold into `hidden_assets` as a `hidden_pension_surplus` component beside associates and net cash, surfaced as a flag, rather than a 9-name archetype.
  3. [data] Tag the archetype US-only in the book and in `docs/spirit_spec.md`; the compendium's UK plays need an ARS/annual-report footnote harvest that no feed provides.

### arch_post_reorg  (line 8211)
- Spirit: compendium Part III Assembly Theory — Verdad: EBIT yield >20% **at emergence** → +61% two-year return, 0–20% → −5%, negative → −21%; de-levering and a surviving moat are gating; nine building blocks (clean balance sheet, NOLs, fresh-start D&A distortion, MEIP, forced sellers into a thin float, distress type, released liabilities, OTC → uplisting catalyst path, warrants). "Diagnose the type of distress before anything else."
- As coded: `reorg_flag == 1` & operating & `_not_melting` & `_reorg_value` (non-cyclical: EBITDA yield ≥10% | EBIT yield ≥10% with D&A not write-down-inflated | FCF yield ≥8%; Energy/Materials: mid-cycle EBITDA yield ≥10%, trailing only where no mid-cycle exists, | FCF ≥8%) & `_reorg_lev_ok` (nde ≤4 | net cash | cyclical mid-cycle nde ≤3 with trailing ≤6 and margin>0) & `_emg_fresh` (reorg_date NaN or ≤5y). No lenses. **2 firers** (62 flagged).
- Lineage: `reorg_flag`, `reorg_date` EES (EDGAR `ReorganizationValue` / fresh-start tags; 62 flagged, 62 dated) — **US-only**; yields MASTER; `normalized_ebitda` MASTER (mid-cycle margin × current revenue); `nol_usd` is on the same EES row but not read here.
- Spirit gaps: the yield is on today's price, not the emergence price (the comment admits; A2 B.33 open); no distress-type proxy (financial vs economic: pre-filing revenue and gross-margin trend), which the source calls the only discriminating block; no forced-seller / thin-float / loan-to-own holder leg; no MEIP or post-emergence insider buying; no catalyst path (OTC → uplisting → coverage); NOLs unused although on the row; 62 → 2 means the value floor on trailing numbers kills nearly every real emergence (fresh-start D&A and peak-cycle trailing EBITDA both bias against passing).
- Methodology issues: nde 99-fill on negative trailing EBITDA fails leverage for a trough emerger (the mid-cycle relief needs EBITDA>0); the cyclical set is a sector label (A1 A.4 measured intensity open); `reorg_date` NaN is permissive; "2 members — spirit NaN" (spirit_spec) so the archetype ranks nothing.
- FMP augmentations: `sec-filings-search/symbol` (U, US) 8-K items 1.03 / 3.03 for a firmer effective date (A1 §c open); `historical-market-capitalization` (N, global) mcap at `reorg_date` → EBITDA yield at emergence (A2 B.33 open); `institutional-ownership/extract-analytics/holder` `isNew` (US) for the loan-to-own base turning over; `insider-trading/statistics` (US) first two quarters' buying; `symbol-change` / `profile.exchange` for the uplisting catalyst; FQ `fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets` (on disk) for "net debt falling".
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Measure the Verdad yield at emergence (HMC mcap at reorg_date + FQ TTM EBITDA at that date) and tier: core = ≥10% at emergence with net debt falling since; exceptional = ≥20% (A2 B.33 open); today's price yield becomes a lens.
  2. [spirit] Add the distress-type block: pre-filing 2-FY revenue ≥ −15% and gross margin held (financial distress) as core; falling revenue and margin (economic distress: the Chapter-22 retail cohort) → `_watch`.
  3. [spirit] Catalyst-path lenses: uplisting ≤24m, 13F holder count rising, insider buying ≤2 quarters post-emergence, `nol_usd/mcap` surfaced — and pool the tiny core with spinoff_* for ranking (spirit_spec's "rank the family jointly").

### arch_qarp  (line 2289)
- Spirit: compendium Tillinghast valuation rule ("single-digit P/E for average businesses; teens for better ones; low-20s only for exceptional franchises") and profitability sustained ~10 years; master ref II (level of profitability, FCF/P); engine: Russo-via-Buffett "pay fair for great" on lindy ROIIC.
- As coded: operating & `_roce_now_ok` & ~(roce known <3%) & `roiic_lindy >= 0.15` & `_qarp_cheap` (EV/EBITDA 0–12 | P/E 0–18 | EV/FCF 0–15) & n_yrs_roic_pos ≥4 & shares_growth_3y ≤2%. Lenses: roiic_lindy, ts_maxdd_5y, fqx_ebit_ttm_g, −ev_ebitda. 2,347 firers (538 US). `_SPIRITED`, not tiered.
- Lineage: `roiic_lindy` EDGAR, ST-filled (27.6k rows; A1: rolling ROIIC on small deltas is noisy), `n_yrs_positive_roic`, `shares_growth_3y` ST-filled (global); multiples MASTER (ev_ebitda 999-default fails the leg, fine); `ts_maxdd_5y` TS (lens only). A1 §c price-lindy core (1,450 of 2,221) and A2 B.14 per-share consistency exceptional are **both open** (spirited, no tiers).
- Spirit gaps: "quality" is one number (ROIIC) with no base-ROIC floor beyond roce ≥3% and no margin durability (`tc_min_opm`); "reasonable" has no growth or return context (Tillinghast's rule scales the multiple to the business's quality — the code applies flat cutoffs); no price-lindy; no FCF strength (Yartseva's strongest driver) except as one of three ORed multiples.
- Methodology issues: `roiic_lindy` has no upper cap (durable_reinvestment caps at 1.0; a tiny-denominator artifact passes here); the three-multiple OR takes the friendliest lens — P/E ≤18 alone is ordinary; missing shares_growth_3y fails (right sign); the engine's lens list and spirit_spec's (roic_lindy, cash_roic_lindy, earnings/FCF/EBIT yields) differ.
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth` (N, global) 5-year per-share revenue and OCF growth on every FY row (A2 B.14 open); TC `tc_min_opm` (on disk); TS `ts_maxdd_5y` (on disk, lens → core per A1); `key-metrics` `returnOnInvestedCapitalTTM` history as the base-ROIC floor for filled rows.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Cap `roiic_lindy <= 1.0`, floor `roic_lindy >= 0.10`; require ≥2 of the 3 cheapness lenses, or one D&A-neutral lens (EV/EBIT ≤12).
  2. [spirit] Tier it: core adds price-lindy (`ts_maxdd_5y` shallower than the name's market median) — A1 §c open; exceptional = FG 5y per-share revenue and OCF ≥+61% in every FY window and `tc_min_opm > 0` (A2 B.14 open).
  3. [spirit] Make "reasonable" relative: EV/EBIT ≤ 1.2 × ROIIC(%) or P/E ≤ 1.5 × `fqx_ebit_ttm_g`(%), capped at 25 — a multiple scaled to quality is the Tillinghast rule, flat cutoffs are not.

### arch_quiet_compounder  (line 2206; tier at 2213: `quiet_compounder_watch/_exceptional/_elite`)
- Spirit: master ref IV flyover / quiet compounders (durable moat, boring essential business, excellent capital allocation, <5 analysts ideally 0, family/insider control, ROIC >15%, strong FCF; "the most inefficient market for quality small-caps is Japan"); Mayer's "boring compounder before it is discovered".
- As coded: `_qc_common` (operating & `_roce_now_ok` & insider ≥10% & 12m return in [−10%, +50%] with a tape measure present) & (US path: roic_lindy ≥15% & ROIC 4/5 & shares_3y ≤3% & history ≥5 | global path: (non-US | lindy NaN) & roce ≥15% & not one-off & `_not_melting` & ~(shares_yoy>3%)). Tier: core = `ts_r52 <= 0.20` & `ts_maxdd_5y > -0.45` (measured where both present); exceptional = `sent_n_analysts <= 3` & `fqx_ebit_ttm_g >= 0.10`; elite = top decile roic_lindy. Watch 2,238 → core 940 (**19 US**) → exceptional 10 → elite **0** in the current tiers CSV. Not spirited.
- Lineage: `insider_ownership_pct` Yahoo; the momentum band reads MASTER snapshots (`_m12_raw`/`_py_raw`; A1 A.2 swap to `ts_r52` done only in the tier); `ts_*` TS (74%); `sent_n_analysts` SENT (27% — NaN ≤3 is False, so 73% of names can never be exceptional); `roic_lindy` ST-filled, yet the global path reads a one-year `roce`. A2 B.15 "quiet measured" is implemented; its founder/N-PORT exceptional leg is open.
- Spirit gaps: the global path has no durability at all (one year of ROCE ≥15% is not "proven ROIC"); the neglect leg (the defining half of the thesis) is only in the exceptional tier and only where SENT reaches; no FCF strength on the global path; no valuation (a quiet compounder at 35× is a description, not a setup); no moat/boring proxy (`tc_min_opm` durability, industry).
- Methodology issues: the US path's four-way AND vs the global one-year test produces 19 US vs 921 non-US core firers — an asymmetry of bars, not of opportunity; `insider >= 0.10` has different semantics per market; elite is empty (roic_lindy present for most core rows, so the quantile cut should exist — worth a look at the tier run); the +50% band cap and the core's `ts_r52 <= 0.20` are consistent but the band still reads stale snapshots.
- FMP augmentations: `analyst-estimates` `numAnalystsEps` (U*, cached, global) and `grades-consensus` bulk (1 call) → observed neglect universe-wide (A1 D#2 / A2 B.15 open); `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` (N, global holdings) "no US fund owns it"; `etf/asset-exposure` passive share <2%; `key-executives` founder title; TC `tc_min_opm`.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Give the global path the same durability as the US path: ST-filled `roic_lindy >= 0.12` (the fill exists) and `tc_min_opm > 0` over ≥5 FYs, in place of a one-year ROCE; read the band from `ts_r52`.
  2. [data] Observed neglect from the cached analyst-estimates payload or one grades-consensus bulk call so the exceptional tier can reach names outside SENT's 27% (A2 B.15 open).
  3. [spirit] Add a price leg to core (EV/EBIT ≤15 or FCF yield ≥5%) and rank elite on ROIC × neglect (1/(1+analysts)) rather than ROIC alone — "quiet" is the axis that makes it a mispricing.

### arch_regime_cyclical  (line 1596)
- Spirit: master ref I.E (9) Regime-Change Cyclical — three-layer test: (i) priced for old-cycle economics, (ii) balance sheet survived the downcycle, (iii) the next cycle has tighter supply / better demand / policy support / better capital discipline; compendium II.D capital cycle (capex cuts, closures, consolidation, falling capex/depreciation, insider buying, share-count reduction; the turn "at maximum pessimism"); failure mode "the new mean is just the old mean with a temporary supply disruption".
- As coded: `sector.isin(HEAVY_ASSET_SECTORS)` & `beaten_down_any(0.20)` & rev_yoy>0 & `_not_melting` & ~(6 < nde < 90) & (ebitda_inflection | ebitda_first_pos | 2pp ≤ ebitda_margin_delta ≤ 20pp | `margin_shock_any`) & `not_priced_in > 0.20`. Lenses: tc_med_opm − op margin, fqx_ebit_ttm_g, −ts_dist_hi260. 1,038 firers (223 US).
- Lineage: sector **label** (the measured `asset_heavy()` helper at line 1155 exists for the demand-shock sibling but is not called here — A1 A.4 open); `beaten_down_any` TS-first (global); annual flags MASTER; `not_priced_in_score` (yartseva_db) = mean of (rev/EBITDA/FCF yoy − Yahoo `price_yoy`) gaps and −ΔEV/S, i.e. a **snapshot** growth-vs-price differential (A1 A.2 class); `margin_shock_any` MASTER + qoq TTM. `nde` = 99 where EBITDA ≤0.
- Spirit gaps: layer (iii), the supply side, is unmeasured — no capex/D&A withdrawal (spirit_spec lists `fq_capex_to_da`, the gate does not), no industry-level capex or consolidation, no capital-discipline change (buybacks, payout); layer (i) is proxied by a growth-vs-price gap rather than price vs mid-cycle earnings (`normalized_ebitda` exists and is used by post_reorg); layer (ii) is a leverage cap only (no worst-year coverage). A 2pp margin lift on rising revenue after a 20% drawdown is any upcycle — nothing separates "regime change" from "ordinary cyclical bounce", which is the source's stated failure mode.
- Methodology issues: `~((nde > 6) & (nde < 90))` **passes the 99 fill**, so unknown or negative-EBITDA leverage — the trough cyclical most in need of a survival test — clears the cap; Consumer Discretionary as "heavy asset" admits restaurants and apparel; the 20pp cap on the direct margin leg is bypassed by the uncapped `margin_shock_any` OR; `not_priced_in > 0.20` mixes three growth gaps with an EV/S change on a stale price.
- FMP augmentations: FQ `fq_capex_to_da` and industry-median FQ capex growth (on disk: a supply-withdrawal lens computable per `industry`); TC `tc_med_opm`, `tc_min_ic`; `historical-market-capitalization` (N) for EV history; `insider-trading/statistics` (US) and `cash-flow` repurchases for the capital-discipline layer; A2 B.31 exceptional (op margin ≥5pp below `tc_med_opm` and turning, measured intensity, `ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.5` with `ts_r13 > 0`, insider/company buying) — open.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Call `asset_heavy(sector label)` (A1 A.4 open) and make leverage NaN-aware: `_nde_known <= 6` or net cash or `tc_min_ic >= 2`; the 99 fill must fail, not pass (Pernas: balance sheet first).
  2. [spirit] Layer (i) measured: EV / (mid-cycle margin × current revenue) ≤ 6 and op margin below `tc_med_opm` (spec lens → gate); replace `not_priced_in` with `fqx_ebit_ttm_g` vs `ts_r52` (panel, not snapshot).
  3. [spirit] Layer (iii) measured: name `fq_capex_to_da < 1` **and** industry-median capex growth ≤0 over two FYs, or a share-count/payout discipline change; exceptional per A2 B.31 with insider/company buying.

### arch_reinvest_inflect  (line 2303)
- Spirit: compendium Meyer Phelps test (3) "a long reinvestment runway"; Mauboussin ROIIC; engine: "ROIIC accelerating from a positive base AND assets actually growing (not financial engineering) — a compounder finding more runway".
- As coded: operating & `_roce_now_ok` & roic_lindy >0 & roiic_lindy ≥5% & roiic_acceleration ≥ +5pp & asset_3y_cagr ≥5%. No engine lenses (spirit_spec: roiic_acceleration, roic_acceleration, roiic_lindy, asset_3y_cagr, revenue_acceleration_lindy). 1,026 firers (226 US).
- Lineage: ROIIC family EDGAR, ST-filled — `roiic_acceleration` filled for 11.7k rows vs `roiic_lindy` 27.6k (the acceleration needs a longer window), `asset_3y_cagr` filled 38.8k; the EDGAR and FMP-recomputed definitions are assumed identical (`fmp_filled_roiic_acceleration` marks which). Global.
- Spirit gaps: "not financial engineering" is unmeasured — an acquisition grows assets and lifts year-one ROIIC exactly like organic runway (goodwill share delta is on disk: `fq_gw_pct_assets`); no revenue confirmation (assets growing faster than revenue is bloat; the spec's `revenue_acceleration_lindy` is not in the gate); no per-share term; no price (a runway is only a mispricing if unpaid-for); no TTM confirmation of the annual acceleration (A2 LEG open: `fqx_inc_ebit_margin`).
- Methodology issues: `roic_lindy > 0` is a near-zero base (siblings use ≥5–10%); +5pp acceleration on a rolling small-delta series is inside its noise (A1); no upper caps on roiic_lindy / acceleration (artifacts); a 5% asset CAGR is roughly inflation.
- FMP augmentations: FQX `fqx_inc_ebit_margin >= 0.20` / `fqx_ebit_ttm_g > 0` (on disk; A2 LEG open); FQ `fq_gw_pct_assets` delta (organic vs acquired); `financial-growth` (N, global) `threeYRevenueGrowthPerShare`; `key-metrics` ROIC TTM for a dated base.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Floors and caps: `roic_lindy >= 0.05`, `roiic_lindy <= 1.0`, acceleration confirmed on the TTM lens (`fqx_inc_ebit_margin >= 0.20` or `fqx_ebit_ttm_g > 0`) — A2 LEG open.
  2. [spirit] Organic test: goodwill/assets not up >5pp over the 3-year window and `rev_3y_cagr >= 0.5 × asset_3y_cagr`; failing names → `_watch` as possible roll-ups (route to xr_compounding_deployer).
  3. [spirit] Add a price term (`cheap_per_roiic <= 2` or EV/EBIT ≤20) or fold the rule into durable_reinvestment as its "accelerating" exceptional tier — on its own it is a quality descriptor.

### arch_retained_earnings_discount  (line 3928)
- Spirit: engine F7 "Buffett's dollar-retained test, priced" — decades of accumulated retained profit exceed the whole market cap while the business still earns; compendium Tillinghast profitability "sustained ~10 years"; scrapyard #6 as the family.
- As coded: operating & local mcap>0 & retained_earnings >0 & `_fx_coherent` & RE/mcap ≥1 & (NI>0 | ni_avg>0) & 0<pb<1.5 & `_not_melting`. No engine lenses (spirit_spec: RE/equity/pb, −pb, earnings_yield, equity_cagr_5y). 1,364 firers (206 US).
- Lineage: `retained_earnings` EDGAR (14%) ST-filled (19.3k rows) — a **level**, so the fill's reporting currency must match `market_cap`'s trading currency (the ni_avg/capex_avg fills go through `fq_conv_`; verify RE takes the same bridge, else GBp/HKD-ADR rows are off by the FX factor); `pb`, `net_income_ttm` MASTER; `ni_avg` ST-filled.
- Spirit gaps: Buffett's test asks whether each retained dollar created ≥$1 of market value — RE > mcap is that test *failing*, so the archetype is really "the market says the retained capital was wasted; is it wrong?" and nothing in the gate answers that (no ROE floor, no distribution); a high-payout company has low RE by construction, so the screen systematically prefers hoarders — the governance trap master ref I.G names as the failure mode; no catalyst (TSE PBR-reform disclosure, activist, buyback); it duplicates tangible_value / cundill where P/B<1 and old profits coincide.
- Methodology issues: US GAAP buybacks sit in treasury stock (RE untouched) while IFRS issuers often charge them to RE — the ratio is not comparable across markets; `ni_avg > 0` admits a currently loss-making name; pb<1.5 is loose for a "discount" thesis; RE is nominal and inflation-swollen for old industrials.
- FMP augmentations: `scores-bulk` `retainedEarnings` (U, global, 1 stream) as a same-source cross-check; `balance-sheet-statement` `retainedEarnings` + treasury stock / `commonStock` (global) for a GAAP/IFRS-consistent "cumulative retained" (RE + treasury); `dividends` (U) and `cash-flow` repurchases for the distribution leg.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Normalise the numerator to RE + treasury stock (or equity − paid-in capital) so buyback history counts; confirm the RE fill is FX-bridged to the mcap currency.
  2. [spirit] Require the retained capital to earn and to move: ROE ≥8% now and `capret_yield_eff >= 0.02` (or a buyback ≥1%) — otherwise the discount is deserved (hoarder / governance trap).
  3. [spirit] Keep the ratio as a lens inside tangible_value / cluseau_realizable_book and reserve the archetype for the catalyst subset (activist 13D ≤365d, TSE/KR value-up disclosure where harvestable, buyback ≥2%).

### arch_roic_inflect  (line 1804; `_roic_turn_q` at 1802)
- Spirit: compendium #8 first clean quarter / earnings divergence ("a large positive change ... that the market has not yet priced"); Pernas "the business motor is strengthening"; master ref II profitability present and robust.
- As coded: operating & (`roic_inflection_flag` | `cash_roic_inflection_flag` | `_roic_turn_q` = bs_ebit_turned & fqx_roic_ttm >0) & `cash_roic_lindy` (NaN→−1) >0 & rev_yoy >0 & `_op_viable(0)`. No engine lenses (spirit_spec: roic_acceleration, roce_delta_yoy, fqx_roic_ttm, fcf_margin_delta_yoy, fq_rev_growth). 1,320 firers (329 US).
- Lineage: inflection flags EDGAR, ST-filled (35k rows for the cash flag; global); `cash_roic_lindy` ST-filled (38.8k; missing fails — right sign); `bs_ebit_turned` BS (52%), `fqx_roic_ttm` FQX (60%); `rev_yoy` MASTER. The A2 PROXY (dated TTM turn) is implemented, but as a third OR rather than a confirmation.
- Spirit gaps: the turn has no size (−1% → +1% ROIC counts) and no level (fqx_roic_ttm > 0, not ≥ a cost of capital); the divergence half of #8 — price has *not* reacted — is absent (narrative_lag carries it); no persistence (one positive year after a dip); `cash_roic_lindy > 0` means the history was mostly positive, so this is dip-recovery, not first-time profitability (fine, but the tag should say so); no valuation.
- Methodology issues: three ORed lenses (two annual EDGAR flags plus a quarterly turn) widen rather than confirm; `rev_yoy > 0` is drift; `_op_viable(0)` is deliberately permissive; ST-filled inflection flags are assumed to match the EDGAR definition.
- FMP augmentations: FQX `fqx_roic_ttm` level (on disk) as a floor; TS `ts_r52` / `ts_rs_pct_mkt` (on disk) for the not-reacted leg; `earnings-surprises-bulk` (N, global, ~13 calls) a beat in the turning quarter; `key-metrics` ROIC TTM history for the delta.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Size and confirm the turn: `fqx_roic_ttm >= 0.05` and ROIC delta ≥5pp; where FQX is measured, the quarterly turn must confirm an annual flag (AND), the OR stays as `_watch` (A2 PROXY spirit).
  2. [spirit] Exceptional = the divergence: `ts_r52 <= 0.20` or `ts_rs_pct_mkt <= 50` while the TTM ROIC has crossed 5% (compendium #8 high change / low reaction).
  3. [spirit] Add a post-inflection price floor (EV/EBIT ≤15 on the TTM) so the archetype is a mispriced turn, not a turn.

### arch_self_funded_returner  (line 4056)
- Spirit: master ref VI Deden ("rejects companies in the business of their common stock"); compendium II.B dilution as the cardinal sin; engine F13 "no-Ponzi financing": the financing line has been a net outflow while FCF is positive, proven by the statement's plumbing.
- As coded: operating & mcap>0 & financing_cf_ttm <0 & ~(outflow-year share <0.8, where ≥4 FYs observed) & fcf_ttm >0 & (P/E 0–15 | 0<pb<1.5) & `_not_melting`. Lenses: −tc_uncov_payout_3y, outflow share, fcf_yield, tc_fcf_pos share. 3,171 firers (747 US). `_SPIRITED`.
- Lineage: `financing_cf_ttm` EDGAR, ST-filled (19.7k; a sign test, currency-neutral); `fmp_st_financing_outflow_years/years` ST (82%, FY sign counts); `fcf_ttm`, `p_e`, `pb` MASTER. A1 §c "FCF ≥ the financing outflow" (1,785 of 3,121) is **open**; A1 notes the forensic_payout_confirmed tautology on this member.
- Spirit gaps: an outflow is also debt repayment under lender pressure — the sign cannot tell returning to owners from de-levering under duress, nor from a token dividend; no size (any outflow); FCF is not compared with the outflow, so a company paying out of balance-sheet cash passes as "self-funded" until the cash runs out; no per-share outcome (share count) — the Deden test is about the stock, and the financing line can net a raise against a larger repayment inside one FY.
- Methodology issues: the cheapness OR (P/E ≤15 or P/B <1.5) is about half the operating universe; FY sign counting hides a raise-and-repay year; NaN-permissive persistence below 4 FYs (acceptable, documented).
- FMP augmentations: `cash-flow-statement` components (U, global, in the ST cache): `commonStockIssued`, `commonStockRepurchased`, `dividendsPaid`, `debtRepayment`, `debtIssued` — decompose the line; `dividends` (U); FQ `fq_shares_yoy` (on disk).
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Core = FCF ≥ the financing outflow **and** outflow ≥2% of mcap (A1 §c open); cheapness on FCF yield ≥6% instead of P/E-or-P/B (the thesis is cash).
  2. [spirit] Decompose the outflow: owner returns (dividends + repurchases − issuance) ≥50% of it → returner; debt paydown dominant → route to levered_inflection / oak_deleveraging; any equity issuance in the TTM → veto (Deden).
  3. [method] Add `fq_shares_yoy <= 0` as the per-share corroboration so a repay-and-issue year cannot net to "self-funded".

### arch_special_situation  (line 8227; deal anchors at 8002–8029)
- Spirit: compendium II.A merger-arb spread math + HSR calendar, odd-lot tenders (SC TO-I), 13E3 going-private / appraisal, DEFM14A "Background of the Merger" and break fees; master ref III transaction-driven; Dalius's two questions ("why does the spread exist?", "what's the downside?"); EV-gate sizing.
- As coded: mcap>0 & ((merger_flag | going_private_flag) — event alone | (tender_flag & `_not_melting` & ((operating & `_excellent_value`) | (financial/REIT & pb<1)))). EDGAR flags NaN-filled with EVT dated anchors ≤270d (`evt_merger_proxy_date`, `evt_ma_target_date`, `evt_tender_date`). No lenses (binary). 315 firers (306 US).
- Lineage: `merger_flag` (300), `going_private_flag` (38), `tender_flag` EES (EDGAR form detection, **US-only**); EVT `sec-filings-search` (US, 2,000-row partial); `mergers-acquisitions-latest` is S-4 based, **US** (A2 corrects A1's "global"). No offer price in any feed, so no spread (spirit_spec: "binary — none").
- Spirit gaps: no spread, consideration type (cash vs stock), time to close, regulatory step or break price — the entire arithmetic of the source; no target-vs-acquirer split (an S-4 filer is often the buyer); non-US mechanisms (UK schemes / PUSU, JP TOB / MBO, KR, AU compulsory acquisition) are absent — 9 non-US firers; the definitive branch is a list of deals, not a screen (the comment accepts this).
- Methodology issues: the 270-day freshness applies to EVT dates only — the EDGAR flags carry no window here, so a closed or broken deal can linger; going-private includes completed squeeze-outs; the tender branch's value floor uses trailing yields that a deal price has already moved; `_not_melting` on a target is irrelevant to spread capture (the comment's own logic for the definitive branch).
- FMP augmentations: `mergers-acquisitions-search` (U, US) `targetedSymbol` vs acquirer; `sec-filings-search/form-type` SC TO-T / SC 14D-9 / DEFM14A / 8-K 1.01 (US) for dated, typed anchors; the weekly panel (on disk) for an announcement-week jump and post-announcement flatness (a spread-like profile without the offer price); `delisted-companies` (done) for closes. Honest limit: no global deal feed exists on this key.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Window the EDGAR flags (≤270d) and split target from acquirer (`targetedSymbol`, 8-K 1.01 vs S-4 filer); mark a deal dead when the price has fallen >20% from its announcement week.
  2. [spirit] Spread proxy from the panel: announcement-week `ts` jump ≥10% and ≤±3% drift since (bounded, dated) as core; exceptional = cash tender (SC TO-T) with flat `ts_r13` — A2 B.24 open.
  3. [data] Say in the book that non-US deals are unmeasured; the only global catalyst lens on this key is DIV irregular rows (special dividends / capital reductions), which belong to net_cash_returner rather than here.

### arch_strong_coverage  (line 2047)
- Spirit: compendium Tillinghast red flag "Debt/EBITDA >4x in the worst year is scary" (an avoid rule); Pernas "balance-sheet and financing risk dominate valuation when they become existential" (balance sheet first). Engine: debt burden trivially serviceable through any lens.
- As coded: operating & mcap ≥$50M & (interest_coverage ≥8 | `tc_min_ic` ≥8 | nde ≤0 | net_cash_pct_sane ≥20%) & ebitda_ttm >0 & ebitda_margin_sane >0. Lenses: tc_min_ic, interest_coverage, net_cash_pct_mcap, tc_fcf_pos share. **8,562 firers** (2,012 US) — the broadest in the slice; `_SPIRITED`, not tiered.

A1 §B "demote to a flag, or core = through-cycle IC / net cash with FCF>0" and A2 Z4 tiering are **open** (no `_tier` call).
- Spirit gaps: not a mispricing — it is the safety filter the sources apply before a thesis (A1 said the same); no principal service (interest only), no maturity wall (unmeasurable), no worst-year test on the net-cash legs (today's net cash can be last year's raise); each ORed leg is ordinary (A1: 78% pass IC ≥8, 66% net cash).
- Methodology issues: `tc_min_ic` counts only FYs with interest >0, so a name that levered up recently reads "strong through the cycle"; where interest ≤0 the fill is NaN and the name passes only via nde ≤0 — a debt-free filer with a stale nde snapshot is dropped; `ebitda_margin_sane` (0, 0.6) used as a gate excludes >60%-margin royalty/IP businesses, which are the strongest coverage names of all.
- FMP augmentations: ENR `fmp_debt_service_coverage`, `fmp_interest_coverage`, `fmp_altman_z` (on disk, 82%, global; spirit_spec lists altman, the gate reads none); `balance-sheet-statement` short- vs long-term debt split (global) as a maturity-wall proxy; `company-notes` (US) listed notes exist (low).
- Suggestions:
  1. [spirit] Demote to `strong_coverage_flag` consumed as a lens/veto by levered_inflection, regime_cyclical, post_reorg and the value family (A1 §B open) — or tier: core = `tc_min_ic >= 8` over ≥5 FYs **or** net cash with `tc_fcf_pos/tc_fcf_years >= 0.8` (A2 Z4 open).
  2. [method] Read `fmp_debt_service_coverage >= 1.5` (principal included) and `fmp_altman_z >= 3` as the exceptional lenses; treat NaN coverage from zero interest as covered only when `_nde_known <= 0`.
  3. [method] Replace the `ebitda_margin_sane` gate with a one-off guard (margin >60% allowed when `tc_med_opm` corroborates) so royalty names are not excluded by a sanity clamp.

### arch_sustainable_scaler  (line 2992)
- Spirit: master ref VI "The MCC formula: growing revenues and earnings and not diluting"; compendium II.B screen (≥25% growth and accelerating, profitable or at a clear inflection, gross margin high and stable/expanding, flat or shrinking count, <3× sales / <20× EBITDA, net cash, insider 20–58%); Andreola–Deden gate (mcap <$300M, per-share revenue growth >15%, share-count CAGR <5%, net cash or low leverage, low institutional ownership, 52-week high or base breakout).
- As coded: operating & mcap <$2bn & USD revenue ≥$20M & shares_3y_cagr (NaN→0) ≤5% & `_durable_growth` (rev_3y_cagr 15–100% | rev_yoy 15–100% with per-share FCF growing on either lens) & `_self_funding` (per-share FCF growth | fcf_margin >3% | roic_after_sbc ≥10%) & `_not_pricey` (EV/S ≤8 or NaN) & `_profit_present`. No engine lenses (spirit_spec: fmp_st_sales_ps_3y_g, fcf_per_share_yoy, −shares_growth_3y, rev_yoy_pos_share_12q, fcf_margin, −ev_sales). 1,016 firers (151 US).
- Lineage: `rev_3y_cagr`, `rev_yoy`, `fcf_per_share_yoy`, `fcf_margin`, `shares_3y_cagr`, `ev_sales` MASTER snapshots; `revenue_ttm_usd` USD-converted (right); `roic_after_sbc` EDGAR (US); `fqx_fcf_ps_g` FQX (25%, the A1 §b per-share panel lens, added as an OR). Global.
- Spirit gaps: growth is total revenue, not per-share (the spec's `fmp_st_sales_ps_3y_g` is on disk and unused in the gate); no earnings growth (the MCC formula has both), no gross-margin stability, no balance-sheet leg (net cash preferred), no neglect / discovery leg, no tape (52-week high / base — the Andreola half); EV/S ≤8 and mcap <$2bn are far looser than the source's <3× sales and <$500m ceiling; "accelerating" is absent.
- Methodology issues: `shares_3y_cagr` NaN → 0 means **missing share history passes as non-diluting**, the wrong sign for the "cardinal sin"; `_not_pricey` NaN-passes; `fcf_margin > 0.03` is a thin self-funding proof; the growth OR mixes a 3-year CAGR with a single YoY print; 100% caps are sound base-effect guards.
- FMP augmentations: ST `fmp_st_sales_ps_3y_g` (on disk); `financial-growth` (N, global) `threeYRevenueGrowthPerShare`, `weightedAverageSharesDilutedGrowth`; FQ gross-margin delta (on disk); TS `ts_dist_hi52` / BS base flags (on disk) for the tape leg; N-PORT / 13F holder counts for "low institutional" (US / global-by-US-funds).
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Missing share history fails (or require `fq_shares_yoy` present and ≤5%); make the growth core per-share (`fmp_st_sales_ps_3y_g >= 0.15`).
  2. [spirit] Add the MCC legs: earnings growing (`fqx_ebit_ttm_g > 0`), gross margin not falling (FQ GM delta ≥ −1pp), net cash or `nde <= 1`.
  3. [spirit] Tier it: exceptional = mcap <$500M, EV/S ≤3, tape confirmation (`ts_dist_hi52 >= 0.9` or a BS base) and observed neglect (≤2 analysts, few 13F/N-PORT holders) — the discovery half of the source.

### arch_tangible_value  (line 1825)
- Spirit: compendium #6 scrapyard ("assets carried at historical cost ... worth a multiple of enterprise value"); Wolf/Oak Archetype 10 distressed deep value (P/B or P/cash <0.5, cash >20% of mcap, a realisation path; reject stale Level-3 marks and serial raisers); Liger asset-backed optionality ("liquidation value is not cash": deduct claims senior to equity, management willing to sell, dilution risk limited); master ref III net-nets.
- As coded: operating & mcap ≥$10M & 0<p_tb<0.7 & ~(tangible_equity/equity ≤0.5) & `_not_melting` & ~(fcf_yield < −15%) & ~(net_cash_pct >1.0) & nde ≤4. No engine lenses (spirit_spec: −p_tb, ncav_pct_mcap, tangible_equity/equity, cash/tangible_equity). 1,097 firers (112 US).

- Spirit gaps: nothing about what the tangible book *is* (cash and receivables vs plant and inventory — the realisability the sources insist on; `fmp_ncav` / `ncav_pct_mcap` are on disk), no senior claims (minorities, preferreds, leases), no catalyst (asset sale, buyback below book, activist, wind-up — Liger's "management has demonstrated willingness to sell or redeploy"), no dilution-risk leg beyond the >100%-cash veto, no runway; the value-trap default of a permanent discount (P/TB history) is unmeasured.
- Methodology issues: `nde <= 4` on the 99 fill **fails every negative-EBITDA name**, i.e. exactly "the money-losing-but-asset-rich names it exists to find" that the gate-audit comment at 1833 says the rule was relaxed to keep; 0.7 is looser than the sources' <0.5; the ≤0.5 tangible-share test is NaN-permissive (documented); sub-book firers overlap negative_ev_value's `pb<0.7` branch (A1 B.1 open there).
- FMP augmentations: ENR `fmp_ncav`, `fmp_graham_net_net`, `fmp_altman_z` (on disk, 82%; the enrichment map calls the distress gate "shipped" for this archetype but the gate reads none); `historical-market-capitalization` (N, global) for a P/TB history (permanent-discount test); `acquisition-of-beneficial-ownership` (US) 13D; `cash-flow` repurchases (buyback below TB); `balance-sheet` minority interest and preferred lines (global) for senior claims.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Leverage NaN-aware: `_nde_known <= 4` or total debt / tangible equity ≤1, so loss-making asset plays survive the 99 fill; add `fmp_altman_z >= 1.8` or runway ≥3 years as the survival leg.
  2. [spirit] Realisability tiers: core = P/TB <0.7 with (cash + receivables) ≥ 50% of tangible book or `ncav_pct_mcap >= 0.5`; plant-and-inventory books → `_watch`; net out minorities and preferreds.
  3. [spirit] Exceptional = a realisation catalyst ≤12 months (buyback ≥2% below TB, 13D, asset-sale 8-K, wind-up) per Liger/Wolf; receive negative_ev_value's sub-book branch here (A1 B.1 open).

### arch_tax_efficient  (line 2031)
- Spirit: engine AB — a legitimately low structural tax rate on real operating profit, as distinct from "no tax because no profit"; compendium III NOL tax assets as the cousin. No source treats a low rate as a setup on its own.
- As coded: operating & 3% ≤ effective_tax_rate < 15% & pretax_income_ttm >0 & `_op_viable(0)`. No engine lenses (spirit_spec: −effective_tax_rate, −fq_cash_tax_rate, roce). 215 firers (**208 US**).
- Lineage: `effective_tax_rate` EDGAR (~2.1k rows) plus a `fq_etr_valid` fill that reaches only 395 rows because `fq_book_tax_rate_fy` (FY tax ÷ FY pretax from the quarterly panel) is 4% covered — so the rate is measured for ~5% of the universe and the archetype is **US-only by data accident**; `pretax_income_ttm` EDGAR, FQ-filled (24.7k, global — the "dead gate" note at 4008 is now stale). ENR `fmp_effective_tax_rate` / `fmp_tax_burden` (ratios-ttm, global, 82%) are on disk and **unread**.
- Spirit gaps: no mispricing — the market already prices after-tax EPS; the only investable question is whether the rate persists, and a 3–15% book rate is exactly the population the OECD Pillar Two 15% minimum (2024–26, groups >€750M revenue) is designed to end, so the "structure" can be a liability; no cash-tax corroboration in the gate (the spec's `fq_cash_tax_rate` is 4% covered); no persistence (A2 EXC "in band ≥3 of 5 FYs" open); no valuation.
- Methodology issues: a single TTM book rate; the 3% floor cannot separate partial NOL usage (5–10%) from structure; `_op_viable(0)` is permissive; the engine's absent lens list vs the spec.
- FMP augmentations: ENR `fmp_effective_tax_rate` (on disk, global) as the primary rate; the ST 8-FY `incomeTaxExpense / incomeBeforeTax` (cached) for persistence (A2 EXC open); FQ `fq_cash_tax_rate` where present; `profile` country + `revenue_ttm_usd` for a Pillar-Two exposure flag.
- Suggestions:
  1. [data] Read `fmp_effective_tax_rate` as the global lens with the EDGAR rate as confirmation; the 5% reach is a plumbing gap, not a thesis limit.
  2. [method] Persistence: rate in band in ≥3 of the last 5 FYs (ST cache) and cash rate ≤20% where measured (A2 EXC open).
  3. [spirit] Demote to `low_tax_structure_flag` with a Pillar-Two exposure note (rate <15% and USD revenue >$800M); keep an archetype only where the structure is unpaid-for (P/E ≤12 on after-tax E).

### arch_tax_verified_earnings  (line 4012)
- Spirit: engine F11 "you do not pay real cash taxes on fake earnings" — the tax authority audits the P&L; enrichment map §232 (P/E screens flattered by accruals; income quality as a negative gate); Liger "clean cash conversion".
- As coded: operating & mcap>0 & NI>0 & 18% ≤ effective_tax_rate ≤ 40% & 0 < P/E ≤ 12 & `_not_melting`. No engine lenses (spirit_spec: −p_e, fq_cash_tax_rate, effective_tax_rate, fcf_yield). 164 firers (**151 US**).
- Lineage: `effective_tax_rate` as above (~5% of the universe measured → US-only in effect); `p_e`, `net_income_ttm` MASTER. ENR `fmp_effective_tax_rate` (82%, global) and `fmp_income_quality` (82%) on disk, unread here.
- Spirit gaps: the thesis is about **cash** taxes paid, but the gate reads the book rate — a 25% book charge can be entirely deferred, which is the accrual the rule exists to exclude; no CFO/NI corroboration (understated_earnings has it); no persistence; the rate test carries the whole thesis and is measured for 5% of names, so the archetype is a P/E ≤12 screen with a US filter.
- Methodology issues: one TTM rate; NI>0 is redundant with the ETR being written only when pretax >0 (documented); the 40% cap is fine; three forensic gates (this, understated_earnings, cash_quality) rank the same "earnings are real" fact under different names.
- FMP augmentations: ENR `fmp_effective_tax_rate` (global) primary; ENR `fmp_income_quality >= 0.8` as the cash corroboration; FQ `fq_cash_tax_rate >= 0.15` (taxes paid ÷ pretax) where present as the exceptional lens; ST 8-FY for persistence (A2 EXC open).
- Suggestions:
  1. [data] Take the global book rate from `fmp_effective_tax_rate`; make `fq_cash_tax_rate` the exceptional tier (the true lens), not a sparse spec lens.
  2. [method] Require `fmp_income_quality >= 0.8` (CFO ≥ 0.8× NI) so a book charge on accrued profit does not count as "verified"; persistence in ≥3 of 5 FYs (A2 EXC open).
  3. [spirit] Merge into one "earnings verified" forensic archetype with tiers (tax-verified, cash-verified, both) beside understated_earnings and cash_quality; rank by P/FCF rather than P/E.

### arch_understated_earnings  (line 3816)
- Spirit: engine F2 — the accruals red flag inverted: CFO persistently far above NI (deferred-revenue float, conservative provisioning, heavy non-cash charges) while the market prices the understated E; Liger "clean cash conversion"; enrichment map §262 warns the same signature is produced by a large SBC add-back.
- As coded: operating & mcap>0 & NI>0 & 1.5 ≤ CFO/NI ≤ 4 (NaN → `fmp_income_quality`) & (`cash_conversion` ≥1.1 | fcf_yield ≥10% | year-ago CFO/NI ≥1.2) & ~(ΔWC > 50% of CFO, where observed) & 0 < P/E ≤ 15 & ~(shares_yoy > 5%) & `_not_melting`. No engine lenses (spirit_spec: fmp_income_quality, fq_cfo_to_ni, year-ago ratio, −fq_sloan_accruals, −p_e). 1,935 firers (456 US).
- Lineage: `cfo_ttm`, `net_income_ttm` MASTER (80%); `fmp_income_quality` ENR (key-metrics TTM, 82%) as the fill — same ratio, provider basis; `cash_conversion` MASTER is **CFO / EBITDA** (yartseva_db 818), not CFO/NI; `fq_cfo_p`, `fq_ni_cf_p`, `fq_chg_wc` FQ (70%); `p_e` MASTER. Global.
- Spirit gaps: the mechanism is never identified — D&A above capex (owner_earnings_power), acquired-intangible amortisation (xr_amortization_mask), deferred-revenue float (customer_float), provisioning, **and SBC add-back** all print CFO ≫ NI, and only the last is a trap; `sbc_polluted_flag` (on disk, 1,689 rows) is not read; no capex term, so a capital-intensive business with CFO ≫ NI and capex ≫ D&A passes (the fcf_yield OR only partly covers); persistence is two windows.
- Methodology issues: CFO/EBITDA ≥1.1 as "durability corroboration" is friendliest to a working-capital release, the very thing the WC veto tries to exclude (and the veto is NaN-permissive for 28% of names); the 4.0 cap is right; P/E on NI while the thesis is cash (P/CFO would be the coherent price term).
- FMP augmentations: `cash-flow-statement` (U, global, in FQ/ST) `stockBasedCompensation`, `depreciationAndAmortization`, `changeInWorkingCapital`, `capitalExpenditure` → decompose CFO − NI; ENR `sbc_polluted_flag`, `fmp_sbc_to_revenue` (on disk); FQ `fq_sloan_accruals` (on disk, spec lens) → gate; FQ `fq_fcf` for FCF/NI.
- Suggestions:
  1. [method] Decompose the gap: (CFO − NI − SBC − ΔWC) / NI ≥ 0.5 — the surplus must be D&A, provisions or deferred revenue, not SBC or a WC swing; veto `sbc_polluted_flag`.
  2. [method] Replace the CFO/EBITDA corroboration with FCF/NI ≥ 1.2 (capex-aware) or `fq_sloan_accruals <= -0.05`; price on P/CFO ≤ 10 beside P/E.
  3. [spirit] Tier by mechanism: exceptional = deferred-revenue or provisioning driven (customer_float overlap, `fq_defrev` rising) at P/FCF ≤10 — the "conservative accounting" the source means; D&A-driven → owner_earnings_power; one forensic family with mechanism tags instead of five overlapping gates.

---

Erratum (retained_earnings_discount, Lineage): the RE fill *is* FX-bridged (`fq_conv_retained_earnings`, line 499–517) and carries an IFRS-reserves sanity cap (RE ≤ 1.05×equity + treasury), so the "verify the bridge" note is resolved; the GAAP/IFRS buyback-treatment point stands.

## 10-line summary (slice rest_b, 37 archetypes)

1. All 37 sections are written (15 by the first reviewer, 22 here); every archetype in `slice_rest_b.txt` now has a Spirit / As coded / Lineage / Gaps / Methodology / FMP / Suggestions block with firer counts from the current `archetype_tags.csv`.
2. Data accident, not thesis: `tax_efficient` (208 of 215 US) and `tax_verified_earnings` (151 of 164 US) are US-only because `effective_tax_rate` reaches ~5% of the universe (EDGAR ~2.1k + a 395-row FQ fill off a 4%-covered `fq_book_tax_rate_fy`) while ENR `fmp_effective_tax_rate` (global, 82%) sits unread.
3. The `nde` 99-fill inverts two gates: `regime_cyclical`'s `~(6 < nde < 90)` **passes** unknown / negative-EBITDA leverage (the trough cyclical most needing a survival test), and `tangible_value`'s `nde <= 4` **fails** every negative-EBITDA name — the loss-making asset plays its own comment says the rule was relaxed to keep.
4. Descriptors without a mispricing: strong_coverage (8,562), no_dilution, owner_operator, quiet_compounder, reinvest_inflect, qarp, tax_efficient, large_cap_quality, low_sbc_quality, geographic_global — each needs a price or neglect leg or demotion to a `_flag` (A1 §B open for strong_coverage / owner_operator / quiet_compounder / no_dilution).
5. The forensic family measures ~3 facts under 6 names (D&A > capex: overdepreciated_assets / owner_earnings_power; CFO > NI: understated_earnings / tax_verified / cash_quality; sub-book: tangible_value / retained_earnings_discount / negative_ev_value's pb<0.7 branch) — mechanism-tagged tiers inside one family would replace overlapping gates.
6. SEC/US-only legs to surface honestly in the book: nol_shell, pension_overfunded, post_reorg, special_situation (EDGAR flags with a 730-day harvest window and no in-gate freshness), lifo_hidden_reserve, insider legs (insider_conviction, owner_operator exceptional, micro_activist 13D); FMP offers no global substitute for any of them.
7. Still-open prior-audit items repeated here: A1 A.4 (regime_cyclical: `asset_heavy()` exists at line 1155 but is not called), A1 A.2 (`ts_r52` in institutional_accumulation and quiet_compounder's band), A1 §c (self_funded FCF ≥ outflow; qarp price-lindy), A1 B.1 (negative_ev sub-book branch), A2 B.13 splits, B.14/B.15/B.16/B.24/B.31/B.33 exceptional specs, A2 Z4 tiering for strong_coverage.
8. Missing-passes patterns worth one sweep: sustainable_scaler `shares_3y_cagr` NaN→0 (missing history counts as non-diluting), the `shares_yoy` vetoes in no_dilution / owner_earnings_power / understated_earnings, net_cash_returner's unclamped `net_cash_pct`, special_situation's unwindowed EDGAR flags.
9. Tier mechanics: elite ranks `roic_lindy` for no_dilution, owner_operator and quiet_compounder instead of each archetype's defining axis (dilution, alignment, neglect); `quiet_compounder_elite` is empty and its US path yields 19 firers vs 921 non-US (asymmetric bars by domicile); spirit_spec lens lists have drifted from the engine's in net_cash_returner, qarp, regime_cyclical.
10. Zero-call closers already on disk: `fmp_effective_tax_rate`, `fmp_altman_z`, `fmp_ncav`, `fmp_debt_service_coverage`, `fq_capex_to_da`, `fq_da_to_ppe`, `fmp_st_sales_ps_3y_g`, `fmp_st_owner_earnings_yield`, `sbc_polluted_flag`, `tc_uncov_payout_3y`, `fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets`, `asset_heavy()` — most of this slice's method gaps are wiring, not fetching.

# Slice sources: Named-investor and uploaded-document archetypes (44)

## Audit 3 — slice "sources": named-investor and uploaded-document archetypes (44)

Read-only audit of `archetype_tags.py` against the five source documents in `S/sources/` (WO = Wolf of Oakville & Oak Bloke recipes;
LC = Liger Cub / Byron Street methodology; AC = Archetype Compendium; MR = Master Archetype Reference; PR = Pernas pitches), the
FMP catalogue (`S/fmp_catalog.md`, A2 §A), the enrichment map, and the prior audits A1 (`docs/archetype_upgrade_audit.md`) and A2
(`docs/fmp_catalog_upgrade_audit.md`). Line numbers are the `df['arch_*'] =` assignment in `archetype_tags.py` (current file).

Conventions. "watch/core" = the `_tier()` split (the dump rule survives as `<name>_watch`; `arch_<name>` is the tightened core, applied only
where `measured`; names the data does not reach keep the watch rule). "reframed" = `_reframe()` replaced the dump rule outright.
Lineage tags: M = master `asymmetry_global.csv` (EDGAR/Yahoo/FMP-filled), ST = `fmp_st_*` (annual statements, global, fills EDGAR
lindy inputs via the fill map at lines 362-398 / 541-562), FQ/FQX = quarterly panel (global), TS/BS = weekly-panel snapshots (global),
TC = through-cycle (global), EVT = `fmp_events` (earnings/dividends global; SEC form dates US-only), SENT = `fmp_sentiment` (partial run),
YF = Yahoo snapshot, EDGAR = US-only XBRL. Where a recipe is not in the five documents (Cundill, Templeton, Weinstein, Kullamägi, Weschler,
Frazzini-Pedersen, Cluseau, Lynch multiples, Mauboussin cash-quality) the source is the author's comment block plus the named investor's
published rule, and this is said explicitly.

---

### arch_bab_becoming  (line 2442; reframed at 2512)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents. Comment block: Frazzini & Pedersen (2014) "Betting Against Beta" — alpha = psi(1-beta), long low-beta quality; "becoming" is the engine's own extension: "(No beta time-series available, so improving fundamental stability stands in for the paper's beta compression.)" F&P define no compression rule.
- As coded (reframed): is_operating; revenue_ttm_usd >= $20M; fcf_margin > -5%; _roce_now_ok; rev_yoy > -5%; nde <= 3; AND [compress = within-market 1y beta rank <= 3y rank - 0.10, OR 1y vol < 0.85 x 3y vol, from a 3y rank > 0.35 with panel beta > 0] OR [no panel 3y beta AND Yahoo band 0.85 < beta_shrunk <= 1.15 with any margin delta >= 1pp or interval_inflect_any, and FCF/EBITDA inflection or fcf_margin > 0]. Lenses: 3y rank - 1y rank; 1 - vol1y/vol3y; ebitda_margin_delta_yoy.
- Fidelity: beta compression — now captured (A1 A.3 done). F&P's long-side "safe, profitable, stable, high-payout" quality (Buffett's Alpha) — approximated weakly (fcf_margin > -5%, nde <= 3 admits loss-makers). Rank-within-market — captured. The paper's mechanism (leverage-constrained investors) — untestable, fine. The fallback proxy branch (any of 11 ORed inflection lenses) is not F&P at all.
- Lineage: ts_beta_1y_rk/3y_rk, ts_vol_1y/3y -> TS (weekly panel vs a per-market pseudo-index, A1 A.0 note); yf_beta -> YF snapshot (fallback); ebitda_margin_delta_yoy, fcf_margin, rev_yoy, nde -> M. Global. No currency issue (ratios).
- Methodology issues: (1) the vol-compression leg is absolute — after any market-wide volatility decline every name's 1y vol < 0.85 x 3y vol, so the leg is a regime flag, not idiosyncratic de-risking. (2) `_pb_rk` mixes 3y rank with 1y rank fillna across names in a rank comparison. (3) The Yahoo-fallback branch is near-universal (interval_inflect_any) — it belongs in `_watch`, not the core. (4) Pseudo-index skews beta levels (A1: median 1.15); ranks mitigate.
- FMP augmentations: `historical-price-eod` index series (^GSPC, ^N225, ^FTSE ... ~25 calls; H, G) for a true local index (A1 D#4); `profile.beta` (S, G) as a third fallback only.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] make the vol leg market-relative: (vol1y/vol3y) / (market's own vol1y/vol3y) <= 0.85. 2 [data] real index series for beta (A1 D#4 open). 3 [spirit] add the F&P/FKP quality side to the gate (op_margin > 0 and payout > 0, as `bab_quality` already has) — "becoming safe" from a loss is not the paper's long leg. 4 [method] demote the Yahoo-proxy branch to `bab_becoming_watch`.

### arch_bab_low_beta  (line 2431; reframed at 2497)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents. F&P (2014): sort on estimated beta (shrunk 0.6 toward 1), long the low-beta decile of quality assets. Comment: "long safe, profitable, low-beta assets (the leg the paper levers up)".
- As coded (reframed): [panel 3y beta rank (fillna 1y) <= 0.35 within market and raw panel beta > 0] OR [no panel rank and Yahoo raw beta >= 0.20 with shrunk <= 0.85]; AND bab_quality (is_operating, fcf_margin > 0, ebitda_margin >= 10%, op_margin > 0, nde <= 2.5, roce >= 10% or cash_conversion >= 0.60); AND liquid (ts_dvol26_usd >= $500k/week, else pew ADV >= $100k/day). Lenses: 3y beta rank (-), 3y down-capture (-), roce, fcf_margin.
- Fidelity: low-beta long leg — captured (bottom 35% within market; the paper's shrinkage is mirrored in the Yahoo fallback). Quality overlay — not in F&P (from Buffett's Alpha), reasonable. Liquidity — not in the paper but necessary against the non-trading artifact; now USD-aware (A1 fixed). Nothing central missing.
- Lineage: ts_beta_* -> TS; yf_beta -> YF (S); fcf_margin, ebitda_margin, op_margin, nde, roce, cash_conversion -> M; ts_dvol26_usd -> TS; avg_dollar_volume -> pew (~8% coverage, fallback only). Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) `_pb_rk = 3y rank fillna 1y rank` — a name judged on 1y sits in the same rank cut as 3y names. (2) `cash_conversion >= 0.60` reads falsely positive when CFO and NI are both negative (the engine's own gate-audit #3 note) — op_margin > 0 protects here but the OR is redundant with roce. (3) Beta is measured against a pseudo-index (A1) — ranks survive, levels do not; the lens `ts_beta_3y_rk` is fine, `ts_down_capture_3y` is level-based and inherits the skew.
- FMP augmentations: `historical-price-eod` indices (A1 D#4); `profile.beta` (S, G) cross-check; `dividends` (H, G) for the FKP "payout" quality dimension.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] FMP index series for beta and down-capture (A1 D#4 open). 2 [method] require both 1y and 3y ranks <= 0.35 when both exist, rather than fillna-mixing. 3 [spirit] add a payout leg (dividend or buyback > 0) — the paper's "safe" assets are the high-payout ones.

### arch_bab_multibagger  (line 2456; reframed at 2498)
- Source recipe: engine synthesis: F&P low beta + MR §II Yartseva screener ("small cap, B/M > 0.40, positive operating profitability, high FCF/P, asset growth > 0 but < EBITDA growth, price near 12-month low, negative 6-month return") + "very cheap".
- As coded (reframed): panel beta rank <= 0.50 within market (else Yahoo shrunk <= 1.0, raw >= 0.20); bab_quality; liquid; AND [yartseva_score >= 0.60 OR inflection_print OR rev_accel > 0.05] OR [cheapness_under_7x flag OR ev_ebit <= 8 OR fcf_yield >= 8% OR berezin >= 0.60]. Lenses: 3y beta rank (-), down-capture (-), yartseva_score, fcf_yield.
- Fidelity: low beta — approximated (<= 50th percentile is half the market, not "low"). Yartseva: profitability level — captured (bab_quality); FCF/P — only via the cheap leg; small cap — missing; near 12-month low / negative 6-month return — missing (and structurally in tension with low beta, which is fine, but nothing tests it); asset growth < EBITDA growth — missing. `inflection_print` (13 ORed lenses incl. rev_yoy >= 10%) makes the "multibagger" leg near-universal.
- Lineage: as bab_low_beta plus yartseva_score, berezin_score, cheapness_under_7x_flag -> M (derived composites); rev_accel -> M.
- Methodology issues: (1) the OR of two ORs (multibagger leg | cheap leg) with a 50th-percentile beta cut is the loosest BAB member; 160 firers today only because bab_quality and liquidity bind. (2) inflection_print inside a "multibagger" claim double-counts the same YoY prints that yartseva_score already scores. (3) The cheap leg has no EV sanity band (FX-corrupt EV -> ev_ebit <= 8 artifact; the engine guards this in midcap_garp, not here).
- FMP augmentations: `historical-market-capitalization` (H, G) for the size band; `key-metrics.freeCashFlowYieldTTM` / `ratios-ttm` (S, G) for FCF/P universe-wide; indices (A1 D#4).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] replace inflection_print with `yartseva_score >= 0.60 | margin_shock_any` and require beta rank <= 0.35 unless yartseva >= 0.60 (the synthesis should not be looser than either parent). 2 [spirit] add Yartseva's size (mcap < $2B) and FCF/P >= 5% as the exceptional tier. 3 [method] EV sanity band (EV/mcap 0.2-5) on the cheap leg.

### arch_capital_discipline  (line 1582)
- Source recipe: MR §I.E sub-archetype (10) "Capital Discipline / Governance Re-rating" (Korea Value-Up, Japan TSE PBR reform; three-layer test: priced for old economics, survived, better capital discipline); AC §D capital-cycle BUY signals: "management focused on returns/discipline not growth/share ... reduced IPO/equity issuance; insider buying; share-count reduction". Comment: "(G6) require a real capital-allocation ACTION ... insider ownership alone is not capital discipline".
- As coded: is_operating; _own_aligned = ACTION (shares_growth_3y <= -1% | buyback_yield >= 2% & shares_yoy <= 0 | financing outflow in >= 80% of >= 5 FYs) OR (insider >= 20% & (roic_after_sbc >= 10% | roce >= 12% | fcf_yield >= 4%)); returns floor (roce >= 10% | roic_after_sbc >= 10% | fcf_yield >= 5%); _roce_now_ok; _op_viable(0); nde <= 1.5; ebitda_margin 5-60%; price_yoy <= 3.0 (artifact guard); yartseva_score >= 0.45. Lenses: financing-outflow share, tc_uncov_payout_3y (-), shares_growth_5y (-), roce.
- Fidelity: "share-count reduction / reduced issuance" — captured (action leg). "Management focused on returns" — approximated by the returns floor. "Re-rating" half (MR: "stock priced for old-cycle economics"; catalyst = Value-Up / PBR reform / activist) — missing: no cheapness leg (yart >= 0.45 is a composite), no market/policy tag, no activist lens. Insider-only path: the comment calls it "not discipline" and A1 B.1 measured 2,198 of 3,293 firers on it — still open. AC's "insider buying" (Form 4) — missing.
- Lineage: shares_growth_3y/5y -> EDGAR + ST fill (global); buyback_yield -> EDGAR (US; check fill); fmp_st_financing_outflow_years -> ST (global); insider_ownership_pct -> YF (includes parent stakes: A1 owner_operator note, 59% of firers >= 50% held); roic_after_sbc -> EDGAR (US); roce, fcf_yield, nde -> M; tc_uncov_payout_3y -> TC.
- Methodology issues: (1) financing outflow >= 80% of FYs counts debt repayment as "discipline" — a deleveraging borrower passes with zero shareholder return. (2) `nde <= 1.5` on the 99-filled default excludes every name with missing net debt (37% per the note at 3189) — for a Japan/Korea thesis that is the wrong population to lose; `_clean_bs` exists for this. (3) The share-shrink action has no reverse-split guard (no_dilution has `_buyback_corrob`; this leg does not). (4) A controlled subsidiary (insider >= 60%) passes the insider path as "aligned".
- FMP augmentations: `splits` (H, G) reverse-split guard (A2 A.4 N); `shares-float` (S, G) to separate strategic stakes (controlled_sub_flag, A1 C#12); `insider-trading/statistics` (H, US) revealed alignment; `dividends` (H, G) payout persistence; `cash-flow-statement.commonStockRepurchased` (H, G) global buyback yield.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] core = ACTION leg, or insider path only with financing-outflow >= 80% (A1 B.1 open). 2 [method] split-guard the share-shrink action (A2 splits open) and use `_clean_bs(1.5)` instead of the 99-filled nde. 3 [spirit] add a "not yet re-rated" leg (pb < 1.2 or ev_ebit <= 10) per MR's three-layer test, and surface a Value-Up/PBR-reform market tag (.KS/.KQ/.T with pb < 1) as the catalyst lens. 4 [data] controlled_sub_flag from shares-float (A1 C#12 open).

### arch_capital_light_pivot  (line 2366; core at 2378)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents (nearest: AC §2 Den Fujita "capital-light"; MR §IV flyover "high ROIC"). Comment: "revenue growing AND assets growing slower AND ROIC turning up. The asset-light transition (franchise / IP / platform mode)."
- As coded: is_operating; _roce_now_ok; revenue_3y_cagr >= 8%; asset_3y_cagr < revenue_3y_cagr; n_yrs_roic_pos >= 3; core: roic_acceleration > 0 OR fqx_roic_ttm > roic_lindy (watch keeps the `roic_lindy > 0.10` escape). Lenses: roic_acceleration, fqx_roic_ttm - roic_lindy, revenue CAGR - asset CAGR.
- Fidelity: all three stated conditions captured; A1 A.3's escape closed in the core (done).
- Lineage: revenue_3y_cagr, asset_3y_cagr, n_yrs_positive_roic, roic_lindy, roic_acceleration -> EDGAR (US) + ST fills (global); fqx_roic_ttm -> FQX (global). Asset CAGR is nominal local currency.
- Methodology issues: (1) "assets growing slower than revenue" is a level test satisfied by any already-asset-light business (software at 5% asset growth on 20% revenue growth) — a PIVOT is a change in asset intensity, i.e. asset turnover rising, not a static gap. (2) Assets shrink on impairments and divestitures — a goodwill write-down passes the gap test; only _roce_now_ok guards. (3) Nominal asset growth in high-inflation currencies (TRY, ARS, EGP) biases the gap either way. (4) `fqx_roic_ttm > roic_lindy` compares an after-tax TTM ROIC with a median historic ROIC of possibly different definition (EDGAR NOPAT/IC vs FMP) — mixed bases.
- FMP augmentations: `key-metrics.capexToDepreciationTTM` (S, G) — a falling capex/D&A is the pivot tell (A2 nuanced #4); `owner-earnings` growthCapex/maintenanceCapex (H, G); `historical-employee-count` (H, US only) revenue per employee (A1 C#11); `financial-statement-symbol-list.reportingCurrency` (S, G) to deflate.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] define the pivot as asset turnover (revenue/assets) up >= 20% over 3y AND capex/D&A < 1.0, replacing the level comparison. 2 [method] exclude asset shrinkage from impairment/divestiture (fq_total_assets down with goodwill down) from the gap. 3 [data] employee-leverage flag as an exceptional lens (A1 C#11 open, US).

### arch_capital_returner  (line 1965; core at 1978)
- Source recipe: WO Archetype 8 filters "Dividends/buybacks material (>3-5% market cap annually)", evidence "Share count: static or falling (buybacks)"; table "buyback or dividend >= 5%"; MR §VIII "Capital Return / Tender: self-tenders, Dutch auctions, special dividends, NCIBs, ASRs". Comment: "Greenblatt-style direct evidence".
- As coded: is_operating; capital_return_yield 5-30% OR (dividend_yield + buyback_yield) 5-30%; watch: fcf_yield > 0 or NaN; core: tc_uncov_payout_3y == 0 (fallback capret <= fcf_yield & fcf_yield > 0); exceptional: evt_div_raise_streak >= 5 and no cut in 2y. Lenses: capret_yield_eff, tc_uncov_payout_3y (-), evt_div_raise_streak, financing-outflow share.
- Fidelity: 5% floor — captured. FCF-funded (WO: "Operating cash flow misreported via big receivable build (faked FCF)" reject) — captured in core (3-FY coverage), NaN-permissive in watch. WO's "falling interest expense / low growth capex" belong to oak_deleveraging. MR's tender/special-dividend EVENTS — missing (the > 30% cap explicitly rejects a large special return as an "artifact").
- Lineage: capital_return_yield, buyback_yield -> EDGAR (US, ~5% coverage; not in the FMP fill map); dividend_yield -> YF snapshot (stale price); tc_uncov_payout_3y -> TC (global); evt_div_raise_streak/cut -> EVT `dividends` (global); fmp_st_financing_* -> ST. Non-US names therefore fire almost only on dividend yield.
- Methodology issues: (1) dividend_yield is quote-time and can mix GBp/GBP or ZAc (A2 §C same-currency yield — open). (2) `measured` = TC present or (fcf & capret present): a non-US name with only a Yahoo dividend yield is "unmeasured" and keeps the NaN-permissive watch rule as its core. (3) The 30% cap drops genuine tenders/special dividends instead of routing them to an event flag. (4) buyback_yield absent globally means "static or falling share count" is unseen ex-US although fq_shares_yoy exists.
- FMP augmentations: `cash-flow-statement.commonStockRepurchased` + `historical-market-capitalization` (H, G) -> global buyback yield (A2 §4 [Q]); `dividends` (H, G) same-currency yield and cuts (shipped as evt_*); `sec-filings-search` SC TO-I (H, US) tender events.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] global buyback yield from FMP repurchases / FY-end mcap so the "5%" reads dividends + buybacks everywhere (A2 §4 open). 2 [method] make FCF coverage a hard leg in the watch rule too, or at least require `fq_shares_yoy <= 0` when fcf is NaN. 3 [spirit] route > 30% yields to a surfaced `special_return_event_flag` (tender / special dividend) instead of dropping them.

### arch_cash_adjusted_pe  (line 3873)
- Source recipe: comment block "F4 — Cash-adjusted P/E, negative-or-cheap (user spec)": "(mcap - net cash)/NI ... negative is only cheap when earnings are positive ... 0 < adj P/E <= 8 is the cheap band. All components LOCAL currency." Not in the five documents (nearest MR §III "net-nets / net cash"; LC "net cash ... carry disproportionate weight").
- As coded: is_operating; mcap > 0; _fx_coherent; NI > 0; NI/mcap >= 2%; net cash (cash - total_debt) > 0; (mcap - netcash)/NI <= 8 OR (mcap - netcash)/ni_avg <= 8; shares_yoy <= 5%; CFO >= 0.3 x NI where CFO known; _not_melting. No lenses (not spirited).
- Fidelity: the user spec is captured faithfully, including the negative case (net cash > mcap passes `<= 8`) and the local-currency discipline (`_fx_coherent`).
- Lineage: net_income_ttm, cash, total_debt, market_cap (local) -> M; ni_avg -> EDGAR + ST fill (converted, global); cfo_ttm, shares_yoy -> M. Currency-coherent by construction. Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) `net cash = cash - total_debt` ignores minority interest and preferred — for a holdco with large NCI the equity-only NI is set against a mcap that excludes claims on that cash. (2) The Graham-average leg is a pure OR: a one-off gain in latest NI still passes via the latest leg (no "latest NI <= 2x ni_avg" sanity). (3) `cash` may include restricted cash (documented) and, for some FMP-filled rows, short-term investments are excluded (conservative). (4) CFO >= 0.3 x NI is a weak accrual guard (income quality 0.3).
- FMP augmentations: `key-metrics.incomeQualityTTM` (S, G) accrual veto at ~0.7 (A2 nuanced #1); `balance-sheet-statement.minorityInterest`, `preferredStock`, `shortTermInvestments` (H, G).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] deduct minority interest and preferred from the net-cash adjustment (or exclude NCI > 10% of equity). 2 [method] when latest NI > 2 x ni_avg require the average leg too (one-off gain guard). 3 [data] incomeQuality >= 0.7 as the accrual veto (A2 #1 open).

### arch_cash_quality  (line 2327)
- Source recipe: comment block "cash-ROIC running materially ahead of NOPAT-ROIC over the lindy window. 'Earnings hide the cash' — quality-of-earnings tell that Mauboussin emphasises." Not in the five documents.
- As coded: is_operating; _roce_now_ok; cash_roic_lindy > 8%; roic_lindy > 0; cash - NOPAT ROIC gap >= 5pp; shares_growth_3y <= 5%; roic_after_sbc not < 0; n_yrs_fcf_pos >= 4. Lenses: the gap, ts_maxdd_5y, fq_cash_leads_earnings_flag, fq_cfo_to_ni.
- Fidelity: the stated rule is captured. The Mauboussin framing (cash earnings above accounting earnings because of conservative accounting / float) is one of two explanations for the gap; the other — a harvester under-investing (capex < D&A) — is not distinguished, and the SBC add-back case is guarded only where roic_after_sbc exists (US).
- Lineage: cash_roic_lindy, roic_lindy, n_yrs_positive_fcf, shares_growth_3y -> EDGAR + ST fills (global); roic_after_sbc -> EDGAR (US only, soft); fq_* -> FQ (global); ts_maxdd_5y -> TS.
- Methodology issues: (1) A positive gap on a lindy MEDIAN can be one or two big working-capital years; the gap is not required to persist year by year. (2) Harvest vs quality ambiguity (capex/D&A < 0.7 with falling revenue produces the same gap). (3) NOPAT-ROIC from EDGAR and cash-ROIC from FMP fills can come from different definitions for the same name (ST fill of one but not the other) — the difference then measures definitions, not quality; the fill map fills both, but fills are per-column.
- FMP augmentations: `key-metrics.capexToDepreciationTTM` (S, G) (A2 #4); `owner-earnings.maintenanceCapex` (H, G); `key-metrics.stockBasedCompensationToRevenueTTM` (S, G) (A2 #11) to apply the SBC veto globally.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] pair the gap with capex/D&A >= 0.8 OR revenue_3y_cagr >= 0 so a melting harvester does not read as quality. 2 [method] require the gap positive in >= 4 of the 5 lindy years (TC), not on medians. 3 [data] SBC/revenue universe-wide for the SBC-add-back veto (A2 #11 open). 4 [method] require both ROICs from the same provenance (both EDGAR or both ST) before differencing.

### arch_cash_reinvest  (line 1786)
- Source recipe: comment "Cash-confirmed reinvestment: cash ROIIC lindy > 12%"; AC §1 Meyer/Phelps tests (2) "high sustained ROE/ROIC (>15%)" and (3) "a long reinvestment runway". Not otherwise in the documents.

- Fidelity: ROIC level and reinvestment (asset growth) captured; Mayer's other tests (small cap, owner-operator, low starting valuation, moat) are deliberately elsewhere. "Runway" is approximated by past asset growth, which is backward-looking.
- Lineage: cash_roic_lindy, cash_roiic_lindy, n_yrs_positive_roic, asset_3y_cagr -> EDGAR + ST fills (global). Asset CAGR nominal local currency.
- Methodology issues: (1) The asset-growth floor biases to asset-heavy reinvestment; the sibling durable_reinvestment relaxed it to `(asset_3y_cagr > 5%) | (roiic >= 20%)` for exactly this reason (asset-light IP/software compounders) — cash_reinvest did not follow. (2) Rolling ROIIC on small invested-capital deltas is noisy (A1 A.3 cheap_per_roiic note) — a 12% cash ROIIC from a 1% capital delta is a base effect; no minimum delta. (3) Nominal asset growth in inflationary currencies.
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth.fiveYOperatingCFGrowthPerShare` / `threeY...` (H, G) — per-share OCF compounding as the cash-reinvestment proof (A2 A.1, endpoint matrix EXC); `financial-growth.weightedAverageSharesDilutedGrowth` (H, G).
- Suggestions: 1 [method] match durable_reinvestment's OR (asset growth OR cash ROIIC >= 20%). 2 [method] require the invested-capital delta >= 10% of base before trusting the ROIIC. 3 [data] add per-share OCF 5y growth >= +61% (10%/yr) as the exceptional tier (A2 A.1 open).

### arch_cluseau_buyback_accel  (line 3627)
