Not in the five documents.
- As coded: is_operating; mcap >= $20M; p_tb 0-1.0; buyback_yield >= 3%; accel = (shares_yoy < 0 & shares_yoy - shares_3y_cagr <= -1pp) OR (FY buyback yields y0 >= 3% > y1 >= y2); shares_yoy not < -30%; cash/mcap <= 15%; profitable (NI > 0 | ni_avg > 0 | fcf_yield > 0); _not_melting. Altman flag surfaced (shipped).
- Fidelity: acceleration — captured (two lenses). Cash deployed — approximated by a cash/MCAP level, while the article's measure is cash as a share of the BALANCE SHEET trending down. "Buying hardest into weakness" (counter-cyclical timing) — missing. Sub-book — captured (P/TB < 1).

(2) A -29% share drop passes as a "buyback"; no split verification (A2 splits). (3) `cash_pct_mcap <= 0.15` punishes a cheap stock (low mcap inflates the ratio) — the wrong denominator for "cash deployed". (4) `_cl_profitable` accepts fcf_yield > 0 alone — one good working-capital year.
- FMP augmentations: `cash-flow-statement.commonStockRepurchased` (H, G) + `historical-market-capitalization` (H, G) for FY repurchases / FY-average mcap and the "into weakness" test (repurchases largest in the lowest-price FY); `splits` (H, G); `balance-sheet-statement.cashAndCashEquivalents` / totalAssets 3-FY trend (H, G) for the 20% -> 3% path.
- Suggestions: 1 [data] let `fmp_st_buyback_yield_y0 >= 0.03` satisfy the pace gate so non-US names can fire (A2 §4 open). 2 [spirit] add the timing leg: FY repurchase $ highest in the FY with the lowest average price (cash-flow + market-cap history). 3 [method] cash deployment as cash/total assets falling >= 10pp over 3 FYs (FQ/ST), not cash/mcap. 4 [method] split guard on the share-count drop (A2 open).

### arch_cundill_deep_value  (line 3166)
- Source recipe: comment block: Peter Cundill's six-point checklist (There's Always Something to Do): (1) price < book, (2) price < half the former high or near an all-time low, (3) P/E < 10 or earnings yield above the long bond, (4) profitable, preferably rising earnings and no deficits over 5 years, (5) paying dividends, (6) debt judiciously employed with room to expand. Not in the five documents.
- As coded: c1 pb 0-1; c2 [52w drawdown <= -50% | 5y range <= 0.20 | ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.5 | ts dd52 <= -50%]; c3 p_e 0-min(10, 1/5.5%) = 10; c4 (roce > 0 | ebitda > 0) & (eps_yoy_positive_share >= 0.6 or NaN); c5 dividend_yield > 0; c6 nde < 2.5 | D/E < 1 | net cash > 0 | is_financial. Score: discount to book (2/3 NAV), net-net, near low, cheapness, no deficits, rising EPS, dividend, balance-sheet strength.
- Fidelity: (1) captured. (2) "half the former high" — captured exactly by hi260 but ORed with weaker lenses (a 52w -50% drawdown from a recent spike; 5y-range <= 0.20 is not "half"); A1 A.4 asked for hi260 as the definition — partly done (added, not made primary). (3) captured with a constant rate [R]. (4) profitable — captured; "no deficits over 5y" — approximated by a US-only EPS share that is NaN-permissive, so non-US names pass on roce > 0 alone; "rising earnings" — score only (as intended). (5) captured on a snapshot yield. (6) captured; financials exempt (Cundill bought banks) — faithful.
- Lineage: pb, p_e, dividend_yield, net_debt_ebitda, debt_to_equity, net_cash_pct_mcap, ncav -> M (YF/FMP snapshots); eps_yoy_positive_share -> EDGAR streaks (US); pct_off_52w_high, price_pct_of_5y_range -> YF (stale, A1 §0.3); ts_dist_hi260 -> TS (global). graham_net_net_flag -> M.
- Methodology issues: (1) c2's OR admits a name at 45% of its 52w high but 90% of its 5-year high (a spike-and-retrace), which is not Cundill's former high. (2) c4's NaN-permissive EPS share means the 5-year-no-deficit test is silently absent for ~29k non-US names although `fmp_st_ni_up_share_5` / TC positive-NI counts exist. (3) p_e from the Yahoo snapshot can be on adjusted EPS; no earnings-yield floor guards a tiny-NI P/E. (4) The 5.5% bond rate is a constant; in a 12% regime (Cundill's 1970s) the cap would be 8.3.
- FMP augmentations: `dividends` (H, G) "paid in each of the last 2 FYs" (A2 A.4 "cundill #5" N); `income-statement` 5 FYs (ST, cached) for no-deficit years; `treasury-rates.year10` (H) for the bond-rate parameter; `historical-price-eod` (done as TS).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] c2 = ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.5 primary, with the 52w/5y-range lenses only when hi260 is NaN (A1 A.4 open). 2 [data] c4's no-deficit test from `fmp_st` / TC NI-positive years (>= 4 of 5) globally instead of the NaN-permissive EPS share. 3 [data] c5 from dividend history (A2 open) rather than a quote-time yield. 4 [method] c3 rate from treasury-rates + a fixed IG spread.

### arch_greenblatt_magic  (line 8118)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents as a recipe (AC §II.A cites Greenblatt only for "securities that fall between the cracks"; MR §III lists him under special situations). Published Magic Formula (The Little Book): rank the universe on earnings yield = EBIT / EV and on return on capital = EBIT / (net working capital + net fixed assets), buy the best COMBINED rank; mcap > $50M; exclude financials and utilities; hold one year. Comment block: "cheap AND good, the two legs Greenblatt ranks the universe on ... roce as the return-on-capital proxy (roic_after_sbc is too sparse)".
- As coded: is_operating; _not_melting; mcap > 0; EBIT/EV = 1/ev_ebit >= 10%; (roce >= 25% OR fqx_roic_ttm in [20%, 100%]); not _roce_oneoff_suspect (roce > 1.0 without a fat margin or a lindy record). No lenses (not spirited).
- Fidelity: EBIT/EV — captured. Return on capital — approximated: provider `roce` is EBIT / (total assets - current liabilities), which INCLUDES goodwill and cash; Greenblatt's tangible capital excludes both ("net working capital + net fixed assets"), so an acquisitive name with a big goodwill base reads LOW and a cash-rich name reads LOW, the opposite bias to the paper's. The combined RANK — missing: two absolute cuts ("top ~quartile", "top ~decile-ish" in the comment) replace a rank, so membership swings with the rate regime and a name can be 1st on one leg and 60th percentile on the other. Financials/utilities exclusion — captured. mcap floor — missing (mcap > 0 only). One-year hold / rebalance — out of scope.
- Lineage: ev_ebit, roce -> M (YF/FMP single-period provider ratios; ev_ebit is local EV / local EBIT, currency-neutral); fqx_roic_ttm -> FQX (global, after-tax EBIT x 0.75 / (equity + debt - cash)). 763 firers (A1). Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) the two ROC lenses are different statistics under one cut: pre-tax provider ROCE vs after-tax panel ROIC at 20% "~25% pre-tax" assumes a 20% tax rate for every market (fallback changes meaning). (2) The 100% upper cap on fqx_roic_ttm drops genuinely capital-light franchises (royalties, software) the formula is meant to surface, while the roce path keeps them if op margin >= 20% — asymmetric. (3) ev_ebit has no EV sanity band (the FX-corrupt-EV guard lives in midcap_garp, not here): an ADR/cross-line with EV ~0 reads as EBIT/EV -> infinity. (4) No mcap or liquidity floor: Greenblatt's $50M floor is what keeps the formula out of shells.
- FMP augmentations: `key-metrics.returnOnCapitalEmployedTTM` and `enterprise-values` (S/H, G) — but the better move is `balance-sheet-statement` (H, G) to compute Greenblatt's own denominator (receivables + inventory - payables + net PP&E) universe-wide; `key-metrics-ttm-bulk` (S, G) for a universe-wide percentile rank in one stream.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] compute ROC on tangible capital (NWC + net PP&E) from fq balance sheets and rank both legs within the investable universe (combined rank <= top decile = core, top quartile = watch) instead of absolute cuts. 2 [method] EV sanity band (EV/mcap 0.2-5) on the EBIT/EV leg, and mcap >= $50M. 3 [method] put both ROC lenses on the same basis (pre-tax EBIT / capital) before applying one threshold. 4 [data] A2 §C "roce single period" -> fqx_roic_ttm + tc_med_opm corroboration (A2 §C open).

### arch_kullamagie_breakout  (line 3037; core at 3050)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents. Kristjan Kullamägi's published setup (qullamaggie.com): the strongest stocks by 1/3/6-month performance ("top 1-2%"), a prior move of 30-100%+, an orderly 2-8-week consolidation with a tightening range and volume drying up, above rising 10/20-day MAs, entry on the opening-range high with the stop at the low of day; the episodic pivot (EP) variant is an earnings gap of 10%+ on huge volume; he trades liquid names only. Comment: "[C] leader across momentum scans; [C] large prior advance (>=30%); [P] orderly tightening near rising highs".
- As coded (watch): live tape; leader = GLOBAL percentile >= 95 on roc_6m or momentum_12m; impulse = max(roc_6m, mom12) >= 30%; tight = monthly squeeze run >= 3 OR 12-month base depth <= 35%; near = 0.5%-25% off the 52w high. Core (_tier, where panel RS present): ts_rs_pct_mkt >= 90 (within market) & ts_dist_hi52 >= 0.75 & above 30-week MA & ts_dvol26_usd >= $1M/week. Exceptional: RS >= 95 & 5-week range <= 10% & >= $5M/week. Lenses: ts_rs_raw, ts_tight5 (-), ts_dist_hi52, ts_dvol26_usd.
- Fidelity: leadership — approximated (6m/12m only; his 1-month and 3-month scans are absent; the core rank is within market, A1 A.1 done). Prior impulse >= 30% — captured. Consolidation 2-8 weeks — missing (no duration test; a 12-month base 35% deep is a different object from a 3-week flag). Tightening range — core does not require it (tight5 <= 0.10 is exceptional only); the watch's "base_depth <= 35%" admits a pullback nearly as deep as his stop-out. Volume dry-up — missing (ts_vol_spike exists for the opposite condition; no VDU lens). Above rising 10/20-day MAs — approximated by the 30-week MA (a slower trend test). EP variant — missing (A2 §C.3 arch_earnings_episodic_pivot, needs `earnings` dates). ORH entry / stop — out of scope, documented.
- Lineage: roc_6m, momentum_12m, pct_off_52w_high, base_depth_12m -> YF/lynch-tape snapshot (stale; A1 §0.3 momentum noise 27-29%); sr_m_squeeze_run -> lynch tape (monthly); ts_* -> TS (weekly panel, global, USD dvol quote-unit aware). Watch legs are snapshot-only.
- Methodology issues: (1) the watch keeps the GLOBAL percentile, so the watch population over-weights RG/BK/BO/NS 2-7x (A1 A.1); the core fixes it only where ts_rs_pct_mkt exists. (2) 18% of watch firers traded under $1M/week (A1); the $1M core floor is far below the liquidity he requires (he sizes into names doing many $M/day) — the exceptional's $5M is closer. (3) Clinical biotech leak: 92 firers (A2 §0, Z2 open) in a binary-event mechanism. (4) ts_dist_hi52 >= 0.75 in the core means up to 25% off the high still counts as "near highs" for a breakout setup.
- FMP augmentations: `earnings` universe-wide (H, G) for the EP leg and dated impulses (A1 D#5 open); `historical-price-eod` daily (H, G) for a 10/20-day MA and a true 2-8-week consolidation window (weekly bars cannot see a 3-week flag); `historical-price-eod` non-split-adjusted for quote-unit sanity.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] make tightness core, not exceptional: ts_tight5 <= 0.10 (or a weekly-range contraction over the last 3-6 bars) plus a duration band 2-10 weeks since the impulse high. 2 [spirit] add the volume dry-up lens (last-4-week volume / 26-week median <= 0.7, from the panel) — the half of his "orderly" the engine does not read. 3 [method] core near-high = ts_dist_hi52 >= 0.90 (25% off is a base, not a breakout). 4 [data] EP variant from `earnings` dates + panel (A2 §C.3 open); exclude is_clinical_biotech into a biotech_momentum_watch (A2 Z2 open).

### arch_liger_asset_backed  (line 3358)
- Source recipe: LC "Asset-backed optionality": "the thesis begins with a valuation floor — cash, saleable property, investments or other hard assets — and then adds a separately researched growth option ... works best when: net cash or monetisable assets cover a substantial portion of market value; the operating business is near break-even; management has demonstrated willingness to sell or redeploy assets; the optionality has independent technical or commercial validation; dilution risk is limited." Also: "liquidation value should not be treated as equivalent to cash ... deducts all claims senior to equity". Neglect is the document's global frame (exec summary: "neglected microcaps").
- As coded: is_operating; mcap 0-$400M; net_cash_pct_mcap >= 20% and nde not known >= 1; pb 0-3; coverage <= 4 (missing = 0 = neglected, via sent -> Yahoo -> pew); op_margin >= -5% or ebitda_margin >= 0; SBC/revenue < 10% where present; not biotech/mining/crypto. No lenses.
- Fidelity: "net cash covers a substantial portion" — approximated: 20% of mcap is a cushion, not a floor (the author's other examples: MAFL/CVV with cash + property near the whole cap). "Monetisable assets" beyond cash — missing: pb < 3 is a sanity band (a name at 2.9x book is not asset-backed); no tangible-book or NCAV leg. Near break-even — captured. Dilution risk limited — approximated by SBC only; no share-count leg although the exec summary's first veto is "serial diluters". Management willingness to sell/redeploy — missing (asset-sale / disc-ops evidence). Optionality validation — unscreenable, correctly absent. Neglect — proxy (A1 A.2 / A2 §B.10 "observed coverage" open).
- Lineage: net_cash_pct_mcap, pb -> M (YF/FMP snapshot, mcap-relative so currency cancels; pence/GBP mixes are the known exception); net_debt_ebitda -> M; sent_n_analysts -> SENT (partial run), n_analysts -> YF, n_analysts_pew -> pew; sbc_pct_revenue -> EDGAR + fq/ST fills (global where disclosed). 2,211 firers (A1 watch band). Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) `_liger_cov` fills missing with 0, so an unrated name is "most neglected" — 84% of liger firers have no coverage field (A1); the thesis needs neglect OBSERVED. (2) pb < 3 with net cash >= 20% admits a growth stock with a modest cash pile; "asset-backed" needs the book or NCAV to carry the mcap. (3) `_netcash_not_contradicted` only vetoes nde >= 1.0; a name with nde 0.8 and "net cash 20%" is internally inconsistent and still passes. (4) No liquidity validity (LC's last scoring dimension: "can a position be entered and exited responsibly?") and no reverse-split distress flag (A2 nuanced #10).
- FMP augmentations: `analyst-estimates.numAnalystsEps` (S, G, cached) for observed neglect (A1 D#2 open); `balance-sheet-statement` shortTermInvestments / longTermInvestments (H, G) for the "investments" part of the floor (A2 §1 [Q]); `key-metrics.tangibleAssetValueTTM` (S, G) for a tangible floor globally; `splits` (H, G) reverse-split veto; `etf/asset-exposure` passive share < 2% as a second neglect lens (A2 §B.10).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] floor leg = (net cash + investments) >= 50% of mcap OR NCAV >= 80% OR p_tb <= 1.0, with the current 20% / pb<3 rule kept as watch. 2 [spirit] dilution leg: fq_shares_yoy <= 3% (LC: "dilution risk is limited"; SBC alone misses placings). 3 [method] neglect = observed count <= 4 where any coverage field exists; missing -> watch only (A1 A.2 open). 4 [data] asset-sale evidence: fq_disc_ops_share > 0 or an 8-K 2.01 (US) as an exceptional lens for "management willing to redeploy".

### arch_liger_lagging_inflect  (line 3373)
- Source recipe: LC "Earnings, backlog and conservative guidance": "the ordinary financial inflection that the market has failed to process ... The most attractive setup normally has: reported rather than purely projected growth; backlog or recurring usage supporting future revenue; clean cash conversion; incremental margins above historical averages; net cash or manageable debt; low expectations following a pullback; a credible near-term confirmation point." Comment: "His signal is ACCELERATION (RCMT consolidated +14.7% but the segment far faster)".
- As coded: is_operating; _roce_now_ok; rev_yoy in (0, 100%]; growth = (rev >= 10% & rev_accel > 0) OR rev >= 15% OR oper_lev_any; cash = (cash_conversion >= 0.8 & ebitda > 0) OR fcf_margin > 0; _clean_bs(1.5); coverage <= 4 (missing = neglected); SBC < 10% soft; sector ok; lag = flat_or_down OR beaten_down_any(0.30) OR bs_is_base OR 1-year coil (TTM sales growth >= 15% over ts_r52) OR 2-year coil >= log 1.20. No lenses.
- Fidelity: reported growth — captured. Clean cash conversion — captured (with the both-negative guard). Net cash / manageable debt — captured. Low expectations after a pullback — approximated: five ORed lag lenses, of which flat_or_down (any non-positive 12-month print, half the universe) and a 30% drawdown are the usual entry; the measured coil (A1 A.2) was ADDED as a sixth OR rather than replacing them, so A1's "lag measured keeps 1,418 of 2,706" tightening is still open. "Incremental margins above historical averages" — approximated weakly: `oper_lev_any` (any of 13 lenses incl. a raw sequential EBITDA uptick) and only as one alternative — a name at rev >= 15% passes with NO margin leg at all; author's words "incremental margins above historical averages" vs code `((rev_yoy_c >= 0.15) | oper_lev_any)`. Backlog / recurring usage — missing (fq_defrev_build_flag exists and is wired into oak_order_conversion, not here; RPO is EDGAR-only). Near-term confirmation point — missing (earnings-calendar). Acceleration (comment) — captured only on the 10-15% branch.
- Lineage: rev_yoy, rev_accel, cash_conversion, fcf_margin -> M (YF/FMP TTM); price_yoy / momentum_12m (flat_or_down) -> YF snapshot (A1 §0.3: 55% differ by > 15pp from ts_r52 — this leg should read ts_r52 first, A1 §0.3 open); bs_* -> base_snapshot (partial, 58% -> 82% after re-run, A1 §0.2 open); fq_rev_growth -> FQ; ts_r52 -> TS; coverage as liger_asset_backed. 2,706 firers (A1). Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) flat_or_down on the stale snapshot is the loosest leg and is not measured against the advance (A1 B.1). (2) cash_conversion = CFO/EBITDA on a one-period basis; "clean" conversion is a multi-year property (tc / fq_cfo_to_ni history exist). (3) `oper_lev_any` includes raw sequential moves that the engine's own seasonality rule says should not fire alone. (4) The 100% rev cap to drop M&A pops is a blunt organic test; goodwill growth vs revenue growth (fq_gw_pct_assets delta) is the direct one.
- FMP augmentations: `earnings-calendar` (H, G) for the confirmation point (A2 §A.2 N); `analyst-estimates.numAnalystsEps` observed neglect (A1 D#2 open); fq deferred-revenue build already computed (wire it); `earning-call-transcript` backlog / book-to-bill for non-US filers (A2 §A.2 N).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] the margin leg explicit and separate from growth: fqx_inc_ebit_margin > op_margin_lindy (incremental above the historical average — the author's exact phrase) or margin_shock_any; growth AND margin, not growth OR margin. 2 [spirit] lag = the measured coil (1-year or 2-year) as core; flat_or_down / beaten-down demoted to watch (A1 A.2 open). 3 [spirit] add fq_defrev_build_flag as the backlog lens (exceptional tier), and earnings-calendar <= 60 days as the confirmation-point score term. 4 [method] flat_or_down reads ts_r52 first (A1 §0.3 open); neglect observed (A1 D#2 open).

### arch_liger_neglected_survivor  (line 3399; core at 3413)
- Source recipe: LC "Initial triage": "Financial survivability. The company appears capable of reaching the catalyst without emergency capital. Narrative tension. Market expectations are low, stale or inconsistent with the new evidence."; LC scoring model: balance-sheet resilience 10 ("Can the company reach the catalyst without damaging financing?"), valuation asymmetry 5, liquidity and implementation 5 ("Can a position be entered and exited responsibly?"); vetoes: "toxic financing, going-concern risk". Aversion section: "Net cash, positive or near-positive operating cash flow ... carry disproportionate weight." Comment: "neglected + financially survivable (no dilution) + cheap, with an early inflection".
- As coded: is_operating; mcap $5M-$400M; n_analysts <= 3 (Yahoo -> pew, missing = 0); (net cash >= 15% & not contradicted) OR (ebitda > 0 & nde <= 1.5); fcf_margin >= 0 OR op_margin >= -2%; SBC < 10% soft; beaten_down_any(0.30) OR ev_sales <= 2; rev_accel > 0 OR oper_lev_any; sector ok. Core (_tier): first-positive EBITDA/NI OR fqx_eps_turned OR fqx_inc_ebit_margin >= 20%. Exceptional: sent_n_analysts <= 1 & TTM EBIT growth >= 30%. Lenses: fqx_ebit_ttm_g, fqx_inc_ebit_margin, sent_n_analysts (-), ev_sales (-).
- Fidelity: survivability — approximated by a point test (fcf_margin >= 0 or op >= -2%) plus net cash / low nde; the author's test is RUNWAY TO THE CATALYST (cash vs burn over the horizon) — a name at op -2% with 15% net cash has ~7 years of runway, one at op -2% on nde 1.4 has none of the cash the leg implies. No-dilution (comment) — missing as a share-count leg (SBC only). Neglect — proxy (missing = neglected; A1 A.2, A2 §B.10 open); note this rule reads `n_analysts_v` (Yahoo/pew) while its two siblings read `_liger_cov` (sent first) — the same thesis on two different coverage fields. Cheap / low expectations — captured (ev_sales <= 2 or 30% drawdown). Early inflection — now real in the core (A1 A.2 shock/first-positive done). Liquidity — missing. Going-concern veto — missing (fmp_distress_flag is shipped for cluseau_*, not here).
- Lineage: n_analysts -> YF, n_analysts_pew -> pew (both snapshot); net_cash_pct_mcap, nde, fcf_margin, op_margin, ev_sales -> M; ebitda_first_positive / net_income_first_positive -> M annual flags; fqx_* -> FQX (global); sent_n_analysts -> SENT (partial). 3,849 firers (A1). Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) The $5M floor with no dollar-volume validity admits untradeable shells the author explicitly scores down. (2) `oper_lev_any` in the watch and `fqx_inc_ebit_margin >= 0.20` in the core are both single-period; a first-positive NI on a one-off gain passes. (3) ev_sales <= 2 as "cheap" is sector-blind (distribution at 0.3x sales is normal). (4) A reverse split in the last year (delisting-avoidance distress) is exactly the "toxic financing" veto and is unscreened (A2 nuanced #10).
- FMP augmentations: `analyst-estimates.numAnalystsEps` (S, G) observed neglect; `funds/disclosure-holders-latest` N-PORT and `etf/asset-exposure` (A2 §B.10) as second neglect lenses; `splits` (H, G) reverse-split veto; `scores-bulk` Altman (S, G) going-concern veto (shipped elsewhere); ts_dvol26_usd already exists for the liquidity band $50k-$2M/week (A2 §B.10).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] runway leg: where fcf < 0, (cash + STI) / |TTM FCF| >= 24 months from fq (same reporting currency), replacing the point margin test for burners. 2 [spirit] no-dilution leg: fq_shares_yoy <= 3% and no reverse split. 3 [method] read `_liger_cov` (sent -> Yahoo -> pew) like the siblings, and treat missing as watch-only (A1 A.2 open). 4 [data] validity band ts_dvol26_usd $50k-$2M/week and the Altman veto (A2 §B.10 open).

### arch_lindy_fcf  (line 1882; core at 1900)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents as a rule; nearest AC §4 Tillinghast "Four Elements of Value: Profitability (ROE > 10-12% sustained ~10 years); life span; growth; certainty (how predictable the cash flows are)" and MR §IV flyover "high ROIC (> 15%) + strong FCF". Comment: "Consistent FCF: positive FCF in 4 of last 5 years AND positive operating income in 4 of last 5. Cash-generation durability test."
- As coded (watch): is_operating; _roce_now_ok; _not_melting; roic_after_sbc not known-negative; years_of_history >= 5; n_yrs_positive_fcf >= 4; n_yrs_positive_opinc >= 4. Core (_tier): every observed through-cycle year FCF-positive with >= 3 years (tc_fcf_pos == tc_fcf_years >= 3), else fmp_st n_yrs_positive_fcf >= min(5, years) with >= 3 years. Exceptional: tc_fcf_years >= 8 & tc_fcf_margin_avg >= 15%. Lenses: FCF-positive share, tc_fcf_years, tc_fcf_margin_avg, cash_roic_lindy.
- Fidelity: durability of cash generation — captured (every-year in the core). Tillinghast's PROFITABILITY LEVEL (ROE > 10-12% sustained) — missing: a name with FCF of +0.1% of sales every year is "lindy" (A1 §8 / A2 §B.11: roic_lindy >= 8% or tc_fcf_margin_avg >= 10% in the core — open; the margin sits only in the exceptional tier at 15%). "Certainty / predictability" — missing: the through-cycle MINIMUM FCF margin or its dispersion is the direct measure and tc has the years. Life span — approximated by years >= 5 in the watch, >= 3 in the core.
- Lineage: n_yrs_positive_fcf / opinc, years_of_history -> EDGAR + ST fills (fmp_st_*, global; fmp_filled_* are provenance flags); tc_* -> TC (global, cached 8 FYs); roic_after_sbc -> EDGAR (US, soft). Currency-neutral (counts and margins). 9,356 firers (A1: 38% of INV = the base rate of profitable firms).
- Methodology issues: (1) The core's "all observed years" with tc_fcf_years >= 3 is weaker in span than the watch's 5 years; the AND with the watch rescues it only because years_of_history is separately >= 5 — two different history clocks (tc vs EDGAR/ST) measure "years". (2) The fallback (fmp_st n_yrs >= min(5, years)) is a different statistic (5-year count) under the same "every year" label — fine in meaning here, but a 3-year-old filer qualifies on 3/3. (3) FCF sign ignores SBC add-backs outside the US (roic_after_sbc is US-only; fq_sbc_pct_revenue is global and unused here). (4) FCF positive in every year is trivially true for asset-light melting names with shrinking capex — no revenue-not-declining leg.
- FMP augmentations: `cash-flow-statement` 8 FYs are already cached (tc_*): emit tc_min_fcf_margin (0 calls); `key-metrics.stockBasedCompensationToRevenueTTM` (S, G) for the SBC veto globally (A2 nuanced #11); `financial-growth.fiveYOperatingCFGrowthPerShare` (H, G) for the per-share growth side (A2 §B.14).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] core adds a level: tc_fcf_margin_avg >= 10% or roic_lindy >= 8% (A1 §8 / A2 §B.11 open). 2 [spirit] certainty = tc_min_fcf_margin >= 0 over >= 7 years (worst year still positive at a margin) as the exceptional, with 7-of-8 as core once emitted (A1 §8 "7-of-8" open). 3 [method] apply the SBC veto through fq_sbc_pct_revenue >= 10% where roic_after_sbc is absent. 4 [method] revenue_3y_cagr >= 0 so a harvesting decliner is not "durable".

### arch_lindy_growth  (line 2162; spirited)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents as a rule; nearest AC §1 Mayer test (7) "the capacity to compound EPS ~20%+ for a decade" and (3) "a long reinvestment runway"; MR §II Yartseva "it is the LEVEL of profitability and cash flow, not the GROWTH RATE"; MR §VI Deden "acquisition-dependent growth is an accident waiting to happen". Comment: "revenue 5y CAGR >= 8% AND topline accelerating (3y CAGR > 5y CAGR) AND asset base growing. Multi-cycle expansion without the single-year base-effect noise."
- As coded: is_operating; _roce_now_ok; revenue_ttm_usd >= $20M; _profit_present; (fcf_yield > 0 | n_yrs_fcf_pos >= 3 | roic_lindy >= 5%); revenue_5y_cagr >= 8%; revenue_acceleration_lindy > 0; asset_5y_cagr > 3%; years_of_history >= 5. Lenses: fq_rev_growth - 0.5 x 5y CAGR, ts_maxdd_5y, 5y CAGR, acceleration.
- Fidelity: durable growth — captured on REVENUE; Mayer's test is per-share EPS compounding — approximated (no per-share, no EPS). Profitability level (Yartseva) — captured (A1 done). Reinvestment runway — approximated by asset growth, which includes acquired goodwill, so acquisition-driven growth (Deden's warning) passes as "durable". Acceleration — captured (3y > 5y). "Still growing now" — spirit lens only (A1 A.3 open: TTM >= half the 5y CAGR as core).
- Lineage: revenue_5y_cagr, revenue_acceleration_lindy, asset_5y_cagr, years_of_history, n_yrs_positive_fcf, roic_lindy -> EDGAR + ST fills (global; the fill map notes 3y/5y are now exact spans); revenue_ttm_usd -> M converted; fq_rev_growth -> FQ; ts_maxdd_5y -> TS. 1,486 firers (A1).
- Methodology issues: (1) [currency] the 5y revenue CAGR is NOMINAL LOCAL currency: in TRY/ARS/EGP/NGN every going concern clears 8%/yr on inflation alone, and the acceleration leg (3y > 5y) is met by any inflation acceleration — the gate measures the currency, not the company, in those markets. (2) No per-share leg: a serial issuer growing revenue 8% on 10% share growth is "durable growth" (financial-growth per-share fields, A2 §A.1 open). (3) asset_5y_cagr > 3% is met by goodwill from deals; organic vs inorganic is not distinguished. (4) The 3y-vs-5y comparison is base-year sensitive (a 2020 trough base inflates every 5y CAGR ending 2025 vs the 3y from 2022).
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth` fiveY/threeY/tenYRevenueGrowthPerShare (H, G) — the per-share and multi-window form (A2 §A.1 EXC); `financial-statement-symbol-list.reportingCurrency` (S, G) + a CPI table to deflate; `balance-sheet-statement.goodwill` history (H, G, cached) for the organic test.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] deflate the CAGR by the reporting currency's inflation (or compute in USD) before the 8% cut, or exclude high-inflation reporting currencies from the accel leg. 2 [spirit] per-share growth as the core growth measure (FG fiveYRevenueGrowthPerShare >= +47% = 8%/yr) — A2 §A.1 open. 3 [spirit] organic guard: goodwill 5y CAGR < revenue 5y CAGR. 4 [spirit] promote the "TTM >= half the 5y CAGR" lens to core (A1 A.3 open).

### arch_lindy_margin  (line 1858; core at 1876)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents as a rule; AC §4 Tillinghast "Profitability (ROE > 10-12% sustained ~10 years)"; AC §2 Den Fujita "thick margins"; MR §IV flyover. Comment: "high op margin AND high EBITDA margin held over 5+ years (compounder signature)".
- As coded (watch): op_margin_lindy 10-60%; ebitda_margin_lindy 12-80%; years >= 5; is_operating; _roce_now_ok. Core (_tier, where measured): worst observed through-cycle op margin (tc_min_opm, where tc_years >= 3) >= 15%, else fmp_st_op_margin_lindy (a 5-year MEDIAN) >= 15%. Exceptional: tc_min_opm >= 20% & tc_min_gm >= 40%. Lenses: tc_min_opm, tc_med_opm, op_margin_lindy, ebitda_margin_lindy, tc_min_gm.
- Fidelity: "high margin held" — captured in the core as the worst year >= 15% (A1 A.3 "15% floor" and "through-cycle minimum" done). "~10 years" (Tillinghast) — approximated: the core accepts as few as 3 observed years; A2 §B.12's ">= 7 FYs" is open (7 years is only the lens/exceptional sense). "Still durable now" (current margin >= 0.6-0.8x the lindy margin, A1 A.3 / A2 §B.12) — missing: _roce_now_ok is a sign test, a name whose margin fell from 30% to 5% this year passes the core on its history. "Thick margins" as pricing power (gross) — exceptional only.
- Lineage: op_margin_lindy, ebitda_margin_lindy, years_of_history -> EDGAR + ST fills (global medians); tc_min_opm, tc_min_gm, tc_years -> TC (global, cached 8 FYs). Currency-neutral (margins). 6,752 firers (A1: 28% of INV).
- Methodology issues: (1) [fallback changes meaning] `_min_opm_chain` applies one 15% cut to a MINIMUM (tc) where available and to a MEDIAN (fmp_st lindy) otherwise — the fallback population is judged on a laxer statistic under the same name. (2) The watch's 0.6 op-margin cap (documented as the INVA royalty guard) also drops genuine 60%+ franchises (exchanges, royalty streamers) the "thick margins" doctrine wants; a corroboration test (gross margin, n_yrs) would separate the one-off from the franchise. (3) EBITDA-margin leg is watch-only; the core is op-margin only — D&A-heavy compounders are judged after depreciation only, which is the stricter and better test, but the docstring promises both. (4) No "now" leg (above).
- FMP augmentations: none needed for the core (tc_* already cached, 0 calls); `financial-growth` (H, G) is not needed; `income-statement` 8 FYs already give tc_med_opm for the "now vs median" leg.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] add the "still durable" leg: current op margin >= 0.8 x tc_med_opm (A2 §B.12 open; A1 A.3 0.6x open). 2 [method] when only the median exists, either require a higher cut on the median or mark the name unmeasured (watch), so one threshold is not applied to two statistics. 3 [spirit] years: core tc_years >= 5, exceptional >= 7 (A2 §B.12 open). 4 [method] replace the 0.6 cap with corroboration (op margin > 0.6 allowed when tc_min_gm >= 0.5 and n_yrs_positive_opinc >= 4).

### arch_lynch_evgy  (line 2572; core at 2590)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents (MR §II lists Lynch among the multibagger studies only). Lynch (One Up on Wall Street): "the P/E ratio of any company that's fairly priced will equal its growth rate"; the dividend-adjusted form (growth + yield) / P/E: "less than 1 is poor, 1.5 is okay, 2 or better is what you're really looking for" — i.e. PEGY <= 0.5-0.67. The EV/EBITDA form is the engine's own extension: comment "threshold scaled to 0.6 since EV/EBITDA runs ~60% of P/E for the same business".
- As coded (watch): is_operating; [ev_ebitda_gy in (0, 0.6] & EBITDA > 0] OR [ev_ebitda_gy missing & EBITDA > 0 & ev_sales >= 0.05 & (psg in (0, 0.06] | evsg in (0, 0.05])]. ev_ebitda_gy = EV/EBITDA / (1-year EBITDA growth% capped 100% + dividend yield%). Core (_tier, where measured): EV/EBITDA / (min(TTM EBIT growth chain, 3y revenue CAGR) capped 50% x 100 + yield x 100) <= 0.6, growth >= 8%. Exceptional <= 0.4. Lenses: lynch_evgy_durable (-), fqx_ebit_ttm_g.
- Fidelity: the ratio's form — captured (engine variant). "Growth rate" in Lynch is the LONG-TERM earnings growth rate — approximated: the watch uses ONE year of EBITDA growth (A1 B.1: median 36%, 25% above 97%); the core's min(TTM EBIT growth, 3y revenue CAGR) is A1's fix (done) but still mixes an earnings rate with a sales rate. The 0.6 threshold is a research parameter [R], not Lynch's. The psg/evsg FALLBACK changes the meaning outright: "cheap relative to SALES growth" is a different ratio (P/S / revenue growth) labelled Lynch EVGY; it supplied 882 firers (A1). Dividend yield — captured from a quote-time snapshot.
- Lineage: ev_ebitda_gy, psg, evsg -> derived (derive_missing_columns.py) from M snapshot ev_ebitda / ebitda_yoy / dividend_yield (YF); fqx_ebit_ttm_g -> FQX (global); fmp_st_ebit_g1 -> ST; ebit_growth_yoy -> M; revenue_3y_cagr -> EDGAR + ST fill. 6,873 firers (A1 watch); core 2,457 measured. Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) [fallback changes meaning] the psg/evsg branch is not an EBITDA-growth ratio; it belongs in cheap_sales_scaler, not under the Lynch name. (2) No EV sanity band: an FX-corrupt EV (ADR / cross-line; the Nitori 0.04x case the midcap_garp guard cites) makes EV/EBITDA ~0 and the ratio ~0 — passes as "very cheap". (3) [currency] dividend_yield from Yahoo can be in the wrong quote unit for GBp/ZAc lines (A2 §C same-currency yield open), inflating the denominator. (4) The core chain's last resort `ebit_growth_yoy` (M snapshot) is again a single year, so the "durable" label holds only for names with fqx or fmp_st coverage.
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth.threeYRevenueGrowthPerShare`, `ebitdaGrowth` history (H, G) for a 3-year EBITDA CAGR (A2 §A.1 [Q] open); `analyst-estimates` forward EBITDA (S, G) for a forward PEG (A2 §1 [+]); `dividends` (H, G) same-currency yield (A2 §C open); `enterprise-values` (H, G) for the sanity band.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] demote the psg/evsg branch to `lynch_evgy_watch` only (or rename it); the core must be EBITDA/EBIT-growth based. 2 [method] EV/mcap 0.2-5 sanity on the EV/EBITDA input (as midcap_garp). 3 [spirit] growth = 3-year EBITDA (or EBIT) CAGR from the cached annual statements rather than min(TTM, revenue CAGR) — same-measure growth is what Lynch's rule means. 4 [data] same-currency yield from `dividends` (A2 §C open).

### arch_lynch_pegy  (line 2541; core at 2559)
- Source recipe: as lynch_evgy: Lynch's dividend-adjusted P/E-to-growth: (long-term growth rate + dividend yield) / P/E, "2 or better is what you're really looking for" (PEGY <= 0.5), "less than 1 is poor" (PEGY > 1). Comment: "PEGY = P/E / (earnings growth% + dividend yield%). Lynch: <=1.0 is fair-or-better".
- As coded (watch): pegy in (0, 1.0] where pegy = p_e / (yf_earnings_growth capped 100% + yield). Financials included (correct for a P/E rule). Core (_tier, where growth measured): growth chain = fqx_ni_ttm_g -> fmp_st_ni_g1 -> yf_earnings_growth capped at 0.50; P/E / (g x 100 + yield x 100) <= 1.0 with g in [8%, 50%] and an EPS-durability share >= 0.75 on any of fqx_eps_pos_share_8 / eps_yoy_positive_share / fmp_st_ni_up_share_5. Exceptional <= 0.5 & fqx_eps_pos_share_8 >= 0.875. Lenses: lynch_pegy_ttm (-), fqx_eps_pos_share_8, fqx_ni_ttm_g.
- Fidelity: the formula — captured. "Fair or better" at <= 1.0 — captured; Lynch's "really looking for" (<= 0.5) is the exceptional (A1 B.1: 75% of watch firers cleared 0.5 on the Yahoo growth, so the exceptional tier was doing the watch's job). Long-term growth — approximated by TTM NET INCOME growth (one year, and NI rather than EPS: dilution not netted); the 8-50% band is A1's (done). EPS durability — approximated by three shares of DIFFERENT definitions under one 0.75 cut: fqx_eps_pos_share_8 = share of 8 quarters with positive EPS, eps_yoy_positive_share = share of years with EPS UP YoY (EDGAR), fmp_st_ni_up_share_5 = share of 5 FYs with NI UP — "positive" vs "rising" are not the same durability.
- Lineage: pegy -> derived from p_e (YF snapshot, can be on adjusted EPS), yf_earnings_growth (YF single quarter, A1 §5), dividend_yield (YF, quote-unit risk); fqx_ni_ttm_g, fqx_eps_pos_share_8 -> FQX (global); fmp_st_ni_g1, fmp_st_ni_up_share_5 -> ST (global); eps_yoy_positive_share -> EDGAR (US). 7,952 watch firers (A1); core 1,836 measured.
- Methodology issues: (1) [fallback changes meaning] the last-resort Yahoo growth is clipped to 0.50 and the band is [0.08, 0.50] inclusive, so every name at Yahoo's 100% cap re-enters the core at exactly g = 50% — A1's "38% of firers at the cap" artifact survives at the band's top edge for names without fqx/fmp_st. (2) NI growth vs EPS growth: a share-issuing acquirer's NI growth is not per-share growth (Lynch's growth is per share). (3) p_e snapshot on a one-off gain: no "latest NI <= 2x ni_avg" guard, so a tax-credit year makes both a low P/E and a high growth rate — double-counted cheapness. (4) [currency] dividend yield quote-unit mix on GBp/ZAc lines (A2 §C open).
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth.threeYNetIncomeGrowthPerShare` (H, G) — 3-year PER-SHARE earnings growth, the closest to Lynch's rate (A2 §A.1 / §B open); `analyst-estimates` forward EPS (S, G) for a forward PEG (A2 §1 [+]); `dividends` (H, G) for yield.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] treat a clipped Yahoo value (>= 0.50 before clipping) as unmeasured (watch), not as g = 50%. 2 [spirit] growth per share: fqx EPS TTM growth (NI / diluted shares) or FG threeYNetIncomeGrowthPerShare, capped 50%; NI growth only as fallback. 3 [method] one durability definition: "EPS positive in >= 75% of the last 8 quarters" (fqx) or, for annual-only filers, "NI positive in >= 4 of 5 FYs" — drop the "up" shares from the same OR. 4 [method] one-off guard: latest NI <= 2 x ni_avg on the P/E leg.

### arch_lynch_reward  (line 6500)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents as a recipe (MR §VI: Lynch supplies "turn over the most rocks ... tenbagger mindset"; Cassel's synthesis). Lynch's own principle is the earnings line: "the company's fundamental progress is what matters; sooner or later earnings progress is rewarded" — a stock whose price has lagged years of earnings growth. The engine's construction (comment block above 6395): years of progress (9 lenses) + accelerating progress + price stagnation (3.5y/10y ROC) + a monthly volatility-asymmetry "coil near 50" + a Squeeze & Release state — the coil/release layer is the engine's technical overlay, not Lynch.
- As coded: mcap > 0; is_operating; _not_melting; progress = (any of 9 lenses OR accel score >= 0.28) AND profit-durable (opinc/FCF 4 of 5 | roiic >= 10% | op lindy >= 8% | (roce >= 10% & ebitda > 0) | (EPS share >= 0.75 & op > 5%)); roce not < 6% (soft); unpaid = ts_r52 <= +35% (fallbacks roc_12m, momentum_12m); live tape; near-50 coil (monthly/quarterly asym flags, continuous coil, or weekly bs vol/range contraction); release (monthly S&R release recent or squeeze >= 6) OR roc setup (3.5y ROC <= 40% & accelerating | 10y ROC <= 100% & accel | price <= 5y avg & accel | ts_r156 <= 40% & 1y pace above 3y pace).
- Fidelity: "years of progress" — approximated loosely: lr_progress_any fires on ONE lens, and two of the nine are single-year prints (gross_profit_yoy >= 8%, inflection_confirm_score) — author's frame "years" vs code `_confirm([...]).any` with `gross_profit_yoy >= 0.08` in the set; the profit-durability AND helps but itself admits the point test `(roce >= 0.10) & (ebitda > 0)`. "Not yet rewarded" — approximated by a 12-month return <= +35% and a subdued 3.5-year ROC; Lynch's test is price RELATIVE TO EARNINGS over years (the earnings line), and no leg compares the price path to the progress path (the coil measures used by liger/narrative_lag do exactly this and are absent here). Coil / release — engine overlay [P]; A1 A.1 weekly-coil leg done (bs_vol_ratio/range_ratio), ts_r156 leg done.
- Lineage: revenue_5y_cagr, revenue_3y_cagr, n_yrs_*, roiic_lindy, op_margin_lindy -> EDGAR + ST fills (global); gross_profit_yoy, eps_yoy_positive_share, eps streak -> M / EDGAR (US); asym_m/q, sr_m_* , roc_3_5y, roc_10y, roc_accel_* -> lynch tape (monthly bars, coverage partial); ts_r52/r156 -> TS; bs_* -> base_snapshot (partial). 1,043 firers (A2).
- Methodology issues: (1) `lr_unpaid` at ts_r52 <= +35% calls a stock up 35% in a year "unpaid"; the 3.5y ROC <= 40% leg is the real stagnation test but it is only one of four ORed setup routes, and the `sr_m_release` route bypasses it entirely — a name can fire with NO multi-year price-vs-progress test. (2) The monthly asym/squeeze fields are proprietary tape constructs with partial coverage; names without them fall to the weekly bs coil, a different object under the same "near 50" name. (3) roc_3_5y and roc_10y are price returns without dividends (lynch tape) while ts_r156 is total return — mixed bases in one OR. (4) `lr_not_capacity_trap` (roce < 6% soft) is a good Lynch-consistent guard; but progress lenses on nominal local CAGR inherit the inflation issue (lindy_growth #1).
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth.threeY/fiveYNetIncomeGrowthPerShare` (H, G) to build the earnings-line gap (per-share earnings growth over 3-5y vs ts_r156/ts_r260); `historical-price-eod` already gives the price side; `dividends` for total-return consistency (done in TS).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] the earnings-line gap as the core "unpaid" test: log(1 + per-share earnings or revenue growth over 3y) - log(1 + ts_r156) >= log 1.25 (price lags progress), replacing the +35% 1-year cut; keep the coil/release as score. 2 [spirit] "years" = at least two multi-year lenses (drop gross_profit_yoy and inflection_confirm_score from the firing set; keep them in the score). 3 [method] one return basis (TS total return) for every price leg. 4 [data] FG per-share growth for the progress side (A2 §A.1 open).

### arch_oak_asset_floor  (line 3517)
- Source recipe: WO Archetype 4 "Asset-Backed Floor + Optional Growth": "Market cap roughly <= (cash + appraised asset value). Low debt (net cash or modest net debt) after any announced sales. Enterprise asset (e.g. property, data centers, fleet) with known market values. Low or no operating leverage priced in (P/E near 0 or negative)."; table: "Mkt cap <= (cash + tangible asset value); Net cash or low debt; production optionality"; reject: "Assets are not liquid ... Significant contingent liabilities ... Core business still burning cash with no path to profitability ... Management repeatedly delays unlocking value". Comment: "Well-captured on the CASH leg (net cash or NCAV >= mcap = Graham floor); does NOT see hidden real-estate-at-market or private-stake value."
- As coded: is_operating; mcap 0-$500M; net_cash_pct_mcap >= 40% OR ncav_pct_mcap >= 80%; pb in (0, 1.5); _not_melting. No lenses.
- Fidelity: "mcap <= cash + tangible assets" — approximated, and the comment overstates it: neither leg is a floor. net cash at 40% of mcap leaves 60% of the cap uncovered, and pb < 1.5 allows the cap to sit 50% ABOVE book; NCAV at 80% is 25% short of covering the cap. Author "Mkt cap <= (cash + tangible asset value)" vs code `(net_cash_pct_c >= 0.40) | (ncav_pct >= 0.80)` & `pb < 1.5`. Low debt after sales — captured on the net-cash leg, absent on the NCAV leg (NCAV nets ALL liabilities, so fine). Known-market-value assets (property, fleet) — missing (documented). "P/E near 0 or negative" (no operating value priced) — missing; a profitable net-cash name passes, which is a different (better) case but not the recipe. Core burning cash — approximated by _not_melting. Recent asset sale / delays — missing.
- Lineage: net_cash_pct_mcap, ncav_pct_mcap, pb -> M (YF/FMP snapshot; mcap-relative so currency cancels except GBp/ZAc quote-unit lines); _not_melting -> M op_margin/roce/cash yields. 1,973 firers (A1 watch band "no lever"). Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) `cash` in net_cash_pct can include restricted cash and excludes securities for some FMP-filled rows (documented in cash_adjusted_pe) — a floor should count marketable securities. (2) No tangible-book leg: pb < 1.5 is on total book incl. goodwill — a goodwill-heavy name with 40% net cash is not "asset-backed". (3) No Altman/going-concern veto (shipped for cluseau only; the enrichment map §1 names this archetype for it). (4) [currency] pb from Yahoo on a GBp/ZAc quote can be off by 100x in either direction (known audit case).
- FMP augmentations: `balance-sheet-statement` shortTermInvestments / longTermInvestments (H, G) into the floor numerator (A2 §1 [Q]); `key-metrics.tangibleAssetValueTTM` / `ratios-ttm.tangibleBookValuePerShareTTM` (S, G) for a global tangible floor; `scores-bulk` Altman (S, G) veto; `financial-statement-symbol-list` currency map for the pb unit check.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] make the floor a floor: core = (net cash + securities) >= 70% of mcap with p_tb <= 1.0, OR NCAV >= 100% of mcap; the current rule stays as watch. 2 [spirit] add the "no operating value priced" lens as a score term (P/E <= 0 or EV <= 0 — cash_gt_ev_flag exists). 3 [data] Altman veto and securities in the numerator (A2 §1 open). 4 [data] asset-sale evidence (fq_disc_ops_share, 8-K 2.01 US) as the exceptional tier — WO's "after any announced sales".

### arch_oak_deep_value  (line 3470)
- Source recipe: WO Archetype 10 "Distressed/Deep-Value Special Situations": "Stock price crash > 50% from recent highs. Price/book or Price/cash/assets very low (< 0.5). Assets identifiable (e.g. cash > 20% mcap, carrying values of subsidiaries). Some near-term liquidity or asset-realization event"; table: "> 50% LTM stock drop; Price/Book < 0.5; Price/Sales < 0.3; sizable cash/assets share"; reject: "Too far gone ... No credible plan (serial profit warnings, repeated dilution) ... Major lawsuits". Comment adds the Belluscura gate: "positive EBITDA alone isn't enough, require FCF/CFO > 0".
- As coded: is_operating; beaten_down_any(0.50); (pb in (0, 0.7) | ncav_pct >= 0.5 | cheap_score >= 0.5); cash_pct_mcap >= 20%; ebitda > 0 & (fcf > 0 | cfo > 0); _not_melting; interest_coverage >= 1.5 soft. No lenses.
- Fidelity: > 50% crash — captured (any of 8 lenses with the 52w contradiction veto; A2 §605 wants ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.5 as the "recent highs" reading — open). P/B < 0.5 — loosened: author "< 0.5" vs code `pb < 0.7 | ncav_pct >= 0.5 | cheap_score >= 0.5`; the third alternative is a composite (defined elsewhere) that admits a name at 1.5x book that is cheap on multiples — a different thesis from "Price/cash/assets very low". P/S < 0.3 — missing. Cash > 20% mcap — captured exactly (gross cash, as the author). Realization event — missing (unscreenable ex-US; 8-K / disc-ops exist). "Repeated dilution" reject — missing (no share-count leg). Cash-generative gate — engine addition (the author's archetype includes burners "rising from the ashes"; the engine's Belluscura lesson narrows it deliberately — documented, [spirit] neutral).
- Lineage: pb, ncav_pct_mcap, cash_pct_mcap -> M (YF/FMP snapshot); cheap_score -> derived composite (M); ebitda/fcf/cfo -> M TTM; interest_coverage -> EDGAR + fq fill (global); drawdown lenses -> TS primary, YF snapshot fallbacks. 668 firers (A2). Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) `cheap_score >= 0.5` under a "deep discount to assets" label is a meaning drift; the author's second lens is P/S < 0.3, not a multiples composite. (2) `cash_pct_mcap` clip(0, 3) is gross cash: a name with 25% cash and 200% debt "has identifiable assets" — the author's own next line asks for the realization path, but the leverage reject ("too far gone") has only the soft IC >= 1.5 (present for ~8% EDGAR + fq fill). (3) No dilution guard (shares_yoy) although "repeated dilution" is the explicit reject. (4) The 50% drawdown on the 5y-average lens (`_pv5_n <= 0.5`) admits a name that halved years ago and is flat since — the "recent highs" wording wants hi260 or dd52.
- FMP augmentations: `ratios-ttm.interestCoverageRatioTTM` (S, G) already mapped (A2 §1 [Q]); `scores-bulk` Altman as the "too far gone" test (enrichment §1); `splits` (H, G) reverse-split veto; `sec-filings-8k` / `mergers-acquisitions-latest` (H, US) for the realization event.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] discount leg per the author: pb < 0.5 OR p_s < 0.3 OR ncav_pct >= 0.5 (composite to watch). 2 [spirit] dilution reject: shares_yoy <= 5% and no reverse split in 12 months. 3 [method] crash = ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.5 or _dd52 <= -0.5 as core; the 5y-average lens only as watch (A2 §605 open). 4 [data] Altman veto and a dated realization-event lens (8-K 2.01 / M&A feed, US) as exceptional.

### arch_oak_deleveraging  (line 3442)
- Source recipe: WO Archetype 8 "Cash-Flow Deleveraging & Yield Plays": "FCF yield > 10%. Net debt/EBITDA 1.5-3x but trending down (scheduled amortization or excess-cash sweeps). Falling interest expense. Dividends/buybacks material (> 3-5% market cap annually). Low or no growth capex requirement."; evidence: "Debt schedule ... Share count: static or falling"; table: "FCF yield > 10%; net debt/EBITDA 1-3x and falling; int. expense down; buyback or dividend >= 5%"; reject: "Debtholders in control ... Heavy maintenance capex ... Operating cash flow misreported via big receivable build (faked FCF) ... Growing share count".
- As coded: is_operating; _roce_now_ok; _op_viable(0); (fcf_yield | robust_cash_yield | owner_earnings_yield) >= 10%; ebitda > 0; nde in [1, 3]; (ebitda_yoy > 0 | ebitda_inflection > 0 | oper_lev_any); shares_yoy not > 2%; (financing_cf_ttm < 0 | net_buyback_ttm > 0 | shares_yoy < 0 | fq_deleveraging_flag == 1); interest_coverage >= 2 soft. No lenses.
- Fidelity: FCF yield > 10% — captured (three yield lenses). ND/EBITDA band — captured (1-3 per the table). "Trending down" — approximated by the DENOMINATOR: rising EBITDA (or any oper_lev_any drift) is read as the ratio falling; the numerator path (fq_deleveraging_flag: net debt down >= 9 months and >= 5% of assets, ops-funded) is only one of four ORed alternatives, and `financing_cf_ttm < 0` includes dividends and buybacks, so a name with flat debt paying a dividend satisfies the "debt reduction confirmation". Author "scheduled amortization" vs code `(financing_cf_ttm < 0) | ... | fq_deleveraging_flag`. Falling interest expense — missing (fq interest expense TTM exists via fq_interest_cover inputs). Payout > 3-5% — DROPPED by the gate-audit ("cash routes to lenders") — a documented divergence from both the prose and the table ("buyback or dividend >= 5%"). Low growth capex — missing (capex/D&A). Faked FCF (receivable build) — missing (DSO trend, A2 nuanced #2). Growing share count — captured.
- Lineage: fcf_yield, robust_cash_yield, owner_earnings_yield -> M derived (USD-consistent); nde -> M (99-filled when missing; EBITDA <= 0 -> 99); financing_cf_ttm, net_buyback_ttm -> EDGAR (US); shares_yoy -> M; fq_deleveraging_flag -> FQ (global); interest_coverage -> EDGAR + fq fill. 837 firers (A2). Non-US names reach the confirmation leg only through shares_yoy < 0 or fq_deleveraging_flag.
- Methodology issues: (1) The confirmation OR is satisfied by dividends (financing CF < 0), which is not deleveraging. (2) `nde <= 3` on the 99-filled default excludes every name whose net debt was not fetched (37%); `_clean_bs`-style NaN handling is used elsewhere. (3) `oper_lev_any` as a "leverage trajectory" lens: a raw sequential EBITDA uptick is not a falling ratio. (4) [currency] none — yields and ratios are same-currency; but interest_coverage from fq is a TTM ratio, not a trend.
- FMP augmentations: `cash-flow-statement.debtRepayment` / `netDebtIssuance` (H, G, cached annuals) for literal repayment vs issuance; `income-statement.interestExpense` history (H, G, cached) for the falling-interest leg; `key-metrics.capexToDepreciationTTM` (S, G) for "low growth capex"; `key-metrics.daysOfSalesOutstandingTTM` (S, G) for the faked-FCF reject (A2 nuanced #2); `dividends` (H, G) for the payout leg.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] core confirmation = fq_deleveraging_flag OR annual debtRepayment > netDebtIssuance (repayment net of new borrowing), with `financing_cf < 0` alone demoted to watch. 2 [spirit] add falling interest expense (TTM interest expense < year-ago) and capex/D&A <= 1.2 as the two missing prose legs. 3 [spirit] restore the payout as an exceptional lens ((dividend + buyback) >= 5%, the table's number) rather than deleting it — the author lists it as a filter. 4 [method] NaN-aware nde (a missing nde is unmeasured, not 99); DSO up >= 15% YoY as a veto (faked FCF).

### arch_oak_nav_discount  (line 3508)
- Source recipe: WO Archetype 5 "Holding-Company / NAV Discount": "Public holding company or investment trust. Price/NAV < 0.70x, ideally < 0.50x. Underlying assets have recent arms-length valuations. Overhead costs low (so discount isn't burned away). Net debt low."; table: "Price/NAV < 0.70; cash + listed stakes >= 50% mcap; O/H expense < 3% NAV; debt low"; SQL: `price/nav < 0.70 AND (cash + quoted_stakes)/market_cap > 0.5 AND overhead_pct < 3`; catalysts: wind-up, tender, buybacks below NAV; reject: "stale Level-3 valuations ... raising at low prices ... indefinite live-for-tenure management". Comment: "PROXY ONLY: book ~ NAV ... Cannot capture true NAV or his dividend-cover >= 1.2x test."
- As coded: sector == 'Financials' & industry regex (asset manager / closed-end / investment trust / holding / capital market / fund / diversified financial / investment company, excluding banks, insurers, brokers, exchanges by industry and name); NAV not eroding (ROE not known-negative; NI < 0 with non-positive book = eroding); (pb < 0.7 | p_tb < 0.7); (dividend_yield >= 5% | capital_return_yield >= 6%). No lenses.
- Fidelity: P/NAV < 0.7 — approximated by P/B (book = NAV only where holdings are at fair value: IFRS investment entities, BDCs; a holdco carrying stakes at cost understates NAV, so the discount reads too SMALL — the test is conservative there but misses AC §6 "scrapyard" names entirely). Cash + listed stakes >= 50% of mcap — missing. Overhead < 3% NAV — missing (fq opex / equity computable). Net debt low — missing: a levered closed-end fund or holdco passes. Yield >= 5% — NOT in WO's recipe (it is the engine's dividend-cover proxy from Oak's trust write-ups); as a mandatory AND it excludes the non-paying holdco with a buyback or wind-up, which the author's catalysts list. Catalysts (tender, buyback below NAV, wind-up) — missing (fq_shares_yoy < 0 global; SC TO-I US).
- Lineage: sector/industry -> M profile; pb -> YF snapshot; p_tb -> EDGAR (US) / fq_p_tb (global, computed at 525); dividend_yield -> YF (quote-unit risk); capital_return_yield -> EDGAR (US, ~5%). 51 firers (A2 "no material gain"). Effectively US + YF-covered vehicles.
- Methodology issues: (1) Sector restricted to 'Financials': Japanese cross-holders (Keisei, MR §I.G / AC §6), UK property-and-stakes holdcos and conglomerate holdcos are tagged Industrials / Consumer / Real Estate and can never fire. (2) [currency] Yahoo dividend_yield on GBp/ZAc trust lines is the archetype's population (UK investment trusts) — the unit risk sits exactly where the thesis lives (A2 §C open). (3) No leverage test at all in a recipe that lists "net debt low" twice. (4) `_nav_not_eroding` on a one-period ROE: a trust marking down NAV this year is "eroding" (excluded) even when that is the discount opportunity — the author's reject is "recurring cash burns by manager", i.e. persistent.
- FMP augmentations: `balance-sheet-statement` shortTermInvestments / longTermInvestments (H, G) for "cash + stakes >= 50% mcap" (enrichment §1 [Q]); `income-statement` operatingExpenses / equity for overhead < 3% (H, G, cached); `dividends` (H, G) same-currency yield and cover; `cash-flow-statement.commonStockRepurchased` (H, G) buybacks below NAV; `sec-filings-search` SC TO-I (US) tenders.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] add the two missing filters: (cash + investments) / mcap >= 0.5 and (total debt / equity) <= 0.3 (net debt low). 2 [spirit] make the yield alternative one of several "discount being addressed" lenses: dividend >= 5% OR fq_shares_yoy <= -2% OR a tender event, not mandatory. 3 [method] widen the vehicle test beyond the Financials sector: industry contains 'holding' / 'investment' in any sector where pb < 0.7 and fq investments >= 50% of assets. 4 [data] overhead ratio from cached opex; same-currency yield (A2 §C open); persistence for "eroding" (2 of 3 FYs NI < 0).

### arch_oak_order_conversion  (line 5581; spirited)
- Source recipe: WO table "Order-Book/Conversion (Nanocaps)": "Cap < £50M; Signed orders/deposits > 20% last-year revenue; GM > 30%; utilization gap"; evidence "Tier1: signed contracts/purchase orders; Tier2: prepayments announced"; catalysts "Delivery of major order; funding injection; production start"; reject "'Pipeline multiple' talk; unnamed counterparties; heavy funding needs; repeated delays or placings"; sample query: `marketCap < 50e6 & order_backlog/rev > 0.75`. Comment: "LAGGING proxy: we can't see order intake / book-to-bill, only the P&L footprint once it lands".
- As coded: is_operating; mcap 0-$1B; ebitda > 0; (cfo > 0 | fcf > 0); _not_melting; (rev_accel > 0 | rev_yoy > 5%); (oper_lev_any | fqx incremental EBIT margin >= 20%); (ebitda_inflection > 0 | ebitda_yoy > 0 | fq_defrev_build_flag == 1). Lenses: fqx_inc_ebit_margin_dt, fq_rev_growth, fq_defrev_growth_minus_rev, ebitda_margin_delta_yoy.
- Fidelity: the forward book ("signed orders/deposits > 20% of revenue") — approximated ONLY by fq_defrev_build_flag (deferred revenue >= 10% of revenue and growing >= 5pp faster than revenue) and only as one OR alternative to "EBITDA up" — author `order_backlog/rev > 0.75` vs code `(ebitda_inflection > 0) | (ebitda_yoy_v > 0) | (fq_defrev_build_flag == 1)`; A1 B.1 measured 3,953 firers with the forward-book leg satisfied by 145. Cap < £50M — code < $1B (20x the author's band; "nanocaps" is the archetype's title). GM > 30% — missing. Utilization gap — missing (unscreenable). Repeated placings — missing (no share-count leg). Named counterparties / delivery events — unscreenable ex-transcripts.
- Lineage: fq_defrev_to_rev, fq_defrev_growth_minus_rev -> FQ (global, where the filer reports deferred revenue / contract liabilities); deferred_revenue (RPO family) -> EDGAR (US); rev_accel, ebitda_yoy, ebitda_inflection -> M; fqx_inc_ebit_margin -> FQX; oper_lev_any -> M. 3,953 firers (A1 very broad).
- Methodology issues: (1) The P&L legs are three ORs of common events (revenue up 5%, any margin drift, EBITDA up) — this is the base rate of a growing profitable microcap, not order conversion (A1 B.1 "shock-sized margin + >= 10% TTM growth" open). (2) Deferred revenue is a proxy for deposits only in made-to-order businesses; for SaaS it is billing timing — the flag is industry-blind. (3) No dilution guard in a thesis whose explicit reject is "repeated placings". (4) The $1B cap admits businesses where a single order cannot move the needle — the author's whole point is order size vs cap.
- FMP augmentations: `earning-call-transcript` (H, G where covered) backlog / book-to-bill / "order intake" legs for non-US filers (A2 §A.2 N, targeted ~3k firers); `sec-filings-8k` item 1.01 material contracts (US); `analyst-estimates` forward revenue vs TTM (S, G) as a book-to-bill proxy; `key-metrics` gross margin (S, G) already in M.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] core = the forward-book leg (fq_defrev_build_flag or EDGAR RPO growth > revenue growth) REQUIRED, with the P&L footprint as confirmation; the P&L-only rule is the watch (A1 B.1 open). 2 [spirit] size and margin per the table as the exceptional tier: mcap <= $100M and gross_margin >= 30%. 3 [spirit] dilution guard fq_shares_yoy <= 5% (the "placings" reject). 4 [data] transcript backlog / book-to-bill leg for non-US names (A2 §A.2 open).

### arch_oak_resource_leverage  (line 3428)

Macro tailwinds"; table: "Cash-cost <= 65% of spot; net debt/EBITDA < 1.5; reserve life > 8 yrs; EV/EBITDA < 6"; SQL: `sector='Materials' AND market_cap < 200M AND EV_EBITDA < 6 AND cash_cost_pct < 65 AND net_debt_EBITDA < 2.5`; reject: "Price rally is speculative ... Added cash floor + high-margin (cost-curve) proxy + bought-on-weakness; loosened EV/EBITDA to 8".
- As coded: sector in {Materials, Energy}; ev_ebitda in (0, 8); _clean_bs(1.5); net_cash_pct_mcap >= 20%; ebitda_margin >= 25%; (fcf_yield | robust_cash_yield | owner_earnings_yield) >= 8%; beaten_down_any(0.20). No lenses.
- Fidelity: EV/EBITDA < 6 — loosened to 8 (documented). ND/EBITDA < 1.5 — captured (clean_bs) AND tightened with a 20% net-cash requirement that is Oak's Thungela pattern, not the WO recipe ("net debt/EBITDA modest" admits modest debt). Cost position (cash cost <= 65% of spot) — approximated by a 25% EBITDA MARGIN: margin is price-cyclical (every producer has a fat margin at a price peak and a thin one at the trough), whereas cost/spot is a position on the cost curve — author "cash cost/AISC <= 60-70% of spot" vs code `ebitda_margin >= 0.25`; the through-cycle minimum margin (tc_min_opm > 0 = survived the trough) is the closer available reading (A2 §603 open). Reserve life — missing (not in any feed). Macro / offtake / hedging — missing. mcap < $200M — missing. Dilution history — missing. "Bought on weakness" — engine addition (Oak's timing), fine.
- Lineage: sector -> M profile (label); ev_ebitda, ebitda_margin, net_cash_pct_mcap, nde -> M (YF/FMP snapshot); cash yields -> M derived; drawdown lenses -> TS + YF fallbacks. 37 firers (A2). Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) "Producer" is not identified: 'Materials' includes chemicals, packaging, building products, and 'Energy' includes refiners, services and midstream — none has commodity-price leverage in the author's sense; the SQL's own sector filter is crude but the prose says "producer of materials (metals, uranium, oil, crypto-miners)". (2) EBITDA margin at a cycle high is the opposite signal from what the archetype wants (buy the low-cost producer when margins are compressed and price is weak); combining margin >= 25% with beaten_down 20% therefore selects the post-peak name, not the trough survivor. (3) No dilution history (shares_3y_cagr) in the one sector where dilution is structural (AC §B: "capital-intensive mining/energy ... dilution structural"). (4) [currency] none (ratios), but net_cash_pct on a GBp/USD dual-line miner is the known unit case.
- FMP augmentations: `profile.industry` (S, G) to select producers (mining, coal, uranium, oil & gas E&P, precious metals) — 0 extra calls; tc_min_opm already cached (through-cycle survival); `financial-growth.weightedAverageSharesDilutedGrowth` (H, G) for the dilution reject; `revenue-product-segmentation` (H, G) to confirm the commodity share of revenue.
- Suggestions: 1 [method] producer identification by industry regex (mining|coal|uranium|oil & gas e&p|gold|silver|copper|lithium) instead of the sector label. 2 [spirit] cost position = tc_min_opm > 0 over >= 5 years (never lost money at the trough) as the core cost-curve reading, with the current 25% margin as watch (A2 §603 open). 3 [spirit] dilution reject: shares_3y_cagr <= 5%. 4 [spirit] restore EV/EBITDA < 6 and mcap < $200M as the exceptional tier per the table; reserve life stays unscreenable (say so in the docstring).

### arch_oneil_canslim  (line 3110; core at 3124)
- Source recipe: MR §II "O'Neil/Tortoriello say buy near 52-week HIGHS"; MR §VI Andreola's CAN SLIM adaptation: "25%+ revenue growth per share, profitable or visibility to profitability, 52-week highs, low institutional ownership". O'Neil's canonical letters (How to Make Money in Stocks): C = current quarterly EPS up >= 25% (the last two quarters, accelerating); A = annual EPS growth >= 25% for three years and ROE >= 17%; N = new products/management/highs; S = supply and demand (small float, volume at breakout); L = leader (RS >= 80); I = institutional sponsorship increasing; M = market direction. Comment: "[C] C ... [C] A: durable annual growth + ROE >= ~17% (roce proxy); [C] N: new price high; [C] L: RS leader; I/M/S ... noted, not faked".
- As coded (watch): live tape; C = EPS YoY growth streak >= 2 quarters OR rev_yoy >= 20%; A = roce >= 15% & not one-off-suspect & (eps_yoy_positive_share >= 0.75 | rev >= 10%); N = within 15% of the 52w high or is_52w_high; L = global percentile >= 80 on roc_6m/mom12 with positive prior run; op_margin > 0. Core (_tier, where fqx and panel RS present): fqx_eps_q_yoy >= 25% & ts_rs_pct_mkt >= 80 & ts_dist_hi52 >= 0.85. Exceptional: fqx_eps_accel > 0 & fqx_eps_pos_share_8 >= 0.75 & ts_mkt_breadth30 >= 0.5 (M). Lenses: fqx_eps_q_yoy, fqx_eps_accel, ts_rs_pct_mkt, ts_dist_hi52, fqx_eps_pos_share_8.
- Fidelity: C — captured in the core (latest quarter EPS >= 25% YoY); the watch substitutes REVENUE growth for EPS (author "current quarterly earnings" vs code `(_eps_streak >= 2) | (_revg >= 0.20)`), and neither reads O'Neil's "last two quarters" or "accelerating" except as exceptional. A — approximated: ROCE 15% for ROE 17%, and the three-year 25% annual EPS growth is absent (A2 §B.20 FG threeYNetIncomeGrowthPerShare open); the watch's `rev >= 10%` alternative makes A a revenue test. N — captured (85-100% of high; O'Neil's "new high" is the breakout itself). L — captured within market in the core (A1 A.1 done); the watch's global percentile inherits the RG/BK over-weight. S — missing (shares-float, A2 §B.20 open). I — missing (13F, A2 §B.20 / enrichment §8 open). M — exceptional only.
- Lineage: fqx_eps_q_yoy, fqx_eps_accel, fqx_eps_pos_share_8 -> FQX (global; EPS = NI / diluted shares, so one-off gains and disc-ops count); ts_rs_pct_mkt, ts_dist_hi52, ts_mkt_breadth30 -> TS (global; RS vs pseudo-index, A1 D#4); eps_yoy_growth_streak_q, eps_yoy_positive_share -> EDGAR (US); roce, rev_yoy, pct_off_52w_high, roc_6m, momentum_12m -> M / YF snapshot (stale). 334 watch firers; core 96 measured (A2).
- Methodology issues: (1) Core C on one quarter of NI-based EPS: a tax credit or gain quarter passes; O'Neil excludes non-recurring items. (2) The core does not carry A at all (only the watch's ROCE leg survives through the AND), so "annual growth" is measured by a single-period ROCE. (3) Clinical biotech leak (5 firers, A2 Z2 open). (4) Watch `_accel` and the watch's L read the stale snapshot tape (A1 §0.3).
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth.threeYNetIncomeGrowthPerShare` (H, G) for A; `shares-float-all` (S, G, 1 call) for S; `institutional-ownership/symbol-positions-summary` newPositions / investorsHoldingChange (H, US-listed) for I; `earnings` revenue surprise (H, G) for the exceptional C; `historical-price-eod` indices (A1 D#4) for a real RS denominator.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] C = two consecutive quarters >= 25% YoY with the latest accelerating, and exclude quarters with fq_disc_ops_share > 0.2 (O'Neil's "continuing operations"). 2 [spirit] A in the core: FG 3y per-share NI growth >= +52% (25%/yr per A2 §B.20 open) AND ROCE >= 15%. 3 [data] S and I legs from shares-float and 13F (A2 §B.20 open; US-listed for I). 4 [data] exclude is_clinical_biotech (A2 Z2 open); index series for RS (A1 D#4 open).

### arch_spinoff_asset  (line 8104)
- Source recipe: AC §II.C Spin-Off Multibagger Playbook: "Forced selling is the master signal: index/style funds dump the stub, and recipients sell shares they never chose. Greenblatt's three preconditions: (1) institutions don't want it; (2) insiders want it / are incentivized; (3) a hidden opportunity is revealed by the separation"; setup checklist "hidden value (SOTP mispricing) ... liquidity tier"; AC §II.A technique (3) "spin-off forced-selling bottoms"; MR §III "Form 10-12B ... THE single best spinoff early-warning". Comment: "forced-selling of a spun entity can leave it priced below its ASSET backing regardless of earnings — sum-of-parts / net-net / hidden-real-estate / deep-net-cash orphan".
- As coded: _spin == 1 (EDGAR Form-10 spin_flag OR fmp_events 10-12B/G filing <= 730 days); is_operating; _not_melting; mcap > 0; _op_viable(-5%); asset floor = pb < 1 | ncav_pct >= 0.5 | net_cash_pct >= 20% | cash_gt_ev | (fx-coherent & off-EV hidden assets >= 25% of mcap). No lenses.
- Fidelity: the spin EVENT — captured (US only). Forced SELLING — missing: nothing tests that the price fell after distribution (A2 §B.23 orphan action `ts_r13 < 0` in months 1-6, open); the flag fires on any spin in a 2-year window whether or not it was dumped. Hidden value — approximated by five balance-sheet floors (the SOTP mispricing itself is unmeasurable; the floors are a reasonable stand-in). Insider incentives (Greenblatt #2) — missing here (spirit lens only on spinoff_quality; Form 4 US). "Institutions don't want it" — missing (13F / ETF passive share). Liquidity tier — missing. Timing: 730 days from the 10-12B FILING spans pre-distribution months (no price) through ~20 months post-spin — Greenblatt's window is the first 6-18 months after distribution; the anchor date is the registration, not the distribution.
- Lineage: spin_flag -> EDGAR Form 10 (US); evt_spin_filing_date -> EVT sec-filings-search (US, ~2,000 names run per A2 §0.8); pb, ncav_pct_mcap, net_cash_pct_mcap, cash_gt_ev_flag -> M; _hidden_pct -> EDGAR non-operating assets (US); _fx_coherent -> M. 2 firers (A2). US-only in practice (A1 §10: spin flags fire on 58 names universe-wide).
- Methodology issues: (1) [SEC/US-only] the event anchor exists only for SEC filers; every UK demerger / Japanese carve-out (the AC's own examples are Vivendi, Canal+, Louis Hachette) is unreachable — A1 A.5 / A2 §A.4 global new-listing detection (profile ipoDate without a 424B4) open. (2) pb from the Yahoo snapshot on a freshly listed spinco is often stale or missing in the first quarters — the window when the thesis lives. (3) ncav / net-cash legs are mcap-relative; a spinco's first-months market cap is itself unstable (partial float, when-issued). (4) No "not a dirty spin" reading at all (deliberately dropped per the user directive — documented), so the asset floor can be a debt-loaded balance sheet whose book is real but pledged (AC anti-pattern "debt-loaded spins (Twin Hospitality -> Chapter 11)").
- FMP augmentations: `profile-bulk.ipoDate` + `ipos-prospectus` (H, G/US) for global spin detection (A2 §A.4 REACH); `symbol-change` (H) identity; `historical-price-eod` first-26-week returns for the orphan pattern; `insider-trading/statistics` (H, US) spinco insider buying; `etf/asset-exposure` (S, G) passive share; `key-metrics.tangibleAssetValueTTM` (S, G) as a fresh tangible floor when pb is stale.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] add the forced-selling reading as the core: ts_r13 <= -10% (or ts_dist_hi52 <= 0.8) within the first 6 months of trading, with the plain spin flag as watch (A2 §B.23 open). 2 [data] global spin anchor (A1 A.5 / A2 §A.4 open) and the distribution date (first panel bar) as the window's start, 3-18 months. 3 [method] floor legs on fq balance-sheet levels (same reporting currency) rather than the Yahoo pb for names listed < 12 months. 4 [spirit] surface (not gate) a `dirty_spin_flag` = nde > 4 at listing, per AC's anti-pattern.

### arch_spinoff_quality  (line 8080; spirited)
- Source recipe: AC §II.C as above; checklist "underlying business quality; an owner-operator with skin in the game"; anti-patterns "re-rated names that already delivered (Sandisk +559%) are templates, not entries; insider selling at spin; externally-managed vehicles". Comment: "a high-return FRANCHISE cast off at a fair (not-bubble) multiple. SanDisk spun with roce 0.35, op-margin 0.61, net cash, EV/EBIT 20.2 ... deliberately NOT on cheapness".
- As coded: _spin == 1; is_operating; _not_melting; mcap > 0; roce >= 20%; op_margin >= 15%; (ev_ebit in (0, 35] | ev_ebitda in (0, 30]); (nde <= 3 | net cash >= 0). Lenses: insider_distinct_buyers, roce, op_margin.
- Fidelity: business quality — captured (returns + margins). Insider incentive (Greenblatt #2) — spirit lens only (insider_distinct_buyers, Form 4 US); the AC's specific tell "does management move to the SpinCo? fresh equity? insider buying post-spin?" is not gated and is unreachable ex-US. Forced selling — missing (as spinoff_asset). "Fair multiple" — captured at a generous 35x EV/EBIT (documented). "Re-rated names are templates, not entries" — missing: a spin that already ran (SanDisk +559%) still qualifies for 24 months; no post-listing return cap. Externally managed vehicles — missing. Debt-loaded spin — partly guarded (nde <= 3 | net cash).
- Lineage: spin anchor as spinoff_asset (US); roce, op_margin, ev_ebit, ev_ebitda -> M snapshot (op_margin on carve-out allocations and separation one-offs — documented noise); nde -> M; insider_distinct_buyers -> EDGAR Form 4 (US). 2 firers (A2).
- Methodology issues: (1) roce / op_margin in the first spinco reporting periods are on carve-out financials with parent allocations — the engine's own comment flags this; fq TTM from the spinco's first standalone filings (fqx_roic_ttm) would be the cleaner read once two quarters exist. (2) The 730-day window contains both "not yet trading" and "already re-rated" states; without a listing-date anchor the "entry" timing the AC insists on is not represented. (3) `_roce_oneoff_suspect` is applied in greenblatt but not here, so a roce > 100% on a tiny carve-out capital base passes. (4) [US-only] all three spin members are SEC-anchored (A1 §10 open).
- FMP augmentations: `insider-trading/statistics` (H, US) purchases in the spinco's first 2 quarters (A2 §B.23 exceptional); `key-executives` (S, G) to see whether parent executives moved to the spinco; `historical-price-eod` for "already delivered" (ts_r26 since listing); global spin anchors (A2 §A.4).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] "not already delivered": exclude names up > 50% since their first panel bar (ts_r26 / return since listing) from the core; keep as watch (AC: "templates, not entries"). 2 [spirit] core adds Greenblatt #2 where observable: insider buying at the spinco in the first two quarters (US) as the exceptional tier (A2 §B.23 open), and parent-executive continuity from key-executives as a score term. 3 [method] apply ~_roce_oneoff_suspect and prefer fqx_roic_ttm once >= 2 standalone quarters exist. 4 [data] global spin reach (A1 A.5 / A2 §A.4 open).

### arch_spinoff_value  (line 8060)

Comment: "the classic forced-selling / orphan discount — a viable operating business trading cheap on an OPERATING yield. Leverage-agnostic (no balance-sheet cap)".
- As coded: _spin == 1; is_operating; _not_melting; mcap > 0; _op_viable(-5%); value = non-cyclicals: (fcf_yield >= 8% | 1/p_e >= 8% | EBIT/EV >= 10% | EBITDA/EV >= 10%); Energy/Materials: mid-cycle EBITDA / EV >= 10% where a margin history exists, else EBITDA/EV >= 10% or FCF yield >= 8%. No lenses.
- Fidelity: spin event — captured (US). Cheap on an operating yield — captured (four lenses; the mid-cycle lens for cyclicals is an engine refinement consistent with AC §II.A "size for the break"). Forced-selling BOTTOM — missing (the AC's method is to buy AFTER the technical selling clears: "The bottom is usually after the technical selling clears" — nothing reads the post-listing price path; A2 §B.23 open). Insider incentives — missing. "Why mispriced now" — unmeasured. (2) The four yield lenses ORed at 8-10% on first-period carve-out earnings: a separation year with a one-off gain gives a fat 1/p_e; the D&A-immune EBITDA lens is the right instinct but is also ORed, not required. (3) A spin whose window has 20 months left and which has already re-rated still fires (no return-since-listing test). (4) `_spin` = 730 days from the 10-12B filing; the AC's drift window is measured from distribution.
