- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] core = spin AND orphan price action (ts_r13 <= -10% or ts_dist_hi52 <= 0.8 within 6 months of the first bar) AND cheap; plain spin + cheap = watch (A2 §B.23 open). 2 [method] require the EBITDA/EV (or mid-cycle) lens alongside any P/E-based lens in the first 4 quarters after listing (separation one-offs). 3 [spirit] surface `dirty_spin_flag` (nde > 4 at listing) without gating. 4 [data] global anchors (A1 A.5 / A2 §A.4 open).

### arch_templeton_pessimism  (line 6177; spirited)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents. Templeton's published maxims: "buy at the point of maximum pessimism"; "search worldwide"; value on a NORMALISED multi-year earnings base (his practice: price against five-year average or forward-normal earnings, "a P/E below 5 on that basis"). Comment: "Cheap against the company's OWN mid-cycle earnings (EV / normalized 5yr EBITDA ...), bought when the price sits near the bottom of its 5-year range / below its 5yr average. Survivability-gated so it is pessimism, not terminal decline."
- As coded: is_operating; EV / mid-cycle EBITDA <= 8 (mid-cycle margin x TTM revenue) or, where absent, EV / normalized EBIT <= 10; near-low = (price_pct_of_5y_range <= 0.35 | price_vs_5y_avg <= 0.85 | ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.65); _dd52 <= -15%; (fcf > 0 | ebitda > 0 | net cash >= 30%); _roce_now_ok. Lenses: ts_dist_hi260 (-), ev_norm_midcyc (-), tc_min_opm.
- Fidelity: cheap on normalised earnings — captured (the margin-based mid-cycle is inflation-neutral; a good engine choice). "Maximum pessimism" — approximated by price position only: 35% below the 5-year high (or below the 5-year average) is an ordinary drawdown, not the point of maximum pessimism — A2 §B.29 (<= 0.5, "1,330 of 2,033") open; and pessimism has a SENTIMENT side (downgrades, estimate cuts, holders leaving) that is not read anywhere although sent_* / 13F change fields exist. Worldwide search — captured by lineage (global). Survivability — captured (Templeton did buy loss-makers at the trough; the mild _roce_now_ok cut is documented and consistent).
- Lineage: enterprise_value, revenue_ttm -> M (local, same currency, so the EV/mid-cycle ratio is currency-neutral); _midcyc_margin -> multi-year statement history (EDGAR + ST, global); normalized_ebit -> EDGAR/derived; price_pct_of_5y_range, price_vs_5y_avg -> YF snapshot (stale, A1 §0.3); ts_dist_hi260, ts_dist_hi52 -> TS (fresh, global); tc_min_opm -> TC. 2,050 firers (A1 watch band).
- Methodology issues: (1) The two snapshot 5-year lenses are ORed with the fresh panel lens, so a stale Yahoo range keeps admitting names the panel shows have recovered (only the 52w veto protects). (2) EV / (mid-cycle margin x TTM revenue): for a name whose revenue has collapsed (secular decline) the mid-cycle margin is applied to the shrunken base, which is correct, but a name whose revenue DOUBLED via acquisition gets the old margin on the new base — acquisitive names read cheap. (3) `_ev_norm_ebit <= 10` fallback is a different numerator base (EBIT vs EBITDA) under a different cut — fine in intent, but the two populations are not comparable in the lens ranking. (4) No EV sanity band (FX-corrupt EV -> ev_norm ~0 passes "cheap").
- FMP augmentations: `grades-historical` / `grades` (H, US-centric) downgrade count over 6 months and `price-target-summary` cuts for the sentiment side (A2 §A.2); `institutional-ownership` investorsHoldingChange <= 0 (H, US-listed) holders leaving (A2 nuanced #7); `historical-price-eod` already gives hi260; `income-statement` cached FYs give tc_min_opm (0 calls).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] near-low = ts_dist_hi260 <= 0.5 as core (A2 §B.29 open), snapshot lenses to watch. 2 [spirit] add a sentiment-pessimism lens where any perception field exists (net downgrades >= 2 in 6 months, or target cuts, or 13F holders down) as the exceptional tier — price alone is not "maximum pessimism". 3 [method] EV/mcap 0.2-5 sanity band on ev_norm; guard acquisitive revenue jumps (revenue TTM vs 3y average > 1.5x -> use the 3y-average revenue base). 4 [data] tc_min_opm > 0 "survived" in the exceptional (A2 §B.29 open).

No lenses (score at 6305).

"Flat or shrinking share count" — approximated loosely: +5%/yr and +10% 3-year CAGR (~33% over three years) are inside the MCC LOSER profile (61% over the whole run), not "flat"; author "flat or shrinking; no serial dilution" vs code `shares_yoy < 0.05 & shares_3y_cagr < 0.10` (soft: missing passes). Global but the share leg is snapshot.

(3) `cash_conversion > 0` in the inflection branch reads falsely positive when CFO and EBITDA are both negative (the engine's own gate-audit #3 caveat) — the `earnings_yield > 0` AND protects only where earnings are positive. ### arch_tenbagger_path  (line 6253)
- Source recipe: MR §II "Universal consensus (all studies agree): Small cap at entry. Some measure of profitability present and robust. Undervalued relative to intrinsic worth ... Yartseva's single most important driver: FCF/P ... Growth in some dimension (but Yartseva shows it is the LEVEL of profitability ... not the GROWTH RATE)"; AC §II.B: "Market cap: core < $100m; ceiling < $500m. Revenue growth >= 25% YoY and accelerating. Profitability: profitable or at a clear inflection. ROE/ROIC >= 20% sustained. Valuation: room to expand (majority of winners < 3x sales / < 20x EBITDA / < 30x P/E). Balance sheet: net cash preferred. Insider ownership meaningful." AC §1 Mayer "small starting market cap (< $1bn, ideally < $500m)". Comment: the engine's own "10x arithmetic": implied_10x = (1+g)^10 x terminal margin x 18 / (P/S) must clear 10.
- As coded: mcap 0-$10B; revenue_ttm_usd >= $5M; P/S (fallback EV/S) in (0, 30); g10 = median of five growth bases (rev_yoy, 3y CAGR, 5y CAGR, annualised TTM-seq, fq TTM) clipped 0-50% >= 15%; (>= 2 bases >= 15% | rev_growth_score >= 0.5); op leverage (oper_lev_any | EBIT growth > rev | EBITDA growth > rev); viable econ (GM >= 20% | ebitda > 0 | op > 0); (_profit_present | (GM >= 40% & FCF first-positive/inflection)); implied_10x >= 10; is_operating. No lenses (score at 6305).
- Fidelity: growth printing and confirmed — captured (median of bases is a sound base-effect guard). Profitability LEVEL (Yartseva) — captured (A1 done). Small cap — loosened: author "< $100m core; ceiling < $500m" (AC), "< $1bn" (Mayer) vs code `mcap < 10e9`. Undervalued / "room to expand" — approximated by P/S < 30, which is not a valuation test (the AC's own bands are < 3x sales / < 20x EBITDA / < 30x P/E); the arithmetic is meant to carry it but A1 measured the median implied return at 33x, so it "almost always closes" — the A1 A.2 "drop the rev_growth_score escape" is open. FCF/P (Yartseva's top driver) — missing as a leg. Net cash — missing. Insider ownership — missing (elsewhere). Low coverage — missing. Acceleration (AC "and accelerating") — missing.
- Lineage: rev_yoy, revenue_3y/5y_cagr (EDGAR + ST fills), rev_qoq_ttm -> M; fq_rev_growth -> FQ; p_s, ev_sales -> M snapshot; op_margin, ebitda_margin, gross_margin -> M; revenue_ttm_usd -> M converted. Unit-free arithmetic (documented). 2,034 firers (A1 watch band).
- Methodology issues: (1) [basis mismatch] term_margin takes max(op_margin, 0.65 x EBITDA margin, 12%) clipped 10-22% as a NET margin proxy and applies an 18x P/E to it; op_margin is pre-tax, so the implied return is overstated by roughly the tax wedge for every profitable name. (2) The floor of 12% on term_margin means a 5%-margin business is modelled at 12% — the "conservative terminal margin" is not conservative below the floor. (3) [currency] the nominal CAGR bases inherit the inflation issue (lindy_growth #1): a TRY name's g10 is inflation. (4) A $9B company needs a $90B outcome — the "small equity" consensus (MR §I master formula: "Small equity") is the one leg every study agrees on and the gate does not carry it.
- FMP augmentations: `financial-growth.threeYRevenueGrowthPerShare` (H, G) per-share growth (A2 §693 EXC); `analyst-estimates` forward revenue (S, G) for the ramp (enrichment §3); `key-metrics.freeCashFlowYieldTTM` (S, G) for the Yartseva FCF/P leg; `historical-market-capitalization` (H, G) for PIT size bands.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] size tier: core mcap <= $1B, exceptional <= $500M (the consensus leg), $10B stays watch. 2 [method] term_margin after tax (x 0.75) with no 12% floor below the name's own margin, so the arithmetic can actually fail. 3 [spirit] add the AC valuation band as the exceptional tier: P/S <= 3 or EV/EBITDA <= 20, and FCF/P >= 3% (Yartseva) or net cash. 4 [spirit] drop the rev_growth_score escape from the confirmation (A1 A.2 open); deflate the CAGR bases for high-inflation reporting currencies.

### arch_weinstein_stage2  (line 3067; core at 3082)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents (MR §II cites O'Neil/Tortoriello for the near-high rule only). Stan Weinstein (Secrets for Profiting in Bull and Bear Markets): Stage 2 begins when price breaks above the Stage 1 base and above a 30-week MA that has stopped falling and turns up, on volume at least double the recent average, with the Mansfield relative-strength line improving and preferably positive; buy at the breakout (2A) or the first pullback; avoid heavy overhead resistance; the sector and the market should also be in Stage 2. Comment: "[C] price advancing above its long trend into little overhead resistance ... [P] 30-week MA slope unavailable -> proxied by 12m momentum > 0 and upper 5y-range".
- As coded (watch): live tape; momentum_12m > 0 & price_pct_of_5y_range >= 0.55; rel_pct_52w_high >= 0.8 | rel_is_52w_high; pct_off_52w_high >= -10% | is_52w_high; momentum_12m > -5%. Core (_tier, where ts_weinstein_stage present): stage == 2 & above 30-week MA & Mansfield RS > 0 & ts_dist_hi52 >= 0.90 & ts_r52 <= +100%. Exceptional (2A): MA slope over 13 weeks < 3% & (RS was <= 0 13 weeks ago | 4-week volume spike >= 2x). Lenses: ts_mrs, ts_ma30_slope13 (-), ts_vol_spike4, ts_dist_hi52.
- Fidelity: rising 30-week MA — captured in the core (A1 A.1 done); the watch's 12-month-momentum proxy is what A1 measured at 38% of INV passing one leg. Mansfield RS — captured (core requires > 0; the watch comment allows improving-but-negative, the core does not — the stricter reading is closer to Weinstein's "preferably positive"). Breakout volume >= 2x — exceptional only. Little overhead resistance — approximated by "within 10% of the 52-WEEK high"; Weinstein's overhead is prior SUPPLY, i.e. multi-year highs — a name at a 52-week high but 40% below its 5-year high has heavy overhead (ts_dist_hi260 exists). Sector in Stage 2 — missing. Market in Stage 2 (M) — exceptional only in oneil, absent here. "Not extended" (ts_r52 <= 100%) — engine addition; Weinstein's "buy the breakout, not late Stage 2" is consistent with it.
- Lineage: momentum_12m, price_pct_of_5y_range, rel_pct_52w_high, pct_off_52w_high, is_52w_high -> YF/lynch-tape snapshot (stale; A1 §0.3); ts_weinstein_stage, ts_above_ma30, ts_mrs, ts_ma30_slope13, ts_vol_spike4 -> TS (weekly, global; MRS vs an equal-weight pseudo-index, A1 D#4). 4,307 watch firers (A1: 17.7% of INV); core 216 measured (A2).
- Methodology issues: (1) The watch is snapshot-only and keeps 4,307 names alive as `weinstein_stage2_watch` where the panel is absent; the panel covers 82% of INV after the re-run (A1 §0.2 open), so the watch population is the least-liquid tail. (2) MRS against a per-market median pseudo-index skews the RS zero line (A1 D#4 open). (3) Clinical biotech leak: 60 firers (A2 Z2 open). (4) `ts_dist_hi52 >= 0.9` and `ts_r52 <= 1.0` together still admit a name 90% up on the year sitting at its high — late Stage 2 in his taxonomy; the 2A exceptional carries the "early" reading.
- FMP augmentations: `historical-price-eod` indices (H, G, ~25 calls) for a true MRS denominator (A1 D#4); `profile.industry` (S, G) + the panel for an industry-group stage (median of members above their 30-week MA); `earnings` (H, G) for the ESB breakout-window beat (A2 §B.19).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] overhead = ts_dist_hi260 >= 0.9 OR the multi-year-high flag (ts_multi_year_high) in the core; 52-week proximity alone to watch. 2 [spirit] sector Stage 2: industry-group breadth (share of the industry's members above a rising 30-week MA) >= 0.5 as the exceptional (Weinstein's "forest, not the tree"). 3 [data] real index series for MRS (A1 D#4 open); exclude is_clinical_biotech (A2 Z2 open). 4 [method] surface the `weinstein_stage` label 1-4 as a column (A1 §C.9 — ts_weinstein_stage exists; confirm it is exported).

### arch_weschler_levered_equity  (line 5660)
- Source recipe: not in the five documents (AC §II.A technique (5) "stub stocks / recaps" is the family). Comment block (5600-5612): Ted Weschler's Valassis — "traded < 1x FCF, ~$1.15B debt, EBIT covered interest even in the 2008 recession, paid down $200-305M/yr from FCF, bought back 16% of shares -> ~52%/yr for six years. The thesis works only when the company can (a) SERVICE the debt, (b) AMORTISE it from cash, and (c) has LONG-DATED maturities ... We can screen (a) and (b); we have NO debt-maturity-schedule field".
- As coded: is_operating; robust_cash_yield 15-200%; fcf_yield 8-200%; ebitda > 0; heavy_debt = not known net cash & (nde 3-30 | (EV/mcap 1.75-30 & nde >= 1.5)); fcf > 0; _not_melting; (ebitda_yoy >= 0 | ebitda_inflection > 0 | oper_lev_any); interest_coverage >= 1 soft; mcap 0-$5B. No lenses.
- Fidelity: cheap on cash (< 1x FCF) — captured (robust cash yield >= 15% = <= 6.7x, the documented "Weschler zone"). Heavy debt — captured. (a) Service — approximated: IC >= 1 SOFT (missing passes) and single-period; the comment's own test is "covered interest EVEN IN THE RECESSION" — a through-cycle minimum coverage (tc has the FYs) is the faithful reading. (b) AMORTISE from cash — MISSING as a leg: `fcf > 0` is not "paying down"; the literal proof exists (fq_deleveraging_flag / fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets, wired into oak_deleveraging) and A1 A.4 flagged exactly this ("these use EBITDA rising as the proxy") — open. Buybacks (16% of shares) — missing (shares_yoy < 0 / net_buyback). (c) Maturities — unscreenable (documented; A2 says no endpoint).
- Lineage: robust_cash_yield, fcf_yield -> M derived (USD-consistent); net_debt_ebitda -> M (NaN-preserving here, good); enterprise_value / market_cap -> M local (FX-neutral ratio, documented); interest_coverage -> EDGAR + fq_interest_cover fill (global TTM); ebitda_yoy, ebitda_inflection -> M. 673 firers (A2).
- Methodology issues: (1) [IFRS 16] net debt for lease-heavy filers (retail, airlines, restaurants) includes lease liabilities: nde 3-30 flags a lessee as a "levered stub" with no amortising term debt — the Valassis mechanism (fixed debt paid from FCF) does not apply; fq lease liabilities would separate them. (2) `_soft_ok_above('interest_coverage', 1.0)` — a name with IC 1.05 passes "can service"; the comment's standard is coverage through a recession. (3) `heavy_debt`'s EV path corroborated by nde >= 1.5 admits nde 1.5-3 names as "enormous debt burden" via EV/mcap >= 1.75 — the two legs disagree on what "heavy" means. (4) robust_cash_yield's median of (FCF, CFO, earnings) yields: for a levered name CFO yield >> FCF yield, so the median can sit on CFO; the separate fcf_yield >= 8% floor is what keeps this honest — good, keep.
- FMP augmentations: fq deleveraging fields already computed (0 calls); `ratios.debtServiceCoverageRatioTTM` (S, G) — principal + interest vs cash flow (A2 nuanced #13); `income-statement.interestExpense` 8 FYs cached -> tc minimum coverage (0 calls); `balance-sheet-statement` lease liabilities (H, G) for the IFRS-16 adjustment; `cash-flow-statement.debtRepayment` (H, G) literal amortisation.
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] require the amortisation leg: fq_deleveraging_flag == 1 or fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets < -5% or annual debtRepayment > netDebtIssuance (A1 A.4 open); "EBITDA rising" alone to watch. 2 [spirit] service = through-cycle minimum interest coverage >= 1.5 over the cached FYs (the "even in the recession" test), or DSCR >= 1 (A2 nuanced #13 open). 3 [method] lease-adjusted net debt where fq lease liabilities exist, so lessees are not stubs. 4 [spirit] buyback lens (shares_yoy < 0 or net_buyback_ttm > 0) as the exceptional tier — Weschler's second engine.

"Cost cutting ... breakeven on SMALLER revenue" (Zoomd) — the code requires rev_yoy >= 0 (the engine's SBBC/Thermal reading), which EXCLUDES the Zoomd shape the document cites as the evidence: author "breakeven on smaller revenue" vs code `(rev_yoy_c >= 0.0) & rev_present`; and the cost-cut itself (SG&A ratio falling) is not read — only emd >= 0. Non-recurring charges in history — missing (impairment / goodwill write-down evidence exists in fq history). New management / strategic review — missing (8-K 5.02 US; key-executives has no tenure). Disposal of a unit — missing (fq_disc_ops_share exists and is read by fq_deleveraging_flag). P/E < 6 — code < 20 (blog cheap-entry rule; documented). Insider doubling down — missing (Form 4 US).
- Lineage: first-positive / inflection flags -> M annual (YF-derived); fqx_eps_turned -> FQX (global, date-matched TTM); bs_ebit_turned -> base_snapshot (partial); op_margin, ebitda_margin_delta_yoy, rev_yoy -> M; ev_ebitda, p_e -> M snapshot; revenue_ttm_usd -> M converted. 731 firers (A2).
- Methodology issues: (1) cfo_first_positive / fcf_inflection fire alone: working-capital swings (A1 B.1 kpi_threshold: 52% of firers on FCF-first-positive, the noisiest) count as "crossing into the black" with no P&L turn. (2) The growth requirement contradicts the source's own cost-cut evidence; the engine's reason (avoid a declining name) is legitimate but should be an alternative (growing OR SG&A ratio down >= 3pp with gross profit flat), not a gate. (3) op_margin < 15% cap uses the impairment-contaminated GAAP margin — the engine notes this for the lower bound (_op_viable) but not the upper (a name with a big impairment reads < 15% and passes as "not an established earner"). (4) No dilution / reverse-split guard in the cohort where "sucker" recaps live (the document's Archetype 10 reject "serial recap efforts").
- FMP augmentations: `income-statement` cached FYs for SG&A / revenue trend (0 calls; the "Recent SG&A >> revenue" then falling); `sec-filings-8k` item 5.02 (H, US) new CEO/CFO within 18 months; `insider-trading/statistics` (H, US) buying after the turn; `splits` (H, G) reverse-split veto; fq_disc_ops_share already computed for the disposal lens; `key-executives` (S, G) for a CEO change cross-check (no tenure field).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] cost-cut leg: SG&A / revenue down >= 3pp YoY (fq or cached FY) or gross-margin-led turn, as an alternative to rev_yoy >= 0 — restores the Zoomd shape. 2 [method] a stand-alone CFO/FCF first-positive counts only with an EBITDA or EPS turn (fqx_eps_turned / bs_ebit_turned / ebitda_first_pos). 3 [spirit] exceptional tier with the document's evidence where observable: disposal (fq_disc_ops_share > 0 in the prior year and ~0 now), new management (8-K 5.02, US), insider buying (US), P/E < 6. 4 [method] reverse-split / dilution veto (shares_yoy <= 10%, no split ratio < 1 in 12 months; A2 nuanced #10).

### arch_wolf_value_catalyst  (line 3270)
- Source recipe: the engine repurposed this from a net-net screen (comment: "He is NOT a net-net investor; his real shape is a growing, cash-generative microcap with a fortress balance sheet at a cheap FCF yield (Progressive Planet: ~12% FCF yld, ~$32M cap, growing)" — blog, outside the documents). The WO document's frame: exec summary "we buy 'underpriced hard evidence + improving fundamentals + an identifiable catalyst'"; Archetype 4 example MTLC "profitable at ~$30M market cap (0.3x sales, 5x earnings) with the upside of nearly $100M guidance"; Archetype 7 catalysts "Year-end or quarter-end wins ... Unexpected dividend initiation or tender".
- As coded: mcap 0-$200M; (net cash >= 20% | cash > EV | NCAV >= 50% | (fcf_yield >= 10% & nde <= 1.5)); (rev_yoy >= 10% | (rev_growth_score >= 0.5 & rev_yoy >= 0)); cfo > 0; _not_melting; (fcf_yield >= 8% | ev_ebitda < 6); is_operating. No lenses.
- Fidelity: growing, cash-generative, cheap on FCF, sound balance sheet — captured (the fortress leg is now optional when FCF yield >= 10% and nde <= 1.5 — documented gate-audit change, reasonable). The CATALYST — absent entirely: the archetype is named for it and the document's formula has it as the third ingredient, but nothing dated is read (A1 A.4 "press-release / 8-K dated catalyst" open; A2 §596 "next earnings within 45d or 8-K <= 90d" LEG open). Guidance upside (MTLC) — missing. Insider buying — missing. "Underpriced hard evidence" — approximated by the cash / FCF yield legs.
- Lineage: net_cash_pct_mcap, cash_gt_ev_flag, ncav_pct_mcap, fcf_yield, nde, rev_yoy, cfo_ttm, ev_ebitda -> M (YF/FMP snapshot; mcap-relative or ratio, currency-neutral except quote-unit lines); rev_growth_score -> derived composite. 732 firers (A2). Global.
- Methodology issues: (1) The EV/EBITDA < 6 alternative has no EV sanity band, so an FX-corrupt EV reads as "cheap" (the midcap_garp guard is not applied). (2) `rev_growth_score >= 0.5 & rev_yoy >= 0` admits a flat year on a composite — "growing" should be a present positive print. (3) ncav_pct >= 0.5 as a "fortress" alternative admits a name whose NCAV is half its cap with net debt (NCAV nets debt but 50% coverage is not a fortress). (4) Without a catalyst leg the archetype is a duplicate of a cheap-growth-cash screen (overlaps sustainable_scaler / net_cash_returner), which is the A1 reason it sits at 732 with no distinguishing lens.
- FMP augmentations: `earnings-calendar` (H, G, ~52 calls/yr) next report <= 45 days; `sec-filings-8k` (H, US) items 1.01 / 2.01 / 8.01 <= 90 days; `news/press-releases` (H, G) count spike; `dividends` (H, G) initiation event (the document's "unexpected dividend initiation"); `analyst-estimates` (S, G) forward revenue >= +25% as the guidance lens; `insider-trading/search` (H, US).
- Suggestions: 1 [spirit] add the catalyst as a scored leg now and a core leg where any catalyst feed exists: earnings within 45 days, an 8-K material event <= 90 days (US), a press-release spike, a dividend initiation (dividends history: first payment in 5 years), or a tender (A1 A.4 / A2 §596 open). 2 [method] EV/mcap 0.2-5 sanity on the EV/EBITDA lens; "growing" = rev_yoy >= 10% present (composite to watch). 3 [spirit] guidance lens: forward revenue consensus >= +25% vs TTM as the exceptional tier (the MTLC shape). 4 [method] fortress alternatives: NCAV >= 100% or net cash >= 20% (drop the 50% NCAV leg).

---

## 12-line summary (slice "sources", 44 archetypes)

1. Fidelity is highest where the source is a named checklist (Cundill, Greenblatt, F&P, Lynch multiples, the lindy family): the FORM is coded; the divergences are in the growth denominators (single-year vs long-term), the ROC basis (provider ROCE vs tangible capital), and thresholds applied to two different statistics under one name (min vs median, pre-tax vs after-tax, NI vs EPS).
2. Fidelity is lowest where the source archetype is an EVENT and the engine coded a fundamentals proxy: wolf_seal (a dated contract/news screen became inflection + momentum), wolf_value_catalyst (no catalyst leg at all), oak_order_conversion (the forward book is one OR alternative; the document's own query is backlog/revenue), oak_nav_discount (P/B for NAV, no cash+stakes, no overhead, no debt leg, a yield the source never asks for).
3. The Wolf / Oak recipes carry explicit quantitative filters the code loosens 2-20x: order-conversion cap £50M -> $1B; deep-value P/B < 0.5 -> 0.7 or a composite; deleveraging payout >= 5% -> removed; resource EV/EBITDA < 6 -> 8; turnaround P/E < 6 -> 20; compounder growth > 50% -> 25%. Each is documented as a blog reading, but the source tiers exist and could be the exceptional tier at no cost.
4. Three legs recur as MISSING across the slice although the data exists: share-count / dilution (liger x3, wolf x5, oak_deep_value, tenbagger_credible's soft defaults), a leverage guard (wolf_trifecta, oak_nav_discount, spinoff value/asset by directive), and any dated catalyst (wolf_seal, wolf_value_catalyst, liger_lagging_inflect's confirmation point).
5. `oper_lev_any` is the slice's most reused proxy and its weakest: it fires on a raw sequential uptick the engine's own seasonality rule disallows, and in wolf_trifecta / liger_lagging_inflect it stands in for TWO source legs (margins up AND leverage). A1 A.4 (TTM incremental margin as the measure) remains open everywhere it was named.
6. Deleveraging is proxied by a rising DENOMINATOR (EBITDA up) or a financing outflow that includes dividends in oak_deleveraging and weschler_levered_equity, while the literal numerator proof (fq_deleveraging_flag / fq_netdebt_change_pct_assets) is either one ORed alternative or unused (A1 A.4 open).
7. SEC/US-only legs: the entire spin-off sleeve (Form 10 / 10-12B anchors), roic_after_sbc, buyback_yield / capital_return_yield / financing_cf, insider buying, RPO, exec comp; oneil's S and I; every "catalyst" feed named in A2 is US-first. Global reach for the event family depends on the profile ipoDate route (A1 A.5 / A2 §A.4 open).
8. Snapshot-only legs still gate: flat_or_down (liger_lagging_inflect) and the Weinstein/Kullamägi/O'Neil WATCH rules read the stale Yahoo tape (A1 §0.3), templeton's two 5-year lenses, cundill's c2 fallbacks, dividend_yield everywhere (quote-unit risk on GBp/ZAc — A2 §C open). The panel fixes reach the core tiers only.
9. Currency / unit-sensitive legs: nominal local-currency CAGRs (lindy_growth, tenbagger g10, lynch_reward progress) pass on inflation in TRY/ARS/EGP; EV-multiple lenses in lynch_evgy, wolf_trifecta, wolf_value_catalyst, templeton and greenblatt have no EV/mcap sanity band (the midcap_garp guard is not shared).
10. Fallbacks that change meaning: lynch_evgy's psg/evsg branch (sales growth under an EBITDA-growth name); lynch_pegy's clipped Yahoo growth re-entering at the band's top; lindy_margin's median-for-minimum chain; greenblatt's after-tax ROIC for pre-tax ROCE; `_liger_cov` / n_analysts missing -> 0 = "most neglected" (A1 D#2 open); liger_neglected_survivor reads a different coverage field from its siblings.
11. Still-open prior-audit items that recur most: A1 D#2 observed neglect (liger x3), A1 D#4 real index series (weinstein, oneil, kullamägi, BAB), A1 D#5 earnings universe-wide (wolf_seal, oneil C, kullamägi EP), A2 Z2 clinical-biotech exclusion (kullamägi 92, weinstein 60, oneil 5), A2 nuanced #10 reverse-split veto, A2 §A.1 financial-growth per-share fields (lindy_growth, lynch, tenbagger, cash_reinvest).
12. Two structural suggestions for the slice: (i) for every practitioner archetype, encode the document's own numeric table row as the exceptional tier and the engine's blog reading as the core, so the source is recoverable from the code; (ii) give the event-named archetypes (seal, value_catalyst, order_conversion, spinoffs) a dated leg from the feeds A2 already catalogued, with the current fundamentals-only rule demoted to `_watch`.
